  | 'mengine'

}

import type { BidhaaKamili } from '../types'

// ============================================================
// Hesabu siku zilizobaki kwa bidhaa
// ============================================================
export function sikuZilizobaki(expiry?: string): number | undefined {
  if (!expiry) return undefined
  const leo = new Date()
  const tarehe = new Date(expiry)
  const tofauti = Math.ceil((tarehe.getTime() - leo.getTime()) / (1000 * 60 * 60 * 24))
  return tofauti
}

// ============================================================
// Hali ya expiry (FR5)
// ============================================================
export type HaliExpiry = 'salama' | 'inaribia' | 'imeisha' | 'hakuna'

export function haliExpiry(expiry?: string): HaliExpiry {
  const siku = sikuZilizobaki(expiry)
  if (siku === undefined) return 'hakuna'
  if (siku <= 0) return 'imeisha'
  if (siku <= 30) return 'inaribia'
  return 'salama'
}

// ============================================================
// Rangi ya hali ya expiry
// ============================================================
export function rangiExpiry(hali: HaliExpiry): string {
  switch (hali) {
    case 'imeisha':
      return 'text-red-700 border-red-200'
    case 'inaribia':
      return 'text-amber-700 border-amber-200'
    case 'salama':
      return 'text-emerald-700 border-emerald-200'
    default:
      return 'text-slate-700 border-slate-200'
  }
}

// ============================================================
// Chuja bidhaa zinazokaribia kuisha (FR5)
// ============================================================
export function bidhaaZinazokaribiaKuisha(bidhaa: BidhaaKamili[]): BidhaaKamili[] {
  return bidhaa.filter((b) => {
    const hali = haliExpiry(b.expiry)
    return hali === 'inaribia' || hali === 'imeisha'
  })
}


import type {
  MauzoYaDuka,
  GharamaOpex,
  FaidaKamili,
  GharamaZote,
} from '../types'

// ============================================================
// Hesabu ya Faida Ghafi kwa mauzo moja
// ============================================================
export function hesabuFaidaGhafi(mauzo: MauzoYaDuka) {
  return mauzo.jumlaMauzo - mauzo.cogs
}

// ============================================================
// Jumla ya gharama za OPEX kwa duka
// ============================================================
export function jumlaOpex(gharama: GharamaOpex[], dukaId?: string) {
  return gharama
    .filter((g) => !dukaId || g.dukaId === dukaId)
    .reduce((s, g) => s + g.kiasi, 0)
}

// ============================================================
// Gharama kwa aina (mishahara, kodi, n.k.)
// ============================================================
export function gharamaKwaAina(
  gharama: GharamaOpex[],
  dukaId?: string
): GharamaZote {
  const filtered = gharama.filter((g) => !dukaId || g.dukaId === dukaId)
  const sum = (aina: string) =>
    filtered.filter((g) => g.aina === aina).reduce((s, g) => s + g.kiasi, 0)

  const result: GharamaZote = {
    mishahara: sum('mishahara'),
    kodi: sum('kodi'),
    umeme: sum('umeme'),
    mafuta: sum('mafuta'),
    usafiri: sum('usafiri'),
    chakula: sum('chakula'),
    matengenezo: sum('matengenezo'),
    vifaa: sum('vifaa'),
    bima: sum('bima'),
    leseni: sum('leseni'),
    maji: sum('maji'),
    nyingine: sum('nyingine'),
    jumla: 0,
  }

  result.jumla =
    result.mishahara +
    result.kodi +
    result.umeme +
    result.mafuta +
    result.usafiri +
    result.chakula +
    result.matengenezo +
    result.vifaa +
    result.bima +
    result.leseni +
    result.maji +
    result.nyingine

  return result
}

// ============================================================
// Hesabu Kamili ya Faida
// ============================================================
export function hesabuFaidaKamili(
  mauzo: MauzoYaDuka[],
  opex: GharamaOpex[],
  dukaId?: string
): FaidaKamili {
  const filteredMauzo = mauzo.filter((m) => !dukaId || m.dukaId === dukaId)
  const filteredOpex = opex.filter((g) => !dukaId || g.dukaId === dukaId)

  const jumlaMauzo = filteredMauzo.reduce((s, m) => s + m.jumlaMauzo, 0)
  const jumlaCogs = filteredMauzo.reduce((s, m) => s + m.cogs, 0)
  const faidaGhafi = jumlaMauzo - jumlaCogs
  const jumlaOpexZote = filteredOpex.reduce((s, g) => s + g.kiasi, 0)
  const faidaSafi = faidaGhafi - jumlaOpexZote

  const faidaKwaMungu = faidaSafi > 0 ? faidaSafi : 0
  const zaka = faidaKwaMungu * 0.1
  const sadaka = faidaKwaMungu * 0.1
  const jumlaYaMungu = zaka + sadaka
  const inayobaki = faidaSafi - jumlaYaMungu

  return {
    mauzo: jumlaMauzo,
    cogs: jumlaCogs,
    faidaGhafi,
    jumlaOpex: jumlaOpexZote,
    faidaSafi,
    zaka,
    sadaka,
    jumlaYaMungu,
    inayobaki,
    hasara: faidaSafi < 0,
  }
}

// ============================================================
// Faida kwa kila bidhaa
// ============================================================
export function faidaKwaBidhaa(mauzo: MauzoYaDuka[]) {
  const map = new Map<
    string,
    { jina: string; sku: string; wingi: number; mauzo: number; cogs: number; faida: number }
  >()

  for (const m of mauzo) {
    const ratio = m.jumlaMauzo > 0 ? m.cogs / m.jumlaMauzo : 0
    for (const b of m.bidhaa) {
      const ex = map.get(b.id)
      if (ex) {
        ex.wingi += b.wingi
        ex.mauzo += b.jumla
        ex.cogs += b.jumla * ratio
        ex.faida = ex.mauzo - ex.cogs
      } else {
        const cogs = b.jumla * ratio
        map.set(b.id, {
          jina: b.jina,
          sku: b.sku,
          wingi: b.wingi,
          mauzo: b.jumla,
          cogs,
          faida: b.jumla - cogs,
        })
      }
    }
  }

  return Array.from(map.values()).sort((a, b) => b.faida - a.faida)
}

// ============================================================
// Format pesa
// ============================================================
export function formatTsh(kiasi: number): string {
  return `Tsh ${Math.round(kiasi).toLocaleString()}`
}

import { Package, Store, User, Wallet, Receipt } from 'lucide-react'
import type { LucideIcon } from 'lucide-react'
import { bidhaa, maduka, wafanyakazi, madeni, gharama } from '../data/demo'

export type SearchType = 'bidhaa' | 'duka' | 'mtumiaji' | 'deni' | 'gharama'

export interface SearchResult {
  type: SearchType
  title: string
  subtitle: string
  Icon: LucideIcon
  route?: string
}

export function searchEverything(query: string): SearchResult[] {
  const q = query.trim().toLowerCase()
  if (!q) return []

  const results: SearchResult[] = []

  bidhaa.forEach((b) => {
    if (
      b.jina.toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
      b.sku.toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
      b.kundi.toLowerCase().includes(q)
    ) {
      results.push({
        type: 'bidhaa',
        title: b.jina,
        subtitle: `${b.sku} - Tsh ${b.beiMauzo.toLocaleString()} - ${b.duka}`,
        Icon: Package,
        route: '/mteja/bidhaa',
      })
    }
  })

  maduka.forEach((d) => {
    if (d.jina.toLowerCase().includes(q) || d.mahali.toLowerCase().includes(q)) {
      results.push({
        type: 'duka',
        title: d.jina,
        subtitle: d.mahali,
        Icon: Store,
        route: '/mmiliki/maduka',
      })
    }
  })

  wafanyakazi.forEach((w) => {
    if (
      w.jina.toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
      w.simu.includes(q) ||
      w.role.toLowerCase().includes(q)
    ) {
      results.push({
        type: 'mtumiaji',
        title: w.jina,
        subtitle: `${w.role} - ${w.duka} - ${w.simu}`,
        Icon: User,
        route: '/mmiliki/wafanyakazi',
      })
    }
  })

  madeni.forEach((d) => {
    if (d.mteja.toLowerCase().includes(q) || d.simu.includes(q)) {
      results.push({
        type: 'deni',
        title: d.mteja,
        subtitle: `Tsh ${d.kiasi.toLocaleString()} - ${d.hali}`,
        Icon: Wallet,
        route: '/mteja/deni',
      })
    }
  })

  gharama.forEach((g) => {
    if (g.aina.toLowerCase().includes(q) || g.maelezo.toLowerCase().includes(q)) {
      results.push({
        type: 'gharama',
        title: g.aina,
        subtitle: `Tsh ${g.kiasi.toLocaleString()} - ${g.maelezo}`,
        Icon: Receipt,
      })
    }
  })

  return results.slice(0, 8)
}

import type { MauzoYaDuka, GharamaOpex, ZakaKipindi } from '../types'
import { hesabuFaidaKamili } from './faida'

// ============================================================
// Hesabu ya Zaka kwa Kipindi
// ============================================================
export function hesabuZakaKwaKipindi(
  mauzo: MauzoYaDuka[],
  opex: GharamaOpex[]
): ZakaKipindi[] {
  // Kundi kwa tarehe
  const map = new Map<string, { mauzo: MauzoYaDuka[]; opex: GharamaOpex[] }>()

  for (const m of mauzo) {
    const entry = map.get(m.tarehe) || { mauzo: [], opex: [] }
    entry.mauzo.push(m)
    map.set(m.tarehe, entry)
  }

  for (const o of opex) {
    const entry = map.get(o.tarehe) || { mauzo: [], opex: [] }
    entry.opex.push(o)
    map.set(o.tarehe, entry)
  }

  // Hesabu kwa kila tarehe
  const result: ZakaKipindi[] = []
  const tareheZote = Array.from(map.keys()).sort()

  for (const tarehe of tareheZote) {
    const { mauzo: m, opex: o } = map.get(tarehe)!
    const f = hesabuFaidaKamili(m, o)
    result.push({
      tarehe,
      faidaHalisi: f.faidaSafi,
      zaka: f.zaka,
      sadaka: f.sadaka,
      jumlaYaMungu: f.jumlaYaMungu,
      salioLako: f.inayobaki,
    })
  }

  return result
}

// ============================================================
// Jumla ya Zaka kwa Kipindi
// ============================================================
export function jumlaZaka(vipindi: ZakaKipindi[]) {
  return vipindi.reduce(
    (a, f) => ({
      faida: a.faida + f.faidaHalisi,
      zaka: a.zaka + f.zaka,
      sadaka: a.sadaka + f.sadaka,
      jumlaYaMungu: a.jumlaYaMungu + f.jumlaYaMungu,
      salioLako: a.salioLako + f.salioLako,
    }),
    { faida: 0, zaka: 0, sadaka: 0, jumlaYaMungu: 0, salioLako: 0 }
  )
}

// ============================================================
// Chuja kwa kipindi
// ============================================================
export function chujaKwaKipindi(
  vipindi: ZakaKipindi[],
  kipindi: 'siku' | 'wiki' | 'mwezi' | 'mwaka',
  tarehe?: string
): ZakaKipindi[] {
  if (kipindi === 'siku') {
    if (!tarehe) return vipindi
    return vipindi.filter((v) => v.tarehe === tarehe)
  }

  const ref = tarehe ? new Date(tarehe) : new Date()
  const year = ref.getFullYear()
  const month = ref.getMonth()
  const day = ref.getDate()

  if (kipindi === 'mwaka') {
    return vipindi.filter((v) => {
      const d = new Date(v.tarehe)
      return d.getFullYear() === year
    })
  }

  if (kipindi === 'mwezi') {
    return vipindi.filter((v) => {
      const d = new Date(v.tarehe)
      return d.getFullYear() === year && d.getMonth() === month
    })
  }

  if (kipindi === 'wiki') {
    const weekStart = new Date(ref)
    weekStart.setDate(day - weekStart.getDay())
    weekStart.setHours(0, 0, 0, 0)
    const weekEnd = new Date(weekStart)
    weekEnd.setDate(weekStart.getDate() + 7)

    return vipindi.filter((v) => {
      const d = new Date(v.tarehe)
      return d >= weekStart && d < weekEnd
    })
  }

  return vipindi
}

import { useParams, useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom'
import {
  ArrowLeft, Bell, CheckCheck, Trash2, ArrowRight,
  Info, AlertTriangle, CheckCircle2, XCircle, Inbox,
} from 'lucide-react'
import { useNotifications } from '../../context/NotificationContext'
import type { AinaNotification } from '../../types'

const AINA_META: Record<
  AinaNotification,
  { color: string; Icon: React.ElementType; label: string }
> = {
  info: { color: 'bg-brand-50 text-brand-700 border-brand-200', Icon: Info, label: 'Taarifa' },
  warning: { color: 'bg-amber-50 text-amber-700 border-amber-200', Icon: AlertTriangle, label: 'Onyo' },
  success: { color: 'bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-700 border-emerald-200', Icon: CheckCircle2, label: 'Mafanikio' },
  danger: { color: 'bg-red-50 text-red-700 border-red-200', Icon: XCircle, label: 'Hatari' },
}

export default function NotificationDetail() {
  const { id } = useParams<{ id: string }>()
  const navigate = useNavigate()
  const { getNotification, markAsRead, futa } = useNotifications()

  const notif = id ? getNotification(id) : undefined

  if (!notif) {
    return (
      <div className="max-w-2xl mx-auto bg-white rounded-xl border border-slate-200 p-10 text-center">
        <Inbox size={40} className="mx-auto mb-3 text-slate-300" />
        <div className="font-semibold">Taarifa haipo</div>
        <p className="text-sm text-slate-500 mt-1">
          Taarifa uliyoitafuta haipatikani au ilifutwa.
        </p>
        <button
          onClick={() => navigate('/notifications')}
          className="mt-4 inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-4 py-2 bg-brand-600 text-white rounded-lg text-sm font-semibold hover:bg-brand-700"
        >
          <ArrowLeft size={16} />
          Rudi Taarifa
        </button>
      </div>
    )
  }

  const meta = AINA_META[notif.type]

  const handleOpenRoute = () => {
    markAsRead(notif.id)
    if (notif.route) navigate(notif.route)
  }

  const handleFuta = () => {
    futa(notif.id)
    navigate('/notifications')
  }

  return (
    <div className="max-w-2xl mx-auto space-y-4">
      <button
        onClick={() => navigate('/notifications')}
        className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 text-sm text-slate-600 hover:text-brand-600"
      >
        <ArrowLeft size={16} />
        Rudi Taarifa
      </button>

      <div className="bg-white rounded-xl border border-slate-200 p-5 md:p-6">
        <div className="flex items-start gap-3">
          <div
            className={`w-12 h-12 rounded-lg grid place-items-center shrink-0 border ${meta.color}`}
          >
            <meta.Icon size={22} />
          </div>
          <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2 flex-wrap">
              <span className={`text-[10px] uppercase font-semibold px-2 py-0.5 rounded border ${meta.color}`}>
                {meta.label}
              </span>
              {!notif.read && (
                <span className="text-[10px] uppercase font-semibold px-2 py-0.5 rounded bg-brand-600 text-white">
                  Mpya
                </span>
              )}
            </div>
            <h1 className="text-xl font-bold mt-2">{notif.title}</h1>
            <div className="text-xs text-slate-500 mt-1">{notif.time}</div>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="mt-5 pt-5 border-t border-slate-200">
          <p className="text-sm text-slate-700">{notif.message}</p>
          {notif.maelezo && (
            <p className="text-sm text-slate-600 mt-3 leading-relaxed">
              {notif.maelezo}
            </p>
          )}
        </div>

        <div className="mt-6 flex flex-col sm:flex-row gap-2">
          {notif.route && (
            <button
              onClick={handleOpenRoute}
              className="inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 px-4 py-2 bg-brand-600 text-white rounded-lg text-sm font-semibold hover:bg-brand-700"
            >
              <ArrowRight size={16} />
              Fungua Ukurasa
            </button>
          )}
          {!notif.read && (
            <button
              onClick={() => markAsRead(notif.id)}
              className="inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 px-4 py-2 bg-slate-100 rounded-lg text-sm hover:bg-slate-200"
            >
              <CheckCheck size={16} />
              Weka Kama Imesomwa
            </button>
          )}
          <button
            onClick={handleFuta}
            className="inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 px-4 py-2 bg-red-50 text-red-600 rounded-lg text-sm hover:bg-red-100 sm:ml-auto"
          >
            <Trash2 size={16} />
            Futa
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}


import { useMemo, useState } from 'react'
import { useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom'
import {
  Bell, Search, Filter, CheckCheck, Trash2, ArrowRight,
  Info, AlertTriangle, CheckCircle2, XCircle, Inbox,
  ShoppingBag, Package, Wallet, CreditCard, FileText,
  Users, MoreHorizontal, X,
} from 'lucide-react'
import { useNotifications } from '../../context/NotificationContext'
import type {
  AinaNotification,
  KundiNotification,
  Notification,
} from '../../types'

const AINA_META: Record<
  AinaNotification,
  { color: string; Icon: React.ElementType }
> = {
  info: { color: 'bg-brand-50 text-brand-700 border-brand-200', Icon: Info },
  warning: { color: 'bg-amber-50 text-amber-700 border-amber-200', Icon: AlertTriangle },
  success: { color: 'bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-700 border-emerald-200', Icon: CheckCircle2 },
  danger: { color: 'bg-red-50 text-red-700 border-red-200', Icon: XCircle },
}

const KUNDI_META: Record<
  KundiNotification,
  { label: string; Icon: React.ElementType }
> = {
  mauzo: { label: 'Mauzo', Icon: ShoppingBag },
  stoki: { label: 'Stoki', Icon: Package },
  deni: { label: 'Deni', Icon: Wallet },
  malipo: { label: 'Malipo', Icon: CreditCard },
  order: { label: 'Order', Icon: FileText },
  gharama: { label: 'Gharama', Icon: CreditCard },
  mtumiaji: { label: 'Mtumiaji', Icon: Users },
  mengine: { label: 'Mengine', Icon: MoreHorizontal },
}

const FILTERS: { key: KundiNotification | 'zote'; label: string }[] = [
  { key: 'zote', label: 'Zote' },
  { key: 'mauzo', label: 'Mauzo' },
  { key: 'stoki', label: 'Stoki' },
  { key: 'deni', label: 'Deni' },
  { key: 'malipo', label: 'Malipo' },
  { key: 'order', label: 'Order' },
  { key: 'gharama', label: 'Gharama' },
]

export default function Notifications() {
  const navigate = useNavigate()
  const {
    notifications,
    unreadCount,
    markAsRead,
    markAllRead,
    futa,
    futaZote,
    filterByKundi,
    search,
  } = useNotifications()

  const [q, setQ] = useState('')
  const [filter, setFilter] = useState<KundiNotification | 'zote'>('zote')
  const [showOnlyUnread, setShowOnlyUnread] = useState(false)

  const filtered = useMemo(() => {
    let list = search(q)
    if (filter !== 'zote') list = list.filter((n) => n.kundi === filter)
    if (showOnlyUnread) list = list.filter((n) => !n.read)
    return list
  }, [q, filter, showOnlyUnread, notifications])

  const handleClick = (n: Notification) => {
    markAsRead(n.id)
    if (n.route) navigate(n.route)
    else navigate(`/notifications/${n.id}`)
  }

  return (
    <div className="space-y-5">
      {/* Header */}
      <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-end sm:justify-between gap-3">
        <div>
          <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold flex items-center gap-2">
            <Bell size={24} className="text-brand-600" />
            Taarifa
          </h1>
          <p className="text-slate-500 text-sm">
            {notifications.length} taarifa - {unreadCount} hazijasomwa
          </p>
        </div>
        <div className="flex gap-2 self-start sm:self-auto">
          {unreadCount > 0 && (
            <button
              onClick={markAllRead}
              className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-2 bg-brand-50 text-brand-700 rounded-lg text-sm font-semibold hover:bg-brand-100"
            >
              <CheckCheck size={14} />
              Soma Zote
            </button>
          )}
          {notifications.length > 0 && (
            <button
              onClick={futaZote}
              className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-2 bg-red-50 text-red-700 rounded-lg text-sm font-semibold hover:bg-red-100"
            >
              <Trash2 size={14} />
              Futa Zote
            </button>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Filters */}
      <div className="bg-white rounded-xl border border-slate-200 p-3 md:p-4 space-y-3">
        <div className="relative">
          <Search
            size={18}
            className="absolute left-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-slate-400 pointer-events-none"
          />
          <input
            value={q}
            onChange={(e) => setQ(e.target.value)}
            placeholder="Tafuta taarifa..."
            className="w-full pl-10 pr-4 py-2.5 rounded-lg border border-slate-200 focus:border-brand-500 outline-none text-sm"
          />
        </div>
        <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2 items-center">
          <Filter size={14} className="text-slate-400" />
          {FILTERS.map((f) => (
            <button
              key={f.key}
              onClick={() => setFilter(f.key)}
              className={`px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg text-sm border transition ${
                filter === f.key
                  ? 'bg-brand-600 text-white border-brand-600'
                  : 'bg-white border-slate-200 hover:bg-slate-50'
              }`}
            >
              {f.label}
            </button>
          ))}
          <button
            onClick={() => setShowOnlyUnread((v) => !v)}
            className={`ml-auto px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg text-sm border transition ${
              showOnlyUnread
                ? 'bg-brand-600 text-white border-brand-600'
                : 'bg-white border-slate-200 hover:bg-slate-50'
            }`}
          >
            Hazijasomwa ({unreadCount})
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* List */}
      {filtered.length === 0 ? (
        <div className="bg-white rounded-xl border border-slate-200 p-10 text-center">
          <Inbox size={40} className="mx-auto mb-3 text-slate-300" />
          <div className="font-semibold">
            {notifications.length === 0
              ? 'Hakuna taarifa'
              : 'Hakuna taarifa inayolingana'}
          </div>
          <p className="text-sm text-slate-500 mt-1">
            {notifications.length === 0
              ? 'Ukipata taarifa mpya, zitaonekana hapa.'
              : 'Badilisha kichujio au utafutaji.'}
          </p>
        </div>
      ) : (
        <div className="space-y-2">
          {filtered.map((n) => {
            const meta = AINA_META[n.type]
            const kundiMeta = KUNDI_META[n.kundi]
            return (
              <div
                key={n.id}
                className={`bg-white rounded-xl border p-4 transition ${
                  !n.read
                    ? 'border-brand-200 bg-brand-50/30'
                    : 'border-slate-200'
                }`}
              >
                <div className="flex items-start gap-3">
                  <div
                    className={`w-10 h-10 rounded-lg grid place-items-center shrink-0 border ${meta.color}`}
                  >
                    <meta.Icon size={18} />
                  </div>
                  <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-2 flex-wrap">
                      <button
                        onClick={() => handleClick(n)}
                        className="font-semibold text-sm text-left hover:text-brand-600"
                      >
                        {n.title}
                      </button>
                      {!n.read && (
                        <span className="w-2 h-2 rounded-full bg-brand-600 shrink-0" />
                      )}
                      <span className="text-[10px] uppercase font-semibold px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded bg-slate-100 text-slate-600">
                        {kundiMeta.label}
                      </span>
                    </div>
                    <div className="text-sm text-slate-600 mt-0.5">
                      {n.message}
                    </div>
                    <div className="text-xs text-slate-400 mt-1">{n.time}</div>
                  </div>
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-1 shrink-0">
                    <button
                      onClick={() => handleClick(n)}
                      className="p-2 rounded-lg text-brand-600 hover:bg-brand-50"
                      title="Fungua"
                    >
                      <ArrowRight size={16} />
                    </button>
                    <button
                      onClick={() => futa(n.id)}
                      className="p-2 rounded-lg text-red-500 hover:bg-red-50"
                      title="Futa"
                    >
                      <Trash2 size={16} />
                    </button>
                  </div>
                </div>
              </div>
            )
          })}
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  )
}


import { useState } from 'react'
import {
  ShoppingCart, Search, Bell, BellOff, CheckCircle2,
  Smartphone, Package, Clock, X, MessageCircle,
} from 'lucide-react'
import { usePendingCarts } from '../../context/PendingCartContext'
import type { HaliCart } from '../../types'

const AINA_META: Record<
  HaliCart,
  { label: string; color: string; Icon: React.ElementType }
> = {
  quotation: { label: 'Quotation', color: 'bg-brand-50 text-brand-700 border-brand-200', Icon: Clock },
  order: { label: 'Order', color: 'bg-amber-50 text-amber-700 border-amber-200', Icon: Package },
  malipo_sehemu: { label: 'Malipo Sehemu', color: 'bg-orange-50 text-orange-700 border-orange-200', Icon: Smartphone },
  imekamilika: { label: 'Imekamilika', color: 'bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-700 border-emerald-200', Icon: CheckCircle2 },
}

export default function MuuzajiCarts() {
  const { carts, tumaNotify, kamilisha } = usePendingCarts()
  const [q, setQ] = useState('')
  const [filter, setFilter] = useState<HaliCart | 'zote'>('zote')
  const [toast, setToast] = useState<string | null>(null)

  const filtered = carts.filter((c) => {
    if (filter !== 'zote' && c.aina !== filter) return false
    const query = q.toLowerCase().trim()
    if (!query) return true
    return (
      c.mteja.toLowerCase().includes(query) ||
      c.simu.includes(query) ||
      c.id.toLowerCase().includes(query)
    )
  })

  const handleNotify = (id: string, mteja: string, simu: string) => {
    tumaNotify(id)
    setToast(`SMS imetumwa kwa ${mteja} (${simu}) kukamilisha cart`)
    setTimeout(() => setToast(null), 3000)
  }

  return (
    <div className="space-y-5">
      <div>
        <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold">Carts Zinazosubiri</h1>
        <p className="text-slate-500 text-sm">
          Quotations na Orders zote zinazosubiri mteja kukamilisha
        </p>
      </div>

      <div className="grid grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4 gap-3">
        <div className="bg-white rounded-xl border p-4">
          <div className="text-xs text-slate-500">Zote</div>
          <div className="text-xl font-bold">{carts.length}</div>
        </div>
        <div className="bg-white rounded-xl border p-4">
          <div className="text-xs text-slate-500">Quotations</div>
          <div className="text-xl font-bold text-brand-600">
            {carts.filter((c) => c.aina === 'quotation').length}
          </div>
        </div>
        <div className="bg-white rounded-xl border p-4">
          <div className="text-xs text-slate-500">Orders</div>
          <div className="text-xl font-bold text-amber-600">
            {carts.filter((c) => c.aina === 'order').length}
          </div>
        </div>
        <div className="bg-white rounded-xl border p-4">
          <div className="text-xs text-slate-500">Malipo Sehemu</div>
          <div className="text-xl font-bold text-orange-600">
            {carts.filter((c) => c.aina === 'malipo_sehemu').length}
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="bg-white rounded-xl border p-3 md:p-4 space-y-3">
        <div className="relative">
          <Search size={18} className="absolute left-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-slate-400" />
          <input
            value={q}
            onChange={(e) => setQ(e.target.value)}
            placeholder="Tafuta mteja, simu, cart ID..."
            className="w-full pl-10 pr-4 py-2.5 rounded-lg border border-slate-200 focus:border-brand-500 outline-none text-sm"
          />
        </div>
        <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
          <button
            onClick={() => setFilter('zote')}
            className={`px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg text-sm border ${
              filter === 'zote'
                ? 'bg-brand-600 text-white border-brand-600'
                : 'bg-white border-slate-200 hover:bg-slate-50'
            }`}
          >
            Zote
          </button>
          {(Object.keys(AINA_META) as HaliCart[]).map((k) => (
            <button
              key={k}
              onClick={() => setFilter(k)}
              className={`px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg text-sm border ${
                filter === k
                  ? 'bg-brand-600 text-white border-brand-600'
                  : 'bg-white border-slate-200 hover:bg-slate-50'
              }`}
            >
              {AINA_META[k].label}
            </button>
          ))}
        </div>
      </div>

      {filtered.length === 0 ? (
        <div className="bg-white rounded-xl border p-10 text-center">
          <ShoppingCart size={40} className="mx-auto mb-3 text-slate-300" />
          <div className="font-semibold">Hakuna cart inayolingana</div>
          <p className="text-sm text-slate-500 mt-1">Badilisha kichujio au utafutaji.</p>
        </div>
      ) : (
        <div className="space-y-3">
          {filtered.map((c) => {
            const meta = AINA_META[c.aina]
            const baki = c.jumla - c.imelipwa
            return (
              <div key={c.id} className="bg-white rounded-xl border border-slate-200 p-4 md:p-5">
                <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:justify-between gap-3">
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-3 min-w-0">
                    <div className={`w-10 h-10 rounded-lg grid place-items-center shrink-0 ${meta.color}`}>
                      <meta.Icon size={18} />
                    </div>
                    <div className="min-w-0">
                      <div className="flex items-center gap-2 flex-wrap">
                        <span className="font-semibold text-sm">{c.id}</span>
                        <span className={`text-[10px] uppercase font-semibold px-2 py-0.5 rounded-md border ${meta.color}`}>
                          {meta.label}
                        </span>
                        {c.imetumwa && (
                          <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 text-[10px] text-emerald-700 bg-emerald-50 px-2 py-0.5 rounded-md border border-emerald-200">
                            <Bell size={10} /> Imetumwa
                          </span>
                        )}
                      </div>
                      <div className="text-xs text-slate-500 mt-0.5">
                        {c.mteja} - {c.simu} - {c.tarehe} {c.muda}
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                  <div className="sm:text-right">
                    <div className="text-base font-bold text-brand-700">
                      Tsh {c.jumla.toLocaleString()}
                    </div>
                    {baki > 0 && (
                      <div className="text-xs text-red-600">
                        Baki: Tsh {baki.toLocaleString()}
                      </div>
                    )}
                  </div>
                </div>

                <div className="mt-3 pt-3 border-t border-slate-100">
                  <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-1.5">
                    {c.bidhaa.map((b) => (
                      <span
                        key={b.id}
                        className="text-xs px-2 py-1 rounded-md bg-slate-100 text-slate-700"
                      >
                        {b.jina} ({b.wingi} {b.uom})
                      </span>
                    ))}
                  </div>
                </div>

                <div className="mt-3 flex flex-col sm:flex-row gap-2">
                  <button
                    onClick={() => handleNotify(c.id, c.mteja, c.simu)}
                    disabled={c.imetumwa || c.aina === 'imekamilika'}
                    className="inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-2 rounded-lg bg-brand-600 text-white text-sm font-semibold hover:bg-brand-700 disabled:opacity-50 disabled:cursor-not-allowed"
                  >
                    {c.imetumwa ? <BellOff size={14} /> : <Bell size={14} />}
                    {c.imetumwa ? 'Imeshatumwa' : 'Notify Mteja'}
                  </button>
                  {c.aina !== 'imekamilika' && (
                    <button
                      onClick={() => {
                        kamilisha(c.id)
                        setToast(`Cart ${c.id} imekamilika`)
                        setTimeout(() => setToast(null), 3000)
                      }}
                      className="inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-2 rounded-lg bg-emerald-600 text-white text-sm font-semibold hover:bg-emerald-700"
                    >
                      <CheckCircle2 size={14} />
                      Kamilisha
                    </button>
                  )}
                  <a
                    href={`https://wa.me/255${c.simu.replace(/[^0-9]/g, '').slice(1)}?text=${encodeURIComponent(
                      `Habari ${c.mteja}, cart yako ${c.id} ya Tsh ${c.jumla.toLocaleString()} iko tayari. Tafadhali kamilisha malipo.`
                    )}`}
                    target="_blank"
                    rel="noopener noreferrer"
                    className="inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-2 rounded-lg bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-700 text-sm font-semibold hover:bg-emerald-100"
                  >
                    <MessageCircle size={14} />
                    WhatsApp
                  </a>
                </div>
              </div>
            )
          })}
        </div>
      )}

      {toast && (
        <div className="fixed bottom-4 right-4 z-50 bg-slate-900 text-white text-sm px-4 py-3 rounded-lg shadow-lg max-w-xs">
          {toast}
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  )
}

import { useMemo, useState } from 'react'
import {
  Wallet, Plus, Search, Phone, MessageSquare, AlertTriangle,
  CheckCircle2, Clock, XCircle, X, Save, Upload, Receipt,
  Filter, ChevronRight, User,
} from 'lucide-react'
import { useMaombi } from '../../context/OmbiContext'
import { AINA_GHARAMA } from '../../data/demo'
import type { AinaGharama, HaliOmbi, OmbiMatumizi } from '../../types'

const HALI_META: Record<
  HaliOmbi,
  { label: string; color: string; Icon: React.ElementType }
> = {
  inasubiri: { label: 'Inasubiri', color: 'bg-amber-50 text-amber-700 border-amber-200', Icon: Clock },
  imekubaliwa: { label: 'Imekubaliwa', color: 'bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-700 border-emerald-200', Icon: CheckCircle2 },
  imekataliwa: { label: 'Imekataliwa', color: 'bg-red-50 text-red-700 border-red-200', Icon: XCircle },
}

type FilterKey = 'zote' | HaliOmbi

const FILTERS: { key: FilterKey; label: string }[] = [
  { key: 'zote', label: 'Zote' },
  { key: 'inasubiri', label: 'Zinasubiri' },
  { key: 'imekubaliwa', label: 'Zilizokubaliwa' },
  { key: 'imekataliwa', label: 'Zilizokataliwa' },
]

export default function MuuzajiMatumizi() {
  const { maombi, ongezaOmbi } = useMaombi()
  const [formOpen, setFormOpen] = useState(false)
  const [filter, setFilter] = useState<FilterKey>('zote')
  const [q, setQ] = useState('')
  const [selected, setSelected] = useState<OmbiMatumizi | null>(null)

  const filtered = useMemo(() => {
    return maombi.filter((m) => {
      if (filter !== 'zote' && m.hali !== filter) return false
      const query = q.toLowerCase().trim()
      if (!query) return true
      return (
        m.maelezo.toLowerCase().includes(query) ||
        m.sababu.toLowerCase().includes(query) ||
        m.id.toLowerCase().includes(query) ||
        m.aina.toLowerCase().includes(query)
      )
    })
  }, [maombi, filter, q])

  const stats = useMemo(() => {
    const zote = maombi.length
    const inasubiri = maombi.filter((m) => m.hali === 'inasubiri').length
    const imekubaliwa = maombi.filter((m) => m.hali === 'imekubaliwa')
    const jumlaIlikubaliwa = imekubaliwa.reduce((s, m) => s + m.kiasi, 0)
    return { zote, inasubiri, imekubaliwa: imekubaliwa.length, jumlaIlikubaliwa }
  }, [maombi])

  return (
    <div className="space-y-5">
      <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-end sm:justify-between gap-3">
        <div>
          <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold">Maombi ya Matumizi</h1>
          <p className="text-slate-500 text-sm">
            Omba matumizi ya duka - Mmiliki ataidhinisha
          </p>
        </div>
        <button
          onClick={() => setFormOpen(true)}
          className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-4 py-2 bg-brand-600 text-white rounded-lg text-sm font-semibold hover:bg-brand-700 self-start sm:self-auto"
        >
          <Plus size={16} />
          Omba Matumizi
        </button>
      </div>

      {/* Stats */}
      <div className="grid grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4 gap-3">
        <StatCard label="Maombi Yote" value={String(stats.zote)} Icon={Receipt} tone="brand" />
        <StatCard label="Zinasubiri" value={String(stats.inasubiri)} Icon={Clock} tone="amber" />
        <StatCard label="Zilizokubaliwa" value={String(stats.imekubaliwa)} Icon={CheckCircle2} tone="emerald" />
        <StatCard
          label="Jumla Iliyokubaliwa"
          value={`Tsh ${stats.jumlaIlikubaliwa.toLocaleString()}`}
          Icon={Wallet}
          tone="emerald"
        />
      </div>

      {/* Search + filters */}
      <div className="bg-white rounded-xl border border-slate-200 p-3 md:p-4 space-y-3">
        <div className="relative">
          <Search
            size={18}
            className="absolute left-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-slate-400 pointer-events-none"
          />
          <input
            value={q}
            onChange={(e) => setQ(e.target.value)}
            placeholder="Tafuta kwa ID, aina, maelezo, au sababu..."
            className="w-full pl-10 pr-4 py-2.5 rounded-lg border border-slate-200 focus:border-brand-500 outline-none text-sm"
          />
        </div>
        <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
          {FILTERS.map((f) => (
            <button
              key={f.key}
              onClick={() => setFilter(f.key)}
              className={`px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg text-sm border transition ${
                filter === f.key
                  ? 'bg-brand-600 text-white border-brand-600'
                  : 'bg-white text-slate-700 border-slate-200 hover:bg-slate-50'
              }`}
            >
              {f.label}
            </button>
          ))}
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* List */}
      {filtered.length === 0 ? (
        <div className="bg-white rounded-xl border border-slate-200 p-10 text-center">
          <Wallet size={40} className="mx-auto mb-3 text-slate-300" />
          <div className="font-semibold">Hakuna maombi</div>
          <p className="text-sm text-slate-500 mt-1">
            Bofya "Omba Matumizi" kuanza kuomba.
          </p>
        </div>
      ) : (
        <div className="space-y-3">
          {filtered.map((m) => {
            const meta = HALI_META[m.hali]
            const ainaLabel =
              AINA_GHARAMA.find((a) => a.key === m.aina)?.label ?? m.aina
            return (
              <button
                key={m.id}
                onClick={() => setSelected(m)}
                className="w-full text-left bg-white rounded-xl border border-slate-200 p-4 hover:border-brand-500 transition"
              >
                <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:justify-between gap-3">
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-3 min-w-0">
                    <div className={`w-10 h-10 rounded-lg grid place-items-center shrink-0 ${meta.color}`}>
                      <meta.Icon size={18} />
                    </div>
                    <div className="min-w-0">
                      <div className="flex items-center gap-2 flex-wrap">
                        <span className="font-semibold text-sm">{m.id}</span>
                        <span className={`text-[10px] uppercase font-semibold px-2 py-0.5 rounded-md border ${meta.color}`}>
                          {meta.label}
                        </span>
                        <span className="text-[10px] uppercase font-semibold px-2 py-0.5 rounded-md bg-slate-100 text-slate-600">
                          {ainaLabel}
                        </span>
                      </div>
                      <div className="text-xs text-slate-500 mt-0.5 truncate">
                        {m.maelezo}
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                  <div className="sm:text-right">
                    <div className="text-base font-bold text-brand-700">
                      Tsh {m.kiasi.toLocaleString()}
                    </div>
                    <div className="text-xs text-slate-500">{m.tarehe}</div>
                  </div>
                </div>
              </button>
            )
          })}
        </div>
      )}

      {formOpen && <OmbiForm onClose={() => setFormOpen(false)} onSubmit={ongezaOmbi} />}
      {selected && <OmbiDetails ombi={selected} onClose={() => setSelected(null)} />}
    </div>
  )
}

// ---------- Fomu ya Ombi ----------
function OmbiForm({
  onClose,
  onSubmit,
}: {
  onClose: () => void
  onSubmit: ReturnType<typeof useMaombi>['ongezaOmbi']
}) {
  const [aina, setAina] = useState<AinaGharama>('mafuta')
  const [kiasi, setKiasi] = useState(0)
  const [maelezo, setMaelezo] = useState('')
  const [simu, setSimu] = useState('')
  const [sababu, setSababu] = useState('')
  const [risitiUrl, setRisitiUrl] = useState('')
  const [error, setError] = useState('')

  const handleSubmit = (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault()
    if (!maelezo.trim()) return setError('Maelezo yanahitajika')
    if (kiasi <= 0) return setError('Kiasi lazima kiwe zaidi ya sifuri')
    if (!/^0[67]\d{2}-?\d{3}-?\d{3}$/.test(simu.replace(/\s/g, '')))
      return setError('Namba ya simu si sahihi (mfano: 0754-123-456)')
    if (!sababu.trim()) return setError('Sababu inahitajika')

    setError('')
    onSubmit({ aina, kiasi, maelezo, simu, sababu, risitiUrl: risitiUrl || undefined })
    onClose()
  }

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-end sm:items-center justify-center p-0 sm:p-4">
      <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-black/40" onClick={onClose} />
      <div className="relative bg-white w-full sm:max-w-lg rounded-t-2xl sm:rounded-2xl shadow-2xl max-h-[92vh] overflow-y-auto">
        <div className="sticky top-0 bg-white border-b border-slate-200 px-4 py-3 flex justify-between items-center">
          <div className="font-semibold">Omba Matumizi</div>
          <button onClick={onClose} className="p-1.5 rounded-lg hover:bg-slate-100">
            <X size={18} />
          </button>
        </div>
        <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="p-4 space-y-3">
          <div>
            <label className="text-sm font-medium">Aina ya Gharama</label>
            <select
              value={aina}
              onChange={(e) => setAina(e.target.value as AinaGharama)}
              className="mt-1 w-full px-3 py-2.5 rounded-lg border border-slate-200 focus:border-brand-500 outline-none text-sm"
            >
              {AINA_GHARAMA.map((a) => (
                <option key={a.key} value={a.key}>
                  {a.label}
                </option>
              ))}
            </select>
          </div>
          <div>
            <label className="text-sm font-medium">Kiasi (Tsh)</label>
            <input
              type="number"
              value={kiasi || ''}
              onChange={(e) => setKiasi(Number(e.target.value))}
              placeholder="Mfano: 80000"
              className="mt-1 w-full px-3 py-2.5 rounded-lg border border-slate-200 focus:border-brand-500 outline-none text-sm"
            />
          </div>
          <div>
            <label className="text-sm font-medium">Maelezo</label>
            <input
              value={maelezo}
              onChange={(e) => setMaelezo(e.target.value)}
              placeholder="Mfano: Mafuta ya pikipiki"
              className="mt-1 w-full px-3 py-2.5 rounded-lg border border-slate-200 focus:border-brand-500 outline-none text-sm"
            />
          </div>
          <div>
            <label className="text-sm font-medium">Namba ya Simu ya Mlipaji</label>
            <input
              value={simu}
              onChange={(e) => setSimu(e.target.value)}
              placeholder="0754-123-456"
              inputMode="tel"
              className="mt-1 w-full px-3 py-2.5 rounded-lg border border-slate-200 focus:border-brand-500 outline-none text-sm"
            />
          </div>
          <div>
            <label className="text-sm font-medium">Sababu</label>
            <textarea
              value={sababu}
              onChange={(e) => setSababu(e.target.value)}
              rows={3}
              placeholder="Eleza kwa nini unahitaji matumizi haya..."
              className="mt-1 w-full px-3 py-2.5 rounded-lg border border-slate-200 focus:border-brand-500 outline-none text-sm resize-none"
            />
          </div>
          <div>
            <label className="text-sm font-medium">Risiti (hiari)</label>
            <div className="mt-1 flex items-center gap-2">
              <input
                value={risitiUrl}
                onChange={(e) => setRisitiUrl(e.target.value)}
                placeholder="URL ya picha ya risiti"
                className="flex-1 px-3 py-2.5 rounded-lg border border-slate-200 focus:border-brand-500 outline-none text-sm"
              />
              <button
                type="button"
                className="p-2.5 rounded-lg bg-slate-100 hover:bg-slate-200"
                title="Pakia picha"
              >
                <Upload size={16} />
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>

          {error && (
            <div className="flex items-start gap-2 p-3 rounded-lg bg-red-50 text-red-700 text-sm">
              <AlertTriangle size={16} className="mt-0.5 shrink-0" />
              {error}
            </div>
          )}

          <div className="flex gap-2 pt-2">
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={onClose}
              className="px-4 py-2.5 rounded-lg bg-slate-100 text-sm hover:bg-slate-200"
            >
              Ghairi
            </button>
            <button
              type="submit"
              className="flex-1 inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 px-4 py-2.5 rounded-lg bg-brand-600 text-white text-sm font-semibold hover:bg-brand-700"
            >
              <Save size={16} />
              Tuma Ombi
            </button>
          </div>
        </form>
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}

// ---------- Maelezo ya Ombi ----------
function OmbiDetails({ ombi, onClose }: { ombi: OmbiMatumizi; onClose: () => void }) {
  const meta = HALI_META[ombi.hali]
  const ainaLabel =
    AINA_GHARAMA.find((a) => a.key === ombi.aina)?.label ?? ombi.aina
  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-end sm:items-center justify-center p-0 sm:p-4">
      <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-black/40" onClick={onClose} />
      <div className="relative bg-white w-full sm:max-w-md rounded-t-2xl sm:rounded-2xl shadow-2xl max-h-[92vh] overflow-y-auto">
        <div className="sticky top-0 bg-white border-b border-slate-200 px-4 py-3 flex justify-between items-center">
          <div>
            <div className="font-semibold">{ombi.id}</div>
            <div className="text-xs text-slate-500">{ombi.tarehe}</div>
          </div>
          <button onClick={onClose} className="p-1.5 rounded-lg hover:bg-slate-100">
            <X size={18} />
          </button>
        </div>
        <div className="p-4 space-y-3 text-sm">
          <div className={`inline-flex items-center gap-1 px-2 py-1 rounded-md border ${meta.color}`}>
            <meta.Icon size={12} />
            {meta.label}
          </div>
          <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
            <div>
              <div className="text-xs text-slate-500">Aina</div>
              <div className="font-medium capitalize">{ainaLabel}</div>
            </div>
            <div>
              <div className="text-xs text-slate-500">Kiasi</div>
              <div className="font-bold text-brand-700">
                Tsh {ombi.kiasi.toLocaleString()}
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
          <div>
            <div className="text-xs text-slate-500">Maelezo</div>
            <div>{ombi.maelezo}</div>
          </div>
          <div>
            <div className="text-xs text-slate-500">Sababu</div>
            <div>{ombi.sababu}</div>
          </div>
          <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs text-slate-600">
            <Phone size={12} /> {ombi.simu}
          </div>
          <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs text-slate-600">
            <User size={12} /> {ombi.aliyeomba} - {ombi.duka}
          </div>
          {ombi.maoni && (
            <div className="p-3 rounded-lg bg-slate-50">
              <div className="text-xs text-slate-500 mb-1">Maoni ya Mmiliki</div>
              <div className="text-sm">{ombi.maoni}</div>
              {ombi.aliyeidhinisha && (
                <div className="text-xs text-slate-500 mt-1">
                  - {ombi.aliyeidhinisha}, {ombi.tareheIdhini}
                </div>
              )}
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}

// ---------- Stat Card ----------
type Tone = 'brand' | 'emerald' | 'amber' | 'red'
const TONE: Record<Tone, string> = {
  brand: 'bg-brand-50 text-brand-700',
  emerald: 'bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-700',
  amber: 'bg-amber-50 text-amber-700',
  red: 'bg-red-50 text-red-700',
}

function StatCard({
  label,
  value,
  Icon,
  tone,
}: {
  label: string
  value: string
  Icon: React.ElementType
  tone: Tone
}) {
  return (
    <div className="bg-white rounded-xl border border-slate-200 p-4">
      <div className={`inline-flex w-9 h-9 rounded-lg items-center justify-center ${TONE[tone]}`}>
        <Icon size={16} />
      </div>
      <div className="text-xs text-slate-500 mt-2">{label}</div>
      <div className="text-base md:text-lg font-bold mt-0.5 truncate">{value}</div>
    </div>
  )
}

import { useMemo, useState } from 'react'
import {
  Wallet, Banknote, Smartphone, Building2,
  CheckCircle2, Clock, XCircle, Download, X,
  Calendar, FileText, TrendingUp, CreditCard, AlertTriangle,
  User, Store, Info,
} from 'lucide-react'
import { useMshahara } from '../../context/MshaharaContext'
import { formatTsh } from '../../utils/faida'
import type {
  HaliMshahara,
  Mshahara,
  NjiaMalipoMshahara,
} from '../../types'

const HALI_META: Record<
  HaliMshahara,
  { label: string; color: string; Icon: React.ElementType }
> = {
  inasubiri: { label: 'Inasubiri', color: 'text-amber-700 border-amber-200', Icon: Clock },
  imelipwa: { label: 'Imelipwa', color: 'text-emerald-700 border-emerald-200', Icon: CheckCircle2 },
  imeshindwa: { label: 'Imeshindwa', color: 'text-red-700 border-red-200', Icon: XCircle },
  imekataliwa: { label: 'Imekataliwa', color: 'text-red-700 border-red-200', Icon: XCircle },
}

const NJIA_META: Record<
  NjiaMalipoMshahara,
  { color: string; Icon: React.ElementType; label: string }
> = {
  mpesa: { color: 'text-brand-700', Icon: Smartphone, label: 'M-Pesa' },
  tigopesa: { color: 'text-orange-700', Icon: Smartphone, label: 'Tigo Pesa' },
  airtel: { color: 'text-red-700', Icon: Smartphone, label: 'Airtel Money' },
  halopesa: { color: 'text-purple-700', Icon: Smartphone, label: 'HaloPesa' },
  crdb: { color: 'text-emerald-700', Icon: Building2, label: 'CRDB' },
  nmb: { color: 'text-brand-700', Icon: Building2, label: 'NMB' },
  nbc: { color: 'text-slate-700', Icon: Building2, label: 'NBC' },
  stanbic: { color: 'text-purple-700', Icon: Building2, label: 'Stanbic' },
  other: { color: 'text-slate-700', Icon: CreditCard, label: 'Nyingine' },
}

// Kwa demo, mtumiaji ni Juma Hassan (u1)
const MTUMIAJI_ID = 'u1'
const JINA_MTUMIAJI = 'Juma Hassan'
const NAFASI = 'Muuzaji'
const DUKA = 'Smart Hardware - Kwembe'

export default function MuuzajiMshahara() {
  const {
    mishahara,
    akaunti,
    getAkauntiMsingi,
  } = useMshahara()
  const [selected, setSelected] = useState<Mshahara | null>(null)

  const mishaharaYake = useMemo(
    () => mishahara.filter((m) => m.mtumiajiId === MTUMIAJI_ID),
    [mishahara]
  )

  // Muuzaji anaona akaunti Yake tu (iliyowekwa na mmiliki)
  const akauntiYake = useMemo(
    () => akaunti.filter((a) => a.mtumiajiId === MTUMIAJI_ID),
    [akaunti]
  )

  const akauntiMsingi = getAkauntiMsingi(MTUMIAJI_ID)

  const stats = useMemo(() => {
    const zote = mishaharaYake.length
    const jumlaIliyolipwa = mishaharaYake
      .filter((m) => m.hali === 'imelipwa')
      .reduce((s, m) => s + m.jumla, 0)
    const inayosubiri = mishaharaYake
      .filter((m) => m.hali === 'inasubiri')
      .reduce((s, m) => s + m.jumla, 0)
    const mshaharaWaMwisho = mishaharaYake[0]
    return { zote, jumlaIliyolipwa, inayosubiri, mshaharaWaMwisho }
  }, [mishaharaYake])

  return (
    <div className="space-y-5">
      {/* Header */}
      <div>
        <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold flex items-center gap-2">
          <Wallet size={24} className="text-brand-600" />
          Mshahara Wangu
        </h1>
        <p className="text-slate-500 text-sm">
          {JINA_MTUMIAJI} - {NAFASI} - {DUKA}
        </p>
      </div>

      {/* Info: Malipo yanaenda kwenye akaunti moja */}
      <div className="bg-white rounded-xl border border-slate-200 p-4 flex items-start gap-2">
        <Info size={16} className="text-brand-600 mt-0.5 shrink-0" />
        <div className="text-sm">
          <div className="font-medium text-slate-900">Kumbuka</div>
          <div className="text-slate-500 mt-0.5">
            Mshahara wako unalipwa kwenye akaunti uliyowekwa na mmiliki.
            Kama unataka kubadilisha akaunti, wasiliana na mmiliki.
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Stats 4 */}
      <div className="grid grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4 gap-3">
        <StatCard
          label="Mishahara Yote"
          value={String(stats.zote)}
          Icon={FileText}
          accent="brand"
        />
        <StatCard
          label="Iliyolipwa"
          value={formatTsh(stats.jumlaIliyolipwa)}
          Icon={CheckCircle2}
          accent="emerald"
        />
        <StatCard
          label="Inasubiri"
          value={formatTsh(stats.inayosubiri)}
          Icon={Clock}
          accent="amber"
        />
        <StatCard
          label="Mshahara wa Mwisho"
          value={
            stats.mshaharaWaMwisho
              ? formatTsh(stats.mshaharaWaMwisho.jumla)
              : 'Hakuna'
          }
          Icon={TrendingUp}
          accent="brand"
          sub={stats.mshaharaWaMwisho?.mwezi}
        />
      </div>

      {/* Onyo kama hana akaunti */}
      {!akauntiMsingi && (
        <div className="bg-amber-50 border border-amber-200 rounded-xl p-4 flex items-start gap-2">
          <AlertTriangle size={16} className="text-amber-600 mt-0.5 shrink-0" />
          <div className="text-sm">
            <div className="font-medium text-amber-900">
              Huna akaunti ya malipo bado
            </div>
            <div className="text-amber-700 mt-0.5">
              Wasiliana na mmiliki ili aweke akaunti yako ya M-Pesa au benki.
              Bila akaunti, mshahara wako hautaingia.
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Akaunti Yake (read-only, iliyowekwa na mmiliki) */}
      <div className="bg-white rounded-xl border border-slate-200 p-4">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-3">
          <CreditCard size={16} className="text-brand-600" />
          <h2 className="font-semibold text-sm">Akaunti Yangu ya Malipo</h2>
        </div>

        {akauntiYake.length === 0 ? (
          <div className="text-sm text-slate-500 text-center py-4">
            Hakuna akaunti bado. Wasiliana na mmiliki ili aweke akaunti yako.
          </div>
        ) : (
          <div className="space-y-2">
            {akauntiYake.map((a) => {
              const Icon = a.aina === 'mobile' ? Smartphone : Building2
              return (
                <div
                  key={a.id}
                  className={`p-3 rounded-lg border ${
                    a.ndiyoMsingi
                      ? 'border-brand-500 bg-brand-50'
                      : 'border-slate-200 bg-slate-50'
                  }`}
                >
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                    <div className="w-9 h-9 rounded-lg bg-white border border-slate-200 grid place-items-center text-slate-700 shrink-0">
                      <Icon size={16} />
                    </div>
                    <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
                      <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                        <span className="font-semibold text-sm">{a.jina}</span>
                        {a.ndiyoMsingi && (
                          <span className="text-[10px] uppercase font-semibold px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded bg-brand-600 text-white">
                            Msingi
                          </span>
                        )}
                      </div>
                      <div className="text-xs text-slate-500 font-mono">
                        {a.namba}
                      </div>
                      <div className="text-xs text-slate-500">
                        {a.jinaMmiliki}
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                </div>
              )
            })}
          </div>
        )}
      </div>

      {/* Mishahara */}
      <div className="bg-white rounded-xl border border-slate-200 p-4">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-3">
          <Calendar size={16} className="text-brand-600" />
          <h2 className="font-semibold text-sm">Historia ya Mishahara</h2>
        </div>

        {mishaharaYake.length === 0 ? (
          <div className="text-sm text-slate-500 text-center py-6">
            Hakuna mishahara bado. Mshahara wako utaonekana hapa mmiliki akishaongeza.
          </div>
        ) : (
          <div className="space-y-2">
            {mishaharaYake.map((m) => {
              const hali = HALI_META[m.hali]
              const njia = m.njiaMalipo ? NJIA_META[m.njiaMalipo] : null
              return (
                <button
                  key={m.id}
                  onClick={() => setSelected(m)}
                  className="w-full text-left bg-white rounded-lg border border-slate-200 p-3 hover:border-brand-500 transition"
                >
                  <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:justify-between gap-2">
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-3 min-w-0 flex-1">
                      <div className="w-10 h-10 rounded-lg bg-slate-50 text-slate-700 grid place-items-center shrink-0">
                        <Calendar size={18} />
                      </div>
                      <div className="min-w-0">
                        <div className="flex items-center gap-2 flex-wrap">
                          <span className="font-semibold text-sm">{m.mwezi}</span>
                          <span
                            className={`text-[10px] uppercase font-semibold inline-flex items-center gap-1 px-2 py-0.5 rounded-md border ${hali.color}`}
                          >
                            <hali.Icon size={10} />
                            {hali.label}
                          </span>
                        </div>
                        <div className="text-xs text-slate-500 mt-0.5">
                          {m.kipindi}
                        </div>
                        {m.tareheMalipo && (
                          <div className="text-xs text-slate-500">
                            Imelipwa: {m.tareheMalipo}
                          </div>
                        )}
                      </div>
                    </div>
                    <div className="sm:text-right shrink-0">
                      <div className="text-base font-bold text-slate-900">
                        {formatTsh(m.jumla)}
                      </div>
                      {m.hali === 'inasubiri' && (
                        <div className="text-xs text-amber-700">
                          Inasubiri malipo
                        </div>
                      )}
                    </div>
                  </div>

                  {m.hali === 'imelipwa' && njia && (
                    <div className="mt-2 pt-2 border-t border-slate-100 flex items-center gap-2 text-xs text-slate-500">
                      <njia.Icon size={12} className={njia.color} />
                      <span className="font-semibold uppercase">{njia.label}</span>
                      <span className="text-slate-300">-</span>
                      <span className="font-mono">{m.kumbukumbu}</span>
                    </div>
                  )}
                </button>
              )
            })}
          </div>
        )}
      </div>

      {selected && (
        <MshaharaDetailModal
          mshahara={selected}
          onClose={() => setSelected(null)}
        />
      )}
    </div>
  )
}

// ============================================================
// Detail Modal (read-only)
// ============================================================
function MshaharaDetailModal({
  mshahara,
  onClose,
}: {
  mshahara: Mshahara
  onClose: () => void
}) {
  const hali = HALI_META[mshahara.hali]
  const njia = mshahara.njiaMalipo ? NJIA_META[mshahara.njiaMalipo] : null

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-end sm:items-center justify-center p-0 sm:p-4">
      <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-black/40" onClick={onClose} />
      <div className="relative bg-white w-full sm:max-w-md rounded-t-2xl sm:rounded-2xl shadow-2xl max-h-[92vh] overflow-y-auto">
        <div className="sticky top-0 bg-white border-b border-slate-200 px-4 py-3 flex justify-between items-center">
          <div>
            <div className="font-semibold">{mshahara.mwezi}</div>
            <div className="text-xs text-slate-500">{mshahara.kipindi}</div>
          </div>
          <button onClick={onClose} className="p-1.5 rounded-lg hover:bg-slate-100">
            <X size={18} />
          </button>
        </div>

        <div className="p-4 space-y-3 text-sm">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
            <span
              className={`inline-flex items-center gap-1 px-2 py-1 rounded-md border text-xs font-semibold ${hali.color}`}
            >
              <hali.Icon size={12} />
              {hali.label}
            </span>
          </div>

          <div className="space-y-1.5">
            <div className="flex justify-between p-2 rounded-md bg-slate-50">
              <span className="text-slate-600">Mshahara Msingi</span>
              <span className="font-semibold">{formatTsh(mshahara.mshaharaMsingi)}</span>
            </div>
            <div className="flex justify-between p-2 rounded-md bg-slate-50">
              <span className="text-slate-600">Posho</span>
              <span className="font-semibold text-emerald-700">
                + {formatTsh(mshahara.posho)}
              </span>
            </div>
            <div className="flex justify-between p-2 rounded-md bg-slate-50">
              <span className="text-slate-600">Overtime</span>
              <span className="font-semibold text-emerald-700">
                + {formatTsh(mshahara.overtime)}
              </span>
            </div>
            <div className="flex justify-between p-2 rounded-md bg-slate-50">
              <span className="text-slate-600">Mafao</span>
              <span className="font-semibold text-emerald-700">
                + {formatTsh(mshahara.mafao)}
              </span>
            </div>
            <div className="flex justify-between p-2 rounded-md bg-slate-50">
              <span className="text-slate-600">Makato</span>
              <span className="font-semibold text-red-700">
                - {formatTsh(mshahara.makato)}
              </span>
            </div>
            <div className="flex justify-between p-3 rounded-lg bg-brand-50 border border-brand-200">
              <span className="font-semibold text-slate-700">JUMLA</span>
              <span className="font-bold text-brand-700 text-lg">
                {formatTsh(mshahara.jumla)}
              </span>
            </div>
          </div>

          {mshahara.hali === 'imelipwa' && njia && (
            <div className="p-3 rounded-lg bg-emerald-50 border border-emerald-100">
              <div className="text-xs text-slate-600 mb-1">Malipo</div>
              <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
                <njia.Icon size={14} className={njia.color} />
                <span className="font-semibold uppercase">{njia.label}</span>
              </div>
              <div className="text-xs text-slate-500 mt-1">
                Akaunti: {mshahara.akauntiMalipo}
              </div>
              <div className="text-xs text-slate-500 font-mono">
                {mshahara.kumbukumbu}
              </div>
              <div className="text-xs text-slate-500 mt-1">
                Tarehe: {mshahara.tareheMalipo}
              </div>
            </div>
          )}

          <div className="flex gap-2 pt-2 border-t border-slate-200">
            {mshahara.hali === 'imelipwa' && (
              <button className="flex-1 inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-2.5 rounded-lg bg-brand-600 text-white text-sm font-semibold hover:bg-brand-700">
                <Download size={14} />
                Pakua Payslip
              </button>
            )}
            <button
              onClick={onClose}
              className="px-3 py-2.5 rounded-lg bg-slate-100 text-sm hover:bg-slate-200"
            >
              Funga
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}

// ============================================================
// Stat Card
// ============================================================
type Accent = 'brand' | 'emerald' | 'amber' | 'red'
const ACCENT_BORDER: Record<Accent, string> = {
  brand: 'border-l-brand-500',
  emerald: 'border-l-emerald-500',
  amber: 'border-l-amber-500',
  red: 'border-l-red-500',
}
const ACCENT_TEXT: Record<Accent, string> = {
  brand: 'text-brand-700',
  emerald: 'text-emerald-700',
  amber: 'text-amber-700',
  red: 'text-red-700',
}

function StatCard({
  label,
  value,
  Icon,
  accent,
  sub,
}: {
  label: string
  value: string
  Icon: React.ElementType
  accent: Accent
  sub?: string
}) {
  return (
    <div
      className={`bg-white rounded-xl border border-slate-200 border-l-4 ${ACCENT_BORDER[accent]} p-4`}
    >
      <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
        <Icon size={16} className={ACCENT_TEXT[accent]} />
        <div className="text-xs text-slate-500">{label}</div>
      </div>
      <div className="text-base md:text-lg font-bold mt-2 truncate text-slate-900">
        {value}
      </div>
      {sub && <div className="text-[10px] text-slate-500 truncate">{sub}</div>}
    </div>
  )
}

import { useState } from 'react'
import {
  Truck, Plus, Minus, Check, AlertTriangle, Package,
  Save, FileText, X,
} from 'lucide-react'
import { useGRN } from '../../context/GRNContext'
import { iconForKundi } from '../../utils/icons'
import { bidhaa } from '../../data/demo'
import type { GRNItem } from '../../types'

export default function MuuzajiPokeaBidhaa() {
  const { grns, pokeaBidhaa } = useGRN()
  const [selected, setSelected] = useState<string | null>(null)

  const grn = grns.find((g) => g.id === selected)

  if (!grn) {
    return (
      <div className="space-y-5">
        <div>
          <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold">Pokea Bidhaa (GRN)</h1>
          <p className="text-slate-500 text-sm">Mizigo inayosubiri kupokelewa</p>
        </div>

        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-3 gap-3 md:gap-4">
          <div className="bg-white rounded-xl border border-slate-200 p-4">
            <div className="text-xs text-slate-500">Inasubiri</div>
            <div className="text-xl font-bold mt-1">
              {grns.filter((g) => g.hali === 'inasubiri').length}
            </div>
          </div>
          <div className="bg-white rounded-xl border border-slate-200 p-4">
            <div className="text-xs text-slate-500">Zimepokelewa</div>
            <div className="text-xl font-bold mt-1 text-emerald-600">
              {grns.filter((g) => g.hali === 'imepokelewa').length}
            </div>
          </div>
          <div className="bg-white rounded-xl border border-slate-200 p-4">
            <div className="text-xs text-slate-500">Upungufu</div>
            <div className="text-xl font-bold mt-1 text-amber-600">
              {grns.filter((g) => g.hali === 'upungufu').length}
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="space-y-3">
          {grns.map((g) => (
            <button
              key={g.id}
              onClick={() => setSelected(g.id)}
              className="w-full text-left bg-white rounded-xl border border-slate-200 p-4 hover:border-brand-500 transition"
            >
              <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                <div className="w-11 h-11 rounded-lg bg-slate-100 text-slate-600 grid place-items-center shrink-0">
                  <Truck size={20} />
                </div>
                <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-2 flex-wrap">
                    <span className="font-semibold text-sm">{g.id}</span>
                    <span
                      className={`text-[10px] uppercase font-semibold px-2 py-0.5 rounded-md border ${
                        g.hali === 'imepokelewa'
                          ? 'bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-700 border-emerald-200'
                          : g.hali === 'upungufu'
                          ? 'bg-amber-50 text-amber-700 border-amber-200'
                          : 'bg-slate-50 text-slate-700 border-slate-200'
                      }`}
                    >
                      {g.hali === 'imepokelewa' && 'Imepokelewa'}
                      {g.hali === 'upungufu' && 'Upungufu'}
                      {g.hali === 'inasubiri' && 'Inasubiri'}
                    </span>
                  </div>
                  <div className="text-xs text-slate-500 mt-0.5">
                    {g.msambazaji} - {g.tarehe} - LPO {g.lpoId}
                  </div>
                </div>
                <div className="text-xs text-slate-500 hidden sm:block">
                  {g.bidhaa.length} bidhaa
                </div>
              </div>
            </button>
          ))}
        </div>
      </div>
    )
  }

  const jumlaIliyoagizwa = grn.bidhaa.reduce((s, b) => s + b.iliyoagizwa, 0)
  const jumlaIliyopokelewa = grn.bidhaa.reduce((s, b) => s + b.iliyopokelewa, 0)
  const upungufu = jumlaIliyoagizwa - jumlaIliyopokelewa

  return (
    <div className="space-y-5">
      <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
        <button
          onClick={() => setSelected(null)}
          className="p-2 rounded-lg hover:bg-slate-100"
        >
          <X size={20} />
        </button>
        <div>
          <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold">{grn.id}</h1>
          <p className="text-slate-500 text-sm">
            {grn.msambazaji} - LPO {grn.lpoId}
          </p>
        </div>
      </div>

      {upungufu > 0 && (
        <div className="flex items-start gap-2 p-3 rounded-lg bg-amber-50 border border-amber-200 text-amber-800 text-sm">
          <AlertTriangle size={16} className="mt-0.5 shrink-0" />
          <div>
            Upungufu wa bidhaa {upungufu} - hakikisha umeandika sababu.
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      <div className="bg-white rounded-xl border border-slate-200 p-4 md:p-5">
        <h2 className="font-semibold mb-3">Bidhaa Zilizoagizwa</h2>
        <div className="space-y-3">
          {grn.bidhaa.map((item, idx) => {
            const b = bidhaa.find((x) => x.id === item.bidhaaId)
            const Icon = iconForKundi(b?.iconKey)
            return (
              <div
                key={idx}
                className="p-3 rounded-lg border border-slate-200 space-y-2"
              >
                <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                  <div className="w-9 h-9 rounded-lg bg-slate-100 text-slate-600 grid place-items-center">
                    <Icon size={16} />
                  </div>
                  <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
                    <div className="font-medium text-sm truncate">{item.jina}</div>
                    <div className="text-xs text-slate-500">{item.sku}</div>
                  </div>
                </div>
                <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2 text-sm">
                  <div>
                    <div className="text-xs text-slate-500">Iliyoagizwa</div>
                    <div className="font-semibold">
                      {item.iliyoagizwa} {item.uom}
                    </div>
                  </div>
                  <div>
                    <div className="text-xs text-slate-500">Iliyopokelewa</div>
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-1">
                      <button
                        onClick={() => {
                          if (item.iliyopokelewa <= 0) return
                          const newItems = grn.bidhaa.map((x, i) =>
                            i === idx
                              ? { ...x, iliyopokelewa: x.iliyopokelewa - 1 }
                              : x
                          )
                          pokeaBidhaa(grn.id, newItems)
                        }}
                        className="w-6 h-6 rounded-md bg-slate-100 hover:bg-slate-200 grid place-items-center"
                      >
                        <Minus size={12} />
                      </button>
                      <span className="w-8 text-center font-semibold">
                        {item.iliyopokelewa}
                      </span>
                      <button
                        onClick={() => {
                          const newItems = grn.bidhaa.map((x, i) =>
                            i === idx
                              ? { ...x, iliyopokelewa: x.iliyopokelewa + 1 }
                              : x
                          )
                          pokeaBidhaa(grn.id, newItems)
                        }}
                        className="w-6 h-6 rounded-md bg-emerald-100 hover:bg-emerald-200 text-emerald-700 grid place-items-center"
                      >
                        <Plus size={12} />
                      </button>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                </div>
              </div>
            )
          })}
        </div>

        <div className="mt-4 p-3 rounded-lg bg-slate-50 text-sm space-y-1">
          <div className="flex justify-between">
            <span>Jumla Iliyoagizwa</span>
            <span className="font-semibold">{jumlaIliyoagizwa}</span>
          </div>
          <div className="flex justify-between">
            <span>Jumla Iliyopokelewa</span>
            <span className="font-semibold text-emerald-700">
              {jumlaIliyopokelewa}
            </span>
          </div>
          {upungufu > 0 && (
            <div className="flex justify-between border-t border-slate-200 pt-1">
              <span>Upungufu</span>
              <span className="font-bold text-amber-700">{upungufu}</span>
            </div>
          )}
        </div>

        <button className="mt-4 w-full py-2.5 rounded-lg bg-emerald-600 text-white text-sm font-semibold hover:bg-emerald-700 inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-2">
          <Check size={16} />
          Thibitisha Kupokea
        </button>
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}


import { useState } from 'react'
import { bidhaa } from '../../data/demo'

export default function MuuzajiPOS() {
  const [cart, setCart] = useState<{ id: string; wingi: number }[]>([])
  const [q, setQ] = useState('')

  const filtered = bidhaa.filter((b) =>
    b.jina.toLowerCase().includes(q.toLowerCase()) || b.sku.toLowerCase().includes(q.toLowerCase())
  )

  const add = (id: string) => {
    setCart((c) => {
      const ex = c.find((x) => x.id === id)
      if (ex) return c.map((x) => (x.id === id ? { ...x, wingi: x.wingi + 1 } : x))
      return [...c, { id, wingi: 1 }]
    })
  }

  const lines = cart.map((c) => {
    const b = bidhaa.find((x) => x.id === c.id)!
    return { ...b, wingi: c.wingi, jumla: b.beiMauzo * c.wingi }
  })
  const total = lines.reduce((s, l) => s + l.jumla, 0)

  return (
    <div className="grid lg:grid-cols-3 gap-6 h-[calc(100vh-4rem)]">
      <div className="lg:col-span-2 space-y-4">
        <div>
          <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold">POS - Duka la Kwembe</h1>
          <p className="text-slate-500 text-sm">Muuzaji: Juma Hassan</p>
        </div>
        <input
          value={q}
          onChange={(e) => setQ(e.target.value)}
          placeholder="Scan barcode au tafuta bidhaa..."
          className="w-full px-4 py-3 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 focus:border-brand-500 outline-none"
        />
        <div className="grid md:grid-cols-2 xl:grid-cols-3 gap-3 overflow-auto max-h-[calc(100vh-14rem)]">
          {filtered.map((b) => (
            <button
              key={b.id}
              onClick={() => add(b.id)}
              className="bg-white rounded-xl border border-slate-200 p-4 text-left hover:border-brand-500 hover:shadow-sm transition"
            >
              <div className="text-3xl">{b.picha}</div>
              <div className="font-semibold text-sm mt-1">{b.jina}</div>
              <div className="text-xs text-slate-500">{b.sku}</div>
              <div className="text-brand-700 font-bold mt-2">Tsh {b.beiMauzo.toLocaleString()}</div>
              <div className="text-xs text-slate-500">Stoki: {b.stoki}</div>
            </button>
          ))}
        </div>
      </div>
      <div className="bg-white rounded-xl border border-slate-200 p-5 flex flex-col">
        <h2 className="font-semibold mb-3">Cart ({lines.length})</h2>
        <div className="flex-1 overflow-auto divide-y divide-slate-100">
          {lines.length === 0 && <div className="text-sm text-slate-400 text-center py-10">Hakuna bidhaa bado</div>}
          {lines.map((l) => (
            <div key={l.id} className="py-3">
              <div className="flex justify-between">
                <div className="text-sm font-medium">{l.jina}</div>
                <div className="text-sm font-semibold">Tsh {l.jumla.toLocaleString()}</div>
              </div>
              <div className="flex justify-between text-xs text-slate-500 mt-1">
                <span>{l.wingi} {l.uom} × {l.beiMauzo.toLocaleString()}</span>
                <button onClick={() => setCart((c) => c.filter((x) => x.id !== l.id))} className="text-red-500">Ondoa</button>
              </div>
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>
        <div className="border-t pt-3 mt-3 space-y-2">
          <div className="flex justify-between text-sm">
            <span>Jumla</span>
            <span className="font-bold text-lg">Tsh {total.toLocaleString()}</span>
          </div>
