const NJIA_FILTERS: { key: NjiaMalipo | 'zote'; label: string }[] = [
  { key: 'zote', label: 'Zote' },
  { key: 'Cash', label: 'Cash' },
  { key: 'M-Pesa', label: 'M-Pesa' },
  { key: 'Tigo Pesa', label: 'Tigo Pesa' },
  { key: 'Split (Cash+M-Pesa)', label: 'Split' },
  { key: 'Benki', label: 'Benki' },
  { key: 'Deni', label: 'Deni' },
]

export default function MuuzajiRisiti() {
  const [q, setQ] = useState('')
  const [filterNjia, setFilterNjia] = useState<NjiaMalipo | 'zote'>('zote')
  const [selected, setSelected] = useState<Risiti | null>(null)

  const filtered = useMemo(() => {
    const query = q.toLowerCase().trim()
    return RISITI.filter((r) => {
      if (filterNjia !== 'zote' && r.njia !== filterNjia) return false
      if (!query) return true
      return (
        r.id.toLowerCase().includes(query) ||
        r.mteja.toLowerCase().includes(query) ||
        (r.simu?.includes(query) ?? false) ||
        r.njia.toLowerCase().includes(query)
      )
    })
  }, [q, filterNjia])

  const stats = useMemo(() => {
    const zote = RISITI.length
    const jumla = RISITI.reduce((s, r) => s + r.kiasi, 0)
    const cash = RISITI.filter((r) => r.njia === 'Cash').reduce((s, r) => s + r.kiasi, 0)
    const mpesa = RISITI.filter((r) => r.njia === 'M-Pesa' || r.njia === 'Split (Cash+M-Pesa)')
      .reduce((s, r) => s + r.kiasi, 0)
    const deni = RISITI.filter((r) => r.njia === 'Deni').reduce((s, r) => s + r.kiasi, 0)
    const bidhaa = RISITI.reduce((s, r) => s + r.bidhaa, 0)
    return { zote, jumla, cash, mpesa, deni, bidhaa }
  }, [])

  return (
    <div className="space-y-5">
      {/* Header */}
      <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-end sm:justify-between gap-3">
        <div>
          <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold flex items-center gap-2">
            <Receipt size={24} className="text-brand-600" />
            Risiti za Leo
          </h1>
          <p className="text-slate-500 text-sm">
            Duka la Kwembe - 09 Nov 2026
          </p>
        </div>
        <button className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-4 py-2 bg-slate-100 rounded-lg text-sm hover:bg-slate-200 self-start sm:self-auto">
          <Download size={16} />
          Pakua Ripoti
        </button>
      </div>

      {/* Stats 4 */}
      <div className="grid grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4 gap-3">
        <StatCard
          label="Risiti Zote"
          value={String(stats.zote)}
          Icon={Receipt}
          accent="brand"
          sub={`${stats.bidhaa} bidhaa`}
        />
        <StatCard
          label="Jumla ya Mauzo"
          value={formatTsh(stats.jumla)}
          Icon={TrendingUp}
          accent="emerald"
        />
        <StatCard
          label="Cash"
          value={formatTsh(stats.cash)}
          Icon={Banknote}
          accent="emerald"
        />
        <StatCard
          label="M-Pesa"
          value={formatTsh(stats.mpesa)}
          Icon={Smartphone}
          accent="brand"
        />
      </div>

      {/* Stats za Deni */}
      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-3">
        <div className="bg-white rounded-xl border border-slate-200 p-4">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
            <Wallet size={16} className="text-red-700" />
            <div className="text-xs text-slate-500">Deni la Leo</div>
          </div>
          <div className="text-lg font-bold text-red-700 mt-2">
            {formatTsh(stats.deni)}
          </div>
          <div className="text-xs text-slate-500 mt-1">
            Wateja {RISITI.filter((r) => r.njia === 'Deni').length}
          </div>
        </div>
        <div className="bg-white rounded-xl border border-slate-200 p-4">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
            <ShoppingBag size={16} className="text-brand-700" />
            <div className="text-xs text-slate-500">Bidhaa Zilizouzwa</div>
          </div>
          <div className="text-lg font-bold text-brand-700 mt-2">
            {stats.bidhaa} pcs
          </div>
          <div className="text-xs text-slate-500 mt-1">
            Kati ya risiti {stats.zote}
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Search + Filter */}
      <div className="bg-white rounded-xl border border-slate-200 p-3 md:p-4 space-y-3">
        <div className="relative">
          <Search
            size={18}
            className="absolute left-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-slate-400 pointer-events-none"
          />
          <input
            value={q}
            onChange={(e) => setQ(e.target.value)}
            placeholder="Tafuta kwa risiti, mteja, simu, au njia..."
            className="w-full pl-10 pr-4 py-2.5 rounded-lg border border-slate-200 focus:border-brand-500 outline-none text-sm"
          />
        </div>
        <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-1.5">
          {NJIA_FILTERS.map((f) => (
            <button
              key={f.key}
              onClick={() => setFilterNjia(f.key)}
              className={`px-2.5 py-1.5 rounded-lg text-xs border transition ${
                filterNjia === f.key
                  ? 'bg-brand-600 text-white border-brand-600'
                  : 'bg-white border-slate-200 hover:bg-slate-50'
              }`}
            >
              {f.label}
            </button>
          ))}
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Risiti List */}
      {filtered.length === 0 ? (
        <div className="bg-white rounded-xl border border-slate-200 p-10 text-center">
          <Receipt size={40} className="mx-auto mb-3 text-slate-300" />
          <div className="font-semibold">Hakuna risiti</div>
          <p className="text-sm text-slate-500 mt-1">Badilisha kichujio au utafutaji.</p>
        </div>
      ) : (
        <div className="space-y-2">
          {filtered.map((r) => {
            const meta = NJIA_META[r.njia]
            return (
              <button
                key={r.id}
                onClick={() => setSelected(r)}
                className="w-full text-left bg-white rounded-xl border border-slate-200 p-4 hover:border-brand-500 hover:shadow-sm transition"
              >
                <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center gap-3">
                  <div className={`w-10 h-10 rounded-lg border grid place-items-center shrink-0 ${meta.color}`}>
                    <meta.Icon size={18} />
                  </div>
                  <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-2 flex-wrap">
                      <span className="font-semibold text-sm">{r.id}</span>
                      <span className={`text-[10px] uppercase font-semibold px-2 py-0.5 rounded-md border ${meta.color}`}>
                        {r.njia}
                      </span>
                    </div>
                    <div className="text-sm text-slate-600 mt-0.5 truncate">
                      {r.mteja}
                      {r.simu && (
                        <span className="text-slate-400 ml-1">- {r.simu}</span>
                      )}
                    </div>
                    <div className="text-xs text-slate-500 mt-0.5 flex items-center gap-2">
                      <Clock size={10} /> {r.saa}
                      <span className="text-slate-300">|</span>
                      <span>{r.bidhaa} bidhaa</span>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                  <div className="sm:text-right shrink-0">
                    <div className="text-base font-bold text-slate-900">
                      {formatTsh(r.kiasi)}
                    </div>
                    <div className="text-xs text-slate-500 flex items-center gap-1 sm:justify-end">
                      Ona
                      <ChevronRight size={12} />
                    </div>
                  </div>
                </div>
              </button>
            )
          })}
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Detail Modal */}
      {selected && (
        <RisitiDetailModal
          risiti={selected}
          onClose={() => setSelected(null)}
        />
      )}
    </div>
  )
}

// ============================================================
// Detail Modal
// ============================================================
function RisitiDetailModal({
  risiti,
  onClose,
}: {
  risiti: Risiti
  onClose: () => void
}) {
  const meta = NJIA_META[risiti.njia]

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-end sm:items-center justify-center p-0 sm:p-4">
      <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-black/40" onClick={onClose} />
      <div className="relative bg-white w-full sm:max-w-md rounded-t-2xl sm:rounded-2xl shadow-2xl max-h-[92vh] overflow-y-auto">
        <div className="sticky top-0 bg-white border-b border-slate-200 px-4 py-3 flex justify-between items-center">
          <div>
            <div className="font-semibold flex items-center gap-2">
              <Receipt size={16} className="text-brand-600" />
              {risiti.id}
            </div>
            <div className="text-xs text-slate-500">{risiti.tarehe} - {risiti.saa}</div>
          </div>
          <button onClick={onClose} className="p-1.5 rounded-lg hover:bg-slate-100">
            <X size={18} />
          </button>
        </div>

        <div className="p-4 space-y-3 text-sm">
          {/* Njia */}
          <div className={`inline-flex items-center gap-1 px-2 py-1 rounded-md border text-xs font-semibold ${meta.color}`}>
            <meta.Icon size={12} />
            {risiti.njia}
          </div>

          {/* Mteja */}
          <div className="p-3 rounded-lg bg-slate-50">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
              <div className="w-9 h-9 rounded-full bg-brand-600 text-white grid place-items-center text-[10px] font-bold shrink-0">
                {risiti.mteja.split(' ').map((n) => n[0]).slice(0, 2).join('')}
              </div>
              <div className="min-w-0">
                <div className="font-medium text-sm truncate">{risiti.mteja}</div>
                {risiti.simu && (
                  <div className="text-xs text-slate-500 flex items-center gap-1">
                    <Phone size={10} /> {risiti.simu}
                  </div>
                )}
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Kiasi */}
          <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
            <div>
              <div className="text-xs text-slate-500">Kiasi</div>
              <div className="text-lg font-bold text-slate-900">
                {formatTsh(risiti.kiasi)}
              </div>
            </div>
            <div>
              <div className="text-xs text-slate-500">Bidhaa</div>
              <div className="font-semibold">{risiti.bidhaa} pcs</div>
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Kumbukumbu */}
          {risiti.kumbukumbu && (
            <div>
              <div className="text-xs text-slate-500">Kumbukumbu</div>
              <div className="font-mono text-xs bg-slate-50 px-2 py-1.5 rounded-md">
                {risiti.kumbukumbu}
              </div>
            </div>
          )}

          {/* Muuzaji + Duka */}
          <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs text-slate-600">
            <User size={12} /> Muuzaji: {risiti.muuzaji}
          </div>
          <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs text-slate-600">
            <Store size={12} /> Duka: {risiti.duka}
          </div>

          {/* Actions */}
          <div className="flex gap-2 pt-2 border-t border-slate-200">
            <button className="flex-1 inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-2.5 rounded-lg bg-brand-600 text-white text-sm font-semibold hover:bg-brand-700">
              <Download size={14} />
              Pakua Risiti
            </button>
            <button
              onClick={onClose}
              className="px-3 py-2.5 rounded-lg bg-slate-100 text-sm hover:bg-slate-200"
            >
              Funga
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}

// ============================================================
// Stat Card
// ============================================================
type Accent = 'brand' | 'emerald' | 'amber' | 'red'
const ACCENT_BORDER: Record<Accent, string> = {
  brand: 'border-l-brand-500',
  emerald: 'border-l-emerald-500',
  amber: 'border-l-amber-500',
  red: 'border-l-red-500',
}
const ACCENT_TEXT: Record<Accent, string> = {
  brand: 'text-brand-700',
  emerald: 'text-emerald-700',
  amber: 'text-amber-700',
  red: 'text-red-700',
}

function StatCard({
  label,
  value,
  Icon,
  accent,
  sub,
}: {
  label: string
  value: string
  Icon: React.ElementType
  accent: Accent
  sub?: string
}) {
  return (
    <div
      className={`bg-white rounded-xl border border-slate-200 border-l-4 ${ACCENT_BORDER[accent]} p-4`}
    >
      <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
        <Icon size={16} className={ACCENT_TEXT[accent]} />
        <div className="text-xs text-slate-500">{label}</div>
      </div>
      <div className="text-base md:text-lg font-bold mt-2 truncate text-slate-900">
        {value}
      </div>
      {sub && <div className="text-[10px] text-slate-500 truncate">{sub}</div>}
    </div>
  )
}

import { useMemo, useState } from 'react'
import {
  Package, Search, Filter, Plus, X, Save, Eye, FileText,
  TrendingDown, Calendar, Tag, AlertTriangle, Clock,
  CheckCircle2, XCircle, Store, User, Layers, RefreshCcw,
  ShoppingBag, RotateCcw, PackageX, Upload,
} from 'lucide-react'
import { useStoki } from '../../context/StokiContext'
import { useDuka } from '../../context/DukaContext'
import { bidhaaKamili } from '../../data/demo'
import { iconForKundi } from '../../utils/icons'
import { formatTsh } from '../../utils/faida'
import {
  sikuZilizobaki,
  haliExpiry,
  rangiExpiry,
  bidhaaZinazokaribiaKuisha,
} from '../../utils/bidhaa'
import type {
  AinaMarekebishoStoki,
  HaliMarekebisho,
  MarekebishoStoki,
} from '../../types'
import { AINA_META, HALI_META, StokiStat } from '../../components/stoki/StokiSubComponents'

const AINA_FILTERS: { key: AinaMarekebishoStoki | 'zote'; label: string }[] = [
  { key: 'zote', label: 'Zote' },
  { key: 'mauzo', label: 'Mauzo' },
  { key: 'uharibifu', label: 'Uharibifu' },
  { key: 'expiry', label: 'Expiry' },
  { key: 'upotevu', label: 'Upotevu' },
  { key: 'kurudisha_msambazaji', label: 'Kurudisha' },
]

// Duka la muuzaji (kwa demo, ni d1)
const DUKA_ID = 'd1'
const DUKA_JINA = 'Smart Hardware - Kwembe'
const ALIYEINGIZA = 'Juma Hassan'

export default function MuuzajiStoreManage() {
  const { marekebisho, ongezaMarekebisho } = useStoki()
  const { maduka } = useDuka()

  const [q, setQ] = useState('')
  const [filterAina, setFilterAina] = useState<AinaMarekebishoStoki | 'zote'>('zote')
  const [filterHali, setFilterHali] = useState<HaliMarekebisho | 'zote'>('zote')
  const [formOpen, setFormOpen] = useState(false)
  const [selected, setSelected] = useState<MarekebishoStoki | null>(null)
  const [tab, setTab] = useState<'stoki' | 'marekebisho' | 'expiry'>('stoki')

  // Chuja marekebisho ya duka la muuzaji tu
  const marekebishoYaDuka = useMemo(
    () => marekebisho.filter((m) => m.dukaId === DUKA_ID),
    [marekebisho]
  )

  // Bidhaa zinazokaribia kuisha (FR5)
  const bidhaaZinakaribia = useMemo(
    () => bidhaaZinazokaribiaKuisha(bidhaaKamili),
    []
  )

  const filteredMarekebisho = useMemo(() => {
    const query = q.toLowerCase().trim()
    return marekebishoYaDuka.filter((m) => {
      if (filterAina !== 'zote' && m.aina !== filterAina) return false
      if (filterHali !== 'zote' && m.hali !== filterHali) return false
      if (!query) return true
      return (
        m.id.toLowerCase().includes(query) ||
        m.jina.toLowerCase().includes(query) ||
        m.sku.toLowerCase().includes(query) ||
        m.sababu.toLowerCase().includes(query)
      )
    })
  }, [marekebishoYaDuka, q, filterAina, filterHali])

  const filteredBidhaa = useMemo(() => {
    const query = q.toLowerCase().trim()
    return bidhaaKamili.filter((b) => {
      if (!query) return true
      return (
        b.jina.toLowerCase().includes(query) ||
        b.sku.toLowerCase().includes(query) ||
        b.kundi.toLowerCase().includes(query) ||
        (b.mtengenezaji?.toLowerCase().includes(query) ?? false)
      )
    })
  }, [q])

  const stats = useMemo(() => {
    const zote = marekebishoYaDuka.length
    const uharibifu = marekebishoYaDuka.filter((m) => m.aina === 'uharibifu').length
    const expiry = marekebishoYaDuka.filter((m) => m.aina === 'expiry').length
    const jumlaHasara = marekebishoYaDuka
      .filter((m) => ['uharibifu', 'expiry', 'upotevu'].includes(m.aina))
      .reduce((s, m) => s + m.thamani, 0)
    const zinazosubiri = marekebishoYaDuka.filter((m) => m.hali === 'inasubiri').length
    return { zote, uharibifu, expiry, jumlaHasara, zinazosubiri }
  }, [marekebishoYaDuka])

  return (
    <div className="space-y-5">
      {/* Header */}
      <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-end sm:justify-between gap-3">
        <div>
          <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold flex items-center gap-2">
            <Package size={24} className="text-brand-600" />
            Usimamizi wa Stoki
          </h1>
          <p className="text-slate-500 text-sm">
            Simamia stoki ya duka lako - ripoti bidhaa zilizoharibika au kuisha
          </p>
        </div>
        <button
          onClick={() => setFormOpen(true)}
          className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-4 py-2 bg-brand-600 text-white rounded-lg text-sm font-semibold hover:bg-brand-700 self-start sm:self-auto"
        >
          <Plus size={16} />
          Ripoti Stoki
        </button>
      </div>

      {/* Duka info */}
      <div className="bg-white rounded-xl border border-slate-200 p-3 md:p-4 flex items-center gap-3">
        <div className="w-10 h-10 rounded-lg bg-slate-50 text-slate-700 grid place-items-center shrink-0">
          <Store size={18} />
        </div>
        <div className="min-w-0">
          <div className="text-sm font-semibold">{DUKA_JINA}</div>
          <div className="text-xs text-slate-500">Muuzaji: {ALIYEINGIZA}</div>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Stats 4 */}
      <div className="grid grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4 gap-3">
        <StokiStat
          label="Marekebisho Yote"
          value={String(stats.zote)}
          Icon={FileText}
          accent="brand"
          sub={`${stats.zinazosubiri} zinasubiri`}
        />
        <StokiStat
          label="Uharibifu"
          value={String(stats.uharibifu)}
          Icon={AlertTriangle}
          accent="red"
        />
        <StokiStat
          label="Expiry"
          value={String(stats.expiry)}
          Icon={Calendar}
          accent="amber"
        />
        <StokiStat
          label="Jumla ya Hasara"
          value={formatTsh(stats.jumlaHasara)}
          Icon={TrendingDown}
          accent="red"
        />
      </div>

      {/* Tabs */}
      <div className="flex gap-2 overflow-x-auto">
        <button
          onClick={() => setTab('stoki')}
          className={`inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-2 rounded-lg text-sm border whitespace-nowrap transition ${
            tab === 'stoki'
              ? 'bg-brand-600 text-white border-brand-600'
              : 'bg-white border-slate-200 hover:bg-slate-50'
          }`}
        >
          <Layers size={14} />
          Stoki ({bidhaaKamili.length})
        </button>
        <button
          onClick={() => setTab('marekebisho')}
          className={`inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-2 rounded-lg text-sm border whitespace-nowrap transition ${
            tab === 'marekebisho'
              ? 'bg-brand-600 text-white border-brand-600'
              : 'bg-white border-slate-200 hover:bg-slate-50'
          }`}
        >
          <RefreshCcw size={14} />
          Ripoti Zangu ({marekebishoYaDuka.length})
        </button>
        <button
          onClick={() => setTab('expiry')}
          className={`inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-2 rounded-lg text-sm border whitespace-nowrap transition ${
            tab === 'expiry'
              ? 'bg-brand-600 text-white border-brand-600'
              : 'bg-white border-slate-200 hover:bg-slate-50'
          }`}
        >
          <Calendar size={14} />
          Zinazokaribia Kuisha ({bidhaaZinakaribia.length})
        </button>
      </div>

      {/* ============================================================ */}
      {/* TAB 1: STOKI */}
      {/* ============================================================ */}
      {tab === 'stoki' && (
        <>
          <div className="bg-white rounded-xl border border-slate-200 p-3 md:p-4">
            <div className="relative">
              <Search
                size={18}
                className="absolute left-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-slate-400 pointer-events-none"
              />
              <input
                value={q}
                onChange={(e) => setQ(e.target.value)}
                placeholder="Tafuta bidhaa kwa jina, SKU, kundi, au mtengenezaji..."
                className="w-full pl-10 pr-4 py-2.5 rounded-lg border border-slate-200 focus:border-brand-500 outline-none text-sm"
              />
            </div>
          </div>

          {filteredBidhaa.length === 0 ? (
            <div className="bg-white rounded-xl border border-slate-200 p-10 text-center">
              <Package size={40} className="mx-auto mb-3 text-slate-300" />
              <div className="font-semibold">Hakuna bidhaa</div>
              <p className="text-sm text-slate-500 mt-1">Badilisha utafutaji.</p>
            </div>
          ) : (
            <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 xl:grid-cols-3 gap-3">
              {filteredBidhaa.map((b) => {
                const Icon = iconForKundi(b.iconKey)
                const siku = sikuZilizobaki(b.expiry)
                const haliE = haliExpiry(b.expiry)
                return (
                  <div
                    key={b.id}
                    className="bg-white rounded-xl border border-slate-200 p-4"
                  >
                    <div className="flex items-start gap-3">
                      <div className="w-11 h-11 rounded-lg bg-slate-50 text-slate-700 grid place-items-center shrink-0">
                        <Icon size={20} />
                      </div>
                      <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
                        <div className="font-semibold text-sm truncate">{b.jina}</div>
                        <div className="text-xs text-slate-500">
                          {b.sku} - {b.kundi}
                        </div>
                        <div className="text-xs text-slate-500">
                          {b.mtengenezaji} - {b.uzito}
                        </div>
                      </div>
                    </div>

                    <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2 mt-3 text-xs">
                      <div>
                        <div className="text-slate-500">Stoki</div>
                        <div className="font-bold text-slate-900">
                          {b.stoki} {b.uom}
                        </div>
                      </div>
                      <div>
                        <div className="text-slate-500">Bei Mauzo</div>
                        <div className="font-semibold text-brand-700">
                          {formatTsh(b.beiMauzo)}
                        </div>
                      </div>
                    </div>

                    {b.expiry && (
                      <div className="mt-2 pt-2 border-t border-slate-100 flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs">
                        <Calendar size={10} className="text-slate-400" />
                        <span className="text-slate-500">Expiry:</span>
                        <span className={`px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded border text-[10px] font-semibold ${rangiExpiry(haliE)}`}>
                          {siku !== undefined && siku > 0
                            ? `${siku} siku`
                            : siku !== undefined && siku <= 0
                            ? `Imeisha`
                            : ''}
                        </span>
                      </div>
                    )}

                    <button
                      onClick={() => setFormOpen(true)}
                      className="mt-3 w-full inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-2 rounded-lg bg-slate-100 hover:bg-slate-200 text-sm font-medium"
                    >
                      <Plus size={14} />
                      Ripoti
                    </button>
                  </div>
                )
              })}
            </div>
          )}
        </>
      )}

      {/* ============================================================ */}
      {/* TAB 2: MAREKEBISHO */}
      {/* ============================================================ */}
      {tab === 'marekebisho' && (
        <>
          <div className="bg-white rounded-xl border border-slate-200 p-3 md:p-4 space-y-3">
            <div className="relative">
              <Search
                size={18}
                className="absolute left-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-slate-400 pointer-events-none"
              />
              <input
                value={q}
                onChange={(e) => setQ(e.target.value)}
                placeholder="Tafuta kwa ID, bidhaa, SKU, au sababu..."
                className="w-full pl-10 pr-4 py-2.5 rounded-lg border border-slate-200 focus:border-brand-500 outline-none text-sm"
              />
            </div>
            <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2 items-center">
              <Filter size={14} className="text-slate-400" />
              <select
                value={filterHali}
                onChange={(e) => setFilterHali(e.target.value as HaliMarekebisho | 'zote')}
                className="px-2 py-1.5 rounded-lg border border-slate-200 text-sm"
              >
                <option value="zote">Hali Zote</option>
                <option value="inasubiri">Inasubiri</option>
                <option value="imethibitishwa">Imethibitishwa</option>
                <option value="imekataliwa">Imekataliwa</option>
              </select>
            </div>
            <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-1.5">
              {AINA_FILTERS.map((f) => (
                <button
                  key={f.key}
                  onClick={() => setFilterAina(f.key)}
                  className={`px-2.5 py-1.5 rounded-lg text-xs border transition ${
                    filterAina === f.key
                      ? 'bg-brand-600 text-white border-brand-600'
                      : 'bg-white border-slate-200 hover:bg-slate-50'
                  }`}
                >
                  {f.label}
                </button>
              ))}
            </div>
          </div>

          {filteredMarekebisho.length === 0 ? (
            <div className="bg-white rounded-xl border border-slate-200 p-10 text-center">
              <RefreshCcw size={40} className="mx-auto mb-3 text-slate-300" />
              <div className="font-semibold">Hakuna ripoti</div>
              <p className="text-sm text-slate-500 mt-1">
                Bofya "Ripoti Stoki" kuanza.
              </p>
            </div>
          ) : (
            <div className="space-y-3">
              {filteredMarekebisho.map((m) => {
                const aina = AINA_META[m.aina]
                const hali = HALI_META[m.hali]
                const bidhaa = bidhaaKamili.find((b) => b.id === m.bidhaaId)
                const Icon = iconForKundi(bidhaa?.iconKey)
                return (
                  <div key={m.id} className="bg-white rounded-xl border border-slate-200 p-4">
                    <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:justify-between gap-3">
                      <button
                        onClick={() => setSelected(m)}
                        className="flex items-center gap-3 min-w-0 flex-1 text-left"
                      >
                        <div className="w-11 h-11 rounded-lg bg-slate-50 text-slate-700 grid place-items-center shrink-0">
                          <Icon size={20} />
                        </div>
                        <div className="min-w-0">
                          <div className="flex items-center gap-2 flex-wrap">
                            <span className="font-semibold text-sm">{m.id}</span>
                            <span className={`text-[10px] uppercase font-semibold px-2 py-0.5 rounded-md border ${aina.color}`}>
                              {aina.label}
                            </span>
                            <span className={`text-[10px] uppercase font-semibold inline-flex items-center gap-1 ${hali.color}`}>
                              <hali.Icon size={10} />
                              {hali.label}
                            </span>
                          </div>
                          <div className="text-sm font-medium mt-0.5 truncate">{m.jina}</div>
                          <div className="text-xs text-slate-500 truncate">
                            {m.sku} - {m.tarehe}
                          </div>
                        </div>
                      </button>
                      <div className="sm:text-right shrink-0">
                        <div className="text-base font-bold text-slate-900">
                          {m.wingi} {m.uom}
                        </div>
                        <div className="text-xs text-slate-500">{formatTsh(m.thamani)}</div>
                      </div>
                    </div>

                    <div className="mt-3 pt-3 border-t border-slate-100 flex gap-2">
                      <button
                        onClick={() => setSelected(m)}
                        className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 px-2.5 py-1.5 rounded-md bg-slate-100 text-slate-700 text-xs font-semibold hover:bg-slate-200"
                      >
                        <Eye size={12} />
                        Ona Maelezo
                      </button>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                )
              })}
            </div>
          )}
        </>
      )}

      {/* ============================================================ */}
      {/* TAB 3: EXPIRY */}
      {/* ============================================================ */}
      {tab === 'expiry' && (
        <div className="space-y-3">
          <div className="bg-white rounded-xl border border-slate-200 p-4">
            <div className="flex items-start gap-2 p-3 rounded-lg border border-amber-200">
              <AlertTriangle size={16} className="text-amber-600 mt-0.5 shrink-0" />
              <div className="text-sm">
                <div className="font-medium text-slate-900">Alamu ya Expiry (FR5)</div>
                <div className="text-slate-500 mt-0.5">
                  Bidhaa {bidhaaZinakaribia.length} zinakaribia kuisha muda (siku 30) au zimeisha.
                  Tafadhali ripoti au uza kwa haraka.
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>

          {bidhaaZinakaribia.length === 0 ? (
            <div className="bg-white rounded-xl border border-slate-200 p-10 text-center">
              <CheckCircle2 size={40} className="mx-auto mb-3 text-emerald-500" />
              <div className="font-semibold">Hakuna bidhaa inayokaribia kuisha</div>
              <p className="text-sm text-slate-500 mt-1">
                Bidhaa zote zina expiry zaidi ya siku 30.
              </p>
            </div>
          ) : (
            <div className="space-y-2">
              {bidhaaZinakaribia.map((b) => {
                const siku = sikuZilizobaki(b.expiry)
                const hali = haliExpiry(b.expiry)
                const Icon = iconForKundi(b.iconKey)
                return (
                  <div
                    key={b.id}
                    className="bg-white rounded-xl border border-slate-200 p-4 flex items-center gap-3"
                  >
                    <div className="w-11 h-11 rounded-lg bg-slate-50 text-slate-700 grid place-items-center shrink-0">
                      <Icon size={20} />
                    </div>
                    <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
                      <div className="font-medium text-sm truncate">{b.jina}</div>
                      <div className="text-xs text-slate-500">
                        {b.sku} - {b.mtengenezaji} - {b.uzito}
                      </div>
                      <div className="text-xs mt-0.5">
                        Expiry: {b.expiry} -{' '}
                        <span className={`px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded border text-[10px] font-semibold ${rangiExpiry(hali)}`}>
                          {siku !== undefined && siku > 0
                            ? `${siku} siku zilizobaki`
                            : siku !== undefined && siku <= 0
                            ? `Imeisha`
                            : ''}
                        </span>
                      </div>
                    </div>
                    <div className="text-right shrink-0">
                      <div className="text-sm font-bold text-slate-900">
                        {b.stoki} {b.uom}
                      </div>
                      <button
                        onClick={() => setFormOpen(true)}
                        className="text-xs text-brand-600 hover:underline"
                      >
                        Ripoti
                      </button>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                )
              })}
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      )}

      {formOpen && (
        <FomuRipoti
          onClose={() => setFormOpen(false)}
          onSubmit={ongezaMarekebisho}
        />
      )}
      {selected && (
        <DetailModal
          marekebisho={selected}
          onClose={() => setSelected(null)}
        />
      )}
    </div>
  )
}

// ============================================================
// Fomu ya Kuripoti (Muuzaji)
// ============================================================
function FomuRipoti({
  onClose,
  onSubmit,
}: {
  onClose: () => void
  onSubmit: ReturnType<typeof useStoki>['ongezaMarekebisho']
}) {
  const [bidhaaId, setBidhaaId] = useState('')
  const [aina, setAina] = useState<AinaMarekebishoStoki>('uharibifu')
  const [wingi, setWingi] = useState(0)
  const [sababu, setSababu] = useState('')
  const [maelezo, setMaelezo] = useState('')
  const [pichaUrl, setPichaUrl] = useState('')
  const [error, setError] = useState('')

  const bidhaa = bidhaaKamili.find((b) => b.id === bidhaaId)

  const handleSubmit = (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault()
    if (!bidhaa) return setError('Chagua bidhaa')
    if (wingi <= 0) return setError('Wingi lazima uwe zaidi ya sifuri')
    if (wingi > bidhaa.stoki) return setError(`Wingi hauwezi kuzidi stoki (${bidhaa.stoki})`)
    if (!sababu.trim()) return setError('Sababu inahitajika')

    setError('')
    onSubmit({
      bidhaaId: bidhaa.id,
      jina: bidhaa.jina,
      sku: bidhaa.sku,
      uom: bidhaa.uom,
      kundi: bidhaa.kundi,
      dukaId: DUKA_ID,
      dukaJina: DUKA_JINA,
      aina,
      wingi,
      beiUnunuzi: bidhaa.beiUnunuzi,
      sababu: sababu.trim(),
      maelezo: maelezo.trim() || undefined,
      pichaUrl: pichaUrl.trim() || undefined,
      expiry: bidhaa.expiry,
      mtengenezaji: bidhaa.mtengenezaji,
      uzito: bidhaa.uzito,
      aliyeingiza: ALIYEINGIZA,
    })
    onClose()
  }

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-end sm:items-center justify-center p-0 sm:p-4">
      <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-black/40" onClick={onClose} />
      <div className="relative bg-white w-full sm:max-w-lg rounded-t-2xl sm:rounded-2xl shadow-2xl max-h-[92vh] overflow-y-auto">
        <div className="sticky top-0 bg-white border-b border-slate-200 px-4 py-3 flex justify-between items-center">
          <div>
            <div className="font-semibold">Ripoti Stoki</div>
            <div className="text-xs text-slate-500">{DUKA_JINA}</div>
          </div>
          <button onClick={onClose} className="p-1.5 rounded-lg hover:bg-slate-100">
            <X size={18} />
          </button>
        </div>

        <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="p-4 space-y-3">
          <div>
            <label className="text-sm font-medium">Chagua Bidhaa</label>
            <select
              value={bidhaaId}
              onChange={(e) => setBidhaaId(e.target.value)}
              className="mt-1 w-full px-3 py-2.5 rounded-lg border border-slate-200 focus:border-brand-500 outline-none text-sm"
            >
              <option value="">-- Chagua bidhaa --</option>
              {bidhaaKamili.map((b) => (
                <option key={b.id} value={b.id}>
                  {b.jina} ({b.sku}) - Stoki: {b.stoki} {b.uom}
                </option>
              ))}
            </select>
          </div>

          {bidhaa && (
            <div className="p-3 rounded-lg bg-slate-50 flex items-center gap-3">
              <div className="w-10 h-10 rounded-lg bg-white border border-slate-200 grid place-items-center text-slate-600 shrink-0">
                <Package size={18} />
              </div>
              <div className="flex-1 min-w-0 text-sm">
                <div className="font-medium truncate">{bidhaa.jina}</div>
                <div className="text-xs text-slate-500">
                  {bidhaa.mtengenezaji} - {bidhaa.uzito} - Expiry: {bidhaa.expiry || 'Hakuna'}
                </div>
                <div className="text-xs text-slate-500">
                  Stoki: {bidhaa.stoki} {bidhaa.uom}
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>
          )}

          <div>
            <label className="text-sm font-medium">Aina ya Ripoti</label>
            <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2 mt-1">
              {(['uharibifu', 'expiry', 'upotevu', 'kurudisha_msambazaji'] as AinaMarekebishoStoki[]).map((a) => {
                const meta = AINA_META[a]
                return (
                  <button
                    key={a}
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => setAina(a)}
                    className={`px-3 py-2 rounded-lg border text-xs text-left ${
                      aina === a
                        ? 'border-brand-500 bg-slate-50 font-semibold'
                        : 'border-slate-200 hover:bg-slate-50'
                    }`}
                  >
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
                      <meta.Icon size={12} />
                      <span>{meta.label}</span>
                    </div>
                  </button>
                )
              })}
            </div>
          </div>

          <div>
            <label className="text-sm font-medium">Wingi</label>
            <input
              type="number"
              value={wingi || ''}
              onChange={(e) => setWingi(Number(e.target.value))}
              placeholder="Mfano: 5"
              className="mt-1 w-full px-3 py-2.5 rounded-lg border border-slate-200 focus:border-brand-500 outline-none text-sm"
            />
            {bidhaa && (
              <div className="text-xs text-slate-500 mt-1">
                Stoki iliyopo: {bidhaa.stoki} {bidhaa.uom}
              </div>
            )}
          </div>

          <div>
            <label className="text-sm font-medium">Sababu</label>
            <input
              value={sababu}
              onChange={(e) => setSababu(e.target.value)}
              placeholder="Mfano: Saruji imekauka"
              className="mt-1 w-full px-3 py-2.5 rounded-lg border border-slate-200 focus:border-brand-500 outline-none text-sm"
            />
          </div>

          <div>
            <label className="text-sm font-medium">Maelezo (hiari)</label>
            <textarea
              value={maelezo}
              onChange={(e) => setMaelezo(e.target.value)}
              rows={2}
              placeholder="Eleza zaidi..."
              className="mt-1 w-full px-3 py-2.5 rounded-lg border border-slate-200 focus:border-brand-500 outline-none text-sm resize-none"
            />
          </div>

          <div>
            <label className="text-sm font-medium">Picha (hiari)</label>
            <div className="mt-1 flex items-center gap-2">
              <input
                value={pichaUrl}
                onChange={(e) => setPichaUrl(e.target.value)}
                placeholder="URL ya picha"
                className="flex-1 px-3 py-2.5 rounded-lg border border-slate-200 focus:border-brand-500 outline-none text-sm"
              />
              <button
                type="button"
                className="p-2.5 rounded-lg bg-slate-100 hover:bg-slate-200"
                title="Pakia picha"
              >
                <Upload size={16} />
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>

          {error && (
            <div className="flex items-start gap-2 p-3 rounded-lg bg-red-50 text-red-700 text-sm">
              <AlertTriangle size={16} className="mt-0.5 shrink-0" />
              {error}
            </div>
          )}

          <div className="flex gap-2 pt-2">
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={onClose}
              className="px-4 py-2.5 rounded-lg bg-slate-100 text-sm hover:bg-slate-200"
            >
              Ghairi
            </button>
            <button
              type="submit"
              className="flex-1 inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 px-4 py-2.5 rounded-lg bg-brand-600 text-white text-sm font-semibold hover:bg-brand-700"
            >
              <Save size={16} />
              Tuma Ripoti
            </button>
          </div>
        </form>
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}

// ============================================================
// Detail Modal (Muuzaji - read only)
// ============================================================
function DetailModal({
  marekebisho,
  onClose,
}: {
  marekebisho: MarekebishoStoki
  onClose: () => void
}) {
  const aina = AINA_META[marekebisho.aina]
  const hali = HALI_META[marekebisho.hali]

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-end sm:items-center justify-center p-0 sm:p-4">
      <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-black/40" onClick={onClose} />
      <div className="relative bg-white w-full sm:max-w-md rounded-t-2xl sm:rounded-2xl shadow-2xl max-h-[92vh] overflow-y-auto">
        <div className="sticky top-0 bg-white border-b border-slate-200 px-4 py-3 flex justify-between items-center">
          <div>
            <div className="font-semibold">{marekebisho.id}</div>
            <div className="text-xs text-slate-500">{marekebisho.tarehe}</div>
          </div>
          <button onClick={onClose} className="p-1.5 rounded-lg hover:bg-slate-100">
            <X size={18} />
          </button>
        </div>

        <div className="p-4 space-y-3 text-sm">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
            <span className={`inline-flex items-center gap-1 px-2 py-1 rounded-md border text-xs font-semibold ${aina.color}`}>
              <aina.Icon size={12} />
              {aina.label}
            </span>
            <span className={`inline-flex items-center gap-1 text-xs font-semibold ${hali.color}`}>
              <hali.Icon size={12} />
              {hali.label}
            </span>
          </div>

          {marekebisho.pichaUrl && (
            <div className="rounded-lg overflow-hidden border border-slate-200">
              <img
                src={marekebisho.pichaUrl}
                alt={marekebisho.jina}
                className="w-full h-40 object-cover"
                onError={(e) => {
                  (e.target as HTMLImageElement).style.display = 'none'
                }}
              />
            </div>
          )}

          <div>
            <div className="text-xs text-slate-500">Bidhaa</div>
            <div className="font-medium">{marekebisho.jina}</div>
            <div className="text-xs text-slate-500">
              {marekebisho.sku} - {marekebisho.kundi}
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs text-slate-600">
            <Store size={12} /> {marekebisho.dukaJina}
          </div>

          {marekebisho.mtengenezaji && (
            <div>
              <div className="text-xs text-slate-500">Mtengenezaji</div>
              <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
                <Tag size={12} className="text-slate-400" />
                {marekebisho.mtengenezaji} - {marekebisho.uzito}
              </div>
            </div>
          )}

          <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
            <div>
              <div className="text-xs text-slate-500">Wingi</div>
              <div className="font-semibold">
                {marekebisho.wingi} {marekebisho.uom}
              </div>
            </div>
            <div>
              <div className="text-xs text-slate-500">Thamani</div>
              <div className="font-bold text-slate-900">
                {formatTsh(marekebisho.thamani)}
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>

          {marekebisho.expiry && (
            <div>
              <div className="text-xs text-slate-500">Expiry</div>
              <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs">
                <Calendar size={12} className="text-slate-400" />
                {marekebisho.expiry}
                {(() => {
                  const siku = sikuZilizobaki(marekebisho.expiry)
                  const haliE = haliExpiry(marekebisho.expiry)
                  return (
                    <span className={`px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded border text-[10px] font-semibold ${rangiExpiry(haliE)}`}>
                      {siku !== undefined && siku > 0
                        ? `${siku} siku`
                        : siku !== undefined && siku <= 0
                        ? `Imeisha`
                        : ''}
                    </span>
                  )
                })()}
              </div>
            </div>
          )}

          <div>
            <div className="text-xs text-slate-500">Sababu</div>
            <div>{marekebisho.sababu}</div>
          </div>

          {marekebisho.maelezo && (
            <div>
              <div className="text-xs text-slate-500">Maelezo</div>
              <div className="text-sm">{marekebisho.maelezo}</div>
            </div>
          )}

          <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs text-slate-600">
            <User size={12} /> {marekebisho.aliyeingiza}
          </div>

          <div className="pt-2 border-t border-slate-200">
            <button
              onClick={onClose}
              className="w-full px-3 py-2.5 rounded-lg bg-slate-100 text-sm hover:bg-slate-200"
            >
              Funga
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}

import { useState } from 'react'
import {
  UserPlus, Search, Phone, MapPin, Wallet, X, Save,
} from 'lucide-react'
import { useCustomers } from '../../context/CustomerContext'
import type { AinaMteja } from '../../types'

const AINA: { key: AinaMteja; label: string }[] = [
  { key: 'fundi', label: 'Fundi' },
  { key: 'mjenzi', label: 'Mjenzi' },
  { key: 'kampuni', label: 'Kampuni' },
  { key: 'mteja', label: 'Mteja' },
]

export default function MuuzajiWateja() {
  const { wateja, ongezaMteja } = useCustomers()
  const [q, setQ] = useState('')
  const [formOpen, setFormOpen] = useState(false)
  const [jina, setJina] = useState('')
  const [simu, setSimu] = useState('')
  const [aina, setAina] = useState<AinaMteja>('mteja')
  const [mahali, setMahali] = useState('')

  const filtered = wateja.filter(
    (m) =>
      m.jina.toLowerCase().includes(q.toLowerCase()) ||
      m.simu.includes(q) ||
      m.aina.includes(q.toLowerCase())
  )

  const handleSubmit = (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault()
    if (!jina.trim() || !simu.trim()) return
    ongezaMteja({ jina, simu, aina, mahali })
    setJina('')
    setSimu('')
    setAina('mteja')
    setMahali('')
    setFormOpen(false)
  }

  return (
    <div className="space-y-5">
      <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-end sm:justify-between gap-3">
        <div>
          <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold">Wateja</h1>
          <p className="text-slate-500 text-sm">Sajili na simamia wateja wa duka</p>
        </div>
        <button
          onClick={() => setFormOpen(true)}
          className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-4 py-2 bg-brand-600 text-white rounded-lg text-sm font-semibold hover:bg-brand-700 self-start sm:self-auto"
        >
          <UserPlus size={16} />
          Ongeza Mteja
        </button>
      </div>

      <div className="grid grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4 gap-3">
        <div className="bg-white rounded-xl border p-4">
          <div className="text-xs text-slate-500">Wateja Wote</div>
          <div className="text-xl font-bold">{wateja.length}</div>
        </div>
        <div className="bg-white rounded-xl border p-4">
          <div className="text-xs text-slate-500">Wenye Deni</div>
          <div className="text-xl font-bold text-red-600">
            {wateja.filter((m) => m.deni > 0).length}
          </div>
        </div>
        <div className="bg-white rounded-xl border p-4">
          <div className="text-xs text-slate-500">Jumla ya Madeni</div>
          <div className="text-sm font-bold text-red-600 truncate">
            Tsh {wateja.reduce((s, m) => s + m.deni, 0).toLocaleString()}
          </div>
        </div>
        <div className="bg-white rounded-xl border p-4">
          <div className="text-xs text-slate-500">Manunuzi Yote</div>
          <div className="text-sm font-bold text-brand-700 truncate">
            Tsh {wateja.reduce((s, m) => s + m.jumlaManunuzi, 0).toLocaleString()}
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="relative">
        <Search size={18} className="absolute left-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-slate-400" />
        <input
          value={q}
          onChange={(e) => setQ(e.target.value)}
          placeholder="Tafuta kwa jina, simu, au aina..."
          className="w-full pl-10 pr-4 py-2.5 rounded-lg border border-slate-200 focus:border-brand-500 outline-none text-sm"
        />
      </div>

      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3 gap-3">
        {filtered.map((m) => (
          <div key={m.id} className="bg-white rounded-xl border border-slate-200 p-4">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
              <div className="w-10 h-10 rounded-full bg-brand-600 text-white grid place-items-center text-sm font-bold">
                {m.jina.split(' ').map((n) => n[0]).slice(0, 2).join('')}
              </div>
              <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
                <div className="font-semibold text-sm truncate">{m.jina}</div>
                <div className="text-xs text-slate-500 capitalize">{m.aina}</div>
              </div>
            </div>
            <div className="mt-3 space-y-1 text-xs text-slate-600">
              <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
                <Phone size={12} /> {m.simu}
              </div>
              {m.mahali && (
                <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
                  <MapPin size={12} /> {m.mahali}
                </div>
              )}
            </div>
            <div className="mt-3 pt-3 border-t border-slate-100 grid grid-cols-2 gap-2 text-xs">
              <div>
                <div className="text-slate-500">Manunuzi</div>
                <div className="font-bold text-brand-700 truncate">
                  Tsh {m.jumlaManunuzi.toLocaleString()}
                </div>
              </div>
              <div>
                <div className="text-slate-500">Deni</div>
                <div className={`font-bold truncate ${m.deni > 0 ? 'text-red-600' : 'text-emerald-600'}`}>
                  Tsh {m.deni.toLocaleString()}
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        ))}
      </div>

      {formOpen && (
        <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center p-4">
          <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-black/40" onClick={() => setFormOpen(false)} />
          <div className="relative bg-white w-full max-w-md rounded-2xl shadow-2xl">
            <div className="flex justify-between items-center p-4 border-b border-slate-200">
              <div className="font-semibold">Ongeza Mteja Mpya</div>
              <button onClick={() => setFormOpen(false)} className="p-1.5 rounded-lg hover:bg-slate-100">
                <X size={18} />
              </button>
            </div>
            <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="p-4 space-y-3">
              <div>
                <label className="text-sm font-medium">Jina</label>
                <input
                  value={jina}
                  onChange={(e) => setJina(e.target.value)}
                  className="mt-1 w-full px-3 py-2.5 rounded-lg border border-slate-200 focus:border-brand-500 outline-none text-sm"
                  required
                />
              </div>
              <div>
                <label className="text-sm font-medium">Simu</label>
                <input
                  value={simu}
                  onChange={(e) => setSimu(e.target.value)}
                  placeholder="0754-123-456"
                  className="mt-1 w-full px-3 py-2.5 rounded-lg border border-slate-200 focus:border-brand-500 outline-none text-sm"
                  required
                />
              </div>
              <div>
                <label className="text-sm font-medium">Aina</label>
                <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2 mt-1">
                  {AINA.map((a) => (
                    <button
                      key={a.key}
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() => setAina(a.key)}
                      className={`px-3 py-2 rounded-lg border text-sm ${
                        aina === a.key
                          ? 'bg-brand-50 text-brand-700 border-brand-500 font-semibold'
                          : 'border-slate-200 hover:bg-slate-50'
                      }`}
                    >
                      {a.label}
                    </button>
                  ))}
                </div>
              </div>
              <div>
                <label className="text-sm font-medium">Mahali (hiari)</label>
                <input
                  value={mahali}
                  onChange={(e) => setMahali(e.target.value)}
                  className="mt-1 w-full px-3 py-2.5 rounded-lg border border-slate-200 focus:border-brand-500 outline-none text-sm"
                />
              </div>
              <div className="flex gap-2 pt-2">
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => setFormOpen(false)}
                  className="px-4 py-2.5 rounded-lg bg-slate-100 text-sm hover:bg-slate-200"
                >
                  Ghairi
                </button>
                <button
                  type="submit"
                  className="flex-1 inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 px-4 py-2.5 rounded-lg bg-brand-600 text-white text-sm font-semibold hover:bg-brand-700"
                >
                  <Save size={16} />
                  Hifadhi
                </button>
              </div>
            </form>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  )
}


import { useState } from 'react'
import { useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom'
import { Search, Plus, Minus, ShoppingCart, Check, Package } from 'lucide-react'
import { bidhaa } from '../../data/demo'
import { useCart } from '../../context/CartContext'
import { iconForKundi } from '../../utils/icons'

export default function MtejaBidhaa() {
  const navigate = useNavigate()
  const [q, setQ] = useState('')
  const { add, inc, dec, detailed, count, total } = useCart()

  const filtered = bidhaa.filter(
    (b) =>
      b.jina.toLowerCase().includes(q.toLowerCase()) ||
      b.sku.toLowerCase().includes(q.toLowerCase()) ||
      b.kundi.toLowerCase().includes(q.toLowerCase())
  )

  const wingiKwenyeCart = (id: string) =>
    detailed.find((l) => l.id === id)?.wingi ?? 0

  return (
    <div className="space-y-5">
      <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-end sm:justify-between gap-3">
        <div>
          <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold">Bidhaa Zetu</h1>
          <p className="text-slate-500 text-sm">Tafuta kwa jina au SKU</p>
        </div>

        {count > 0 && (
          <button
            onClick={() => navigate('/mteja/quotation')}
            className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 px-4 py-2 rounded-lg bg-brand-600 text-white text-sm font-semibold hover:bg-brand-700 self-start sm:self-auto"
          >
            <ShoppingCart size={16} />
            Cart ({count}) - Tsh {total.toLocaleString()}
          </button>
        )}
      </div>

      <div className="relative">
        <Search
          size={18}
          className="absolute left-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-slate-400 pointer-events-none"
        />
        <input
          value={q}
          onChange={(e) => setQ(e.target.value)}
          placeholder="Tafuta bidhaa... (mfano: D12, saruji)"
          className="w-full pl-10 pr-4 py-3 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 focus:border-brand-500 outline-none"
        />
      </div>

      {filtered.length === 0 ? (
        <div className="bg-white rounded-xl border border-slate-200 p-10 text-center text-sm text-slate-500">
          <Package size={32} className="mx-auto mb-2 text-slate-300" />
          Hakuna bidhaa iliyopatikana kwa "{q}"
        </div>
      ) : (
        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3 gap-4">
          {filtered.map((b) => {
            const c = wingiKwenyeCart(b.id)
            const inCart = c > 0
            const Icon = iconForKundi(b.iconKey)

            return (
              <div
                key={b.id}
                className={`bg-white rounded-xl border p-5 transition ${
                  inCart ? 'border-brand-500 ring-2 ring-brand-100' : 'border-slate-200'
                }`}
              >
                <div className="flex items-start justify-between">
                  <div className="w-11 h-11 rounded-lg bg-slate-100 text-slate-600 grid place-items-center">
                    <Icon size={20} />
                  </div>
                  {inCart && (
                    <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 text-[10px] uppercase font-semibold text-emerald-700 bg-emerald-50 px-2 py-1 rounded-md">
                      <Check size={12} />
                      Kwenye Cart
                    </span>
                  )}
                </div>

                <div className="mt-3">
                  <div className="font-semibold">{b.jina}</div>
                  <div className="text-xs text-slate-500">
                    {b.sku} - {b.duka}
                  </div>
                </div>

                <div className="flex justify-between items-end mt-4">
                  <div>
                    <div className="text-lg font-bold text-brand-700">
                      Tsh {b.beiMauzo.toLocaleString()}
                    </div>
                    <div className="text-xs text-slate-500">kwa {b.uom}</div>
                  </div>

                  {inCart ? (
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 bg-brand-50 rounded-lg p-1">
                      <button
                        onClick={() => dec(b.id)}
                        className="w-7 h-7 rounded-md bg-white hover:bg-slate-100 grid place-items-center text-brand-700 shadow-sm"
                        aria-label="Punguza"
                      >
                        <Minus size={14} />
                      </button>
                      <span className="min-w-6 text-center text-sm font-bold text-brand-700">
                        {c}
                      </span>
                      <button
                        onClick={() => inc(b.id)}
                        className="w-7 h-7 rounded-md bg-brand-600 hover:bg-brand-700 grid place-items-center text-white shadow-sm"
                        aria-label="Ongeza"
                      >
                        <Plus size={14} />
                      </button>
                    </div>
                  ) : (
                    <button
                      onClick={() => add(b.id)}
                      className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1.5 bg-brand-600 text-white rounded-lg text-sm font-semibold hover:bg-brand-700"
                    >
                      <Plus size={14} />
                      Ongeza
                    </button>
                  )}
                </div>

                {inCart && (
                  <div className="mt-3 pt-3 border-t border-slate-100 flex justify-between text-sm">
                    <span className="text-slate-500">Jumla ndogo</span>
                    <span className="font-semibold">
                      Tsh {(b.beiMauzo * c).toLocaleString()}
                    </span>
                  </div>
                )}
              </div>
            )
          })}
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  )
}


import { madeni } from '../../data/demo'

import { useParams, useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom'
import {
  CheckCircle2,
  Package,
  Smartphone,
  ArrowLeft,
  Download,
  Home,
  FileText,
} from 'lucide-react'
import { useOrders } from '../../context/OrderContext'

export default function MtejaOrder() {
  const { id } = useParams<{ id: string }>()
  const navigate = useNavigate()
  const { getOrder } = useOrders()

  const order = id ? getOrder(id) : undefined

  if (!order) {
    return (
      <div className="max-w-2xl mx-auto bg-white rounded-xl border border-slate-200 p-10 text-center">
        <FileText size={40} className="mx-auto mb-3 text-slate-300" />
        <div className="font-semibold">Order haipo</div>
        <p className="text-sm text-slate-500 mt-1">
          Order uliyoitafuta haipatikani. Labda ilifutwa au haijakamilika.
        </p>
        <button
          onClick={() => navigate('/mteja')}
          className="mt-4 inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-4 py-2 bg-brand-600 text-white rounded-lg text-sm font-semibold hover:bg-brand-700"
        >
          <Home size={16} />
          Nyumbani
        </button>
      </div>
    )
  }

  return (
    <div className="space-y-5 max-w-3xl">
      <div className="bg-gradient-to-r from-emerald-600 to-emerald-500 text-white rounded-2xl p-6">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
          <div className="w-12 h-12 rounded-full bg-white/20 grid place-items-center">
            <CheckCircle2 size={24} />
          </div>
          <div>
            <div className="text-lg font-bold">Malipo Yamekamilika</div>
            <div className="text-sm opacity-90">
              Order {order.id} imethibitishwa
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
        <div className="mt-4 text-sm opacity-90">Tarehe: {order.tarehe}</div>
      </div>

      <div className="bg-white rounded-xl border border-slate-200 p-4 md:p-6">
        <h2 className="font-semibold mb-3 flex items-center gap-2">
          <Package size={18} className="text-brand-600" />
          Bidhaa Zilizonunuliwa
        </h2>
        <div className="overflow-x-auto">
          <table className="w-full text-sm min-w-[480px]">
            <thead className="text-left text-slate-500 border-b">
              <tr>
                <th className="py-2">Bidhaa</th>
                <th className="py-2 text-right">Wingi</th>
                <th className="py-2 text-right">Jumla</th>
              </tr>
            </thead>
            <tbody>
              {order.bidhaa.map((b) => (
                <tr key={b.id} className="border-b border-slate-100">
                  <td className="py-2">
                    <div className="font-medium">{b.jina}</div>
                    <div className="text-xs text-slate-500">{b.sku}</div>
                  </td>
                  <td className="py-2 text-right">
                    {b.wingi} {b.uom}
                  </td>
                  <td className="py-2 text-right font-semibold">
                    Tsh {b.jumla.toLocaleString()}
                  </td>
                </tr>
              ))}
            </tbody>
            <tfoot>
              <tr>
                <td colSpan={2} className="py-2 text-right font-semibold">
                  JUMLA
                </td>
                <td className="py-2 text-right font-bold text-brand-700">
                  Tsh {order.jumla.toLocaleString()}
                </td>
              </tr>
            </tfoot>
          </table>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="bg-white rounded-xl border border-slate-200 p-4 md:p-6">
        <h2 className="font-semibold mb-3 flex items-center gap-2">
          <Smartphone size={18} className="text-emerald-600" />
          Malipo
        </h2>
        <div className="space-y-2">
          {order.malipo.map((m) => (
            <div
              key={m.id}
              className="flex items-center gap-3 p-3 rounded-lg bg-emerald-50 border border-emerald-100"
            >
              <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
                <div className="text-sm font-medium">
                  {m.njia.toUpperCase()} - {m.simu}
                </div>
                <div className="text-xs text-slate-600 font-mono">
                  Kumbukumbu: {m.kumbukumbu}
                </div>
                <div className="text-[10px] text-slate-500">{m.tarehe}</div>
              </div>
              <div className="text-sm font-bold text-emerald-700 whitespace-nowrap">
                Tsh {m.kiasi.toLocaleString()}
              </div>
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row gap-2">
        <button
          onClick={() => navigate('/mteja')}
          className="inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 px-4 py-2 bg-brand-600 text-white rounded-lg text-sm font-semibold hover:bg-brand-700"
        >
          <Home size={16} />
          Nyumbani
        </button>
        <button className="inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 px-4 py-2 bg-slate-100 rounded-lg text-sm hover:bg-slate-200">
          <Download size={16} />
          Pakua Risiti (PDF)
        </button>
        <button
          onClick={() => navigate('/mteja/bidhaa')}
          className="inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 px-4 py-2 bg-slate-100 rounded-lg text-sm hover:bg-slate-200 sm:ml-auto"
        >
          <ArrowLeft size={16} />
          Nunua Tena
        </button>
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}

import { useMemo, useState } from 'react'
import { useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom'
import {
  ShoppingBag,
  Search,
  Filter,
  CheckCircle2,
  Clock,
  XCircle,
  Package,
  Smartphone,
  ChevronRight,
  Receipt,
  TrendingUp,
  Wallet,
  ArrowLeft,
  Inbox,
} from 'lucide-react'
import { useOrders } from '../../context/OrderContext'
import type { HaliOrder } from '../../types'

type FilterKey = 'zote' | HaliOrder

const FILTERS: { key: FilterKey; label: string; Icon: React.ElementType }[] = [
  { key: 'zote', label: 'Zote', Icon: Inbox },
  { key: 'imekamilika', label: 'Zilizokamilika', Icon: CheckCircle2 },
  { key: 'inasubiri_malipo', label: 'Zinasubiri Malipo', Icon: Clock },
  { key: 'imekataliwa', label: 'Zilizokataliwa', Icon: XCircle },
]

const HALI_META: Record<
  HaliOrder,
  { label: string; color: string; Icon: React.ElementType }
> = {
  imekamilika: {
    label: 'Imekamilika',
    color: 'bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-700 border-emerald-200',
    Icon: CheckCircle2,
  },
  inasubiri_malipo: {
    label: 'Inasubiri Malipo',
    color: 'bg-amber-50 text-amber-700 border-amber-200',
    Icon: Clock,
  },
  imekataliwa: {
    label: 'Imekataliwa',
    color: 'bg-red-50 text-red-700 border-red-200',
    Icon: XCircle,
  },
}

export default function MtejaOrders() {
  const navigate = useNavigate()
  const { orders } = useOrders()

  const [filter, setFilter] = useState<FilterKey>('zote')
  const [q, setQ] = useState('')

  const filtered = useMemo(() => {
    const query = q.trim().toLowerCase()
    return orders.filter((o) => {
      if (filter !== 'zote' && o.hali !== filter) return false
      if (!query) return true
      if (o.id.toLowerCase().includes(query)) return true
      if (o.quotationId.toLowerCase().includes(query)) return true
      return o.bidhaa.some((b) => b.jina.toLowerCase().includes(query))
    })
  }, [orders, filter, q])

  const stats = useMemo(() => {
    const totalOrders = orders.length
    const totalSpent = orders.reduce((s, o) => s + o.jumla, 0)
    const avg = totalOrders > 0 ? totalSpent / totalOrders : 0
    const completed = orders.filter((o) => o.hali === 'imekamilika').length
    return { totalOrders, totalSpent, avg, completed }
  }, [orders])

  return (
    <div className="space-y-5">
      <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-end sm:justify-between gap-3">
        <div>
          <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold">Order Zangu</h1>
          <p className="text-slate-500 text-sm">
            Fuatilia orders zote ulizokamilisha
          </p>
        </div>
        <button
          onClick={() => navigate('/mteja/bidhaa')}
          className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-4 py-2 bg-brand-600 text-white rounded-lg text-sm font-semibold hover:bg-brand-700 self-start sm:self-auto"
        >
          <ArrowLeft size={16} />
          Nunua Tena
        </button>
      </div>

      {/* Stats */}
      <div className="grid grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4 gap-3 md:gap-4">
        <StatCard
          label="Orders Zote"
          value={String(stats.totalOrders)}
          Icon={ShoppingBag}
          tone="brand"
        />
        <StatCard
          label="Zilizokamilika"
          value={String(stats.completed)}
          Icon={CheckCircle2}
          tone="emerald"
        />
        <StatCard
          label="Jumla ya Matumizi"
          value={`Tsh ${stats.totalSpent.toLocaleString()}`}
          Icon={Wallet}
          tone="amber"
        />
        <StatCard
          label="Wastani kwa Order"
          value={`Tsh ${Math.round(stats.avg).toLocaleString()}`}
          Icon={TrendingUp}
          tone="brand"
        />
      </div>

      {/* Search + filters */}
      <div className="bg-white rounded-xl border border-slate-200 p-3 md:p-4 space-y-3">
        <div className="relative">
          <Search
            size={18}
            className="absolute left-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-slate-400 pointer-events-none"
          />
          <input
            value={q}
            onChange={(e) => setQ(e.target.value)}
            placeholder="Tafuta kwa Order ID, Quotation ID, au bidhaa..."
            className="w-full pl-10 pr-4 py-2.5 rounded-lg border border-slate-200 focus:border-brand-500 outline-none text-sm"
          />
        </div>
        <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
          {FILTERS.map((f) => {
            const active = filter === f.key
            return (
              <button
                key={f.key}
                onClick={() => setFilter(f.key)}
                className={`inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg text-sm border transition ${
                  active
                    ? 'bg-brand-600 text-white border-brand-600'
                    : 'bg-white text-slate-700 border-slate-200 hover:bg-slate-50'
                }`}
              >
                <f.Icon size={14} />
                {f.label}
              </button>
            )
          })}
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Orders list */}
      {filtered.length === 0 ? (
        <EmptyState
          hasOrders={orders.length > 0}
          onShop={() => navigate('/mteja/bidhaa')}
        />
      ) : (
        <div className="space-y-3">
          {filtered.map((o) => {
            const meta = HALI_META[o.hali]
            const totalItems = o.bidhaa.reduce((s, b) => s + b.wingi, 0)
            const firstPayment = o.malipo[0]

            return (
              <button
                key={o.id}
                onClick={() => navigate(`/mteja/order/${o.id}`)}
                className="w-full text-left bg-white rounded-xl border border-slate-200 p-4 md:p-5 hover:border-brand-500 hover:shadow-sm transition"
              >
                <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center sm:justify-between gap-3">
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-3 min-w-0">
                    <div className="w-11 h-11 rounded-lg bg-brand-50 text-brand-600 grid place-items-center shrink-0">
                      <Receipt size={20} />
                    </div>
                    <div className="min-w-0">
                      <div className="flex items-center gap-2 flex-wrap">
                        <span className="font-semibold text-sm">{o.id}</span>
                        <span
                          className={`inline-flex items-center gap-1 text-[10px] uppercase font-semibold px-2 py-0.5 rounded-md border ${meta.color}`}
                        >
                          <meta.Icon size={10} />
                          {meta.label}
                        </span>
                      </div>
                      <div className="text-xs text-slate-500 mt-0.5 truncate">
                        {o.tarehe} - Quotation {o.quotationId}
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  </div>

                  <div className="sm:text-right">
                    <div className="text-lg font-bold text-brand-700">
                      Tsh {o.jumla.toLocaleString()}
                    </div>
                    <div className="text-xs text-slate-500">
                      Bidhaa {o.bidhaa.length} ({totalItems} pcs)
                    </div>
                  </div>
                </div>

                <div className="mt-3 pt-3 border-t border-slate-100 grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-2 text-xs text-slate-600">
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 min-w-0">
                    <Package size={14} className="text-slate-400 shrink-0" />
                    <span className="truncate">
                      {o.bidhaa.map((b) => b.jina).join(', ')}
                    </span>
                  </div>
                  {firstPayment && (
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 sm:justify-end">
                      <Smartphone size={14} className="text-emerald-600 shrink-0" />
                      <span className="truncate">
                        {firstPayment.njia.toUpperCase()} - {firstPayment.kumbukumbu}
                      </span>
                    </div>
                  )}
                </div>

                <div className="mt-3 flex items-center justify-end text-xs font-semibold text-brand-600">
                  Ona Maelezo
                  <ChevronRight size={14} />
                </div>
              </button>
            )
          })}
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  )
}

// ---------- Sub-components ----------

type Tone = 'brand' | 'emerald' | 'amber' | 'red'

const TONE: Record<Tone, string> = {
  brand: 'bg-brand-50 text-brand-700',
  emerald: 'bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-700',
  amber: 'bg-amber-50 text-amber-700',
  red: 'bg-red-50 text-red-700',
}

function StatCard({
  label,
  value,
  Icon,
  tone,
}: {
  label: string
  value: string
  Icon: React.ElementType
  tone: Tone
}) {
  return (
    <div className="bg-white rounded-xl border border-slate-200 p-4">
      <div className={`inline-flex w-9 h-9 rounded-lg items-center justify-center ${TONE[tone]}`}>
        <Icon size={16} />
      </div>
      <div className="text-xs text-slate-500 mt-2">{label}</div>
      <div className="text-base md:text-lg font-bold mt-0.5 truncate">{value}</div>
    </div>
  )
}

function EmptyState({
  hasOrders,
  onShop,
}: {
  hasOrders: boolean
  onShop: () => void
}) {
  return (
    <div className="bg-white rounded-xl border border-slate-200 p-10 text-center">
      <ShoppingBag size={40} className="mx-auto mb-3 text-slate-300" />
      <div className="font-semibold">
        {hasOrders ? 'Hakuna order inayolingana' : 'Hakuna order bado'}
      </div>
      <p className="text-sm text-slate-500 mt-1">
        {hasOrders
          ? 'Jaribu kubadilisha kichujio au utafutaji.'
          : 'Ukinunua bidhaa na kulipa, order yako itaonekana hapa.'}
      </p>
      <button
        onClick={onShop}
        className="mt-4 inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-4 py-2 bg-brand-600 text-white rounded-lg text-sm font-semibold hover:bg-brand-700"
      >
        <Package size={16} />
        Nenda Bidhaa
      </button>
    </div>
  )
}


import { useState } from 'react'
import { useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom'
import {
  FileText,
  Send,
  Plus,
  Minus,
  Trash2,
  ArrowLeft,
  Smartphone,
  BadgeCheck,
  CheckCircle2,
  ShoppingBag,
} from 'lucide-react'
import { useCart } from '../../context/CartContext'
import { useOrders } from '../../context/OrderContext'
import PaymentModal from '../../components/PaymentModal'
import { iconForKundi } from '../../utils/icons'
import type { Malipo, OrderItem } from '../../types'

export default function MtejaQuotation() {
  const navigate = useNavigate()
  const { detailed, total, count, inc, dec, remove, clear } = useCart()
  const { createOrder, orders } = useOrders()

  const [payOpen, setPayOpen] = useState(false)
  const [malipo, setMalipo] = useState<Malipo[]>([])

  const lines = detailed
  const jumlaIliyolipwa = malipo.reduce((s, m) => s + m.kiasi, 0)
  const baki = total - jumlaIliyolipwa

  const handlePaymentSuccess = (m: Malipo) => {
    const malipoYote = [...malipo, m]
    setMalipo(malipoYote)

    const jumlaMpya = malipoYote.reduce((s, x) => s + x.kiasi, 0)

    if (jumlaMpya >= total && lines.length > 0) {
      const items: OrderItem[] = lines.map((l) => ({
        id: l.id,
        jina: l.jina,
        sku: l.sku,
        uom: l.uom,
        bei: l.bei,
        wingi: l.wingi,
        jumla: l.jumla,
      }))

      const order = createOrder(items, total, malipoYote)

      clear()
      setMalipo([])

      setTimeout(() => {
        navigate(`/mteja/order/${order.id}`)
      }, 400)
    }
  }

  if (lines.length === 0) {
    const orderZaMwisho = orders.slice(0, 3)
    return (
      <div className="space-y-5 max-w-4xl">
        <div>
          <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold">Quotation Yangu</h1>
          <p className="text-slate-500 text-sm">Valid kwa siku 7</p>
        </div>

        <div className="bg-white rounded-xl border border-slate-200 p-10 text-center">
          <FileText size={40} className="mx-auto mb-3 text-slate-300" />
          <div className="font-semibold">Hakuna bidhaa kwenye cart</div>
          <p className="text-sm text-slate-500 mt-1">
            {orderZaMwisho.length > 0
              ? 'Umekamilisha malipo. Unaweza kuona order zako hapa chini.'
              : 'Ongeza bidhaa kwanza kwenye ukurasa wa Bidhaa.'}
          </p>
          <button
            onClick={() => navigate('/mteja/bidhaa')}
