            className="mt-4 inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-4 py-2 bg-brand-600 text-white rounded-lg text-sm font-semibold hover:bg-brand-700"
          >
            <ArrowLeft size={16} />
            Nenda Bidhaa
          </button>
        </div>

        {orderZaMwisho.length > 0 && (
          <div className="bg-white rounded-xl border border-slate-200 p-4 md:p-6">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-3">
              <ShoppingBag size={18} className="text-emerald-600" />
              <h2 className="font-semibold">Order Zangu za Karibuni</h2>
            </div>
            <div className="space-y-2">
              {orderZaMwisho.map((o) => (
                <button
                  key={o.id}
                  onClick={() => navigate(`/mteja/order/${o.id}`)}
                  className="w-full flex items-center justify-between gap-3 p-3 rounded-lg border border-slate-200 hover:border-brand-500 hover:bg-brand-50/40 text-left"
                >
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-3 min-w-0">
                    <div className="w-10 h-10 rounded-lg bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-700 grid place-items-center shrink-0">
                      <CheckCircle2 size={18} />
                    </div>
                    <div className="min-w-0">
                      <div className="text-sm font-semibold truncate">
                        Order {o.id}
                      </div>
                      <div className="text-xs text-slate-500">
                        {o.tarehe} - Bidhaa {o.bidhaa.length}
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                  <div className="text-sm font-bold text-brand-700 whitespace-nowrap">
                    Tsh {o.jumla.toLocaleString()}
                  </div>
                </button>
              ))}
            </div>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
    )
  }

  return (
    <div className="space-y-5 max-w-4xl">
      <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-end sm:justify-between gap-3">
        <div>
          <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold">Quotation Yangu</h1>
          <p className="text-slate-500 text-sm">Valid kwa siku 7</p>
        </div>
        <button
          onClick={clear}
          className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-2 rounded-lg bg-red-50 text-red-600 text-sm hover:bg-red-100 self-start sm:self-auto"
        >
          <Trash2 size={14} />
          Futa Cart
        </button>
      </div>

      <div className="bg-white rounded-xl border border-slate-200 p-4 md:p-6">
        <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:justify-between sm:items-start gap-3 border-b pb-4 mb-4">
          <div>
            <div className="font-bold">SMART HARDWARE</div>
            <div className="text-xs text-slate-500">
              TIN: 123-456-789 - VRN: 4001234
            </div>
          </div>
          <div className="text-left sm:text-right text-sm">
            <div className="font-semibold">QUOTATION #Q-2026-0114</div>
            <div className="text-xs text-slate-500">Tarehe: 09 Nov 2026</div>
            <div className="text-xs text-amber-600">Inaisha: 16 Nov 2026</div>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="overflow-x-auto -mx-4 md:mx-0">
          <table className="w-full text-sm min-w-[560px]">
            <thead className="text-left text-slate-500 border-b">
              <tr>
                <th className="py-2 pl-4 md:pl-0">Bidhaa</th>
                <th className="py-2 text-right">Bei</th>
                <th className="py-2 text-center">Wingi</th>
                <th className="py-2 text-right">Jumla</th>
                <th className="py-2 pr-4 md:pr-0"></th>
              </tr>
            </thead>
            <tbody>
              {lines.map((l) => {
                const Icon = iconForKundi(l.iconKey)
                return (
                  <tr key={l.id} className="border-b border-slate-100">
                    <td className="py-3 pl-4 md:pl-0">
                      <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                        <div className="w-9 h-9 rounded-lg bg-slate-100 text-slate-600 grid place-items-center shrink-0">
                          <Icon size={16} />
                        </div>
                        <div className="min-w-0">
                          <div className="font-medium truncate">{l.jina}</div>
                          <div className="text-xs text-slate-500">{l.sku}</div>
                        </div>
                      </div>
                    </td>
                    <td className="py-3 text-right whitespace-nowrap">
                      Tsh {l.bei.toLocaleString()}
                    </td>
                    <td className="py-3">
                      <div className="flex items-center justify-center gap-1">
                        <button
                          onClick={() => dec(l.id)}
                          className="w-6 h-6 rounded-md bg-slate-100 hover:bg-slate-200 grid place-items-center"
                          aria-label="Punguza"
                        >
                          <Minus size={12} />
                        </button>
                        <span className="w-8 text-center font-semibold">{l.wingi}</span>
                        <button
                          onClick={() => inc(l.id)}
                          className="w-6 h-6 rounded-md bg-slate-100 hover:bg-slate-200 grid place-items-center"
                          aria-label="Ongeza"
                        >
                          <Plus size={12} />
                        </button>
                      </div>
                      <div className="text-[10px] text-slate-400 text-center mt-0.5">
                        {l.uom}
                      </div>
                    </td>
                    <td className="py-3 text-right font-semibold whitespace-nowrap">
                      Tsh {l.jumla.toLocaleString()}
                    </td>
                    <td className="py-3 pr-4 md:pr-0 text-right">
                      <button
                        onClick={() => remove(l.id)}
                        className="p-1.5 rounded-md text-red-500 hover:bg-red-50"
                        aria-label="Ondoa"
                      >
                        <Trash2 size={14} />
                      </button>
                    </td>
                  </tr>
                )
              })}
            </tbody>
            <tfoot>
              <tr>
                <td colSpan={3} className="py-3 text-right font-semibold">
                  JUMLA ({count} bidhaa)
                </td>
                <td className="py-3 text-right font-bold text-brand-700 text-lg whitespace-nowrap">
                  Tsh {total.toLocaleString()}
                </td>
                <td></td>
              </tr>
            </tfoot>
          </table>
        </div>

        {malipo.length > 0 && (
          <div className="mt-5 border-t border-slate-200 pt-4">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-2">
              <BadgeCheck size={16} className="text-emerald-600" />
              <div className="font-semibold text-sm">Malipo Yaliyothibitishwa</div>
            </div>
            <div className="space-y-2">
              {malipo.map((m) => (
                <div
                  key={m.id}
                  className="flex items-center gap-3 p-3 rounded-lg bg-emerald-50 border border-emerald-100"
                >
                  <Smartphone size={16} className="text-emerald-700 shrink-0" />
                  <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
                    <div className="text-sm font-medium">
                      {m.simu} - {m.njia.toUpperCase()}
                    </div>
                    <div className="text-xs text-slate-600 font-mono">
                      Kumbukumbu: {m.kumbukumbu}
                    </div>
                    <div className="text-[10px] text-slate-500">{m.tarehe}</div>
                  </div>
                  <div className="text-sm font-bold text-emerald-700 whitespace-nowrap">
                    Tsh {m.kiasi.toLocaleString()}
                  </div>
                </div>
              ))}
            </div>

            <div className="mt-3 p-3 rounded-lg bg-slate-50 text-sm space-y-1">
              <div className="flex justify-between">
                <span className="text-slate-600">Jumla</span>
                <span className="font-semibold">Tsh {total.toLocaleString()}</span>
              </div>
              <div className="flex justify-between">
                <span className="text-slate-600">Iliyolipwa</span>
                <span className="font-semibold text-emerald-700">
                  Tsh {jumlaIliyolipwa.toLocaleString()}
                </span>
              </div>
              <div className="flex justify-between border-t border-slate-200 pt-1">
                <span className="text-slate-700 font-medium">Baki</span>
                <span className="font-bold text-red-600">
                  Tsh {Math.max(baki, 0).toLocaleString()}
                </span>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        )}

        <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row gap-2 mt-6">
          <button
            onClick={() => setPayOpen(true)}
            disabled={baki <= 0}
            className="inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 px-4 py-2 bg-emerald-600 text-white rounded-lg text-sm font-semibold hover:bg-emerald-700 disabled:opacity-50 disabled:cursor-not-allowed"
          >
            <Smartphone size={16} />
            Lipa kwa Simu
          </button>
          <button className="inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 px-4 py-2 bg-slate-100 rounded-lg text-sm hover:bg-slate-200">
            <Send size={16} />
            Tuma WhatsApp
          </button>
          <button
            onClick={() => navigate('/mteja/bidhaa')}
            className="inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 px-4 py-2 bg-slate-100 rounded-lg text-sm hover:bg-slate-200 sm:ml-auto"
          >
            <ArrowLeft size={16} />
            Endelea Kununua
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>

      <PaymentModal
        open={payOpen}
        jumla={baki}
        onClose={() => setPayOpen(false)}
        onSuccess={handlePaymentSuccess}
      />
    </div>
  )
}



import { useMemo } from 'react'
import {
  Wallet, TrendingUp, TrendingDown, Store, Users, HandCoins,
  Package, AlertTriangle, ArrowRight, ShoppingBag, Receipt,
  BarChart3, ChevronRight, Building2,
} from 'lucide-react'
import { useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom'
import { useDuka } from '../../context/DukaContext'
import { useMauzo } from '../../context/MauzoContext'
import { useOrderAdmin } from '../../context/OrderAdminContext'
import { useMaombi } from '../../context/OmbiContext'
import { hesabuFaidaKamili, formatTsh } from '../../utils/faida'

export default function MmilikiDashboard() {
  const navigate = useNavigate()
  const { maduka } = useDuka()
  const { mauzo, opex } = useMauzo()
  const { orders } = useOrderAdmin()
  const { maombi } = useMaombi()

  // ============================================================
  // Hesabu za jumla
  // ============================================================
  const faida = useMemo(
    () => hesabuFaidaKamili(mauzo, opex),
    [mauzo, opex]
  )

  const stats = useMemo(() => {
    const watejaWote = new Set(orders.map((o) => o.mteja)).size
    const ordersZinazosubiri = orders.filter(
      (o) => o.hali === 'inasubiri_malipo'
    ).length
    const maombiYanasubiri = maombi.filter((m) => m.hali === 'inasubiri').length
    const jumlaDeni = orders
      .filter((o) => o.hali === 'inasubiri_malipo')
      .reduce((s, o) => s + o.jumla, 0)
    return {
      watejaWote,
      ordersZinazosubiri,
      maombiYanasubiri,
      jumlaDeni,
    }
  }, [orders, maombi])

  // Faida kwa kila duka
  const faidaKwaDuka = useMemo(() => {
    return maduka.map((d) => {
      const f = hesabuFaidaKamili(mauzo, opex, d.id)
      return { duka: d, ...f }
    })
  }, [maduka, mauzo, opex])

  // Orders 5 za karibuni
  const ordersZaKaribuni = useMemo(
    () => orders.slice(0, 5),
    [orders]
  )

  // Maombi 3 yanayosubiri
  const maombiYaKaribuni = useMemo(
    () => maombi.filter((m) => m.hali === 'inasubiri').slice(0, 3),
    [maombi]
  )

  return (
    <div className="space-y-5">
      {/* ============================================================ */}
      {/* HEADER */}
      {/* ============================================================ */}
      <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-end sm:justify-between gap-3">
        <div>
          <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold">Dashboard ya Mmiliki</h1>
          <p className="text-slate-500 text-sm">
            Muhtasari wa maduka yote - mauzo, faida, zaka, na orders
          </p>
        </div>
        <div className="text-xs text-slate-500">
          Leo, {new Date().toLocaleDateString('sw-TZ', {
            day: '2-digit',
            month: 'long',
            year: 'numeric',
          })}
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* ============================================================ */}
      {/* STATS 4 - cards nyeupe bila rangi */}
      {/* ============================================================ */}
      <div className="grid grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4 gap-3 md:gap-4">
        <DashboardStat
          label="Mauzo Yote"
          value={formatTsh(faida.mauzo)}
          sub={`Maduka ${maduka.length}`}
          Icon={ShoppingBag}
          trend="up"
        />
        <DashboardStat
          label="Faida Safi"
          value={formatTsh(faida.faidaSafi)}
          sub={
            faida.hasara
              ? 'Biashara inapata hasara'
              : 'Baada ya gharama zote'
          }
          Icon={faida.hasara ? TrendingDown : TrendingUp}
          trend={faida.hasara ? 'down' : 'up'}
        />
        <DashboardStat
          label="Deni la Kukusanya"
          value={formatTsh(stats.jumlaDeni)}
          sub={`Orders ${stats.ordersZinazosubiri}`}
          Icon={Wallet}
          trend="down"
        />
        <DashboardStat
          label="Maombi Yanayosubiri"
          value={String(stats.maombiYanasubiri)}
          sub={`Wateja ${stats.watejaWote}`}
          Icon={AlertTriangle}
          trend="neutral"
        />
      </div>

      {/* ============================================================ */}
      {/* ZAKA NA SADAKA - hero card */}
      {/* ============================================================ */}
      <div className="bg-white rounded-xl border border-slate-200 p-5 md:p-6">
        <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center sm:justify-between gap-4">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-4">
            <div className="w-12 h-12 rounded-xl bg-brand-50 text-brand-700 grid place-items-center shrink-0">
              <HandCoins size={24} />
            </div>
            <div>
              <div className="text-sm text-slate-500">Kwa Bwana Wiki Hii</div>
              <div className="text-2xl font-bold text-slate-900">
                {formatTsh(faida.jumlaYaMungu)}
              </div>
              <div className="text-xs text-slate-500 mt-0.5">
                Zaka {formatTsh(faida.zaka)} + Sadaka {formatTsh(faida.sadaka)}
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
          <button
            onClick={() => navigate('/mmiliki/zaka')}
            className="inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 px-4 py-2.5 bg-brand-600 text-white rounded-lg text-sm font-semibold hover:bg-brand-700 self-start sm:self-auto"
          >
            <HandCoins size={16} />
            Nenda Zaka
            <ChevronRight size={14} />
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* ============================================================ */}
      {/* FAIDA KWA DUKA + WADENI WAKUU */}
      {/* ============================================================ */}
      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-2 gap-4">
        {/* Faida kwa Duka */}
        <div className="bg-white rounded-xl border border-slate-200 p-4 md:p-5">
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-4">
            <h2 className="font-semibold flex items-center gap-2">
              <Building2 size={18} className="text-brand-600" />
              Faida kwa Duka
            </h2>
            <button
              onClick={() => navigate('/mmiliki/mauzo')}
              className="text-xs text-brand-600 hover:underline inline-flex items-center gap-1"
            >
              Ona Zote
              <ChevronRight size={12} />
            </button>
          </div>
          <div className="space-y-3">
            {faidaKwaDuka.map((f) => {
              const maxMauzo = Math.max(...faidaKwaDuka.map((x) => x.mauzo), 1)
              const percentage = (f.mauzo / maxMauzo) * 100
              return (
                <div key={f.duka.id}>
                  <div className="flex justify-between items-center text-sm mb-1">
                    <span className="font-medium text-slate-700 truncate">
                      {f.duka.jina.replace('Smart Hardware - ', '')}
                    </span>
                    <span className="font-semibold text-slate-900 whitespace-nowrap ml-2">
                      {formatTsh(f.mauzo)}
                    </span>
                  </div>
                  <div className="w-full bg-slate-100 rounded-full h-2">
                    <div
                      className="bg-brand-600 h-2 rounded-full transition-all"
                      style={{ width: `${percentage}%` }}
                    />
                  </div>
                  <div className="flex justify-between text-xs text-slate-500 mt-1">
                    <span>Faida: {formatTsh(f.faidaSafi)}</span>
                    {f.hasara ? (
                      <span className="text-red-600 font-medium">Hasara</span>
                    ) : (
                      <span className="text-emerald-600 font-medium">Faida</span>
                    )}
                  </div>
                </div>
              )
            })}
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Maombi Yanayosubiri */}
        <div className="bg-white rounded-xl border border-slate-200 p-4 md:p-5">
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-4">
            <h2 className="font-semibold flex items-center gap-2">
              <Receipt size={18} className="text-brand-600" />
              Maombi Yanayosubiri
            </h2>
            <button
              onClick={() => navigate('/mmiliki/matumizi')}
              className="text-xs text-brand-600 hover:underline inline-flex items-center gap-1"
            >
              Ona Zote
              <ChevronRight size={12} />
            </button>
          </div>
          {maombiYaKaribuni.length === 0 ? (
            <div className="text-sm text-slate-500 text-center py-6">
              Hakuna maombi yanayosubiri
            </div>
          ) : (
            <div className="space-y-2">
              {maombiYaKaribuni.map((m) => (
                <button
                  key={m.id}
                  onClick={() => navigate('/mmiliki/matumizi')}
                  className="w-full text-left flex items-center gap-3 p-2.5 rounded-lg hover:bg-slate-50 transition border border-slate-100"
                >
                  <div className="w-9 h-9 rounded-lg bg-amber-50 text-amber-700 grid place-items-center shrink-0">
                    <Receipt size={16} />
                  </div>
                  <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
                    <div className="text-sm font-medium truncate">
                      {m.maelezo}
                    </div>
                    <div className="text-xs text-slate-500">
                      {m.aliyeomba} - {m.duka}
                    </div>
                  </div>
                  <div className="text-sm font-bold text-slate-900 whitespace-nowrap">
                    {formatTsh(m.kiasi)}
                  </div>
                </button>
              ))}
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* ============================================================ */}
      {/* ORDERS ZA KARIBUNI */}
      {/* ============================================================ */}
      <div className="bg-white rounded-xl border border-slate-200 p-4 md:p-5">
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-4">
          <h2 className="font-semibold flex items-center gap-2">
            <ShoppingBag size={18} className="text-brand-600" />
            Orders za Karibuni
          </h2>
          <button
            onClick={() => navigate('/mmiliki/orders')}
            className="text-xs text-brand-600 hover:underline inline-flex items-center gap-1"
          >
            Ona Zote
            <ChevronRight size={12} />
          </button>
        </div>
        {ordersZaKaribuni.length === 0 ? (
          <div className="text-sm text-slate-500 text-center py-6">
            Hakuna orders bado
          </div>
        ) : (
          <div className="divide-y divide-slate-100">
            {ordersZaKaribuni.map((o) => (
              <button
                key={o.id}
                onClick={() => navigate('/mmiliki/orders')}
                className="w-full text-left py-3 flex items-center gap-3 hover:bg-slate-50 -mx-2 px-2 rounded-lg transition"
              >
                <div className="w-9 h-9 rounded-lg bg-brand-50 text-brand-700 grid place-items-center shrink-0">
                  <ShoppingBag size={16} />
                </div>
                <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
                  <div className="text-sm font-medium truncate">
                    {o.id} - {o.mteja}
                  </div>
                  <div className="text-xs text-slate-500 truncate">
                    {o.dukaJina.replace('Smart Hardware - ', '')} - {o.tarehe}{' '}
                    {o.muda}
                  </div>
                </div>
                <div className="text-right shrink-0">
                  <div className="text-sm font-bold text-slate-900">
                    {formatTsh(o.jumla)}
                  </div>
                  <div
                    className={`text-[10px] uppercase font-semibold ${
                      o.hali === 'imekamilika'
                        ? 'text-emerald-600'
                        : o.hali === 'inasubiri_malipo'
                        ? 'text-amber-600'
                        : o.hali === 'imesafirishwa'
                        ? 'text-brand-600'
                        : 'text-red-600'
                    }`}
                  >
                    {o.hali.replace('_', ' ')}
                  </div>
                </div>
              </button>
            ))}
          </div>
        )}
      </div>

      {/* ============================================================ */}
      {/* SHORTCUTS */}
      {/* ============================================================ */}
      <div className="grid grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4 gap-3">
        <ShortcutCard
          label="Maduka"
          desc={`${maduka.length} maduka`}
          Icon={Store}
          onClick={() => navigate('/mmiliki/maduka')}
        />
        <ShortcutCard
          label="Wafanyakazi"
          desc="Simamia wafanyakazi"
          Icon={Users}
          onClick={() => navigate('/mmiliki/wafanyakazi')}
        />
        <ShortcutCard
          label="Wateja"
          desc="Wateja wote"
          Icon={Users}
          onClick={() => navigate('/mmiliki/wateja')}
        />
        <ShortcutCard
          label="Mauzo na Faida"
          desc="Ripoti kamili"
          Icon={BarChart3}
          onClick={() => navigate('/mmiliki/mauzo')}
        />
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}

// ============================================================
// Dashboard Stat Card (nyeupe, bila rangi ya background)
// ============================================================
function DashboardStat({
  label,
  value,
  sub,
  Icon,
  trend = 'neutral',
}: {
  label: string
  value: string
  sub?: string
  Icon: React.ElementType
  trend?: 'up' | 'down' | 'neutral'
}) {
  const trendColor =
    trend === 'up'
      ? 'text-emerald-600'
      : trend === 'down'
      ? 'text-red-600'
      : 'text-slate-500'

  const trendIcon =
    trend === 'up'
      ? TrendingUp
      : trend === 'down'
      ? TrendingDown
      : null

  const TrendIcon = trendIcon

  return (
    <div className="bg-white rounded-xl border border-slate-200 p-4 md:p-5">
      <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-2">
        <div className="w-10 h-10 rounded-lg bg-slate-50 text-slate-700 grid place-items-center shrink-0">
          <Icon size={20} />
        </div>
        {TrendIcon && (
          <div className={`${trendColor} opacity-80`}>
            <TrendIcon size={16} />
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
      <div className="text-xs text-slate-500 mt-3">{label}</div>
      <div className="text-lg md:text-xl font-bold text-slate-900 mt-0.5 truncate">
        {value}
      </div>
      {sub && (
        <div className="text-xs text-slate-500 mt-0.5 truncate">{sub}</div>
      )}
    </div>
  )
}

// ============================================================
// Shortcut Card
// ============================================================
function ShortcutCard({
  label,
  desc,
  Icon,
  onClick,
}: {
  label: string
  desc: string
  Icon: React.ElementType
  onClick: () => void
}) {
  return (
    <button
      onClick={onClick}
      className="bg-white rounded-xl border border-slate-200 p-4 text-left hover:border-brand-500 hover:shadow-sm transition"
    >
      <div className="w-9 h-9 rounded-lg bg-brand-50 text-brand-700 grid place-items-center">
        <Icon size={18} />
      </div>
      <div className="font-semibold text-sm mt-3">{label}</div>
      <div className="text-xs text-slate-500 mt-0.5">{desc}</div>
      <div className="mt-2 flex items-center text-xs text-brand-600 font-medium">
        Fungua
        <ArrowRight size={12} className="ml-1" />
      </div>
    </button>
  )
}

import { faidaVipindi } from '../../data/demo'
import { LineChart, Line, XAxis, YAxis, Tooltip, ResponsiveContainer, CartesianGrid } from 'recharts'

export default function MmilikiFaida() {
  const jumla = faidaVipindi.reduce(
    (a, f) => ({
      mauzo: a.mauzo + f.mauzo,
      manunuzi: a.manunuzi + f.manunuzi,
      gharama: a.gharama + f.gharama,
      faida: a.faida + f.faidaHalisi,
    }),
    { mauzo: 0, manunuzi: 0, gharama: 0, faida: 0 }
  )

  return (
    <div className="space-y-5">
      <div>
        <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold">Faida / Hasara</h1>
        <p className="text-slate-500 text-sm">Wiki ya 01 - 07 Nov 2026</p>
      </div>
      <div className="grid md:grid-cols-4 gap-4">
        <div className="bg-white rounded-xl border p-5"><div className="text-xs text-slate-500">Mauzo</div><div className="text-xl font-bold">Tsh {jumla.mauzo.toLocaleString()}</div></div>
        <div className="bg-white rounded-xl border p-5"><div className="text-xs text-slate-500">Manunuzi</div><div className="text-xl font-bold">Tsh {jumla.manunuzi.toLocaleString()}</div></div>
        <div className="bg-white rounded-xl border p-5"><div className="text-xs text-slate-500">Gharama</div><div className="text-xl font-bold">Tsh {jumla.gharama.toLocaleString()}</div></div>
        <div className="bg-emerald-50 rounded-xl border border-emerald-200 p-5"><div className="text-xs text-emerald-700">Faida Halisi</div><div className="text-xl font-bold text-emerald-700">Tsh {jumla.faida.toLocaleString()}</div></div>
      </div>
      <div className="bg-white rounded-xl border p-5">
        <h2 className="font-semibold mb-4">Chati ya Faida (Siku 7)</h2>
        <ResponsiveContainer width="100%" height={300}>
          <LineChart data={faidaVipindi}>
            <CartesianGrid strokeDasharray="3 3" stroke="#e2e8f0" />
            <XAxis dataKey="tarehe" tick={{ fontSize: 11 }} />
            <YAxis tick={{ fontSize: 11 }} tickFormatter={(v) => `${v / 1000}k`} />
            <Tooltip formatter={(v: number) => `Tsh ${v.toLocaleString()}`} />
            <Line type="monotone" dataKey="faidaHalisi" stroke="#2563eb" strokeWidth={2} name="Faida" />
            <Line type="monotone" dataKey="mauzo" stroke="#10b981" strokeWidth={2} name="Mauzo" />
          </LineChart>
        </ResponsiveContainer>
      </div>
      <div className="bg-white rounded-xl border overflow-hidden">
        <table className="w-full text-sm">
          <thead className="bg-slate-50 text-left text-slate-500">
            <tr>
              <th className="p-3">Tarehe</th>
              <th className="p-3 text-right">Mauzo</th>
              <th className="p-3 text-right">Manunuzi</th>
              <th className="p-3 text-right">Gharama</th>
              <th className="p-3 text-right">Faida Halisi</th>
            </tr>
          </thead>
          <tbody className="divide-y divide-slate-100">
            {faidaVipindi.map((f) => (
              <tr key={f.tarehe}>
                <td className="p-3">{f.tarehe}</td>
                <td className="p-3 text-right">{f.mauzo.toLocaleString()}</td>
                <td className="p-3 text-right">{f.manunuzi.toLocaleString()}</td>
                <td className="p-3 text-right">{f.gharama.toLocaleString()}</td>
                <td className="p-3 text-right font-semibold text-emerald-600">{f.faidaHalisi.toLocaleString()}</td>
              </tr>
            ))}
          </tbody>
        </table>
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}

import { useMemo, useState } from 'react'
import {
  Store, Plus, Search, MapPin, Phone, User, Users,
  TrendingUp, Wallet, Package, CheckCircle2, AlertTriangle,
  XCircle, X, Save, Edit, Pause, Play, Ban,
} from 'lucide-react'
import { useDuka } from '../../context/DukaContext'
import type { DukaKamili, HaliDuka, MtumiajiKamili } from '../../types'

const HALI_META: Record<
  HaliDuka,
  { label: string; color: string; Icon: React.ElementType }
> = {
  hai: { label: 'Hai', color: 'bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-700 border-emerald-200', Icon: CheckCircle2 },
  imesimamishwa: { label: 'Imesimamishwa', color: 'bg-amber-50 text-amber-700 border-amber-200', Icon: Pause },
  imefungwa: { label: 'Imefungwa', color: 'bg-red-50 text-red-700 border-red-200', Icon: Ban },
}

export default function MmilikiMaduka() {
  const { maduka, wafanyakazi } = useDuka()
  const [q, setQ] = useState('')
  const [funguaOpen, setFunguaOpen] = useState(false)
  const [selected, setSelected] = useState<DukaKamili | null>(null)

  const filtered = useMemo(() => {
    const query = q.toLowerCase().trim()
    if (!query) return maduka
    return maduka.filter(
      (d) =>
        d.jina.toLowerCase().includes(query) ||
        d.mahali.toLowerCase().includes(query) ||
        (d.msimamiziJina?.toLowerCase().includes(query) ?? false)
    )
  }, [maduka, q])

  const stats = useMemo(() => {
    const hai = maduka.filter((d) => d.hali === 'hai').length
    const mauzoLeo = maduka.reduce((s, d) => s + d.mauzoLeo, 0)
    const faidaLeo = maduka.reduce((s, d) => s + d.faidaLeo, 0)
    return { zote: maduka.length, hai, mauzoLeo, faidaLeo }
  }, [maduka])

  return (
    <div className="space-y-5">
      <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-end sm:justify-between gap-3">
        <div>
          <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold">Maduka Yangu</h1>
          <p className="text-slate-500 text-sm">Fungua, simamia, na panga wasimamizi</p>
        </div>
        <button
          onClick={() => setFunguaOpen(true)}
          className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-4 py-2 bg-brand-600 text-white rounded-lg text-sm font-semibold hover:bg-brand-700 self-start sm:self-auto"
        >
          <Plus size={16} />
          Fungua Duka
        </button>
      </div>

      <div className="grid grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4 gap-3">
        <StatCard label="Maduka Yote" value={String(stats.zote)} Icon={Store} tone="brand" />
        <StatCard label="Maduka Hai" value={String(stats.hai)} Icon={CheckCircle2} tone="emerald" />
        <StatCard label="Mauzo Leo" value={`Tsh ${stats.mauzoLeo.toLocaleString()}`} Icon={TrendingUp} tone="brand" />
        <StatCard label="Faida Leo" value={`Tsh ${stats.faidaLeo.toLocaleString()}`} Icon={Wallet} tone="emerald" />
      </div>

      <div className="relative">
        <Search size={18} className="absolute left-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-slate-400 pointer-events-none" />
        <input
          value={q}
          onChange={(e) => setQ(e.target.value)}
          placeholder="Tafuta duka kwa jina, mahali, au msimamizi..."
          className="w-full pl-10 pr-4 py-2.5 rounded-lg border border-slate-200 focus:border-brand-500 outline-none text-sm"
        />
      </div>

      {filtered.length === 0 ? (
        <div className="bg-white rounded-xl border border-slate-200 p-10 text-center">
          <Store size={40} className="mx-auto mb-3 text-slate-300" />
          <div className="font-semibold">Hakuna duka</div>
          <p className="text-sm text-slate-500 mt-1">
            Bofya "Fungua Duka" kuanza biashara mpya.
          </p>
        </div>
      ) : (
        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 xl:grid-cols-3 gap-3 md:gap-4">
          {filtered.map((d) => {
            const meta = HALI_META[d.hali]
            return (
              <div key={d.id} className="bg-white rounded-xl border border-slate-200 p-4 md:p-5">
                <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-2">
                  <div className="w-11 h-11 rounded-lg bg-brand-50 text-brand-600 grid place-items-center shrink-0">
                    <Store size={20} />
                  </div>
                  <span className={`text-[10px] uppercase font-semibold px-2 py-0.5 rounded-md border ${meta.color}`}>
                    {meta.label}
                  </span>
                </div>

                <div className="mt-3">
                  <div className="font-semibold">{d.jina}</div>
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-1 text-xs text-slate-500 mt-1">
                    <MapPin size={12} /> {d.mahali}
                  </div>
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-1 text-xs text-slate-500 mt-0.5">
                    <Phone size={12} /> {d.simu}
                  </div>
                </div>

                <div className="mt-3 p-2 rounded-lg bg-slate-50">
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
                    <User size={12} className="text-slate-400" />
                    <span className="text-xs text-slate-600">
                      Msimamizi:{' '}
                      <span className="font-semibold text-slate-800">
                        {d.msimamiziJina || 'Hakuna'}
                      </span>
                    </span>
                  </div>
                </div>

                <div className="grid grid-cols-3 gap-2 mt-3 text-xs">
                  <div>
                    <div className="text-slate-500">Mauzo</div>
                    <div className="font-bold text-brand-700 truncate">
                      {d.mauzoLeo.toLocaleString()}
                    </div>
                  </div>
                  <div>
                    <div className="text-slate-500">Faida</div>
                    <div className="font-bold text-emerald-700 truncate">
                      {d.faidaLeo.toLocaleString()}
                    </div>
                  </div>
                  <div>
                    <div className="text-slate-500">Stoki</div>
                    <div className="font-bold truncate">{d.stokiJumla}</div>
                  </div>
                </div>

                <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 mt-2 text-xs text-slate-500">
                  <Users size={12} /> {d.wafanyakazi} wafanyakazi
                  <span className="mx-1">-</span>
                  <Package size={12} /> {d.stokiJumla} bidhaa
                </div>

                <button
                  onClick={() => setSelected(d)}
                  className="mt-3 w-full inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-2 rounded-lg bg-slate-100 hover:bg-slate-200 text-sm font-medium"
                >
                  <Edit size={14} />
                  Simamia
                </button>
              </div>
            )
          })}
        </div>
      )}

      {funguaOpen && <FunguaDukaForm onClose={() => setFunguaOpen(false)} />}
      {selected && <DukaDetails duka={selected} onClose={() => setSelected(null)} />}
    </div>
  )
}

// ---------- Fomu ya Kufungua Duka ----------
function FunguaDukaForm({ onClose }: { onClose: () => void }) {
  const { funguaDuka, wafanyakazi } = useDuka()
  const [jina, setJina] = useState('')
  const [mahali, setMahali] = useState('')
  const [simu, setSimu] = useState('')
  const [msimamiziId, setMsimamiziId] = useState<string>('')
  const [error, setError] = useState('')

  const wagombea = wafanyakazi.filter((w) => w.active)

  const handleSubmit = (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault()
    if (!jina.trim()) return setError('Jina la duka linahitajika')
    if (!mahali.trim()) return setError('Mahali panahitajika')
    if (!/^0[67]\d{2}-?\d{3}-?\d{3}$/.test(simu.replace(/\s/g, '')))
      return setError('Namba ya simu si sahihi')

    setError('')
    funguaDuka({
      jina,
      mahali,
      simu,
      msimamiziId: msimamiziId || undefined,
    })
    onClose()
  }

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-end sm:items-center justify-center p-0 sm:p-4">
      <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-black/40" onClick={onClose} />
      <div className="relative bg-white w-full sm:max-w-lg rounded-t-2xl sm:rounded-2xl shadow-2xl max-h-[92vh] overflow-y-auto">
        <div className="sticky top-0 bg-white border-b border-slate-200 px-4 py-3 flex justify-between items-center">
          <div>
            <div className="font-semibold">Fungua Duka Jipya</div>
            <div className="text-xs text-slate-500">Weka taarifa za duka na msimamizi</div>
          </div>
          <button onClick={onClose} className="p-1.5 rounded-lg hover:bg-slate-100">
            <X size={18} />
          </button>
        </div>

        <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="p-4 space-y-3">
          <div>
            <label className="text-sm font-medium">Jina la Duka</label>
            <input
              value={jina}
              onChange={(e) => setJina(e.target.value)}
              placeholder="Mfano: Smart Hardware - Mbezi"
              className="mt-1 w-full px-3 py-2.5 rounded-lg border border-slate-200 focus:border-brand-500 outline-none text-sm"
            />
          </div>
          <div>
            <label className="text-sm font-medium">Mahali</label>
            <input
              value={mahali}
              onChange={(e) => setMahali(e.target.value)}
              placeholder="Mfano: Mbezi, Dar"
              className="mt-1 w-full px-3 py-2.5 rounded-lg border border-slate-200 focus:border-brand-500 outline-none text-sm"
            />
          </div>
          <div>
            <label className="text-sm font-medium">Namba ya Simu ya Duka</label>
            <input
              value={simu}
              onChange={(e) => setSimu(e.target.value)}
              placeholder="0754-123-456"
              inputMode="tel"
              className="mt-1 w-full px-3 py-2.5 rounded-lg border border-slate-200 focus:border-brand-500 outline-none text-sm"
            />
          </div>
          <div>
            <label className="text-sm font-medium">Msimamizi wa Duka</label>
            <select
              value={msimamiziId}
              onChange={(e) => setMsimamiziId(e.target.value)}
              className="mt-1 w-full px-3 py-2.5 rounded-lg border border-slate-200 focus:border-brand-500 outline-none text-sm"
            >
              <option value="">-- Chagua msimamizi (hiari) --</option>
              {wagombea.map((w) => (
                <option key={w.id} value={w.id}>
                  {w.jina} - {w.role} {w.dukaJina ? `(${w.dukaJina})` : '(hana duka)'}
                </option>
              ))}
            </select>
            <div className="text-xs text-slate-500 mt-1">
              Unaweza kubadilisha msimamizi baadaye.
            </div>
          </div>

          {error && (
            <div className="flex items-start gap-2 p-3 rounded-lg bg-red-50 text-red-700 text-sm">
              <AlertTriangle size={16} className="mt-0.5 shrink-0" />
              {error}
            </div>
          )}

          <div className="flex gap-2 pt-2">
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={onClose}
              className="px-4 py-2.5 rounded-lg bg-slate-100 text-sm hover:bg-slate-200"
            >
              Ghairi
            </button>
            <button
              type="submit"
              className="flex-1 inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 px-4 py-2.5 rounded-lg bg-brand-600 text-white text-sm font-semibold hover:bg-brand-700"
            >
              <Save size={16} />
              Fungua Duka
            </button>
          </div>
        </form>
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}

// ---------- Maelezo ya Duka ----------
function DukaDetails({ duka, onClose }: { duka: DukaKamili; onClose: () => void }) {
  const { wafanyakaziWaDuka, wekaMsimamizi, wafanyakazi, badilishaHali } = useDuka()
  const [msimamiziId, setMsimamiziId] = useState(duka.msimamiziId || '')
  const [hali, setHali] = useState<HaliDuka>(duka.hali)

  const wafanyakaziWake = wafanyakaziWaDuka(duka.id)
  const wagombea = wafanyakazi.filter((w) => w.active)

  const handleSave = () => {
    if (msimamiziId) wekaMsimamizi(duka.id, msimamiziId)
    if (hali !== duka.hali) badilishaHali(duka.id, hali)
    onClose()
  }

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-end sm:items-center justify-center p-0 sm:p-4">
      <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-black/40" onClick={onClose} />
      <div className="relative bg-white w-full sm:max-w-lg rounded-t-2xl sm:rounded-2xl shadow-2xl max-h-[92vh] overflow-y-auto">
        <div className="sticky top-0 bg-white border-b border-slate-200 px-4 py-3 flex justify-between items-center">
          <div>
            <div className="font-semibold">{duka.jina}</div>
            <div className="text-xs text-slate-500">{duka.mahali}</div>
          </div>
          <button onClick={onClose} className="p-1.5 rounded-lg hover:bg-slate-100">
            <X size={18} />
          </button>
        </div>

        <div className="p-4 space-y-4">
          {/* Msimamizi */}
          <div>
            <label className="text-sm font-medium">Msimamizi wa Duka</label>
            <select
              value={msimamiziId}
              onChange={(e) => setMsimamiziId(e.target.value)}
              className="mt-1 w-full px-3 py-2.5 rounded-lg border border-slate-200 focus:border-brand-500 outline-none text-sm"
            >
              <option value="">-- Hakuna msimamizi --</option>
              {wagombea.map((w) => (
                <option key={w.id} value={w.id}>
                  {w.jina} - {w.role} {w.dukaJina ? `(${w.dukaJina})` : '(hana duka)'}
                </option>
              ))}
            </select>
          </div>

          {/* Hali ya duka */}
          <div>
            <label className="text-sm font-medium">Hali ya Duka</label>
            <div className="grid grid-cols-3 gap-2 mt-1">
              {(['hai', 'imesimamishwa', 'imefungwa'] as HaliDuka[]).map((h) => {
                const meta = HALI_META[h]
                return (
                  <button
                    key={h}
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => setHali(h)}
                    className={`px-2 py-2 rounded-lg border text-xs font-medium flex items-center justify-center gap-1 ${
                      hali === h
                        ? `${meta.color}`
                        : 'border-slate-200 text-slate-600 hover:bg-slate-50'
                    }`}
                  >
                    <meta.Icon size={12} />
                    {meta.label}
                  </button>
                )
              })}
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Wafanyakazi wa duka */}
          <div>
            <div className="text-sm font-medium mb-2">
              Wafanyakazi wa Duka ({wafanyakaziWake.length})
            </div>
            {wafanyakaziWake.length === 0 ? (
              <div className="text-xs text-slate-500 p-3 rounded-lg bg-slate-50">
                Hakuna wafanyakazi bado. Weka msimamizi kwanza.
              </div>
            ) : (
              <div className="space-y-1.5">
                {wafanyakaziWake.map((w) => (
                  <div
                    key={w.id}
                    className="flex items-center gap-2 p-2 rounded-lg bg-slate-50 text-sm"
                  >
                    <div className="w-7 h-7 rounded-full bg-brand-600 text-white grid place-items-center text-[10px] font-bold">
                      {w.jina.split(' ').map((n) => n[0]).slice(0, 2).join('')}
                    </div>
                    <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
                      <div className="font-medium truncate">{w.jina}</div>
                      <div className="text-xs text-slate-500 capitalize">
                        {w.role} - {w.simu}
                      </div>
                    </div>
                    {duka.msimamiziId === w.id && (
                      <span className="text-[10px] uppercase font-semibold text-emerald-700 bg-emerald-50 px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded">
                        Msimamizi
                      </span>
                    )}
                  </div>
                ))}
              </div>
            )}
          </div>

          <button
            onClick={handleSave}
            className="w-full inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 px-4 py-2.5 rounded-lg bg-brand-600 text-white text-sm font-semibold hover:bg-brand-700"
          >
            <Save size={16} />
            Hifadhi Mabadiliko
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}

// ---------- Stat Card ----------
type Tone = 'brand' | 'emerald' | 'amber' | 'red'
const TONE: Record<Tone, string> = {
  brand: 'bg-brand-50 text-brand-700',
  emerald: 'bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-700',
  amber: 'bg-amber-50 text-amber-700',
  red: 'bg-red-50 text-red-700',
}

function StatCard({
  label,
  value,
  Icon,
  tone,
}: {
  label: string
  value: string
  Icon: React.ElementType
  tone: Tone
}) {
  return (
    <div className="bg-white rounded-xl border border-slate-200 p-4">
      <div className={`inline-flex w-9 h-9 rounded-lg items-center justify-center ${TONE[tone]}`}>
        <Icon size={16} />
      </div>
      <div className="text-xs text-slate-500 mt-2">{label}</div>
      <div className="text-base md:text-lg font-bold mt-0.5 truncate">{value}</div>
    </div>
  )
}

import { useMemo, useState } from 'react'
import {
  Wallet, Search, Filter, Store, User, Phone, Clock,
  CheckCircle2, XCircle, Trash2, Plus, X, Save,
  AlertTriangle, Eye, FileText, Upload, TrendingUp,
  Pencil, MoreVertical, Receipt,
} from 'lucide-react'
import { useMaombi } from '../../context/OmbiContext'
import { useDuka } from '../../context/DukaContext'
import { AINA_GHARAMA } from '../../data/demo'
import type {
  AinaGharama,
  HaliOmbi,
  OmbiMatumizi,
} from '../../types'

const HALI_META: Record<
  HaliOmbi,
  { label: string; color: string; Icon: React.ElementType }
> = {
  inasubiri: { label: 'Inasubiri', color: 'bg-amber-50 text-amber-700 border-amber-200', Icon: Clock },
  imekubaliwa: { label: 'Imekubaliwa', color: 'bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-700 border-emerald-200', Icon: CheckCircle2 },
  imekataliwa: { label: 'Imekataliwa', color: 'bg-red-50 text-red-700 border-red-200', Icon: XCircle },
}

const HALI_FILTERS: { key: HaliOmbi | 'zote'; label: string }[] = [
  { key: 'zote', label: 'Zote' },
  { key: 'inasubiri', label: 'Zinasubiri' },
  { key: 'imekubaliwa', label: 'Zilizokubaliwa' },
  { key: 'imekataliwa', label: 'Zilizokataliwa' },
]

export default function MmilikiMatumizi() {
  const { maombi, idhinisha, kataa, futa, ongezaOmbi } = useMaombi()
  const { maduka, wafanyakazi } = useDuka()

  const [q, setQ] = useState('')
  const [filterHali, setFilterHali] = useState<HaliOmbi | 'zote'>('zote')
  const [filterDuka, setFilterDuka] = useState<string>('zote')
  const [selected, setSelected] = useState<OmbiMatumizi | null>(null)
  const [formOpen, setFormOpen] = useState(false)

  const filtered = useMemo(() => {
    const query = q.toLowerCase().trim()
    return maombi.filter((m) => {
      if (filterHali !== 'zote' && m.hali !== filterHali) return false
      if (filterDuka !== 'zote' && m.duka !== filterDuka) return false
      if (!query) return true
      return (
        m.id.toLowerCase().includes(query) ||
        m.maelezo.toLowerCase().includes(query) ||
        m.sababu.toLowerCase().includes(query) ||
        m.aliyeomba.toLowerCase().includes(query) ||
        m.simu.includes(query)
      )
    })
  }, [maombi, q, filterHali, filterDuka])

  const stats = useMemo(() => {
    const zote = maombi.length
    const inasubiri = maombi.filter((m) => m.hali === 'inasubiri').length
    const zilizokubaliwa = maombi.filter((m) => m.hali === 'imekubaliwa')
    const jumlaIlikubaliwa = zilizokubaliwa.reduce((s, m) => s + m.kiasi, 0)
    const jumlaInasubiri = maombi
      .filter((m) => m.hali === 'inasubiri')
      .reduce((s, m) => s + m.kiasi, 0)
    return { zote, inasubiri, zilizokubaliwa: zilizokubaliwa.length, jumlaIlikubaliwa, jumlaInasubiri }
  }, [maombi])

  // Maduka ya kipekee kutoka kwenye maombi
  const madukaYaMaombi = useMemo(() => {
    const set = new Set(maombi.map((m) => m.duka))
    return Array.from(set)
  }, [maombi])

  return (
    <div className="space-y-5">
      {/* Header */}
      <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-end sm:justify-between gap-3">
        <div>
          <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold flex items-center gap-2">
            <Wallet size={24} className="text-brand-600" />
            Maombi ya Matumizi - Maduka Yote
          </h1>
          <p className="text-slate-500 text-sm">
            Idhinisha, kataa, au ongeza maombi ya wafanyakazi
          </p>
        </div>
        <button
          onClick={() => setFormOpen(true)}
          className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-4 py-2 bg-brand-600 text-white rounded-lg text-sm font-semibold hover:bg-brand-700 self-start sm:self-auto"
        >
          <Plus size={16} />
          Ongeza Ombi
        </button>
      </div>

      {/* Stats */}
      <div className="grid grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4 gap-3">
        <StatCard label="Maombi Yote" value={String(stats.zote)} Icon={Receipt} tone="brand" />
        <StatCard
          label="Zinasubiri"
          value={String(stats.inasubiri)}
          Icon={Clock}
          tone="amber"
          sub={`Tsh ${stats.jumlaInasubiri.toLocaleString()}`}
        />
        <StatCard
          label="Zilizokubaliwa"
          value={String(stats.zilizokubaliwa)}
          Icon={CheckCircle2}
          tone="emerald"
        />
        <StatCard
          label="Jumla Iliyokubaliwa"
          value={`Tsh ${stats.jumlaIlikubaliwa.toLocaleString()}`}
          Icon={TrendingUp}
          tone="emerald"
        />
      </div>

      {/* Filters */}
      <div className="bg-white rounded-xl border border-slate-200 p-3 md:p-4 space-y-3">
        <div className="relative">
          <Search
            size={18}
            className="absolute left-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-slate-400 pointer-events-none"
          />
          <input
            value={q}
            onChange={(e) => setQ(e.target.value)}
            placeholder="Tafuta kwa ID, maelezo, mfanyakazi, au simu..."
            className="w-full pl-10 pr-4 py-2.5 rounded-lg border border-slate-200 focus:border-brand-500 outline-none text-sm"
          />
        </div>
        <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2 items-center">
          <Filter size={14} className="text-slate-400" />
          <select
            value={filterDuka}
            onChange={(e) => setFilterDuka(e.target.value)}
            className="px-2 py-1.5 rounded-lg border border-slate-200 text-sm"
          >
            <option value="zote">Maduka Yote</option>
            {madukaYaMaombi.map((d) => (
              <option key={d} value={d}>{d}</option>
            ))}
          </select>
          <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-1.5">
            {HALI_FILTERS.map((f) => (
              <button
                key={f.key}
                onClick={() => setFilterHali(f.key)}
                className={`px-2.5 py-1.5 rounded-lg text-xs border transition ${
                  filterHali === f.key
                    ? 'bg-brand-600 text-white border-brand-600'
                    : 'bg-white border-slate-200 hover:bg-slate-50'
                }`}
              >
                {f.label}
              </button>
            ))}
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* List */}
      {filtered.length === 0 ? (
        <div className="bg-white rounded-xl border border-slate-200 p-10 text-center">
          <Wallet size={40} className="mx-auto mb-3 text-slate-300" />
          <div className="font-semibold">Hakuna maombi</div>
          <p className="text-sm text-slate-500 mt-1">
            Badilisha kichujio au ongeza ombi jipya.
          </p>
        </div>
      ) : (
        <div className="space-y-3">
          {filtered.map((m) => {
            const meta = HALI_META[m.hali]
            const ainaLabel =
              AINA_GHARAMA.find((a) => a.key === m.aina)?.label ?? m.aina
            return (
              <div
                key={m.id}
                className="bg-white rounded-xl border border-slate-200 p-4"
              >
                <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:justify-between gap-3">
                  <button
                    onClick={() => setSelected(m)}
                    className="flex items-center gap-3 min-w-0 flex-1 text-left"
                  >
                    <div className={`w-10 h-10 rounded-lg grid place-items-center shrink-0 ${meta.color}`}>
                      <meta.Icon size={18} />
                    </div>
                    <div className="min-w-0">
                      <div className="flex items-center gap-2 flex-wrap">
                        <span className="font-semibold text-sm">{m.id}</span>
                        <span className={`text-[10px] uppercase font-semibold px-2 py-0.5 rounded-md border ${meta.color}`}>
                          {meta.label}
                        </span>
                        <span className="text-[10px] uppercase font-semibold px-2 py-0.5 rounded-md bg-slate-100 text-slate-600">
                          {ainaLabel}
                        </span>
                      </div>
                      <div className="text-xs text-slate-500 mt-0.5 truncate">
                        {m.maelezo}
                      </div>
                      <div className="text-xs text-slate-500 mt-0.5 truncate">
                        {m.aliyeomba} - {m.duka} - {m.tarehe}
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  </button>
                  <div className="sm:text-right shrink-0">
                    <div className="text-base font-bold text-brand-700">
                      Tsh {m.kiasi.toLocaleString()}
                    </div>
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-1 mt-1 sm:justify-end">
                      {m.hali === 'inasubiri' && (
                        <>
                          <button
                            onClick={() => idhinisha(m.id, 'Imekubaliwa')}
                            className="px-2 py-1 rounded-md bg-emerald-600 text-white text-[10px] font-semibold hover:bg-emerald-700 inline-flex items-center gap-1"
                          >
                            <CheckCircle2 size={10} />
                            Kubali
                          </button>
                          <button
                            onClick={() => setSelected(m)}
                            className="px-2 py-1 rounded-md bg-red-600 text-white text-[10px] font-semibold hover:bg-red-700 inline-flex items-center gap-1"
                          >
                            <XCircle size={10} />
                            Kataa
                          </button>
                        </>
                      )}
                      <button
                        onClick={() => setSelected(m)}
                        className="p-1.5 rounded-md hover:bg-slate-100"
                        title="Ona"
                      >
                        <Eye size={14} className="text-slate-500" />
                      </button>
                      <button
                        onClick={() => futa(m.id)}
                        className="p-1.5 rounded-md hover:bg-red-50"
                        title="Futa"
                      >
                        <Trash2 size={14} className="text-red-500" />
                      </button>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                </div>
              </div>
            )
          })}
        </div>
      )}

      {selected && (
        <OmbiDetailsModal
          ombi={selected}
          onClose={() => setSelected(null)}
          onIdhinisha={(maoni) => {
            idhinisha(selected.id, maoni)
            setSelected(null)
          }}
          onKataa={(maoni) => {
            kataa(selected.id, maoni)
            setSelected(null)
          }}
          onFuta={() => {
            futa(selected.id)
            setSelected(null)
          }}
        />
      )}

      {formOpen && (
        <OngezaOmbiForm
          onClose={() => setFormOpen(false)}
          onSubmit={(data) => {
            ongezaOmbi(data)
            setFormOpen(false)
          }}
          wafanyakazi={wafanyakazi}
        />
      )}
    </div>
  )
}

// ============================================================
// Ombi Details Modal
// ============================================================
function OmbiDetailsModal({
  ombi,
  onClose,
  onIdhinisha,
  onKataa,
  onFuta,
}: {
  ombi: OmbiMatumizi
  onClose: () => void
  onIdhinisha: (maoni?: string) => void
  onKataa: (maoni: string) => void
  onFuta: () => void
}) {
  const meta = HALI_META[ombi.hali]
  const ainaLabel =
    AINA_GHARAMA.find((a) => a.key === ombi.aina)?.label ?? ombi.aina
  const [mode, setMode] = useState<'view' | 'kubali' | 'kataa' | 'futa'>('view')
  const [maoni, setMaoni] = useState('')
  const [error, setError] = useState('')

  const handleKubali = () => {
    onIdhinisha(maoni.trim() || undefined)
  }

  const handleKataa = () => {
    if (!maoni.trim()) {
      setError('Sababu ya kukataa inahitajika')
      return
    }
    onKataa(maoni.trim())
  }

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-end sm:items-center justify-center p-0 sm:p-4">
      <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-black/40" onClick={onClose} />
      <div className="relative bg-white w-full sm:max-w-lg rounded-t-2xl sm:rounded-2xl shadow-2xl max-h-[92vh] overflow-y-auto">
        <div className="sticky top-0 bg-white border-b border-slate-200 px-4 py-3 flex justify-between items-center">
          <div>
            <div className="font-semibold">{ombi.id}</div>
            <div className="text-xs text-slate-500">{ombi.tarehe}</div>
          </div>
          <button onClick={onClose} className="p-1.5 rounded-lg hover:bg-slate-100">
            <X size={18} />
          </button>
        </div>

        <div className="p-4 space-y-3 text-sm">
          <div className={`inline-flex items-center gap-1 px-2 py-1 rounded-md border ${meta.color}`}>
            <meta.Icon size={12} />
            {meta.label}
          </div>

          <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
            <div>
              <div className="text-xs text-slate-500">Aina</div>
              <div className="font-medium capitalize">{ainaLabel}</div>
            </div>
            <div>
              <div className="text-xs text-slate-500">Kiasi</div>
              <div className="font-bold text-brand-700">
                Tsh {ombi.kiasi.toLocaleString()}
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div>
            <div className="text-xs text-slate-500">Maelezo</div>
            <div>{ombi.maelezo}</div>
          </div>
          <div>
            <div className="text-xs text-slate-500">Sababu</div>
            <div>{ombi.sababu}</div>
          </div>
          <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs text-slate-600">
            <Phone size={12} /> {ombi.simu}
          </div>
          <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs text-slate-600">
            <User size={12} /> {ombi.aliyeomba} - {ombi.duka}
          </div>

          {ombi.maoni && (
            <div className="p-3 rounded-lg bg-slate-50">
              <div className="text-xs text-slate-500 mb-1">Maoni ya Mmiliki</div>
              <div className="text-sm">{ombi.maoni}</div>
              {ombi.aliyeidhinisha && (
                <div className="text-xs text-slate-500 mt-1">
                  - {ombi.aliyeidhinisha}, {ombi.tareheIdhini}
                </div>
              )}
            </div>
          )}

          {/* Mode selection */}
          {ombi.hali === 'inasubiri' && mode === 'view' && (
            <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2 pt-2">
              <button
                onClick={() => setMode('kubali')}
                className="inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-2.5 rounded-lg bg-emerald-600 text-white text-sm font-semibold hover:bg-emerald-700"
              >
                <CheckCircle2 size={14} />
                Kubali
              </button>
              <button
                onClick={() => setMode('kataa')}
                className="inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-2.5 rounded-lg bg-red-600 text-white text-sm font-semibold hover:bg-red-700"
              >
                <XCircle size={14} />
                Kataa
              </button>
            </div>
          )}

          {/* Kubali mode */}
          {mode === 'kubali' && (
            <div className="space-y-2 pt-2">
              <label className="text-sm font-medium">Maoni (hiari)</label>
              <textarea
                value={maoni}
                onChange={(e) => setMaoni(e.target.value)}
                rows={2}
                placeholder="Mfano: Sawa, endelea"
                className="w-full px-3 py-2 rounded-lg border border-slate-200 focus:border-brand-500 outline-none text-sm resize-none"
              />
              <div className="flex gap-2">
                <button
                  onClick={() => { setMode('view'); setMaoni('') }}
                  className="px-3 py-2 rounded-lg bg-slate-100 text-sm hover:bg-slate-200"
                >
                  Ghairi
                </button>
                <button
                  onClick={handleKubali}
                  className="flex-1 px-3 py-2 rounded-lg bg-emerald-600 text-white text-sm font-semibold hover:bg-emerald-700"
                >
                  Thibitisha Kukubali
                </button>
              </div>
            </div>
          )}

          {/* Kataa mode */}
          {mode === 'kataa' && (
            <div className="space-y-2 pt-2">
              <label className="text-sm font-medium">
                Sababu ya Kukataa <span className="text-red-500">*</span>
              </label>
              <textarea
                value={maoni}
                onChange={(e) => { setMaoni(e.target.value); setError('') }}
                rows={2}
                placeholder="Eleza kwa nini unakataa ombi hili..."
                className="w-full px-3 py-2 rounded-lg border border-slate-200 focus:border-brand-500 outline-none text-sm resize-none"
              />
              {error && (
                <div className="flex items-start gap-2 p-2 rounded-lg bg-red-50 text-red-700 text-xs">
                  <AlertTriangle size={12} className="mt-0.5 shrink-0" />
                  {error}
                </div>
              )}
              <div className="flex gap-2">
                <button
                  onClick={() => { setMode('view'); setMaoni(''); setError('') }}
                  className="px-3 py-2 rounded-lg bg-slate-100 text-sm hover:bg-slate-200"
                >
                  Ghairi
                </button>
                <button
                  onClick={handleKataa}
                  className="flex-1 px-3 py-2 rounded-lg bg-red-600 text-white text-sm font-semibold hover:bg-red-700"
                >
                  Thibitisha Kukataa
                </button>
              </div>
            </div>
          )}

          {/* Futa mode */}
          {mode === 'futa' && (
            <div className="space-y-2 pt-2">
              <div className="flex items-start gap-2 p-3 rounded-lg bg-red-50 text-red-700 text-sm">
                <AlertTriangle size={16} className="mt-0.5 shrink-0" />
                Una uhakika unataka kufuta ombi hili? Haiwezi kurudishwa.
              </div>
              <div className="flex gap-2">
                <button
                  onClick={() => setMode('view')}
                  className="px-3 py-2 rounded-lg bg-slate-100 text-sm hover:bg-slate-200"
                >
                  Ghairi
                </button>
                <button
                  onClick={onFuta}
                  className="flex-1 px-3 py-2 rounded-lg bg-red-600 text-white text-sm font-semibold hover:bg-red-700"
                >
                  Ndiyo, Futa
                </button>
              </div>
            </div>
          )}

          {/* Bottom actions */}
          {mode === 'view' && (
            <div className="flex gap-2 pt-2 border-t border-slate-200">
              <button
                onClick={() => setMode('futa')}
                className="inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-2.5 rounded-lg bg-red-50 text-red-600 text-sm font-semibold hover:bg-red-100"
              >
                <Trash2 size={14} />
                Futa
              </button>
              <button
                onClick={onClose}
                className="px-3 py-2.5 rounded-lg bg-slate-100 text-sm hover:bg-slate-200 sm:ml-auto"
              >
                Funga
              </button>
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}

// ============================================================
// Ongeza Ombi Form
// ============================================================
function OngezaOmbiForm({
  onClose,
  onSubmit,
  wafanyakazi,
}: {
  onClose: () => void
  onSubmit: (data: {
    aina: string
    kiasi: number
    maelezo: string
    simu: string
    sababu: string
    risitiUrl?: string
    aliyeomba: string
    duka: string
  }) => void
  wafanyakazi: { jina: string; dukaJina?: string; simu: string }[]
}) {
  const [aina, setAina] = useState<AinaGharama>('mafuta')
  const [kiasi, setKiasi] = useState(0)
  const [maelezo, setMaelezo] = useState('')
  const [simu, setSimu] = useState('')
  const [sababu, setSababu] = useState('')
  const [risitiUrl, setRisitiUrl] = useState('')
  const [aliyeomba, setAliyeomba] = useState(wafanyakazi[0]?.jina || '')
  const [duka, setDuka] = useState(wafanyakazi[0]?.dukaJina || '')
  const [error, setError] = useState('')

  const handleMfanyakaziChange = (jina: string) => {
    setAliyeomba(jina)
    const w = wafanyakazi.find((x) => x.jina === jina)
    if (w?.dukaJina) setDuka(w.dukaJina)
    if (w?.simu) setSimu(w.simu)
  }

  const handleSubmit = (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault()
    if (!maelezo.trim()) return setError('Maelezo yanahitajika')
    if (kiasi <= 0) return setError('Kiasi lazima kiwe zaidi ya sifuri')
    if (!/^0[67]\d{2}-?\d{3}-?\d{3}$/.test(simu.replace(/\s/g, '')))
      return setError('Namba ya simu si sahihi')
    if (!sababu.trim()) return setError('Sababu inahitajika')
    if (!aliyeomba.trim()) return setError('Chagua mfanyakazi')

    onSubmit({
      aina,
      kiasi,
      maelezo,
      simu,
      sababu,
      risitiUrl: risitiUrl || undefined,
      aliyeomba,
      duka,
    })
  }

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-end sm:items-center justify-center p-0 sm:p-4">
      <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-black/40" onClick={onClose} />
      <div className="relative bg-white w-full sm:max-w-lg rounded-t-2xl sm:rounded-2xl shadow-2xl max-h-[92vh] overflow-y-auto">
        <div className="sticky top-0 bg-white border-b border-slate-200 px-4 py-3 flex justify-between items-center">
          <div>
            <div className="font-semibold">Ongeza Ombi Jipya</div>
            <div className="text-xs text-slate-500">Kwa niaba ya mfanyakazi</div>
          </div>
          <button onClick={onClose} className="p-1.5 rounded-lg hover:bg-slate-100">
            <X size={18} />
          </button>
        </div>
        <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="p-4 space-y-3">
          <div>
            <label className="text-sm font-medium">Mfanyakazi</label>
            <select
              value={aliyeomba}
              onChange={(e) => handleMfanyakaziChange(e.target.value)}
              className="mt-1 w-full px-3 py-2.5 rounded-lg border border-slate-200 focus:border-brand-500 outline-none text-sm"
            >
              {wafanyakazi.map((w) => (
                <option key={w.jina} value={w.jina}>
                  {w.jina} {w.dukaJina ? `(${w.dukaJina})` : ''}
                </option>
              ))}
            </select>
          </div>
          <div>
            <label className="text-sm font-medium">Duka</label>
            <input
              value={duka}
              onChange={(e) => setDuka(e.target.value)}
              className="mt-1 w-full px-3 py-2.5 rounded-lg border border-slate-200 focus:border-brand-500 outline-none text-sm"
            />
          </div>
          <div>
            <label className="text-sm font-medium">Aina ya Gharama</label>
            <select
              value={aina}
              onChange={(e) => setAina(e.target.value as AinaGharama)}
              className="mt-1 w-full px-3 py-2.5 rounded-lg border border-slate-200 focus:border-brand-500 outline-none text-sm"
            >
              {AINA_GHARAMA.map((a) => (
                <option key={a.key} value={a.key}>
                  {a.label}
                </option>
              ))}
            </select>
          </div>
          <div>
            <label className="text-sm font-medium">Kiasi (Tsh)</label>
            <input
              type="number"
              value={kiasi || ''}
              onChange={(e) => setKiasi(Number(e.target.value))}
              placeholder="80000"
              className="mt-1 w-full px-3 py-2.5 rounded-lg border border-slate-200 focus:border-brand-500 outline-none text-sm"
            />
          </div>
          <div>
            <label className="text-sm font-medium">Maelezo</label>
            <input
              value={maelezo}
              onChange={(e) => setMaelezo(e.target.value)}
              placeholder="Mfano: Mafuta ya pikipiki"
              className="mt-1 w-full px-3 py-2.5 rounded-lg border border-slate-200 focus:border-brand-500 outline-none text-sm"
            />
          </div>
          <div>
            <label className="text-sm font-medium">Namba ya Simu ya Mlipaji</label>
            <input
              value={simu}
              onChange={(e) => setSimu(e.target.value)}
              placeholder="0754-123-456"
              inputMode="tel"
              className="mt-1 w-full px-3 py-2.5 rounded-lg border border-slate-200 focus:border-brand-500 outline-none text-sm"
            />
          </div>
          <div>
            <label className="text-sm font-medium">Sababu</label>
            <textarea
              value={sababu}
              onChange={(e) => setSababu(e.target.value)}
              rows={2}
              placeholder="Eleza kwa nini..."
              className="mt-1 w-full px-3 py-2.5 rounded-lg border border-slate-200 focus:border-brand-500 outline-none text-sm resize-none"
            />
          </div>
          <div>
            <label className="text-sm font-medium">Risiti (hiari)</label>
            <div className="mt-1 flex items-center gap-2">
              <input
                value={risitiUrl}
                onChange={(e) => setRisitiUrl(e.target.value)}
                placeholder="URL ya picha"
                className="flex-1 px-3 py-2.5 rounded-lg border border-slate-200 focus:border-brand-500 outline-none text-sm"
              />
              <button
                type="button"
                className="p-2.5 rounded-lg bg-slate-100 hover:bg-slate-200"
              >
                <Upload size={16} />
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>

          {error && (
            <div className="flex items-start gap-2 p-3 rounded-lg bg-red-50 text-red-700 text-sm">
              <AlertTriangle size={16} className="mt-0.5 shrink-0" />
              {error}
            </div>
          )}

          <div className="flex gap-2 pt-2">
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={onClose}
              className="px-4 py-2.5 rounded-lg bg-slate-100 text-sm hover:bg-slate-200"
            >
              Ghairi
            </button>
            <button
              type="submit"
              className="flex-1 inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 px-4 py-2.5 rounded-lg bg-brand-600 text-white text-sm font-semibold hover:bg-brand-700"
            >
              <Save size={16} />
              Hifadhi Ombi
            </button>
          </div>
        </form>
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}

// ============================================================
// Stat Card
// ============================================================
type Tone = 'brand' | 'emerald' | 'amber' | 'red'
const TONE: Record<Tone, string> = {
  brand: 'bg-brand-50 text-brand-700',
  emerald: 'bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-700',
  amber: 'bg-amber-50 text-amber-700',
  red: 'bg-red-50 text-red-700',
}

function StatCard({
  label,
  value,
  Icon,
  tone,
  sub,
}: {
  label: string
  value: string
  Icon: React.ElementType
  tone: Tone
  sub?: string
}) {
  return (
    <div className="bg-white rounded-xl border border-slate-200 p-4">
      <div className={`inline-flex w-9 h-9 rounded-lg items-center justify-center ${TONE[tone]}`}>
        <Icon size={16} />
      </div>
      <div className="text-xs text-slate-500 mt-2">{label}</div>
      <div className="text-base md:text-lg font-bold mt-0.5 truncate">{value}</div>
      {sub && <div className="text-[10px] text-slate-500 truncate">{sub}</div>}
    </div>
  )
}


import { useMemo, useState } from 'react'
import {
  TrendingUp, TrendingDown, Wallet, Receipt, HandCoins,
  Package, Store, PiggyBank, Filter, Download, Building2,
  Users, ChevronRight, X, BarChart3, Calendar,
  CheckCircle2,
} from 'lucide-react'
import {
  LineChart, Line, XAxis, YAxis, Tooltip, ResponsiveContainer,
  CartesianGrid, BarChart, Bar, Legend,
} from 'recharts'
import { useMauzo } from '../../context/MauzoContext'
import { useDuka } from '../../context/DukaContext'
import {
  hesabuFaidaKamili,
  gharamaKwaAina,
  formatTsh,
} from '../../utils/faida'

export default function MmilikiMauzo() {
  const { mauzo, opex } = useMauzo()
  const { maduka } = useDuka()
  const [selectedDuka, setSelectedDuka] = useState<string>('zote')

  const dukaId = selectedDuka === 'zote' ? undefined : selectedDuka

  const faidaJumla = useMemo(
    () => hesabuFaidaKamili(mauzo, opex, dukaId),
    [mauzo, opex, dukaId]
  )

  const gharamaJumla = useMemo(
    () => gharamaKwaAina(opex, dukaId),
    [opex, dukaId]
  )

  const faidaKwaDuka = useMemo(() => {
    return maduka.map((d) => {
      const f = hesabuFaidaKamili(mauzo, opex, d.id)
      return { duka: d, ...f }
    })
  }, [maduka, mauzo, opex])

  const chartData = faidaKwaDuka.map((f) => ({
    jina: f.duka.jina.replace('Smart Hardware - ', ''),
    mauzo: f.mauzo,
    cogs: f.cogs,
    faidaGhafi: f.faidaGhafi,
    opex: f.jumlaOpex,
    faidaSafi: f.faidaSafi,
  }))

  return (
    <div className="space-y-5">
      {/* Header */}
      <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-end sm:justify-between gap-3">
        <div>
          <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold">Mauzo na Faida - Maduka Yote</h1>
          <p className="text-slate-500 text-sm">
            Muhtasari wa biashara yote - faida ghafi, gharama, faida safi, zaka na sadaka
          </p>
        </div>
        <button className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-4 py-2 bg-slate-100 rounded-lg text-sm hover:bg-slate-200 self-start sm:self-auto">
          <Download size={16} />
          Pakua Ripoti
        </button>
      </div>

      {/* Chagua duka */}
      <div className="bg-white rounded-xl border border-slate-200 p-3 md:p-4">
        <label className="text-xs font-medium text-slate-700 flex items-center gap-1.5">
          <Filter size={14} />
          Chuja kwa Duka
        </label>
        <div className="mt-2 flex flex-wrap gap-2">
          <button
            onClick={() => setSelectedDuka('zote')}
            className={`inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-2 rounded-lg text-sm border ${
              selectedDuka === 'zote'
                ? 'bg-brand-600 text-white border-brand-600 font-semibold'
                : 'bg-white border-slate-200 hover:bg-slate-50'
            }`}
          >
            <Building2 size={14} />
            Maduka Yote
          </button>
          {maduka.map((d) => (
            <button
              key={d.id}
              onClick={() => setSelectedDuka(d.id)}
              className={`inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-2 rounded-lg text-sm border ${
                selectedDuka === d.id
                  ? 'bg-brand-600 text-white border-brand-600 font-semibold'
                  : 'bg-white border-slate-200 hover:bg-slate-50'
              }`}
            >
              <Store size={14} />
              {d.jina.replace('Smart Hardware - ', '')}
            </button>
          ))}
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* HERO CARD - Faida Safi ya Jumla (Nyeupe) */}
      <div className="bg-white rounded-xl border border-slate-200 p-5 md:p-6">
        <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-3">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-4">
            <div className="w-14 h-14 rounded-2xl bg-slate-50 text-slate-700 grid place-items-center shrink-0">
              {faidaJumla.hasara ? (
                <TrendingDown size={28} />
              ) : (
                <TrendingUp size={28} />
              )}
            </div>
            <div>
              <div className="text-sm text-slate-500">
                {selectedDuka === 'zote'
                  ? 'Faida Safi - Maduka Yote'
                  : 'Faida Safi - Duka Moja'}
              </div>
              <div className="text-3xl md:text-4xl font-bold text-slate-900 mt-1">
                {formatTsh(faidaJumla.faidaSafi)}
              </div>
