              <div className="text-xs text-slate-500 mt-1">
                {faidaJumla.hasara
                  ? 'Biashara inapata HASARA'
                  : 'Biashara inapata FAIDA'}
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-3 gap-3 mt-5 pt-5 border-t border-slate-200">
          <div>
            <div className="text-xs text-slate-500">Zaka (10%)</div>
            <div className="text-lg font-bold text-slate-900">
              {formatTsh(faidaJumla.zaka)}
            </div>
          </div>
          <div>
            <div className="text-xs text-slate-500">Sadaka (10%)</div>
            <div className="text-lg font-bold text-slate-900">
              {formatTsh(faidaJumla.sadaka)}
            </div>
          </div>
          <div>
            <div className="text-xs text-slate-500">Jumla ya Bwana (20%)</div>
            <div className="text-lg font-bold text-slate-900">
              {formatTsh(faidaJumla.jumlaYaMungu)}
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="mt-4 pt-4 border-t border-slate-200 flex items-center justify-between">
          <div>
            <div className="text-xs text-slate-500">Inayobaki Kwako</div>
            <div className="text-2xl font-bold text-slate-900">
              {formatTsh(faidaJumla.inayobaki)}
            </div>
          </div>
          <div className="w-12 h-12 rounded-xl bg-slate-50 text-slate-600 grid place-items-center">
            <HandCoins size={22} />
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Stats 4 za Jumla */}
      <div className="grid grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4 gap-3">
        <StatCard
          label="Mauzo Yote"
          value={formatTsh(faidaJumla.mauzo)}
          Icon={Receipt}
          accent="brand"
        />
        <StatCard
          label="Gharama ya Bidhaa (COGS)"
          value={formatTsh(faidaJumla.cogs)}
          Icon={Package}
          accent="amber"
        />
        <StatCard
          label="Faida Ghafi"
          value={formatTsh(faidaJumla.faidaGhafi)}
          Icon={TrendingUp}
          accent="emerald"
        />
        <StatCard
          label="Gharama za Uendeshaji"
          value={formatTsh(faidaJumla.jumlaOpex)}
          Icon={Wallet}
          accent="red"
        />
      </div>

      {/* Chati - Faida kwa kila Duka */}
      <div className="bg-white rounded-xl border border-slate-200 p-4 md:p-5">
        <h2 className="font-semibold mb-4 flex items-center gap-2">
          <BarChart3 size={18} className="text-brand-600" />
          Ulinganisho wa Maduka
        </h2>
        <div className="w-full h-72">
          <ResponsiveContainer width="100%" height="100%">
            <BarChart data={chartData}>
              <CartesianGrid strokeDasharray="3 3" stroke="#e2e8f0" />
              <XAxis dataKey="jina" tick={{ fontSize: 11 }} />
              <YAxis
                tick={{ fontSize: 11 }}
                tickFormatter={(v) => `${v / 1000000}M`}
              />
              <Tooltip formatter={(v) => formatTsh(Number(v ?? 0))} />
              <Legend wrapperStyle={{ fontSize: 11 }} />
              <Bar dataKey="mauzo" name="Mauzo" fill="#2563eb" radius={[4, 4, 0, 0]} />
              <Bar dataKey="faidaGhafi" name="Faida Ghafi" fill="#f59e0b" radius={[4, 4, 0, 0]} />
              <Bar dataKey="faidaSafi" name="Faida Safi" fill="#10b981" radius={[4, 4, 0, 0]} />
            </BarChart>
          </ResponsiveContainer>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Faida kwa Kila Duka (Card za kila duka) */}
      <div>
        <h2 className="font-semibold mb-3 flex items-center gap-2">
          <Store size={18} className="text-brand-600" />
          Faida kwa Kila Duka
        </h2>
        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 xl:grid-cols-3 gap-3 md:gap-4">
          {faidaKwaDuka.map((f) => (
            <div
              key={f.duka.id}
              className="bg-white rounded-xl border border-slate-200 p-4 md:p-5"
            >
              <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2 mb-3">
                <div className="flex items-center gap-2 min-w-0">
                  <div className="w-10 h-10 rounded-lg bg-slate-50 text-slate-700 grid place-items-center shrink-0">
                    <Store size={18} />
                  </div>
                  <div className="min-w-0">
                    <div className="font-semibold text-sm truncate">
                      {f.duka.jina.replace('Smart Hardware - ', '')}
                    </div>
                    <div className="text-xs text-slate-500 truncate">
                      {f.duka.mahali}
                    </div>
                  </div>
                </div>
                <div
                  className={`text-xs font-bold px-2 py-1 rounded-md shrink-0 ${
                    f.hasara
                      ? 'bg-red-50 text-red-700'
                      : 'bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-700'
                  }`}
                >
                  {f.hasara ? 'HASARA' : 'FAIDA'}
                </div>
              </div>

              <div className="space-y-1.5 text-sm">
                <div className="flex justify-between">
                  <span className="text-slate-600">Mauzo</span>
                  <span className="font-semibold">{formatTsh(f.mauzo)}</span>
                </div>
                <div className="flex justify-between">
                  <span className="text-slate-600">COGS</span>
                  <span className="font-semibold text-amber-700">
                    {formatTsh(f.cogs)}
                  </span>
                </div>
                <div className="flex justify-between">
                  <span className="text-slate-600">Faida Ghafi</span>
                  <span className="font-semibold text-brand-700">
                    {formatTsh(f.faidaGhafi)}
                  </span>
                </div>
                <div className="flex justify-between">
                  <span className="text-slate-600">Gharama</span>
                  <span className="font-semibold text-red-600">
                    {formatTsh(f.jumlaOpex)}
                  </span>
                </div>
              </div>

              <div className="mt-3 pt-3 border-t border-slate-100 space-y-1.5 text-sm">
                <div className="flex justify-between">
                  <span className="font-medium text-slate-700">Faida Safi</span>
                  <span
                    className={`font-bold ${
                      f.hasara ? 'text-red-700' : 'text-emerald-700'
                    }`}
                  >
                    {formatTsh(f.faidaSafi)}
                  </span>
                </div>
                <div className="flex justify-between">
                  <span className="text-slate-500">Zaka 10%</span>
                  <span className="font-semibold text-brand-600">
                    {formatTsh(f.zaka)}
                  </span>
                </div>
                <div className="flex justify-between">
                  <span className="text-slate-500">Sadaka 10%</span>
                  <span className="font-semibold text-brand-600">
                    {formatTsh(f.sadaka)}
                  </span>
                </div>
                <div className="flex justify-between pt-1 border-t border-slate-100">
                  <span className="text-slate-500">Inayobaki</span>
                  <span className="font-bold text-emerald-700">
                    {formatTsh(f.inayobaki)}
                  </span>
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Breakdown ya Gharama za Uendeshaji */}
      <div className="bg-white rounded-xl border border-slate-200 p-4 md:p-5">
        <h2 className="font-semibold mb-3 flex items-center gap-2">
          <PiggyBank size={18} className="text-brand-600" />
          Gharama za Uendeshaji -{' '}
          {selectedDuka === 'zote' ? 'Maduka Yote' : 'Duka Moja'}
        </h2>

        <div className="grid grid-cols-2 md:grid-cols-3 lg:grid-cols-4 gap-2 text-sm">
          <GharamaItem label="Mishahara" value={gharamaJumla.mishahara} />
          <GharamaItem label="Kodi" value={gharamaJumla.kodi} />
          <GharamaItem label="Umeme" value={gharamaJumla.umeme} />
          <GharamaItem label="Mafuta" value={gharamaJumla.mafuta} />
          <GharamaItem label="Usafiri" value={gharamaJumla.usafiri} />
          <GharamaItem label="Chakula" value={gharamaJumla.chakula} />
          <GharamaItem label="Matengenezo" value={gharamaJumla.matengenezo} />
          <GharamaItem label="Vifaa" value={gharamaJumla.vifaa} />
          <GharamaItem label="Bima" value={gharamaJumla.bima} />
          <GharamaItem label="Leseni" value={gharamaJumla.leseni} />
          <GharamaItem label="Maji" value={gharamaJumla.maji} />
          <GharamaItem label="Nyingine" value={gharamaJumla.nyingine} />
        </div>

        <div className="mt-3 p-3 rounded-lg bg-slate-50 flex justify-between">
          <span className="text-sm font-medium">Jumla ya Gharama</span>
          <span className="text-sm font-bold text-red-700">
            {formatTsh(gharamaJumla.jumla)}
          </span>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Ripoti ya Kila Duka - Table */}
      <div className="bg-white rounded-xl border border-slate-200 p-4 md:p-5">
        <h2 className="font-semibold mb-3 flex items-center gap-2">
          <Receipt size={18} className="text-brand-600" />
          Jedwali la Maduka Yote
        </h2>
        <div className="overflow-x-auto">
          <table className="w-full text-sm min-w-[720px]">
            <thead className="text-left text-slate-500 border-b">
              <tr>
                <th className="py-2">Duka</th>
                <th className="py-2 text-right">Mauzo</th>
                <th className="py-2 text-right">COGS</th>
                <th className="py-2 text-right">Faida Ghafi</th>
                <th className="py-2 text-right">Gharama</th>
                <th className="py-2 text-right">Faida Safi</th>
                <th className="py-2 text-right">Zaka</th>
                <th className="py-2 text-right">Sadaka</th>
              </tr>
            </thead>
            <tbody>
              {faidaKwaDuka.map((f) => (
                <tr key={f.duka.id} className="border-b border-slate-100">
                  <td className="py-2">
                    <div className="font-medium text-sm">
                      {f.duka.jina.replace('Smart Hardware - ', '')}
                    </div>
                    <div className="text-xs text-slate-500">{f.duka.mahali}</div>
                  </td>
                  <td className="py-2 text-right">{f.mauzo.toLocaleString()}</td>
                  <td className="py-2 text-right text-amber-700">
                    {f.cogs.toLocaleString()}
                  </td>
                  <td className="py-2 text-right font-semibold text-brand-700">
                    {f.faidaGhafi.toLocaleString()}
                  </td>
                  <td className="py-2 text-right text-red-600">
                    {f.jumlaOpex.toLocaleString()}
                  </td>
                  <td
                    className={`py-2 text-right font-bold ${
                      f.hasara ? 'text-red-700' : 'text-emerald-700'
                    }`}
                  >
                    {f.faidaSafi.toLocaleString()}
                  </td>
                  <td className="py-2 text-right text-brand-600">
                    {f.zaka.toLocaleString()}
                  </td>
                  <td className="py-2 text-right text-brand-600">
                    {f.sadaka.toLocaleString()}
                  </td>
                </tr>
              ))}
            </tbody>
            <tfoot>
              <tr className="border-t-2 border-slate-300">
                <td className="py-2 font-bold">JUMLA</td>
                <td className="py-2 text-right font-bold">
                  {faidaJumla.mauzo.toLocaleString()}
                </td>
                <td className="py-2 text-right font-bold text-amber-700">
                  {faidaJumla.cogs.toLocaleString()}
                </td>
                <td className="py-2 text-right font-bold text-brand-700">
                  {faidaJumla.faidaGhafi.toLocaleString()}
                </td>
                <td className="py-2 text-right font-bold text-red-600">
                  {faidaJumla.jumlaOpex.toLocaleString()}
                </td>
                <td
                  className={`py-2 text-right font-bold ${
                    faidaJumla.hasara ? 'text-red-700' : 'text-emerald-700'
                  }`}
                >
                  {faidaJumla.faidaSafi.toLocaleString()}
                </td>
                <td className="py-2 text-right font-bold text-brand-600">
                  {faidaJumla.zaka.toLocaleString()}
                </td>
                <td className="py-2 text-right font-bold text-brand-600">
                  {faidaJumla.sadaka.toLocaleString()}
                </td>
              </tr>
            </tfoot>
          </table>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Zaka na Sadaka - Muhtasari wa Wiki (Nyeupe) */}
      <div className="bg-white rounded-xl border border-slate-200 p-5 md:p-6">
        <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-3">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-4">
            <div className="w-12 h-12 rounded-xl bg-slate-50 text-slate-700 grid place-items-center shrink-0">
              <HandCoins size={24} />
            </div>
            <div>
              <div className="text-sm text-slate-500">Zaka na Sadaka - Jumla</div>
              <div className="text-2xl font-bold text-slate-900">
                {formatTsh(faidaJumla.jumlaYaMungu)}
              </div>
              <div className="text-xs text-slate-500 mt-0.5">
                Zaka {formatTsh(faidaJumla.zaka)} + Sadaka{' '}
                {formatTsh(faidaJumla.sadaka)}
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="mt-4 pt-4 border-t border-slate-200 flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between gap-3">
          <div>
            <div className="text-xs text-slate-500">
              Inayobaki Kwako (Baada ya Zaka & Sadaka)
            </div>
            <div className="text-xl font-bold text-slate-900">
              {formatTsh(faidaJumla.inayobaki)}
            </div>
          </div>
          <button className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-4 py-2.5 bg-brand-600 text-white rounded-lg text-sm font-semibold hover:bg-brand-700">
            <CheckCircle2 size={16} />
            Nimelipa
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}

// ============================================================
// Sub-components
// ============================================================
function GharamaItem({ label, value }: { label: string; value: number }) {
  return (
    <div className="p-2.5 rounded-lg bg-slate-50">
      <div className="text-xs text-slate-500">{label}</div>
      <div className="font-semibold text-sm truncate">{formatTsh(value)}</div>
    </div>
  )
}

// ============================================================
// Stat Card (nyeupe + accent border-l)
// ============================================================
type Accent = 'brand' | 'emerald' | 'amber' | 'red'

const ACCENT_BORDER: Record<Accent, string> = {
  brand: 'border-l-brand-500',
  emerald: 'border-l-emerald-500',
  amber: 'border-l-amber-500',
  red: 'border-l-red-500',
}

const ACCENT_TEXT: Record<Accent, string> = {
  brand: 'text-brand-700',
  emerald: 'text-emerald-700',
  amber: 'text-amber-700',
  red: 'text-red-700',
}

function StatCard({
  label,
  value,
  Icon,
  accent,
}: {
  label: string
  value: string
  Icon: React.ElementType
  accent: Accent
}) {
  return (
    <div
      className={`bg-white rounded-xl border border-slate-200 border-l-4 ${ACCENT_BORDER[accent]} p-4`}
    >
      <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
        <Icon size={16} className={ACCENT_TEXT[accent]} />
        <div className="text-xs text-slate-500">{label}</div>
      </div>
      <div className="text-base md:text-lg font-bold mt-2 truncate text-slate-900">
        {value}
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}


import { useMemo, useState } from 'react'
import {
  Wallet, Search, Filter, User, Plus, X, Save, Trash2,
  CheckCircle2, Clock, XCircle, Eye, Download, TrendingUp,
  Banknote, Smartphone, Building2, CreditCard, Calendar,
  Users, ArrowRight, AlertTriangle, DollarSign, Store,
  Layers, CheckSquare, Square, Zap, Sparkles,
} from 'lucide-react'
import { useMshahara } from '../../context/MshaharaContext'
import { useDuka } from '../../context/DukaContext'
import { formatTsh } from '../../utils/faida'
import type {
  HaliMshahara,
  Mshahara,
  NjiaMalipoMshahara,
  BulkPaymentResult,
} from '../../types'

const HALI_META: Record<
  HaliMshahara,
  { label: string; color: string; Icon: React.ElementType }
> = {
  inasubiri: { label: 'Inasubiri', color: 'text-amber-700 border-amber-200', Icon: Clock },
  imelipwa: { label: 'Imelipwa', color: 'text-emerald-700 border-emerald-200', Icon: CheckCircle2 },
  imeshindwa: { label: 'Imeshindwa', color: 'text-red-700 border-red-200', Icon: XCircle },
  imekataliwa: { label: 'Imekataliwa', color: 'text-red-700 border-red-200', Icon: XCircle },
}

const NJIA_META: Record<
  NjiaMalipoMshahara,
  { color: string; Icon: React.ElementType; label: string }
> = {
  mpesa: { color: 'text-brand-700', Icon: Smartphone, label: 'M-Pesa' },
  tigopesa: { color: 'text-orange-700', Icon: Smartphone, label: 'Tigo Pesa' },
  airtel: { color: 'text-red-700', Icon: Smartphone, label: 'Airtel Money' },
  halopesa: { color: 'text-purple-700', Icon: Smartphone, label: 'HaloPesa' },
  crdb: { color: 'text-emerald-700', Icon: Building2, label: 'CRDB' },
  nmb: { color: 'text-brand-700', Icon: Building2, label: 'NMB' },
  nbc: { color: 'text-slate-700', Icon: Building2, label: 'NBC' },
  stanbic: { color: 'text-purple-700', Icon: Building2, label: 'Stanbic' },
  other: { color: 'text-slate-700', Icon: CreditCard, label: 'Nyingine' },
}

export default function MmilikiMishahara() {
  const {
    mishahara,
    lipaZoteZinazosubiri,
    lipaBulk,
    lipaMshaharaMmoja,
    futaMshahara,
  } = useMshahara()
  const { maduka } = useDuka()

  const [q, setQ] = useState('')
  const [filterDuka, setFilterDuka] = useState<string>('zote')
  const [filterHali, setFilterHali] = useState<HaliMshahara | 'zote'>('zote')
  const [selected, setSelected] = useState<Mshahara | null>(null)
  const [selectedIds, setSelectedIds] = useState<string[]>([])
  const [confirmBulk, setConfirmBulk] = useState(false)
  const [matokeo, setMatokeo] = useState<BulkPaymentResult | null>(null)

  const filtered = useMemo(() => {
    const query = q.toLowerCase().trim()
    return mishahara.filter((m) => {
      if (filterDuka !== 'zote' && m.dukaId !== filterDuka) return false
      if (filterHali !== 'zote' && m.hali !== filterHali) return false
      if (!query) return true
      return (
        m.id.toLowerCase().includes(query) ||
        m.jinaMtumiaji.toLowerCase().includes(query) ||
        m.dukaJina.toLowerCase().includes(query)
      )
    })
  }, [mishahara, q, filterDuka, filterHali])

  const inasubiriZote = useMemo(
    () => mishahara.filter((m) => m.hali === 'inasubiri'),
    [mishahara]
  )

  const stats = useMemo(() => {
    const zote = mishahara.length
    const jumlaMishahara = mishahara.reduce((s, m) => s + m.jumla, 0)
    const zilizolipwa = mishahara.filter((m) => m.hali === 'imelipwa')
    const jumlaIliyolipwa = zilizolipwa.reduce((s, m) => s + m.jumla, 0)
    const zinazosubiri = inasubiriZote
    const jumlaInasubiri = zinazosubiri.reduce((s, m) => s + m.jumla, 0)
    return {
      zote,
      jumlaMishahara,
      zilizolipwa: zilizolipwa.length,
      jumlaIliyolipwa,
      zinazosubiri: zinazosubiri.length,
      jumlaInasubiri,
    }
  }, [mishahara, inasubiriZote])

  const toggleSelect = (id: string) => {
    setSelectedIds((prev) =>
      prev.includes(id) ? prev.filter((x) => x !== id) : [...prev, id]
    )
  }

  const toggleSelectAll = () => {
    const inasubiriIds = filtered
      .filter((m) => m.hali === 'inasubiri')
      .map((m) => m.id)
    if (selectedIds.length === inasubiriIds.length) {
      setSelectedIds([])
    } else {
      setSelectedIds(inasubiriIds)
    }
  }

  const jumlaIliyochaguliwa = useMemo(() => {
    return filtered
      .filter((m) => selectedIds.includes(m.id))
      .reduce((s, m) => s + m.jumla, 0)
  }, [filtered, selectedIds])

  // Lipa zote kwa button moja
  const handleLipaZote = () => {
    const result = lipaZoteZinazosubiri()
    setMatokeo(result)
    setConfirmBulk(false)
    setSelectedIds([])
  }

  // Lipa waliochaguliwa
  const handleLipaWaliochaguliwa = () => {
    const result = lipaBulk(selectedIds)
    setMatokeo(result)
    setSelectedIds([])
  }

  return (
    <div className="space-y-5">
      {/* Header */}
      <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-end sm:justify-between gap-3">
        <div>
          <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold flex items-center gap-2">
            <Wallet size={24} className="text-brand-600" />
            Usimamizi wa Mishahara
          </h1>
          <p className="text-slate-500 text-sm">
            Lipa mishahara kwa button moja - pesa inaenda moja kwa moja kwa wafanyakazi
          </p>
        </div>
        <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2 self-start sm:self-auto">
          {inasubiriZote.length > 0 && (
            <button
              onClick={() => setConfirmBulk(true)}
              className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-4 py-2 bg-emerald-600 text-white rounded-lg text-sm font-semibold hover:bg-emerald-700"
            >
              <Zap size={16} />
              Lipa Wote ({inasubiriZote.length})
            </button>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Stats 4 */}
      <div className="grid grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4 gap-3">
        <StatCard
          label="Mishahara Yote"
          value={String(stats.zote)}
          Icon={Users}
          accent="brand"
        />
        <StatCard
          label="Jumla ya Mshahara"
          value={formatTsh(stats.jumlaMishahara)}
          Icon={DollarSign}
          accent="brand"
        />
        <StatCard
          label="Zilizolipwa"
          value={formatTsh(stats.jumlaIliyolipwa)}
          Icon={CheckCircle2}
          accent="emerald"
          sub={`${stats.zilizolipwa} wafanyakazi`}
        />
        <StatCard
          label="Zinasubiri"
          value={formatTsh(stats.jumlaInasubiri)}
          Icon={Clock}
          accent="amber"
          sub={`${stats.zinazosubiri} wafanyakazi`}
        />
      </div>

      {/* Info: Malipo yanaenda kwa akaunti za wafanyakazi */}
      <div className="bg-white rounded-xl border border-slate-200 p-4 flex items-start gap-2">
        <Sparkles size={16} className="text-brand-600 mt-0.5 shrink-0" />
        <div className="text-sm">
          <div className="font-medium text-slate-900">Malipo ya Moja kwa Moja</div>
          <div className="text-slate-500 mt-0.5">
            Pesa inaenda moja kwa moja kwenye akaunti ya kila mfanyakazi (mobile money au benki).
            Hakikisha kila mfanyakazi ameweka akaunti yake kwenye mfumo.
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Filters */}
      <div className="bg-white rounded-xl border border-slate-200 p-3 md:p-4 space-y-3">
        <div className="relative">
          <Search
            size={18}
            className="absolute left-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-slate-400 pointer-events-none"
          />
          <input
            value={q}
            onChange={(e) => setQ(e.target.value)}
            placeholder="Tafuta kwa mshahara ID, jina, au duka..."
            className="w-full pl-10 pr-4 py-2.5 rounded-lg border border-slate-200 focus:border-brand-500 outline-none text-sm"
          />
        </div>
        <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2 items-center">
          <Filter size={14} className="text-slate-400" />
          <select
            value={filterDuka}
            onChange={(e) => setFilterDuka(e.target.value)}
            className="px-2 py-1.5 rounded-lg border border-slate-200 text-sm"
          >
            <option value="zote">Maduka Yote</option>
            {maduka.map((d) => (
              <option key={d.id} value={d.id}>{d.jina}</option>
            ))}
          </select>
          <select
            value={filterHali}
            onChange={(e) => setFilterHali(e.target.value as HaliMshahara | 'zote')}
            className="px-2 py-1.5 rounded-lg border border-slate-200 text-sm"
          >
            <option value="zote">Hali Zote</option>
            <option value="inasubiri">Inasubiri</option>
            <option value="imelipwa">Imelipwa</option>
          </select>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Selection bar */}
      {selectedIds.length > 0 && (
        <div className="bg-brand-50 border border-brand-200 rounded-xl p-3 flex items-center gap-3">
          <CheckSquare size={18} className="text-brand-600 shrink-0" />
          <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
            <div className="text-sm font-semibold text-slate-900">
              {selectedIds.length} wamechaguliwa
            </div>
            <div className="text-xs text-slate-600">
              Jumla: {formatTsh(jumlaIliyochaguliwa)}
            </div>
          </div>
          <button
            onClick={handleLipaWaliochaguliwa}
            className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-2 bg-emerald-600 text-white rounded-lg text-sm font-semibold hover:bg-emerald-700"
          >
            <Banknote size={14} />
            Lipa
          </button>
          <button
            onClick={() => setSelectedIds([])}
            className="p-2 rounded-lg hover:bg-brand-100"
          >
            <X size={16} />
          </button>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* List */}
      {filtered.length === 0 ? (
        <div className="bg-white rounded-xl border border-slate-200 p-10 text-center">
          <Wallet size={40} className="mx-auto mb-3 text-slate-300" />
          <div className="font-semibold">Hakuna mishahara</div>
          <p className="text-sm text-slate-500 mt-1">
            Badilisha kichujio au ongeza mshahara mpya.
          </p>
        </div>
      ) : (
        <div className="space-y-3">
          {/* Select all */}
          {filtered.some((m) => m.hali === 'inasubiri') && (
            <button
              onClick={toggleSelectAll}
              className="flex items-center gap-2 px-3 py-2 rounded-lg bg-slate-50 hover:bg-slate-100 text-sm w-full text-left"
            >
              {selectedIds.length > 0 &&
              selectedIds.length ===
                filtered.filter((m) => m.hali === 'inasubiri').length ? (
                <CheckSquare size={16} className="text-brand-600" />
              ) : (
                <Square size={16} className="text-slate-400" />
              )}
              <span className="text-slate-600">
                Chagua wote wanaosubiri (
                {filtered.filter((m) => m.hali === 'inasubiri').length})
              </span>
            </button>
          )}

          {filtered.map((m) => {
            const hali = HALI_META[m.hali]
            const njia = m.njiaMalipo ? NJIA_META[m.njiaMalipo] : null
            const isSelected = selectedIds.includes(m.id)
            const canSelect = m.hali === 'inasubiri'
            return (
              <div
                key={m.id}
                className={`bg-white rounded-xl border p-4 transition ${
                  isSelected
                    ? 'border-brand-500 ring-2 ring-brand-100'
                    : 'border-slate-200'
                }`}
              >
                <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:justify-between gap-3">
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-3 min-w-0 flex-1">
                    {canSelect && (
                      <button
                        onClick={() => toggleSelect(m.id)}
                        className="shrink-0"
                      >
                        {isSelected ? (
                          <CheckSquare size={20} className="text-brand-600" />
                        ) : (
                          <Square size={20} className="text-slate-400" />
                        )}
                      </button>
                    )}
                    <button
                      onClick={() => setSelected(m)}
                      className="flex items-center gap-3 min-w-0 flex-1 text-left"
                    >
                      <div className="w-11 h-11 rounded-full bg-slate-50 text-slate-700 grid place-items-center shrink-0">
                        <User size={20} />
                      </div>
                      <div className="min-w-0">
                        <div className="flex items-center gap-2 flex-wrap">
                          <span className="font-semibold text-sm">
                            {m.jinaMtumiaji}
                          </span>
                          <span className="text-[10px] uppercase font-semibold px-2 py-0.5 rounded-md bg-slate-100 text-slate-600">
                            {m.nafasi}
                          </span>
                          <span
                            className={`text-[10px] uppercase font-semibold inline-flex items-center gap-1 px-2 py-0.5 rounded-md border ${hali.color}`}
                          >
                            <hali.Icon size={10} />
                            {hali.label}
                          </span>
                        </div>
                        <div className="text-xs text-slate-500 mt-0.5">
                          {m.id} - {m.dukaJina.replace('Smart Hardware - ', '')}
                        </div>
                        <div className="text-xs text-slate-500">
                          {m.mwezi} - {m.kipindi}
                        </div>
                      </div>
                    </button>
                  </div>
                  <div className="sm:text-right shrink-0">
                    <div className="text-base font-bold text-slate-900">
                      {formatTsh(m.jumla)}
                    </div>
                    {m.tareheMalipo && (
                      <div className="text-xs text-slate-500">
                        {m.tareheMalipo}
                      </div>
                    )}
                  </div>
                </div>

                <div className="mt-3 pt-3 border-t border-slate-100 flex flex-wrap gap-2">
                  <button
                    onClick={() => setSelected(m)}
                    className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 px-2.5 py-1.5 rounded-md bg-slate-100 text-slate-700 text-xs font-semibold hover:bg-slate-200"
                  >
                    <Eye size={12} />
                    Ona
                  </button>
                  {m.hali === 'inasubiri' && (
                    <button
                      onClick={() => lipaMshaharaMmoja(m.id)}
                      className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 px-2.5 py-1.5 rounded-md bg-emerald-600 text-white text-xs font-semibold hover:bg-emerald-700"
                    >
                      <Banknote size={12} />
                      Lipa
                    </button>
                  )}
                  {m.hali === 'imelipwa' && njia && (
                    <div
                      className={`inline-flex items-center gap-1 px-2.5 py-1.5 rounded-md bg-slate-50 text-xs ${njia.color}`}
                    >
                      <njia.Icon size={12} />
                      {m.kumbukumbu}
                    </div>
                  )}
                  <button
                    onClick={() => futaMshahara(m.id)}
                    className="ml-auto inline-flex items-center gap-1 px-2.5 py-1.5 rounded-md bg-red-50 text-red-600 text-xs font-semibold hover:bg-red-100"
                  >
                    <Trash2 size={12} />
                    Futa
                  </button>
                </div>
              </div>
            )
          })}
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Confirm Bulk Modal */}
      {confirmBulk && (
        <ConfirmBulkModal
          mishahara={inasubiriZote}
          onClose={() => setConfirmBulk(false)}
          onConfirm={handleLipaZote}
        />
      )}

      {/* Matokeo Modal */}
      {matokeo && (
        <MatokeoModal
          matokeo={matokeo}
          onClose={() => setMatokeo(null)}
        />
      )}

      {/* Detail Modal */}
      {selected && (
        <MshaharaDetailModal
          mshahara={selected}
          onClose={() => setSelected(null)}
        />
      )}
    </div>
  )
}

// ============================================================
// Confirm Bulk Modal
// ============================================================
function ConfirmBulkModal({
  mishahara,
  onClose,
  onConfirm,
}: {
  mishahara: Mshahara[]
  onClose: () => void
  onConfirm: () => void
}) {
  const jumla = mishahara.reduce((s, m) => s + m.jumla, 0)

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-end sm:items-center justify-center p-0 sm:p-4">
      <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-black/40" onClick={onClose} />
      <div className="relative bg-white w-full sm:max-w-lg rounded-t-2xl sm:rounded-2xl shadow-2xl max-h-[92vh] overflow-y-auto">
        <div className="sticky top-0 bg-white border-b border-slate-200 px-4 py-3 flex justify-between items-center">
          <div>
            <div className="font-semibold flex items-center gap-2">
              <Zap size={16} className="text-emerald-600" />
              Lipa Mishahara Yote
            </div>
            <div className="text-xs text-slate-500">
              {mishahara.length} wafanyakazi - {formatTsh(jumla)}
            </div>
          </div>
          <button onClick={onClose} className="p-1.5 rounded-lg hover:bg-slate-100">
            <X size={18} />
          </button>
        </div>

        <div className="p-4 space-y-3">
          <div className="max-h-60 overflow-y-auto rounded-lg bg-slate-50 p-3 space-y-1.5">
            {mishahara.map((m) => (
              <div
                key={m.id}
                className="flex items-center justify-between text-sm py-1"
              >
                <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
                  <div className="font-medium truncate">{m.jinaMtumiaji}</div>
                  <div className="text-xs text-slate-500">
                    {m.nafasi} - {m.dukaJina.replace('Smart Hardware - ', '')}
                  </div>
                </div>
                <div className="text-right shrink-0 ml-2">
                  <div className="font-bold">{formatTsh(m.jumla)}</div>
                </div>
              </div>
            ))}
          </div>

          <div className="flex items-start gap-2 p-3 rounded-lg bg-amber-50 border border-amber-200 text-amber-800 text-sm">
            <AlertTriangle size={16} className="mt-0.5 shrink-0" />
            <div>
              Pesa itaenda moja kwa moja kwenye akaunti za wafanyakazi (mobile money/benki).
              Wafanyakazi wasio na akaunti hawatapokea malipo.
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="p-3 rounded-lg bg-slate-50 flex justify-between">
            <span className="text-sm font-medium">Jumla ya Kulipa</span>
            <span className="text-lg font-bold text-slate-900">{formatTsh(jumla)}</span>
          </div>

          <div className="flex gap-2 pt-2">
            <button
              onClick={onClose}
              className="px-4 py-2.5 rounded-lg bg-slate-100 text-sm hover:bg-slate-200"
            >
              Ghairi
            </button>
            <button
              onClick={onConfirm}
              className="flex-1 inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 px-4 py-2.5 rounded-lg bg-emerald-600 text-white text-sm font-semibold hover:bg-emerald-700"
            >
              <Zap size={16} />
              Lipa Sasa
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}

// ============================================================
// Matokeo Modal
// ============================================================
function MatokeoModal({
  matokeo,
  onClose,
}: {
  matokeo: BulkPaymentResult
  onClose: () => void
}) {
  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-end sm:items-center justify-center p-0 sm:p-4">
      <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-black/40" onClick={onClose} />
      <div className="relative bg-white w-full sm:max-w-md rounded-t-2xl sm:rounded-2xl shadow-2xl max-h-[92vh] overflow-y-auto">
        <div className="p-6 text-center">
          <div className="w-16 h-16 rounded-full bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-700 grid place-items-center mx-auto">
            <CheckCircle2 size={32} />
          </div>
          <div className="text-lg font-bold mt-3">Malipo Yamekamilika</div>
          <div className="text-sm text-slate-500">
            Wafanyakazi {matokeo.zilizofanikiwa} wamelipwa kwa mafanikio
          </div>

          <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2 mt-4">
            <div className="p-3 rounded-lg bg-emerald-50">
              <div className="text-xs text-slate-600">Zilizofanikiwa</div>
              <div className="text-lg font-bold text-emerald-700">
                {matokeo.zilizofanikiwa}
              </div>
            </div>
            <div className="p-3 rounded-lg bg-red-50">
              <div className="text-xs text-slate-600">Zilizoshindwa</div>
              <div className="text-lg font-bold text-red-700">
                {matokeo.zilizoshindwa}
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="p-3 rounded-lg bg-slate-50 mt-2 flex justify-between text-sm">
            <span className="text-slate-600">Jumla Iliyolipwa</span>
            <span className="font-bold text-slate-900">
              {formatTsh(matokeo.jumla)}
            </span>
          </div>

          {matokeo.waliokosaAkaunti.length > 0 && (
            <div className="mt-3 p-3 rounded-lg bg-amber-50 border border-amber-200 text-left text-sm">
              <div className="font-medium text-amber-800 mb-1">
                Waliokosa Akaunti ({matokeo.waliokosaAkaunti.length})
              </div>
              <div className="text-xs text-amber-700">
                {matokeo.waliokosaAkaunti.join(', ')}
              </div>
            </div>
          )}

          <button
            onClick={onClose}
            className="mt-4 w-full px-4 py-2.5 rounded-lg bg-brand-600 text-white text-sm font-semibold hover:bg-brand-700"
          >
            Sawa
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}

// ============================================================
// Detail Modal
// ============================================================
function MshaharaDetailModal({
  mshahara,
  onClose,
}: {
  mshahara: Mshahara
  onClose: () => void
}) {
  const hali = HALI_META[mshahara.hali]
  const njia = mshahara.njiaMalipo ? NJIA_META[mshahara.njiaMalipo] : null

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-end sm:items-center justify-center p-0 sm:p-4">
      <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-black/40" onClick={onClose} />
      <div className="relative bg-white w-full sm:max-w-md rounded-t-2xl sm:rounded-2xl shadow-2xl max-h-[92vh] overflow-y-auto">
        <div className="sticky top-0 bg-white border-b border-slate-200 px-4 py-3 flex justify-between items-center">
          <div>
            <div className="font-semibold">{mshahara.id}</div>
            <div className="text-xs text-slate-500">
              {mshahara.jinaMtumiaji} - {mshahara.nafasi}
            </div>
          </div>
          <button onClick={onClose} className="p-1.5 rounded-lg hover:bg-slate-100">
            <X size={18} />
          </button>
        </div>

        <div className="p-4 space-y-3 text-sm">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
            <span
              className={`inline-flex items-center gap-1 px-2 py-1 rounded-md border text-xs font-semibold ${hali.color}`}
            >
              <hali.Icon size={12} />
              {hali.label}
            </span>
          </div>

          <div className="space-y-1.5">
            <div className="flex justify-between p-2 rounded-md bg-slate-50">
              <span className="text-slate-600">Mshahara Msingi</span>
              <span className="font-semibold">{formatTsh(mshahara.mshaharaMsingi)}</span>
            </div>
            <div className="flex justify-between p-2 rounded-md bg-slate-50">
              <span className="text-slate-600">Posho</span>
              <span className="font-semibold text-emerald-700">
                + {formatTsh(mshahara.posho)}
              </span>
            </div>
            <div className="flex justify-between p-2 rounded-md bg-slate-50">
              <span className="text-slate-600">Overtime</span>
              <span className="font-semibold text-emerald-700">
                + {formatTsh(mshahara.overtime)}
              </span>
            </div>
            <div className="flex justify-between p-2 rounded-md bg-slate-50">
              <span className="text-slate-600">Mafao</span>
              <span className="font-semibold text-emerald-700">
                + {formatTsh(mshahara.mafao)}
              </span>
            </div>
            <div className="flex justify-between p-2 rounded-md bg-slate-50">
              <span className="text-slate-600">Makato</span>
              <span className="font-semibold text-red-700">
                - {formatTsh(mshahara.makato)}
              </span>
            </div>
            <div className="flex justify-between p-3 rounded-lg bg-brand-50 border border-brand-200">
              <span className="font-semibold text-slate-700">JUMLA</span>
              <span className="font-bold text-brand-700 text-lg">
                {formatTsh(mshahara.jumla)}
              </span>
            </div>
          </div>

          {mshahara.hali === 'imelipwa' && njia && (
            <div className="p-3 rounded-lg bg-emerald-50 border border-emerald-100">
              <div className="text-xs text-slate-600 mb-1">Malipo</div>
              <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
                <njia.Icon size={14} className={njia.color} />
                <span className="font-semibold uppercase">{njia.label}</span>
              </div>
              <div className="text-xs text-slate-500 mt-1">
                Akaunti: {mshahara.akauntiMalipo}
              </div>
              <div className="text-xs text-slate-500 font-mono">
                {mshahara.kumbukumbu}
              </div>
              <div className="text-xs text-slate-500 mt-1">
                Tarehe: {mshahara.tareheMalipo}
              </div>
            </div>
          )}

          <div className="flex gap-2 pt-2 border-t border-slate-200">
            {mshahara.hali === 'imelipwa' && (
              <button className="flex-1 inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-2.5 rounded-lg bg-brand-600 text-white text-sm font-semibold hover:bg-brand-700">
                <Download size={14} />
                Pakua Payslip
              </button>
            )}
            <button
              onClick={onClose}
              className="px-3 py-2.5 rounded-lg bg-slate-100 text-sm hover:bg-slate-200"
            >
              Funga
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}

// ============================================================
// Stat Card
// ============================================================
type Accent = 'brand' | 'emerald' | 'amber' | 'red'
const ACCENT_BORDER: Record<Accent, string> = {
  brand: 'border-l-brand-500',
  emerald: 'border-l-emerald-500',
  amber: 'border-l-amber-500',
  red: 'border-l-red-500',
}
const ACCENT_TEXT: Record<Accent, string> = {
  brand: 'text-brand-700',
  emerald: 'text-emerald-700',
  amber: 'text-amber-700',
  red: 'text-red-700',
}

function StatCard({
  label,
  value,
  Icon,
  accent,
  sub,
}: {
  label: string
  value: string
  Icon: React.ElementType
  accent: Accent
  sub?: string
}) {
  return (
    <div
      className={`bg-white rounded-xl border border-slate-200 border-l-4 ${ACCENT_BORDER[accent]} p-4`}
    >
      <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
        <Icon size={16} className={ACCENT_TEXT[accent]} />
        <div className="text-xs text-slate-500">{label}</div>
      </div>
      <div className="text-base md:text-lg font-bold mt-2 truncate text-slate-900">
        {value}
      </div>
      {sub && <div className="text-[10px] text-slate-500 truncate">{sub}</div>}
    </div>
  )
}


import { useMemo, useState } from 'react'
import {
  ShoppingBag, Search, Filter, Store, Phone, CheckCircle2,
  Clock, XCircle, Truck, Trash2, X, Save, Receipt,
  Package, CreditCard, AlertTriangle, ChevronRight,
  TrendingUp, Download, Building2,
} from 'lucide-react'
import { useOrderAdmin } from '../../context/OrderAdminContext'
import { useDuka } from '../../context/DukaContext'
import { iconForKundi } from '../../utils/icons'
import type { HaliOrderAdmin, OrderAdmin } from '../../types'

const HALI_META: Record<
  HaliOrderAdmin,
  { label: string; color: string; Icon: React.ElementType }
> = {
  inasubiri_malipo: { label: 'Inasubiri Malipo', color: 'bg-amber-50 text-amber-700 border-amber-200', Icon: Clock },
  imekamilika: { label: 'Imekamilika', color: 'bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-700 border-emerald-200', Icon: CheckCircle2 },
  imekataliwa: { label: 'Imekataliwa', color: 'bg-red-50 text-red-700 border-red-200', Icon: XCircle },
  imesafirishwa: { label: 'Imesafirishwa', color: 'bg-brand-50 text-brand-700 border-brand-200', Icon: Truck },
}

const HALI_FILTERS: { key: HaliOrderAdmin | 'zote'; label: string }[] = [
  { key: 'zote', label: 'Zote' },
  { key: 'inasubiri_malipo', label: 'Zinasubiri' },
  { key: 'imekamilika', label: 'Zilizokamilika' },
  { key: 'imesafirishwa', label: 'Zimesafirishwa' },
  { key: 'imekataliwa', label: 'Zilizokataliwa' },
]

export default function MmilikiOrders() {
  const { orders, badilishaHali, thibitishaMalipo, futa } = useOrderAdmin()
  const { maduka } = useDuka()

  const [q, setQ] = useState('')
  const [filterDuka, setFilterDuka] = useState<string>('zote')
  const [filterHali, setFilterHali] = useState<HaliOrderAdmin | 'zote'>('zote')
  const [selected, setSelected] = useState<OrderAdmin | null>(null)

  const filtered = useMemo(() => {
    const query = q.toLowerCase().trim()
    return orders.filter((o) => {
      if (filterDuka !== 'zote' && o.dukaId !== filterDuka) return false
      if (filterHali !== 'zote' && o.hali !== filterHali) return false
      if (!query) return true
      return (
        o.id.toLowerCase().includes(query) ||
        o.mteja.toLowerCase().includes(query) ||
        o.simu.includes(query) ||
        o.dukaJina.toLowerCase().includes(query) ||
        o.bidhaa.some((b) => b.jina.toLowerCase().includes(query))
      )
    })
  }, [orders, q, filterDuka, filterHali])

  const stats = useMemo(() => {
    const zote = orders.length
    const zinazosubiri = orders.filter((o) => o.hali === 'inasubiri_malipo').length
    const zilizokamilika = orders.filter((o) => o.hali === 'imekamilika').length
    const jumlaMapato = orders
      .filter((o) => o.hali === 'imekamilika')
      .reduce((s, o) => s + o.jumla, 0)
    const jumlaInayosubiri = orders
      .filter((o) => o.hali === 'inasubiri_malipo')
      .reduce((s, o) => s + o.jumla, 0)
    return { zote, zinazosubiri, zilizokamilika, jumlaMapato, jumlaInayosubiri }
  }, [orders])

  return (
    <div className="space-y-5">
      {/* Header */}
      <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-end sm:justify-between gap-3">
        <div>
          <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold flex items-center gap-2">
            <ShoppingBag size={24} className="text-brand-600" />
            Orders - Maduka Yote
          </h1>
          <p className="text-slate-500 text-sm">
            Simamia orders zote za maduka yako
          </p>
        </div>
        <button className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-4 py-2 bg-slate-100 rounded-lg text-sm hover:bg-slate-200 self-start sm:self-auto">
          <Download size={16} />
          Pakua Ripoti
        </button>
      </div>

      {/* Stats */}
      <div className="grid grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4 gap-3">
        <StatCard label="Orders Zote" value={String(stats.zote)} Icon={ShoppingBag} tone="brand" />
        <StatCard
          label="Zinasubiri Malipo"
          value={String(stats.zinazosubiri)}
          Icon={Clock}
          tone="amber"
          sub={`Tsh ${stats.jumlaInayosubiri.toLocaleString()}`}
        />
        <StatCard label="Zilizokamilika" value={String(stats.zilizokamilika)} Icon={CheckCircle2} tone="emerald" />
        <StatCard
          label="Mapato Yote"
          value={`Tsh ${stats.jumlaMapato.toLocaleString()}`}
          Icon={TrendingUp}
          tone="brand"
        />
      </div>

      {/* Filters */}
      <div className="bg-white rounded-xl border border-slate-200 p-3 md:p-4 space-y-3">
        <div className="relative">
          <Search
            size={18}
            className="absolute left-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-slate-400 pointer-events-none"
          />
          <input
            value={q}
            onChange={(e) => setQ(e.target.value)}
            placeholder="Tafuta kwa Order ID, mteja, simu, au bidhaa..."
            className="w-full pl-10 pr-4 py-2.5 rounded-lg border border-slate-200 focus:border-brand-500 outline-none text-sm"
          />
        </div>
        <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2 items-center">
          <Filter size={14} className="text-slate-400" />
          <select
            value={filterDuka}
            onChange={(e) => setFilterDuka(e.target.value)}
            className="px-2 py-1.5 rounded-lg border border-slate-200 text-sm"
          >
            <option value="zote">Maduka Yote</option>
            {maduka.map((d) => (
              <option key={d.id} value={d.id}>{d.jina}</option>
            ))}
          </select>
          <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-1.5">
            {HALI_FILTERS.map((f) => (
              <button
                key={f.key}
                onClick={() => setFilterHali(f.key)}
                className={`px-2.5 py-1.5 rounded-lg text-xs border transition ${
                  filterHali === f.key
                    ? 'bg-brand-600 text-white border-brand-600'
                    : 'bg-white border-slate-200 hover:bg-slate-50'
                }`}
              >
                {f.label}
              </button>
            ))}
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Orders List */}
      {filtered.length === 0 ? (
        <div className="bg-white rounded-xl border border-slate-200 p-10 text-center">
          <ShoppingBag size={40} className="mx-auto mb-3 text-slate-300" />
          <div className="font-semibold">Hakuna orders</div>
          <p className="text-sm text-slate-500 mt-1">Badilisha kichujio au utafutaji.</p>
        </div>
      ) : (
        <div className="space-y-3">
          {filtered.map((o) => {
            const meta = HALI_META[o.hali]
            const jumlaIlipwa = o.malipo
              .filter((m) => m.hali === 'imethibitishwa')
              .reduce((s, m) => s + m.kiasi, 0)
            const baki = o.jumla - jumlaIlipwa

            return (
              <button
                key={o.id}
                onClick={() => setSelected(o)}
                className="w-full text-left bg-white rounded-xl border border-slate-200 p-4 hover:border-brand-500 hover:shadow-sm transition"
              >
                <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:justify-between gap-3">
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-3 min-w-0">
                    <div className={`w-10 h-10 rounded-lg grid place-items-center shrink-0 ${meta.color}`}>
                      <meta.Icon size={18} />
                    </div>
                    <div className="min-w-0">
                      <div className="flex items-center gap-2 flex-wrap">
                        <span className="font-semibold text-sm">{o.id}</span>
                        <span className={`text-[10px] uppercase font-semibold px-2 py-0.5 rounded-md border ${meta.color}`}>
                          {meta.label}
                        </span>
                        <span className="text-[10px] uppercase font-semibold px-2 py-0.5 rounded-md bg-slate-100 text-slate-600">
                          {o.dukaJina.replace('Smart Hardware - ', '')}
                        </span>
                      </div>
                      <div className="text-xs text-slate-500 mt-0.5 truncate">
                        {o.mteja} - {o.simu}
                      </div>
                      <div className="text-xs text-slate-500 mt-0.5">
                        {o.tarehe} {o.muda} - {o.bidhaa.length} bidhaa - Muuzaji: {o.muuzaji}
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                  <div className="sm:text-right shrink-0">
                    <div className="text-base font-bold text-brand-700">
                      Tsh {o.jumla.toLocaleString()}
                    </div>
                    {baki > 0 && (
                      <div className="text-xs text-amber-700">
                        Baki: Tsh {baki.toLocaleString()}
                      </div>
                    )}
                    {baki <= 0 && (
                      <div className="text-xs text-emerald-700 font-semibold">
                        Imelipwa Yote
                      </div>
                    )}
                  </div>
                </div>

                <div className="mt-3 pt-3 border-t border-slate-100 flex flex-wrap gap-1">
                  {o.bidhaa.slice(0, 3).map((b) => (
                    <span key={b.id} className="text-[10px] px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded bg-slate-100 text-slate-700">
                      {b.jina} ({b.wingi} {b.uom})
                    </span>
                  ))}
                  {o.bidhaa.length > 3 && (
                    <span className="text-[10px] px-1.5 py-0.5 text-slate-500">
                      +{o.bidhaa.length - 3}
                    </span>
                  )}
                </div>

                <div className="mt-3 flex items-center justify-end text-xs font-semibold text-brand-600">
                  Simamia
                  <ChevronRight size={14} />
                </div>
              </button>
            )
          })}
        </div>
      )}

      {selected && (
        <OrderDetailModal
          order={selected}
          onClose={() => setSelected(null)}
          onBadilishaHali={badilishaHali}
          onThibitishaMalipo={thibitishaMalipo}
          onFuta={futa}
        />
      )}
    </div>
  )
}

// ============================================================
// Order Detail Modal
// ============================================================
function OrderDetailModal({
  order,
  onClose,
  onBadilishaHali,
  onThibitishaMalipo,
  onFuta,
}: {
  order: OrderAdmin
  onClose: () => void
  onBadilishaHali: (id: string, hali: HaliOrderAdmin) => void
  onThibitishaMalipo: (orderId: string, malipoId: string) => void
  onFuta: (id: string) => void
}) {
  const jumlaIlipwa = order.malipo
    .filter((m) => m.hali === 'imethibitishwa')
    .reduce((s, m) => s + m.kiasi, 0)
  const baki = order.jumla - jumlaIlipwa

  const [hali, setHali] = useState<HaliOrderAdmin>(order.hali)
  const [confirmFuta, setConfirmFuta] = useState(false)

  const handleSave = () => {
    if (hali !== order.hali) onBadilishaHali(order.id, hali)
    onClose()
  }

  const handleFuta = () => {
    onFuta(order.id)
    onClose()
  }

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-end sm:items-center justify-center p-0 sm:p-4">
      <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-black/40" onClick={onClose} />
      <div className="relative bg-white w-full sm:max-w-2xl rounded-t-2xl sm:rounded-2xl shadow-2xl max-h-[92vh] overflow-y-auto">
        <div className="sticky top-0 bg-white border-b border-slate-200 px-4 py-3 flex justify-between items-center">
          <div>
            <div className="font-semibold">{order.id}</div>
            <div className="text-xs text-slate-500">
              {order.tarehe} {order.muda} - {order.dukaJina}
            </div>
          </div>
          <button onClick={onClose} className="p-1.5 rounded-lg hover:bg-slate-100">
            <X size={18} />
          </button>
        </div>

        <div className="p-4 space-y-4">
          {/* Mteja */}
          <div className="p-3 rounded-lg bg-slate-50">
            <div className="text-xs text-slate-500 mb-1">Mteja</div>
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
              <div className="w-8 h-8 rounded-full bg-brand-600 text-white grid place-items-center text-[10px] font-bold shrink-0">
                {order.mteja.split(' ').map((n) => n[0]).slice(0, 2).join('')}
              </div>
              <div className="min-w-0">
                <div className="font-medium text-sm truncate">{order.mteja}</div>
                <div className="text-xs text-slate-500 flex items-center gap-1">
                  <Phone size={10} /> {order.simu}
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Bidhaa */}
          <div>
            <h3 className="text-sm font-semibold mb-2 flex items-center gap-2">
              <Package size={14} className="text-brand-600" />
              Bidhaa ({order.bidhaa.length})
            </h3>
            <div className="space-y-1.5">
              {order.bidhaa.map((b) => {
                const Icon = iconForKundi(
                  b.jina.includes('Saruji') ? 'Saruji'
                  : b.jina.includes('Nondo') ? 'Nondo'
                  : b.jina.includes('Mabati') ? 'Mabati'
                  : 'Mbao'
                )
                return (
                  <div key={b.id} className="flex items-center gap-2 p-2 rounded-lg bg-slate-50 text-sm">
                    <div className="w-7 h-7 rounded-md bg-white border border-slate-200 grid place-items-center text-slate-600 shrink-0">
                      <Icon size={12} />
                    </div>
                    <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
                      <div className="font-medium truncate">{b.jina}</div>
                      <div className="text-xs text-slate-500">
                        {b.wingi} {b.uom} x Tsh {b.bei.toLocaleString()}
                      </div>
                    </div>
                    <div className="font-semibold whitespace-nowrap">
                      Tsh {b.jumla.toLocaleString()}
                    </div>
                  </div>
                )
              })}
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Malipo */}
          <div>
            <h3 className="text-sm font-semibold mb-2 flex items-center gap-2">
              <CreditCard size={14} className="text-brand-600" />
              Malipo ({order.malipo.length})
            </h3>
            {order.malipo.length === 0 ? (
              <div className="text-sm text-slate-500 p-3 rounded-lg bg-slate-50 text-center">
                Hakuna malipo bado
              </div>
            ) : (
              <div className="space-y-1.5">
                {order.malipo.map((m) => (
                  <div
                    key={m.id}
                    className={`flex items-center gap-2 p-2 rounded-lg text-sm ${
                      m.hali === 'imethibitishwa'
                        ? 'bg-emerald-50 border border-emerald-100'
                        : 'bg-amber-50 border border-amber-100'
                    }`}
                  >
                    <div className={`w-7 h-7 rounded-md grid place-items-center shrink-0 ${
                      m.hali === 'imethibitishwa'
                        ? 'bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-700'
                        : 'bg-amber-100 text-amber-700'
                    }`}>
                      {m.hali === 'imethibitishwa' ? <CheckCircle2 size={12} /> : <Clock size={12} />}
                    </div>
                    <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
                      <div className="font-medium uppercase text-xs">{m.njia} - {m.simu}</div>
                      <div className="text-xs text-slate-600 font-mono">{m.kumbukumbu}</div>
                    </div>
                    <div className="font-semibold whitespace-nowrap">
                      Tsh {m.kiasi.toLocaleString()}
                    </div>
                    {m.hali !== 'imethibitishwa' && (
                      <button
                        onClick={() => onThibitishaMalipo(order.id, m.id)}
                        className="px-2 py-1 rounded-md bg-emerald-600 text-white text-[10px] font-semibold hover:bg-emerald-700"
                      >
                        Thibitisha
                      </button>
                    )}
                  </div>
                ))}
              </div>
            )}

            <div className="mt-3 p-3 rounded-lg bg-slate-50 text-sm space-y-1">
              <div className="flex justify-between">
                <span>Jumla</span>
                <span className="font-semibold">Tsh {order.jumla.toLocaleString()}</span>
              </div>
              <div className="flex justify-between">
                <span>Iliyolipwa</span>
                <span className="font-semibold text-emerald-700">
                  Tsh {jumlaIlipwa.toLocaleString()}
                </span>
              </div>
              {baki > 0 && (
                <div className="flex justify-between border-t border-slate-200 pt-1">
                  <span className="font-medium">Baki</span>
                  <span className="font-bold text-amber-700">
                    Tsh {baki.toLocaleString()}
                  </span>
                </div>
              )}
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Badilisha Hali */}
          <div>
            <h3 className="text-sm font-semibold mb-2 flex items-center gap-2">
              <Receipt size={14} className="text-brand-600" />
              Hali ya Order
            </h3>
            <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2">
              {(Object.keys(HALI_META) as HaliOrderAdmin[]).map((h) => {
                const m = HALI_META[h]
                return (
                  <button
                    key={h}
                    onClick={() => setHali(h)}
                    className={`inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-2 rounded-lg border text-xs font-medium ${
                      hali === h
                        ? `${m.color} font-semibold`
                        : 'border-slate-200 hover:bg-slate-50'
                    }`}
                  >
                    <m.Icon size={12} />
                    {m.label}
                  </button>
                )
              })}
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Actions */}
          <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row gap-2 pt-2 border-t border-slate-200">
            {confirmFuta ? (
              <>
                <div className="flex-1 p-3 rounded-lg bg-red-50 text-red-700 text-xs flex items-center gap-2">
                  <AlertTriangle size={14} className="shrink-0" />
                  Una uhakika unataka kufuta order hii?
                </div>
                <button
                  onClick={handleFuta}
                  className="px-4 py-2.5 rounded-lg bg-red-600 text-white text-sm font-semibold hover:bg-red-700"
                >
                  Ndiyo, Futa
                </button>
                <button
                  onClick={() => setConfirmFuta(false)}
                  className="px-4 py-2.5 rounded-lg bg-slate-100 text-sm hover:bg-slate-200"
                >
                  Ghairi
                </button>
              </>
            ) : (
              <>
                <button
                  onClick={() => setConfirmFuta(true)}
                  className="inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 px-4 py-2.5 rounded-lg bg-red-50 text-red-600 text-sm font-semibold hover:bg-red-100"
                >
                  <Trash2 size={14} />
                  Futa
                </button>
                <button
                  onClick={onClose}
                  className="px-4 py-2.5 rounded-lg bg-slate-100 text-sm hover:bg-slate-200 sm:ml-auto"
                >
                  Funga
                </button>
                <button
                  onClick={handleSave}
                  disabled={hali === order.hali}
                  className="inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 px-4 py-2.5 rounded-lg bg-brand-600 text-white text-sm font-semibold hover:bg-brand-700 disabled:opacity-50 disabled:cursor-not-allowed"
                >
                  <Save size={14} />
                  Hifadhi
                </button>
              </>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}

// ============================================================
// Stat Card
// ============================================================
type Tone = 'brand' | 'emerald' | 'amber' | 'red'
const TONE: Record<Tone, string> = {
  brand: 'bg-brand-50 text-brand-700',
  emerald: 'bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-700',
  amber: 'bg-amber-50 text-amber-700',
  red: 'bg-red-50 text-red-700',
}

function StatCard({
  label,
  value,
  Icon,
  tone,
  sub,
}: {
  label: string
  value: string
  Icon: React.ElementType
  tone: Tone
  sub?: string
}) {
  return (
    <div className="bg-white rounded-xl border border-slate-200 p-4">
      <div className={`inline-flex w-9 h-9 rounded-lg items-center justify-center ${TONE[tone]}`}>
        <Icon size={16} />
      </div>
      <div className="text-xs text-slate-500 mt-2">{label}</div>
      <div className="text-base md:text-lg font-bold mt-0.5 truncate">{value}</div>
      {sub && <div className="text-[10px] text-slate-500 truncate">{sub}</div>}
    </div>
  )
}

import { useMemo, useState } from 'react'
import {
  Package, Search, Filter, Store, Plus, X, Save, Trash2,
  CheckCircle2, Clock, XCircle, AlertTriangle, Calendar,
  Eye, FileText, TrendingDown, PackageX, Tag, Upload,
  RotateCcw, Building2, User,
} from 'lucide-react'
import { useRipotiBidhaa } from '../../context/RipotiBidhaaContext'
import { useDuka } from '../../context/DukaContext'
import { bidhaaKamili } from '../../data/demo'
import { iconForKundi } from '../../utils/icons'
import { formatTsh } from '../../utils/faida'
import {
  sikuZilizobaki,
  haliExpiry,
  rangiExpiry,
} from '../../utils/bidhaa'
import type {
  AinaRipotiBidhaa,
  HaliRipoti,
  RipotiBidhaa,
} from '../../types'

const AINA_META: Record<
  AinaRipotiBidhaa,
  { label: string; color: string; Icon: React.ElementType }
> = {
  uharibifu: { label: 'Uharibifu', color: 'text-red-700 border-red-200', Icon: AlertTriangle },
  expiry: { label: 'Expiry', color: 'text-amber-700 border-amber-200', Icon: Calendar },
  upotevu: { label: 'Upotevu', color: 'text-orange-700 border-orange-200', Icon: PackageX },
  kurudisha_msambazaji: { label: 'Kurudisha', color: 'text-purple-700 border-purple-200', Icon: RotateCcw },
}

const HALI_META: Record<
  HaliRipoti,
  { label: string; Icon: React.ElementType; color: string }
> = {
  inasubiri: { label: 'Inasubiri', Icon: Clock, color: 'text-amber-700' },
  imethibitishwa: { label: 'Imethibitishwa', Icon: CheckCircle2, color: 'text-emerald-700' },
  imekataliwa: { label: 'Imekataliwa', Icon: XCircle, color: 'text-red-700' },
}

const AINA_FILTERS: { key: AinaRipotiBidhaa | 'zote'; label: string }[] = [
  { key: 'zote', label: 'Zote' },
  { key: 'uharibifu', label: 'Uharibifu' },
  { key: 'expiry', label: 'Expiry' },
  { key: 'upotevu', label: 'Upotevu' },
  { key: 'kurudisha_msambazaji', label: 'Kurudisha' },
]

export default function MmilikiRipotiBidhaa() {
  const { ripoti, ongezaRipoti, thibitisha, kataa, futa } = useRipotiBidhaa()
  const { maduka } = useDuka()

  const [q, setQ] = useState('')
  const [filterAina, setFilterAina] = useState<AinaRipotiBidhaa | 'zote'>('zote')
  const [filterDuka, setFilterDuka] = useState<string>('zote')
  const [filterHali, setFilterHali] = useState<HaliRipoti | 'zote'>('zote')
  const [formOpen, setFormOpen] = useState(false)
  const [selected, setSelected] = useState<RipotiBidhaa | null>(null)

  const filtered = useMemo(() => {
    const query = q.toLowerCase().trim()
    return ripoti.filter((r) => {
      if (filterAina !== 'zote' && r.aina !== filterAina) return false
      if (filterDuka !== 'zote' && r.dukaId !== filterDuka) return false
      if (filterHali !== 'zote' && r.hali !== filterHali) return false
      if (!query) return true
      return (
        r.id.toLowerCase().includes(query) ||
        r.jina.toLowerCase().includes(query) ||
        r.sku.toLowerCase().includes(query) ||
        r.sababu.toLowerCase().includes(query) ||
        r.dukaJina.toLowerCase().includes(query)
      )
    })
  }, [ripoti, q, filterAina, filterDuka, filterHali])

  const stats = useMemo(() => {
    const zote = ripoti.length
    const uharibifu = ripoti.filter((r) => r.aina === 'uharibifu').length
    const expiry = ripoti.filter((r) => r.aina === 'expiry').length
    const jumlaHasara = ripoti
      .filter((r) => r.aina === 'uharibifu' || r.aina === 'expiry' || r.aina === 'upotevu')
      .reduce((s, r) => s + r.thamani, 0)
    const zinazosubiri = ripoti.filter((r) => r.hali === 'inasubiri').length
    return { zote, uharibifu, expiry, jumlaHasara, zinazosubiri }
  }, [ripoti])

  return (
    <div className="space-y-5">
      {/* Header */}
      <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-end sm:justify-between gap-3">
        <div>
          <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold flex items-center gap-2">
            <Package size={24} className="text-brand-600" />
            Ripoti za Bidhaa - Maduka Yote
          </h1>
          <p className="text-slate-500 text-sm">
            Thibitisha au kataa ripoti za bidhaa zilizoharibika, kuisha muda, au kupotea
          </p>
        </div>
        <button
          onClick={() => setFormOpen(true)}
          className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-4 py-2 bg-brand-600 text-white rounded-lg text-sm font-semibold hover:bg-brand-700 self-start sm:self-auto"
        >
          <Plus size={16} />
          Ripoti Mpya
        </button>
      </div>

      {/* Stats 4 */}
      <div className="grid grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4 gap-3">
        <StatCard
          label="Ripoti Zote"
          value={String(stats.zote)}
          Icon={FileText}
          accent="brand"
          sub={`${stats.zinazosubiri} zinasubiri`}
        />
        <StatCard
          label="Uharibifu"
          value={String(stats.uharibifu)}
          Icon={AlertTriangle}
          accent="red"
        />
        <StatCard
          label="Expiry"
          value={String(stats.expiry)}
          Icon={Calendar}
          accent="amber"
        />
        <StatCard
          label="Jumla ya Hasara"
          value={formatTsh(stats.jumlaHasara)}
          Icon={TrendingDown}
          accent="red"
        />
      </div>

      {/* Filters */}
      <div className="bg-white rounded-xl border border-slate-200 p-3 md:p-4 space-y-3">
        <div className="relative">
          <Search
            size={18}
            className="absolute left-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-slate-400 pointer-events-none"
          />
          <input
            value={q}
            onChange={(e) => setQ(e.target.value)}
            placeholder="Tafuta kwa ID, bidhaa, SKU, sababu, au duka..."
            className="w-full pl-10 pr-4 py-2.5 rounded-lg border border-slate-200 focus:border-brand-500 outline-none text-sm"
          />
        </div>
        <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2 items-center">
          <Filter size={14} className="text-slate-400" />
          <select
            value={filterDuka}
            onChange={(e) => setFilterDuka(e.target.value)}
            className="px-2 py-1.5 rounded-lg border border-slate-200 text-sm"
          >
            <option value="zote">Maduka Yote</option>
            {maduka.map((d) => (
              <option key={d.id} value={d.id}>{d.jina}</option>
            ))}
          </select>
          <select
            value={filterHali}
            onChange={(e) => setFilterHali(e.target.value as HaliRipoti | 'zote')}
            className="px-2 py-1.5 rounded-lg border border-slate-200 text-sm"
          >
            <option value="zote">Hali Zote</option>
            <option value="inasubiri">Inasubiri</option>
            <option value="imethibitishwa">Imethibitishwa</option>
            <option value="imekataliwa">Imekataliwa</option>
          </select>
        </div>
        <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-1.5">
          {AINA_FILTERS.map((f) => (
            <button
              key={f.key}
              onClick={() => setFilterAina(f.key)}
              className={`px-2.5 py-1.5 rounded-lg text-xs border transition ${
                filterAina === f.key
                  ? 'bg-brand-600 text-white border-brand-600'
                  : 'bg-white border-slate-200 hover:bg-slate-50'
              }`}
            >
              {f.label}
            </button>
          ))}
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* List */}
      {filtered.length === 0 ? (
        <div className="bg-white rounded-xl border border-slate-200 p-10 text-center">
          <Package size={40} className="mx-auto mb-3 text-slate-300" />
          <div className="font-semibold">Hakuna ripoti</div>
          <p className="text-sm text-slate-500 mt-1">
            Badilisha kichujio au ongeza ripoti mpya.
          </p>
        </div>
      ) : (
        <div className="space-y-3">
          {filtered.map((r) => {
            const aina = AINA_META[r.aina]
            const hali = HALI_META[r.hali]
            const bidhaa = bidhaaKamili.find((b) => b.id === r.bidhaaId)
            const Icon = iconForKundi(bidhaa?.iconKey)
            return (
              <div key={r.id} className="bg-white rounded-xl border border-slate-200 p-4">
                <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:justify-between gap-3">
                  <button
                    onClick={() => setSelected(r)}
                    className="flex items-center gap-3 min-w-0 flex-1 text-left"
                  >
                    <div className="w-11 h-11 rounded-lg bg-slate-50 text-slate-700 grid place-items-center shrink-0">
                      <Icon size={20} />
                    </div>
                    <div className="min-w-0">
                      <div className="flex items-center gap-2 flex-wrap">
                        <span className="font-semibold text-sm">{r.id}</span>
                        <span className={`text-[10px] uppercase font-semibold px-2 py-0.5 rounded-md border ${aina.color}`}>
                          {aina.label}
                        </span>
                        <span className={`text-[10px] uppercase font-semibold inline-flex items-center gap-1 ${hali.color}`}>
                          <hali.Icon size={10} />
                          {hali.label}
                        </span>
                      </div>
                      <div className="text-sm font-medium mt-0.5 truncate">{r.jina}</div>
                      <div className="text-xs text-slate-500 truncate">
                        {r.sku} - {r.dukaJina.replace('Smart Hardware - ', '')} - {r.tarehe}
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  </button>
                  <div className="sm:text-right shrink-0">
                    <div className="text-base font-bold text-slate-900">
                      {r.wingi} {r.uom}
                    </div>
                    <div className="text-xs text-slate-500">{formatTsh(r.thamani)}</div>
                  </div>
                </div>

                {r.hali === 'inasubiri' && (
                  <div className="mt-3 pt-3 border-t border-slate-100 flex gap-2">
                    <button
                      onClick={() => thibitisha(r.id)}
                      className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 px-2.5 py-1.5 rounded-md bg-emerald-600 text-white text-xs font-semibold hover:bg-emerald-700"
                    >
                      <CheckCircle2 size={12} />
                      Thibitisha
                    </button>
                    <button
                      onClick={() => setSelected(r)}
                      className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 px-2.5 py-1.5 rounded-md bg-red-600 text-white text-xs font-semibold hover:bg-red-700"
                    >
                      <XCircle size={12} />
                      Kataa
                    </button>
                    <button
                      onClick={() => setSelected(r)}
                      className="ml-auto inline-flex items-center gap-1 px-2.5 py-1.5 rounded-md bg-slate-100 text-slate-700 text-xs font-semibold hover:bg-slate-200"
                    >
                      <Eye size={12} />
                      Ona
                    </button>
                  </div>
                )}
              </div>
            )
          })}
        </div>
      )}

      {formOpen && (
        <FomuRipotiMmiliki
          onClose={() => setFormOpen(false)}
          onSubmit={ongezaRipoti}
          maduka={maduka}
        />
      )}
      {selected && (
        <RipotiDetailModal
          ripoti={selected}
          onClose={() => setSelected(null)}
          onThibitisha={() => {
            thibitisha(selected.id)
            setSelected(null)
          }}
          onKataa={() => {
            kataa(selected.id, 'Imekataliwa na mmiliki')
            setSelected(null)
          }}
          onFuta={() => {
            futa(selected.id)
            setSelected(null)
          }}
        />
      )}
    </div>
  )
}

// ============================================================
// Fomu ya Kuripoti Bidhaa (Mmiliki)
// ============================================================
function FomuRipotiMmiliki({
  onClose,
  onSubmit,
  maduka,
}: {
  onClose: () => void
  onSubmit: ReturnType<typeof useRipotiBidhaa>['ongezaRipoti']
  maduka: { id: string; jina: string }[]
}) {
  const [dukaId, setDukaId] = useState(maduka[0]?.id || '')
  const [bidhaaId, setBidhaaId] = useState('')
  const [aina, setAina] = useState<AinaRipotiBidhaa>('uharibifu')
  const [wingi, setWingi] = useState(0)
  const [sababu, setSababu] = useState('')
  const [maelezo, setMaelezo] = useState('')
  const [pichaUrl, setPichaUrl] = useState('')
  const [error, setError] = useState('')

  const bidhaa = bidhaaKamili.find((b) => b.id === bidhaaId)
  const duka = maduka.find((d) => d.id === dukaId)

  const handleSubmit = (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault()
    if (!duka) return setError('Chagua duka')
    if (!bidhaa) return setError('Chagua bidhaa')
    if (wingi <= 0) return setError('Wingi lazima uwe zaidi ya sifuri')
    if (wingi > bidhaa.stoki) return setError(`Wingi hauwezi kuzidi stoki (${bidhaa.stoki})`)
