    if (!sababu.trim()) return setError('Sababu inahitajika')

    setError('')
    onSubmit({
      bidhaaId: bidhaa.id,
      jina: bidhaa.jina,
      sku: bidhaa.sku,
      uom: bidhaa.uom,
      kundi: bidhaa.kundi,
      dukaId: duka.id,
      dukaJina: duka.jina,
      aina,
      wingi,
      beiUnunuzi: bidhaa.beiUnunuzi,
      sababu: sababu.trim(),
      maelezo: maelezo.trim() || undefined,
      pichaUrl: pichaUrl.trim() || undefined,
      expiry: bidhaa.expiry,
      mtengenezaji: bidhaa.mtengenezaji,
      uzito: bidhaa.uzito,
    })
    onClose()
  }

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-end sm:items-center justify-center p-0 sm:p-4">
      <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-black/40" onClick={onClose} />
      <div className="relative bg-white w-full sm:max-w-lg rounded-t-2xl sm:rounded-2xl shadow-2xl max-h-[92vh] overflow-y-auto">
        <div className="sticky top-0 bg-white border-b border-slate-200 px-4 py-3 flex justify-between items-center">
          <div>
            <div className="font-semibold">Ripoti Bidhaa</div>
            <div className="text-xs text-slate-500">Kwa niaba ya duka</div>
          </div>
          <button onClick={onClose} className="p-1.5 rounded-lg hover:bg-slate-100">
            <X size={18} />
          </button>
        </div>

        <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="p-4 space-y-3">
          <div>
            <label className="text-sm font-medium">Duka</label>
            <select
              value={dukaId}
              onChange={(e) => setDukaId(e.target.value)}
              className="mt-1 w-full px-3 py-2.5 rounded-lg border border-slate-200 focus:border-brand-500 outline-none text-sm"
            >
              {maduka.map((d) => (
                <option key={d.id} value={d.id}>{d.jina}</option>
              ))}
            </select>
          </div>

          <div>
            <label className="text-sm font-medium">Chagua Bidhaa</label>
            <select
              value={bidhaaId}
              onChange={(e) => setBidhaaId(e.target.value)}
              className="mt-1 w-full px-3 py-2.5 rounded-lg border border-slate-200 focus:border-brand-500 outline-none text-sm"
            >
              <option value="">-- Chagua bidhaa --</option>
              {bidhaaKamili.map((b) => (
                <option key={b.id} value={b.id}>
                  {b.jina} ({b.sku}) - Stoki: {b.stoki} {b.uom}
                </option>
              ))}
            </select>
          </div>

          {bidhaa && (
            <div className="p-3 rounded-lg bg-slate-50 flex items-center gap-3">
              <div className="w-10 h-10 rounded-lg bg-white border border-slate-200 grid place-items-center text-slate-600 shrink-0">
                <Package size={18} />
              </div>
              <div className="flex-1 min-w-0 text-sm">
                <div className="font-medium truncate">{bidhaa.jina}</div>
                <div className="text-xs text-slate-500">
                  {bidhaa.mtengenezaji} - {bidhaa.uzito} - Expiry: {bidhaa.expiry || 'Hakuna'}
                </div>
                <div className="text-xs text-slate-500">
                  Stoki: {bidhaa.stoki} {bidhaa.uom}
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>
          )}

          <div>
            <label className="text-sm font-medium">Aina ya Ripoti</label>
            <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2 mt-1">
              {(Object.keys(AINA_META) as AinaRipotiBidhaa[]).map((a) => {
                const meta = AINA_META[a]
                return (
                  <button
                    key={a}
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => setAina(a)}
                    className={`px-3 py-2 rounded-lg border text-xs text-left ${
                      aina === a
                        ? 'border-brand-500 bg-slate-50 font-semibold'
                        : 'border-slate-200 hover:bg-slate-50'
                    }`}
                  >
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
                      <meta.Icon size={12} />
                      <span>{meta.label}</span>
                    </div>
                  </button>
                )
              })}
            </div>
          </div>

          <div>
            <label className="text-sm font-medium">Wingi</label>
            <input
              type="number"
              value={wingi || ''}
              onChange={(e) => setWingi(Number(e.target.value))}
              placeholder="Mfano: 5"
              className="mt-1 w-full px-3 py-2.5 rounded-lg border border-slate-200 focus:border-brand-500 outline-none text-sm"
            />
            {bidhaa && (
              <div className="text-xs text-slate-500 mt-1">
                Stoki iliyopo: {bidhaa.stoki} {bidhaa.uom}
              </div>
            )}
          </div>

          <div>
            <label className="text-sm font-medium">Sababu</label>
            <input
              value={sababu}
              onChange={(e) => setSababu(e.target.value)}
              placeholder="Mfano: Saruji imekauka"
              className="mt-1 w-full px-3 py-2.5 rounded-lg border border-slate-200 focus:border-brand-500 outline-none text-sm"
            />
          </div>

          <div>
            <label className="text-sm font-medium">Maelezo (hiari)</label>
            <textarea
              value={maelezo}
              onChange={(e) => setMaelezo(e.target.value)}
              rows={2}
              placeholder="Eleza zaidi..."
              className="mt-1 w-full px-3 py-2.5 rounded-lg border border-slate-200 focus:border-brand-500 outline-none text-sm resize-none"
            />
          </div>

          <div>
            <label className="text-sm font-medium">Picha (hiari)</label>
            <div className="mt-1 flex items-center gap-2">
              <input
                value={pichaUrl}
                onChange={(e) => setPichaUrl(e.target.value)}
                placeholder="URL ya picha"
                className="flex-1 px-3 py-2.5 rounded-lg border border-slate-200 focus:border-brand-500 outline-none text-sm"
              />
              <button
                type="button"
                className="p-2.5 rounded-lg bg-slate-100 hover:bg-slate-200"
                title="Pakia picha"
              >
                <Upload size={16} />
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>

          {error && (
            <div className="flex items-start gap-2 p-3 rounded-lg bg-red-50 text-red-700 text-sm">
              <AlertTriangle size={16} className="mt-0.5 shrink-0" />
              {error}
            </div>
          )}

          <div className="flex gap-2 pt-2">
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={onClose}
              className="px-4 py-2.5 rounded-lg bg-slate-100 text-sm hover:bg-slate-200"
            >
              Ghairi
            </button>
            <button
              type="submit"
              className="flex-1 inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 px-4 py-2.5 rounded-lg bg-brand-600 text-white text-sm font-semibold hover:bg-brand-700"
            >
              <Save size={16} />
              Tuma Ripoti
            </button>
          </div>
        </form>
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}

// ============================================================
// Ripoti Detail Modal
// ============================================================
function RipotiDetailModal({
  ripoti,
  onClose,
  onThibitisha,
  onKataa,
  onFuta,
}: {
  ripoti: RipotiBidhaa
  onClose: () => void
  onThibitisha: () => void
  onKataa: () => void
  onFuta: () => void
}) {
  const aina = AINA_META[ripoti.aina]
  const hali = HALI_META[ripoti.hali]
  const [confirmFuta, setConfirmFuta] = useState(false)

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-end sm:items-center justify-center p-0 sm:p-4">
      <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-black/40" onClick={onClose} />
      <div className="relative bg-white w-full sm:max-w-md rounded-t-2xl sm:rounded-2xl shadow-2xl max-h-[92vh] overflow-y-auto">
        <div className="sticky top-0 bg-white border-b border-slate-200 px-4 py-3 flex justify-between items-center">
          <div>
            <div className="font-semibold">{ripoti.id}</div>
            <div className="text-xs text-slate-500">{ripoti.tarehe}</div>
          </div>
          <button onClick={onClose} className="p-1.5 rounded-lg hover:bg-slate-100">
            <X size={18} />
          </button>
        </div>

        <div className="p-4 space-y-3 text-sm">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
            <span className={`inline-flex items-center gap-1 px-2 py-1 rounded-md border text-xs font-semibold ${aina.color}`}>
              <aina.Icon size={12} />
              {aina.label}
            </span>
            <span className={`inline-flex items-center gap-1 text-xs font-semibold ${hali.color}`}>
              <hali.Icon size={12} />
              {hali.label}
            </span>
          </div>

          {ripoti.pichaUrl && (
            <div className="rounded-lg overflow-hidden border border-slate-200">
              <img
                src={ripoti.pichaUrl}
                alt={ripoti.jina}
                className="w-full h-40 object-cover"
                onError={(e) => {
                  (e.target as HTMLImageElement).style.display = 'none'
                }}
              />
            </div>
          )}

          <div>
            <div className="text-xs text-slate-500">Bidhaa</div>
            <div className="font-medium">{ripoti.jina}</div>
            <div className="text-xs text-slate-500">
              {ripoti.sku} - {ripoti.kundi}
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs text-slate-600">
            <Store size={12} /> {ripoti.dukaJina}
          </div>

          {ripoti.mtengenezaji && (
            <div>
              <div className="text-xs text-slate-500">Mtengenezaji</div>
              <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
                <Tag size={12} className="text-slate-400" />
                {ripoti.mtengenezaji} - {ripoti.uzito}
              </div>
            </div>
          )}

          <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
            <div>
              <div className="text-xs text-slate-500">Wingi</div>
              <div className="font-semibold">
                {ripoti.wingi} {ripoti.uom}
              </div>
            </div>
            <div>
              <div className="text-xs text-slate-500">Thamani</div>
              <div className="font-bold text-slate-900">
                {formatTsh(ripoti.thamani)}
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>

          {ripoti.expiry && (
            <div>
              <div className="text-xs text-slate-500">Expiry</div>
              <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs">
                <Calendar size={12} className="text-slate-400" />
                {ripoti.expiry}
                {(() => {
                  const siku = sikuZilizobaki(ripoti.expiry)
                  const haliE = haliExpiry(ripoti.expiry)
                  return (
                    <span className={`px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded border text-[10px] font-semibold ${rangiExpiry(haliE)}`}>
                      {siku !== undefined && siku > 0
                        ? `${siku} siku`
                        : siku !== undefined && siku <= 0
                        ? `Imeisha`
                        : ''}
                    </span>
                  )
                })()}
              </div>
            </div>
          )}

          <div>
            <div className="text-xs text-slate-500">Sababu</div>
            <div>{ripoti.sababu}</div>
          </div>

          {ripoti.maelezo && (
            <div>
              <div className="text-xs text-slate-500">Maelezo</div>
              <div className="text-sm">{ripoti.maelezo}</div>
            </div>
          )}

          <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs text-slate-600">
            <User size={12} /> {ripoti.aliyeingiza}
          </div>

          {/* Actions */}
          {ripoti.hali === 'inasubiri' && !confirmFuta && (
            <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2 pt-2 border-t border-slate-200">
              <button
                onClick={onThibitisha}
                className="inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-2.5 rounded-lg bg-emerald-600 text-white text-sm font-semibold hover:bg-emerald-700"
              >
                <CheckCircle2 size={14} />
                Thibitisha
              </button>
              <button
                onClick={onKataa}
                className="inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-2.5 rounded-lg bg-red-600 text-white text-sm font-semibold hover:bg-red-700"
              >
                <XCircle size={14} />
                Kataa
              </button>
            </div>
          )}

          {ripoti.hali !== 'inasubiri' && !confirmFuta && (
            <div className="flex gap-2 pt-2 border-t border-slate-200">
              <button
                onClick={() => setConfirmFuta(true)}
                className="inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-2.5 rounded-lg bg-red-50 text-red-600 text-sm font-semibold hover:bg-red-100"
              >
                <Trash2 size={14} />
                Futa
              </button>
              <button
                onClick={onClose}
                className="px-3 py-2.5 rounded-lg bg-slate-100 text-sm hover:bg-slate-200 sm:ml-auto"
              >
                Funga
              </button>
            </div>
          )}

          {confirmFuta && (
            <div className="space-y-2 pt-2 border-t border-slate-200">
              <div className="flex items-start gap-2 p-3 rounded-lg bg-red-50 text-red-700 text-sm">
                <AlertTriangle size={16} className="mt-0.5 shrink-0" />
                Una uhakika unataka kufuta ripoti hii?
              </div>
              <div className="flex gap-2">
                <button
                  onClick={() => setConfirmFuta(false)}
                  className="px-3 py-2.5 rounded-lg bg-slate-100 text-sm hover:bg-slate-200"
                >
                  Ghairi
                </button>
                <button
                  onClick={onFuta}
                  className="flex-1 px-3 py-2.5 rounded-lg bg-red-600 text-white text-sm font-semibold hover:bg-red-700"
                >
                  Ndiyo, Futa
                </button>
              </div>
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}

// ============================================================
// Stat Card
// ============================================================
type Accent = 'brand' | 'emerald' | 'amber' | 'red'
const ACCENT_BORDER: Record<Accent, string> = {
  brand: 'border-l-brand-500',
  emerald: 'border-l-emerald-500',
  amber: 'border-l-amber-500',
  red: 'border-l-red-500',
}
const ACCENT_TEXT: Record<Accent, string> = {
  brand: 'text-brand-700',
  emerald: 'text-emerald-700',
  amber: 'text-amber-700',
  red: 'text-red-700',
}

function StatCard({
  label,
  value,
  Icon,
  accent,
  sub,
}: {
  label: string
  value: string
  Icon: React.ElementType
  accent: Accent
  sub?: string
}) {
  return (
    <div
      className={`bg-white rounded-xl border border-slate-200 border-l-4 ${ACCENT_BORDER[accent]} p-4`}
    >
      <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
        <Icon size={16} className={ACCENT_TEXT[accent]} />
        <div className="text-xs text-slate-500">{label}</div>
      </div>
      <div className="text-base md:text-lg font-bold mt-2 truncate text-slate-900">
        {value}
      </div>
      {sub && <div className="text-[10px] text-slate-500 truncate">{sub}</div>}
    </div>
  )
}


import { useMemo, useState } from 'react'
import {
  Package, Search, Filter, Store, X, Trash2, CheckCircle2,
  XCircle, Eye, FileText, TrendingDown, Calendar, Tag,
  AlertTriangle, Clock, User, Building2, Layers, RefreshCcw,
} from 'lucide-react'
import { useStoki } from '../../context/StokiContext'
import { useDuka } from '../../context/DukaContext'
import { bidhaaKamili } from '../../data/demo'
import { iconForKundi } from '../../utils/icons'
import { formatTsh } from '../../utils/faida'
import { sikuZilizobaki, haliExpiry, rangiExpiry } from '../../utils/bidhaa'
import type {
  AinaMarekebishoStoki,
  HaliMarekebisho,
  MarekebishoStoki,
} from '../../types'
import { AINA_META, HALI_META, StokiStat } from '../../components/stoki/StokiSubComponents'

const AINA_FILTERS: { key: AinaMarekebishoStoki | 'zote'; label: string }[] = [
  { key: 'zote', label: 'Zote' },
  { key: 'mauzo', label: 'Mauzo' },
  { key: 'uharibifu', label: 'Uharibifu' },
  { key: 'expiry', label: 'Expiry' },
  { key: 'upotevu', label: 'Upotevu' },
  { key: 'kurudisha_msambazaji', label: 'Kurudisha' },
]

export default function MmilikiStoreManage() {
  const { marekebisho, thibitisha, kataa, futa } = useStoki()
  const { maduka } = useDuka()

  const [q, setQ] = useState('')
  const [filterAina, setFilterAina] = useState<AinaMarekebishoStoki | 'zote'>('zote')
  const [filterDuka, setFilterDuka] = useState<string>('zote')
  const [filterHali, setFilterHali] = useState<HaliMarekebisho | 'zote'>('zote')
  const [selected, setSelected] = useState<MarekebishoStoki | null>(null)

  const filtered = useMemo(() => {
    const query = q.toLowerCase().trim()
    return marekebisho.filter((m) => {
      if (filterAina !== 'zote' && m.aina !== filterAina) return false
      if (filterDuka !== 'zote' && m.dukaId !== filterDuka) return false
      if (filterHali !== 'zote' && m.hali !== filterHali) return false
      if (!query) return true
      return (
        m.id.toLowerCase().includes(query) ||
        m.jina.toLowerCase().includes(query) ||
        m.sku.toLowerCase().includes(query) ||
        m.sababu.toLowerCase().includes(query) ||
        m.dukaJina.toLowerCase().includes(query)
      )
    })
  }, [marekebisho, q, filterAina, filterDuka, filterHali])

  const stats = useMemo(() => {
    const zote = marekebisho.length
    const uharibifu = marekebisho.filter((m) => m.aina === 'uharibifu').length
    const expiry = marekebisho.filter((m) => m.aina === 'expiry').length
    const jumlaHasara = marekebisho
      .filter((m) => ['uharibifu', 'expiry', 'upotevu'].includes(m.aina))
      .reduce((s, m) => s + m.thamani, 0)
    const zinazosubiri = marekebisho.filter((m) => m.hali === 'inasubiri').length
    return { zote, uharibifu, expiry, jumlaHasara, zinazosubiri }
  }, [marekebisho])

  return (
    <div className="space-y-5">
      {/* Header */}
      <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-end sm:justify-between gap-3">
        <div>
          <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold flex items-center gap-2">
            <Package size={24} className="text-brand-600" />
            Usimamizi wa Stoki - Maduka Yote
          </h1>
          <p className="text-slate-500 text-sm">
            Thibitisha, kataa, au fuatilia marekebisho ya stoki ya maduka yako yote
          </p>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Stats 4 */}
      <div className="grid grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4 gap-3">
        <StokiStat
          label="Marekebisho Yote"
          value={String(stats.zote)}
          Icon={FileText}
          accent="brand"
          sub={`${stats.zinazosubiri} zinasubiri`}
        />
        <StokiStat
          label="Uharibifu"
          value={String(stats.uharibifu)}
          Icon={AlertTriangle}
          accent="red"
        />
        <StokiStat
          label="Expiry"
          value={String(stats.expiry)}
          Icon={Calendar}
          accent="amber"
        />
        <StokiStat
          label="Jumla ya Hasara"
          value={formatTsh(stats.jumlaHasara)}
          Icon={TrendingDown}
          accent="red"
        />
      </div>

      {/* Filters */}
      <div className="bg-white rounded-xl border border-slate-200 p-3 md:p-4 space-y-3">
        <div className="relative">
          <Search
            size={18}
            className="absolute left-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-slate-400 pointer-events-none"
          />
          <input
            value={q}
            onChange={(e) => setQ(e.target.value)}
            placeholder="Tafuta kwa ID, bidhaa, SKU, sababu, au duka..."
            className="w-full pl-10 pr-4 py-2.5 rounded-lg border border-slate-200 focus:border-brand-500 outline-none text-sm"
          />
        </div>
        <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2 items-center">
          <Filter size={14} className="text-slate-400" />
          <select
            value={filterDuka}
            onChange={(e) => setFilterDuka(e.target.value)}
            className="px-2 py-1.5 rounded-lg border border-slate-200 text-sm"
          >
            <option value="zote">Maduka Yote</option>
            {maduka.map((d) => (
              <option key={d.id} value={d.id}>{d.jina}</option>
            ))}
          </select>
          <select
            value={filterHali}
            onChange={(e) => setFilterHali(e.target.value as HaliMarekebisho | 'zote')}
            className="px-2 py-1.5 rounded-lg border border-slate-200 text-sm"
          >
            <option value="zote">Hali Zote</option>
            <option value="inasubiri">Inasubiri</option>
            <option value="imethibitishwa">Imethibitishwa</option>
            <option value="imekataliwa">Imekataliwa</option>
          </select>
        </div>
        <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-1.5">
          {AINA_FILTERS.map((f) => (
            <button
              key={f.key}
              onClick={() => setFilterAina(f.key)}
              className={`px-2.5 py-1.5 rounded-lg text-xs border transition ${
                filterAina === f.key
                  ? 'bg-brand-600 text-white border-brand-600'
                  : 'bg-white border-slate-200 hover:bg-slate-50'
              }`}
            >
              {f.label}
            </button>
          ))}
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* List */}
      {filtered.length === 0 ? (
        <div className="bg-white rounded-xl border border-slate-200 p-10 text-center">
          <RefreshCcw size={40} className="mx-auto mb-3 text-slate-300" />
          <div className="font-semibold">Hakuna marekebisho</div>
          <p className="text-sm text-slate-500 mt-1">Badilisha kichujio au utafutaji.</p>
        </div>
      ) : (
        <div className="space-y-3">
          {filtered.map((m) => {
            const aina = AINA_META[m.aina]
            const hali = HALI_META[m.hali]
            const bidhaa = bidhaaKamili.find((b) => b.id === m.bidhaaId)
            const Icon = iconForKundi(bidhaa?.iconKey)
            return (
              <div key={m.id} className="bg-white rounded-xl border border-slate-200 p-4">
                <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:justify-between gap-3">
                  <button
                    onClick={() => setSelected(m)}
                    className="flex items-center gap-3 min-w-0 flex-1 text-left"
                  >
                    <div className="w-11 h-11 rounded-lg bg-slate-50 text-slate-700 grid place-items-center shrink-0">
                      <Icon size={20} />
                    </div>
                    <div className="min-w-0">
                      <div className="flex items-center gap-2 flex-wrap">
                        <span className="font-semibold text-sm">{m.id}</span>
                        <span className={`text-[10px] uppercase font-semibold px-2 py-0.5 rounded-md border ${aina.color}`}>
                          {aina.label}
                        </span>
                        <span className={`text-[10px] uppercase font-semibold inline-flex items-center gap-1 ${hali.color}`}>
                          <hali.Icon size={10} />
                          {hali.label}
                        </span>
                      </div>
                      <div className="text-sm font-medium mt-0.5 truncate">{m.jina}</div>
                      <div className="text-xs text-slate-500 truncate">
                        {m.sku} - {m.dukaJina.replace('Smart Hardware - ', '')} - {m.tarehe}
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  </button>
                  <div className="sm:text-right shrink-0">
                    <div className="text-base font-bold text-slate-900">
                      {m.wingi} {m.uom}
                    </div>
                    <div className="text-xs text-slate-500">{formatTsh(m.thamani)}</div>
                  </div>
                </div>

                {m.hali === 'inasubiri' && (
                  <div className="mt-3 pt-3 border-t border-slate-100 flex gap-2">
                    <button
                      onClick={() => thibitisha(m.id)}
                      className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 px-2.5 py-1.5 rounded-md bg-emerald-600 text-white text-xs font-semibold hover:bg-emerald-700"
                    >
                      <CheckCircle2 size={12} />
                      Thibitisha
                    </button>
                    <button
                      onClick={() => setSelected(m)}
                      className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 px-2.5 py-1.5 rounded-md bg-red-600 text-white text-xs font-semibold hover:bg-red-700"
                    >
                      <XCircle size={12} />
                      Kataa
                    </button>
                    <button
                      onClick={() => setSelected(m)}
                      className="ml-auto inline-flex items-center gap-1 px-2.5 py-1.5 rounded-md bg-slate-100 text-slate-700 text-xs font-semibold hover:bg-slate-200"
                    >
                      <Eye size={12} />
                      Ona
                    </button>
                  </div>
                )}
              </div>
            )
          })}
        </div>
      )}

      {selected && (
        <DetailModal
          marekebisho={selected}
          role="mmiliki"
          onClose={() => setSelected(null)}
          onThibitisha={() => {
            thibitisha(selected.id)
            setSelected(null)
          }}
          onKataa={() => {
            kataa(selected.id, 'Imekataliwa na mmiliki')
            setSelected(null)
          }}
          onFuta={() => {
            futa(selected.id)
            setSelected(null)
          }}
        />
      )}
    </div>
  )
}

// ============================================================
// Detail Modal
// ============================================================
function DetailModal({
  marekebisho,
  role,
  onClose,
  onThibitisha,
  onKataa,
  onFuta,
}: {
  marekebisho: MarekebishoStoki
  role: 'mmiliki' | 'muuzaji'
  onClose: () => void
  onThibitisha: () => void
  onKataa: () => void
  onFuta: () => void
}) {
  const aina = AINA_META[marekebisho.aina]
  const hali = HALI_META[marekebisho.hali]
  const [confirmFuta, setConfirmFuta] = useState(false)

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-end sm:items-center justify-center p-0 sm:p-4">
      <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-black/40" onClick={onClose} />
      <div className="relative bg-white w-full sm:max-w-md rounded-t-2xl sm:rounded-2xl shadow-2xl max-h-[92vh] overflow-y-auto">
        <div className="sticky top-0 bg-white border-b border-slate-200 px-4 py-3 flex justify-between items-center">
          <div>
            <div className="font-semibold">{marekebisho.id}</div>
            <div className="text-xs text-slate-500">{marekebisho.tarehe}</div>
          </div>
          <button onClick={onClose} className="p-1.5 rounded-lg hover:bg-slate-100">
            <X size={18} />
          </button>
        </div>

        <div className="p-4 space-y-3 text-sm">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
            <span className={`inline-flex items-center gap-1 px-2 py-1 rounded-md border text-xs font-semibold ${aina.color}`}>
              <aina.Icon size={12} />
              {aina.label}
            </span>
            <span className={`inline-flex items-center gap-1 text-xs font-semibold ${hali.color}`}>
              <hali.Icon size={12} />
              {hali.label}
            </span>
          </div>

          {marekebisho.pichaUrl && (
            <div className="rounded-lg overflow-hidden border border-slate-200">
              <img
                src={marekebisho.pichaUrl}
                alt={marekebisho.jina}
                className="w-full h-40 object-cover"
                onError={(e) => {
                  (e.target as HTMLImageElement).style.display = 'none'
                }}
              />
            </div>
          )}

          <div>
            <div className="text-xs text-slate-500">Bidhaa</div>
            <div className="font-medium">{marekebisho.jina}</div>
            <div className="text-xs text-slate-500">
              {marekebisho.sku} - {marekebisho.kundi}
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs text-slate-600">
            <Store size={12} /> {marekebisho.dukaJina}
          </div>

          {marekebisho.mtengenezaji && (
            <div>
              <div className="text-xs text-slate-500">Mtengenezaji</div>
              <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
                <Tag size={12} className="text-slate-400" />
                {marekebisho.mtengenezaji} - {marekebisho.uzito}
              </div>
            </div>
          )}

          <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
            <div>
              <div className="text-xs text-slate-500">Wingi</div>
              <div className="font-semibold">
                {marekebisho.wingi} {marekebisho.uom}
              </div>
            </div>
            <div>
              <div className="text-xs text-slate-500">Thamani</div>
              <div className="font-bold text-slate-900">
                {formatTsh(marekebisho.thamani)}
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>

          {marekebisho.expiry && (
            <div>
              <div className="text-xs text-slate-500">Expiry</div>
              <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs">
                <Calendar size={12} className="text-slate-400" />
                {marekebisho.expiry}
                {(() => {
                  const siku = sikuZilizobaki(marekebisho.expiry)
                  const haliE = haliExpiry(marekebisho.expiry)
                  return (
                    <span className={`px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded border text-[10px] font-semibold ${rangiExpiry(haliE)}`}>
                      {siku !== undefined && siku > 0
                        ? `${siku} siku`
                        : siku !== undefined && siku <= 0
                        ? `Imeisha`
                        : ''}
                    </span>
                  )
                })()}
              </div>
            </div>
          )}

          <div>
            <div className="text-xs text-slate-500">Sababu</div>
            <div>{marekebisho.sababu}</div>
          </div>

          {marekebisho.maelezo && (
            <div>
              <div className="text-xs text-slate-500">Maelezo</div>
              <div className="text-sm">{marekebisho.maelezo}</div>
            </div>
          )}

          <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs text-slate-600">
            <User size={12} /> {marekebisho.aliyeingiza}
          </div>

          {role === 'mmiliki' && marekebisho.hali === 'inasubiri' && !confirmFuta && (
            <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2 pt-2 border-t border-slate-200">
              <button
                onClick={onThibitisha}
                className="inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-2.5 rounded-lg bg-emerald-600 text-white text-sm font-semibold hover:bg-emerald-700"
              >
                <CheckCircle2 size={14} />
                Thibitisha
              </button>
              <button
                onClick={onKataa}
                className="inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-2.5 rounded-lg bg-red-600 text-white text-sm font-semibold hover:bg-red-700"
              >
                <XCircle size={14} />
                Kataa
              </button>
            </div>
          )}

          {role === 'mmiliki' && marekebisho.hali !== 'inasubiri' && !confirmFuta && (
            <div className="flex gap-2 pt-2 border-t border-slate-200">
              <button
                onClick={() => setConfirmFuta(true)}
                className="inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-2.5 rounded-lg bg-red-50 text-red-600 text-sm font-semibold hover:bg-red-100"
              >
                <Trash2 size={14} />
                Futa
              </button>
              <button
                onClick={onClose}
                className="px-3 py-2.5 rounded-lg bg-slate-100 text-sm hover:bg-slate-200 sm:ml-auto"
              >
                Funga
              </button>
            </div>
          )}

          {role === 'muuzaji' && !confirmFuta && (
            <div className="flex gap-2 pt-2 border-t border-slate-200">
              <button
                onClick={onClose}
                className="px-3 py-2.5 rounded-lg bg-slate-100 text-sm hover:bg-slate-200 sm:ml-auto"
              >
                Funga
              </button>
            </div>
          )}

          {confirmFuta && (
            <div className="space-y-2 pt-2 border-t border-slate-200">
              <div className="flex items-start gap-2 p-3 rounded-lg bg-red-50 text-red-700 text-sm">
                <AlertTriangle size={16} className="mt-0.5 shrink-0" />
                Una uhakika unataka kufuta marekebisho haya?
              </div>
              <div className="flex gap-2">
                <button
                  onClick={() => setConfirmFuta(false)}
                  className="px-3 py-2.5 rounded-lg bg-slate-100 text-sm hover:bg-slate-200"
                >
                  Ghairi
                </button>
                <button
                  onClick={onFuta}
                  className="flex-1 px-3 py-2.5 rounded-lg bg-red-600 text-white text-sm font-semibold hover:bg-red-700"
                >
                  Ndiyo, Futa
                </button>
              </div>
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}


import { useMemo, useState } from 'react'
import {
  Users, UserPlus, Search, Phone, Store, Edit, X, Save,
  CheckCircle2, XCircle, Shield, Briefcase, Package,
  Trash2, AlertTriangle, Crown, Filter,
} from 'lucide-react'
import { useDuka } from '../../context/DukaContext'
import type { MtumiajiKamili } from '../../types'

// ============================================================
// Nafasi na rangi zake
// ============================================================
type Nafasi = 'muuzaji' | 'mhazini' | 'admin' | 'mmiliki'

const NAFASI_META: Record<
  Nafasi,
  { label: string; color: string; Icon: React.ElementType }
> = {
  mmiliki: { label: 'Mmiliki', color: 'bg-purple-50 text-purple-700 border-purple-200', Icon: Crown },
  admin: { label: 'Admin', color: 'bg-brand-50 text-brand-700 border-brand-200', Icon: Shield },
  muuzaji: { label: 'Muuzaji', color: 'bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-700 border-emerald-200', Icon: Briefcase },
  mhazini: { label: 'Mhazini', color: 'bg-amber-50 text-amber-700 border-amber-200', Icon: Package },
}

const NAFASI_ZOTE: Nafasi[] = ['mmiliki', 'admin', 'muuzaji', 'mhazini']

export default function MmilikiWafanyakazi() {
  const { wafanyakazi, maduka, ongezaMtumiaji } = useDuka()
  const [q, setQ] = useState('')
  const [filterDuka, setFilterDuka] = useState<string>('zote')
  const [filterNafasi, setFilterNafasi] = useState<Nafasi | 'zote'>('zote')
  const [ongezaOpen, setOngezaOpen] = useState(false)
  const [selected, setSelected] = useState<MtumiajiKamili | null>(null)

  const filtered = useMemo(() => {
    const query = q.toLowerCase().trim()
    return wafanyakazi.filter((w) => {
      if (filterDuka !== 'zote' && w.dukaId !== filterDuka) return false
      if (filterNafasi !== 'zote' && w.role !== filterNafasi) return false
      if (!query) return true
      return (
        w.jina.toLowerCase().includes(query) ||
        w.simu.includes(query) ||
        w.role.includes(query)
      )
    })
  }, [wafanyakazi, q, filterDuka, filterNafasi])

  const stats = useMemo(() => {
    const zote = wafanyakazi.length
    const active = wafanyakazi.filter((w) => w.active).length
    const hai = maduka.filter((d) => d.hali === 'hai').length
    const wasioNaDuka = wafanyakazi.filter((w) => !w.dukaId).length
    return { zote, active, hai, wasioNaDuka }
  }, [wafanyakazi, maduka])

  // Wafanyakazi kwa kila duka (kwa muhtasari)
  const kwaDuka = useMemo(() => {
    return maduka.map((d) => ({
      duka: d,
      wafanyakazi: wafanyakazi.filter((w) => w.dukaId === d.id),
      msimamizi: wafanyakazi.find((w) => w.id === d.msimamiziId),
    }))
  }, [maduka, wafanyakazi])

  return (
    <div className="space-y-5">
      <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-end sm:justify-between gap-3">
        <div>
          <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold">Wafanyakazi</h1>
          <p className="text-slate-500 text-sm">
            Duka moja linaweza kuwa na wafanyakazi wengi
          </p>
        </div>
        <button
          onClick={() => setOngezaOpen(true)}
          className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-4 py-2 bg-brand-600 text-white rounded-lg text-sm font-semibold hover:bg-brand-700 self-start sm:self-auto"
        >
          <UserPlus size={16} />
          Ongeza Mtumiaji
        </button>
      </div>

      {/* Stats */}
      <div className="grid grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4 gap-3">
        <StatCard label="Wafanyakazi Wote" value={String(stats.zote)} Icon={Users} tone="brand" />
        <StatCard label="Wanaofanya Kazi" value={String(stats.active)} Icon={CheckCircle2} tone="emerald" />
        <StatCard label="Maduka Hai" value={String(stats.hai)} Icon={Store} tone="brand" />
        <StatCard label="Wasio na Duka" value={String(stats.wasioNaDuka)} Icon={AlertTriangle} tone="amber" />
      </div>

      {/* Muhtasari kwa kila duka */}
      <div className="space-y-2">
        <h2 className="text-sm font-semibold text-slate-700">Wafanyakazi kwa Duka</h2>
        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 xl:grid-cols-3 gap-3">
          {kwaDuka.map(({ duka, wafanyakazi: wafanyakaziWaDuka, msimamizi }) => (
            <div key={duka.id} className="bg-white rounded-xl border border-slate-200 p-4">
              <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2">
                <div className="flex items-center gap-2 min-w-0">
                  <div className="w-9 h-9 rounded-lg bg-brand-50 text-brand-600 grid place-items-center shrink-0">
                    <Store size={16} />
                  </div>
                  <div className="min-w-0">
                    <div className="font-semibold text-sm truncate">{duka.jina}</div>
                    <div className="text-xs text-slate-500 truncate">{duka.mahali}</div>
                  </div>
                </div>
                <span className="text-xs font-bold text-brand-700 bg-brand-50 px-2 py-0.5 rounded-md shrink-0">
                  {wafanyakaziWaDuka.length}
                </span>
              </div>

              {msimamizi ? (
                <div className="mt-2 flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs">
                  <Crown size={12} className="text-amber-500" />
                  <span className="text-slate-600">Msimamizi:</span>
                  <span className="font-semibold text-slate-800">{msimamizi.jina}</span>
                </div>
              ) : (
                <div className="mt-2 flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs text-amber-700">
                  <AlertTriangle size={12} />
                  Hakuna msimamizi
                </div>
              )}

              {wafanyakaziWaDuka.length > 0 && (
                <div className="mt-2 flex flex-wrap gap-1">
                  {wafanyakaziWaDuka.slice(0, 4).map((w) => {
                    const meta = NAFASI_META[w.role]
                    return (
                      <span
                        key={w.id}
                        className={`inline-flex items-center gap-1 text-[10px] px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded-md border ${meta.color}`}
                      >
                        <meta.Icon size={8} />
                        {w.jina.split(' ')[0]}
                      </span>
                    )
                  })}
                  {wafanyakaziWaDuka.length > 4 && (
                    <span className="text-[10px] text-slate-500 px-1.5 py-0.5">
                      +{wafanyakaziWaDuka.length - 4}
                    </span>
                  )}
                </div>
              )}
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Filters */}
      <div className="bg-white rounded-xl border border-slate-200 p-3 md:p-4 space-y-3">
        <div className="relative">
          <Search
            size={18}
            className="absolute left-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-slate-400 pointer-events-none"
          />
          <input
            value={q}
            onChange={(e) => setQ(e.target.value)}
            placeholder="Tafuta kwa jina, simu, au nafasi..."
            className="w-full pl-10 pr-4 py-2.5 rounded-lg border border-slate-200 focus:border-brand-500 outline-none text-sm"
          />
        </div>
        <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2 items-center">
          <Filter size={14} className="text-slate-400" />
          <select
            value={filterDuka}
            onChange={(e) => setFilterDuka(e.target.value)}
            className="px-2 py-1.5 rounded-lg border border-slate-200 text-sm"
          >
            <option value="zote">Duka zote</option>
            {maduka.map((d) => (
              <option key={d.id} value={d.id}>
                {d.jina}
              </option>
            ))}
          </select>
          <select
            value={filterNafasi}
            onChange={(e) => setFilterNafasi(e.target.value as Nafasi | 'zote')}
            className="px-2 py-1.5 rounded-lg border border-slate-200 text-sm"
          >
            <option value="zote">Nafasi zote</option>
            {NAFASI_ZOTE.map((n) => (
              <option key={n} value={n}>
                {NAFASI_META[n].label}
              </option>
            ))}
          </select>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Table */}
      <div className="bg-white rounded-xl border border-slate-200 overflow-hidden">
        <div className="overflow-x-auto">
          <table className="w-full text-sm min-w-[760px]">
            <thead className="bg-slate-50 text-left text-slate-500">
              <tr>
                <th className="p-3">Jina</th>
                <th className="p-3">Nafasi</th>
                <th className="p-3">Duka</th>
                <th className="p-3">Simu</th>
                <th className="p-3">Hali</th>
                <th className="p-3"></th>
              </tr>
            </thead>
            <tbody className="divide-y divide-slate-100">
              {filtered.length === 0 ? (
                <tr>
                  <td colSpan={6} className="p-10 text-center">
                    <Users size={32} className="mx-auto mb-2 text-slate-300" />
                    <div className="text-sm text-slate-500">
                      Hakuna wafanyakazi wanaolingana na kichujio.
                    </div>
                  </td>
                </tr>
              ) : (
                filtered.map((w) => {
                  const meta = NAFASI_META[w.role]
                  return (
                    <tr key={w.id}>
                      <td className="p-3">
                        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                          <div className="w-8 h-8 rounded-full bg-brand-600 text-white grid place-items-center text-[10px] font-bold shrink-0">
                            {w.jina.split(' ').map((n) => n[0]).slice(0, 2).join('')}
                          </div>
                          <div className="font-medium">{w.jina}</div>
                        </div>
                      </td>
                      <td className="p-3">
                        <span
                          className={`inline-flex items-center gap-1 text-xs px-2 py-0.5 rounded-md border ${meta.color}`}
                        >
                          <meta.Icon size={10} />
                          {meta.label}
                        </span>
                      </td>
                      <td className="p-3">
                        {w.dukaJina ? (
                          <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 text-xs">
                            <Store size={12} className="text-slate-400" />
                            {w.dukaJina}
                          </span>
                        ) : (
                          <span className="text-xs text-slate-400">Hana duka</span>
                        )}
                      </td>
                      <td className="p-3 text-slate-500">
                        <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 text-xs">
                          <Phone size={12} />
                          {w.simu}
                        </span>
                      </td>
                      <td className="p-3">
                        {w.active ? (
                          <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 text-xs text-emerald-700 bg-emerald-50 px-2 py-0.5 rounded-md">
                            <CheckCircle2 size={10} /> Active
                          </span>
                        ) : (
                          <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 text-xs text-red-700 bg-red-50 px-2 py-0.5 rounded-md">
                            <XCircle size={10} /> Imezuiwa
                          </span>
                        )}
                      </td>
                      <td className="p-3 text-right">
                        <button
                          onClick={() => setSelected(w)}
                          className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 text-xs text-brand-600 hover:underline"
                        >
                          <Edit size={12} />
                          Badilisha
                        </button>
                      </td>
                    </tr>
                  )
                })
              )}
            </tbody>
          </table>
        </div>
      </div>

      {ongezaOpen && <OngezaMtumiajiForm onClose={() => setOngezaOpen(false)} onSubmit={ongezaMtumiaji} />}
      {selected && <MtumiajiDetails mtumiaji={selected} onClose={() => setSelected(null)} />}
    </div>
  )
}

// ============================================================
// Fomu ya Kuongeza Mtumiaji
// ============================================================
function OngezaMtumiajiForm({
  onClose,
  onSubmit,
}: {
  onClose: () => void
  onSubmit: ReturnType<typeof useDuka>['ongezaMtumiaji']
}) {
  const { maduka } = useDuka()
  const [jina, setJina] = useState('')
  const [simu, setSimu] = useState('')
  const [role, setRole] = useState<Nafasi>('muuzaji')
  const [dukaId, setDukaId] = useState('')
  const [error, setError] = useState('')

  const handleSubmit = (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault()
    if (!jina.trim()) return setError('Jina linahitajika')
    if (!/^0[67]\d{2}-?\d{3}-?\d{3}$/.test(simu.replace(/\s/g, '')))
      return setError('Namba ya simu si sahihi (mfano: 0754-123-456)')

    setError('')
    const duka = maduka.find((d) => d.id === dukaId)
    onSubmit({
      jina,
      simu,
      role,
      dukaId: dukaId || undefined,
      dukaJina: duka?.jina,
    })
    onClose()
  }

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-end sm:items-center justify-center p-0 sm:p-4">
      <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-black/40" onClick={onClose} />
      <div className="relative bg-white w-full sm:max-w-lg rounded-t-2xl sm:rounded-2xl shadow-2xl max-h-[92vh] overflow-y-auto">
        <div className="sticky top-0 bg-white border-b border-slate-200 px-4 py-3 flex justify-between items-center">
          <div>
            <div className="font-semibold">Ongeza Mtumiaji</div>
            <div className="text-xs text-slate-500">Weka taarifa za mfanyakazi</div>
          </div>
          <button onClick={onClose} className="p-1.5 rounded-lg hover:bg-slate-100">
            <X size={18} />
          </button>
        </div>

        <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="p-4 space-y-3">
          <div>
            <label className="text-sm font-medium">Jina Kamili</label>
            <input
              value={jina}
              onChange={(e) => setJina(e.target.value)}
              placeholder="Mfano: Juma Hassan"
              className="mt-1 w-full px-3 py-2.5 rounded-lg border border-slate-200 focus:border-brand-500 outline-none text-sm"
            />
          </div>
          <div>
            <label className="text-sm font-medium">Namba ya Simu</label>
            <input
              value={simu}
              onChange={(e) => setSimu(e.target.value)}
              placeholder="0754-123-456"
              inputMode="tel"
              className="mt-1 w-full px-3 py-2.5 rounded-lg border border-slate-200 focus:border-brand-500 outline-none text-sm"
            />
          </div>
          <div>
            <label className="text-sm font-medium">Nafasi</label>
            <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2 mt-1">
              {NAFASI_ZOTE.filter((n) => n !== 'mmiliki').map((n) => {
                const meta = NAFASI_META[n]
                return (
                  <button
                    key={n}
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => setRole(n)}
                    className={`inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-2 rounded-lg border text-sm ${
                      role === n
                        ? `${meta.color} font-semibold`
                        : 'border-slate-200 hover:bg-slate-50'
                    }`}
                  >
                    <meta.Icon size={14} />
                    {meta.label}
                  </button>
                )
              })}
            </div>
          </div>
          <div>
            <label className="text-sm font-medium">Duka (hiari)</label>
            <select
              value={dukaId}
              onChange={(e) => setDukaId(e.target.value)}
              className="mt-1 w-full px-3 py-2.5 rounded-lg border border-slate-200 focus:border-brand-500 outline-none text-sm"
            >
              <option value="">-- Hana duka bado --</option>
              {maduka.map((d) => (
                <option key={d.id} value={d.id}>
                  {d.jina}
                </option>
              ))}
            </select>
            <div className="text-xs text-slate-500 mt-1">
              Unaweza kumpanga baadaye kwenye ukurasa wa maduka.
            </div>
          </div>

          {error && (
            <div className="flex items-start gap-2 p-3 rounded-lg bg-red-50 text-red-700 text-sm">
              <AlertTriangle size={16} className="mt-0.5 shrink-0" />
              {error}
            </div>
          )}

          <div className="flex gap-2 pt-2">
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={onClose}
              className="px-4 py-2.5 rounded-lg bg-slate-100 text-sm hover:bg-slate-200"
            >
              Ghairi
            </button>
            <button
              type="submit"
              className="flex-1 inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 px-4 py-2.5 rounded-lg bg-brand-600 text-white text-sm font-semibold hover:bg-brand-700"
            >
              <Save size={16} />
              Hifadhi
            </button>
          </div>
        </form>
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}

// ============================================================
// Maelezo ya Mtumiaji (Edit)
// ============================================================
function MtumiajiDetails({
  mtumiaji,
  onClose,
}: {
  mtumiaji: MtumiajiKamili
  onClose: () => void
}) {
  const { maduka, wafanyakazi } = useDuka()
  const [dukaId, setDukaId] = useState(mtumiaji.dukaId || '')
  const [role, setRole] = useState<Nafasi>(mtumiaji.role)
  const [active, setActive] = useState(mtumiaji.active)

  // Simulizi: kwa sasa hakuna update method kwenye context
  // Hii inaonyesha UI pekee

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-end sm:items-center justify-center p-0 sm:p-4">
      <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-black/40" onClick={onClose} />
      <div className="relative bg-white w-full sm:max-w-md rounded-t-2xl sm:rounded-2xl shadow-2xl max-h-[92vh] overflow-y-auto">
        <div className="sticky top-0 bg-white border-b border-slate-200 px-4 py-3 flex justify-between items-center">
          <div>
            <div className="font-semibold">{mtumiaji.jina}</div>
            <div className="text-xs text-slate-500">{mtumiaji.simu}</div>
          </div>
          <button onClick={onClose} className="p-1.5 rounded-lg hover:bg-slate-100">
            <X size={18} />
          </button>
        </div>

        <div className="p-4 space-y-3">
          <div>
            <label className="text-sm font-medium">Nafasi</label>
            <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2 mt-1">
              {NAFASI_ZOTE.map((n) => {
                const meta = NAFASI_META[n]
                return (
                  <button
                    key={n}
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => setRole(n)}
                    className={`inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-2 rounded-lg border text-sm ${
                      role === n
                        ? `${meta.color} font-semibold`
                        : 'border-slate-200 hover:bg-slate-50'
                    }`}
                  >
                    <meta.Icon size={14} />
                    {meta.label}
                  </button>
                )
              })}
            </div>
          </div>

          <div>
            <label className="text-sm font-medium">Duka</label>
            <select
              value={dukaId}
              onChange={(e) => setDukaId(e.target.value)}
              className="mt-1 w-full px-3 py-2.5 rounded-lg border border-slate-200 focus:border-brand-500 outline-none text-sm"
            >
              <option value="">-- Hana duka --</option>
              {maduka.map((d) => (
                <option key={d.id} value={d.id}>
                  {d.jina}
                </option>
              ))}
            </select>
          </div>

          <div>
            <label className="text-sm font-medium">Hali</label>
            <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2 mt-1">
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setActive(true)}
                className={`inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-2 rounded-lg border text-sm ${
                  active
                    ? 'bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-700 border-emerald-200 font-semibold'
                    : 'border-slate-200 hover:bg-slate-50'
                }`}
              >
                <CheckCircle2 size={14} /> Active
              </button>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setActive(false)}
                className={`inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-2 rounded-lg border text-sm ${
                  !active
                    ? 'bg-red-50 text-red-700 border-red-200 font-semibold'
                    : 'border-slate-200 hover:bg-slate-50'
                }`}
              >
                <XCircle size={14} /> Imezuiwa
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="pt-2 text-xs text-slate-500">
            Ilisajiliwa: {mtumiaji.tareheSajili}
          </div>

          <div className="flex gap-2 pt-2">
            <button
              onClick={onClose}
              className="px-4 py-2.5 rounded-lg bg-slate-100 text-sm hover:bg-slate-200"
            >
              Ghairi
            </button>
            <button
              onClick={onClose}
              className="flex-1 inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 px-4 py-2.5 rounded-lg bg-brand-600 text-white text-sm font-semibold hover:bg-brand-700"
            >
              <Save size={16} />
              Hifadhi
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}

// ============================================================
// Stat Card
// ============================================================
type Tone = 'brand' | 'emerald' | 'amber' | 'red'
const TONE: Record<Tone, string> = {
  brand: 'bg-brand-50 text-brand-700',
  emerald: 'bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-700',
  amber: 'bg-amber-50 text-amber-700',
  red: 'bg-red-50 text-red-700',
}

function StatCard({
  label,
  value,
  Icon,
  tone,
}: {
  label: string
  value: string
  Icon: React.ElementType
  tone: Tone
}) {
  return (
    <div className="bg-white rounded-xl border border-slate-200 p-4">
      <div className={`inline-flex w-9 h-9 rounded-lg items-center justify-center ${TONE[tone]}`}>
        <Icon size={16} />
      </div>
      <div className="text-xs text-slate-500 mt-2">{label}</div>
      <div className="text-base md:text-lg font-bold mt-0.5 truncate">{value}</div>
    </div>
  )
}

import { useMemo, useState } from 'react'
import {
  Users, Search, Filter, Phone, MapPin, Wallet, Store,
  TrendingUp, User, Building2, Wrench, Hammer, Eye,
  ArrowRight, X, ShoppingBag, Receipt, AlertTriangle,
  CheckCircle2, Download, Mail,
} from 'lucide-react'
import { watejaKamili } from '../../data/demo'
import { useDuka } from '../../context/DukaContext'
import type { AinaMteja, MtejaKamili } from '../../types'

const AINA_META: Record<
  AinaMteja,
  { label: string; color: string; Icon: React.ElementType }
> = {
  fundi: { label: 'Fundi', color: 'bg-amber-50 text-amber-700 border-amber-200', Icon: Wrench },
  mjenzi: { label: 'Mjenzi', color: 'bg-brand-50 text-brand-700 border-brand-200', Icon: Hammer },
  kampuni: { label: 'Kampuni', color: 'bg-purple-50 text-purple-700 border-purple-200', Icon: Building2 },
  mteja: { label: 'Mteja', color: 'bg-slate-50 text-slate-700 border-slate-200', Icon: User },
}

const AINA_FILTERS: { key: AinaMteja | 'zote'; label: string }[] = [
  { key: 'zote', label: 'Zote' },
  { key: 'fundi', label: 'Fundi' },
  { key: 'mjenzi', label: 'Mjenzi' },
  { key: 'kampuni', label: 'Kampuni' },
  { key: 'mteja', label: 'Mteja' },
]

export default function MmilikiWateja() {
  const { maduka } = useDuka()
  const [q, setQ] = useState('')
  const [filterAina, setFilterAina] = useState<AinaMteja | 'zote'>('zote')
  const [filterDuka, setFilterDuka] = useState<string>('zote')
  const [showOnlyDeni, setShowOnlyDeni] = useState(false)
  const [selected, setSelected] = useState<MtejaKamili | null>(null)

  const filtered = useMemo(() => {
    const query = q.toLowerCase().trim()
    return watejaKamili.filter((m) => {
      if (filterAina !== 'zote' && m.aina !== filterAina) return false
      if (filterDuka !== 'zote' && m.dukaId !== filterDuka) return false
      if (showOnlyDeni && m.deni <= 0) return false
      if (!query) return true
      return (
        m.jina.toLowerCase().includes(query) ||
        m.simu.includes(query) ||
        (m.mahali?.toLowerCase().includes(query) ?? false) ||
        (m.tin?.includes(query) ?? false)
      )
    })
  }, [q, filterAina, filterDuka, showOnlyDeni])

  const stats = useMemo(() => {
    const zote = watejaKamili.length
    const wenyeDeni = watejaKamili.filter((m) => m.deni > 0).length
    const jumlaMadeni = watejaKamili.reduce((s, m) => s + m.deni, 0)
    const jumlaManunuzi = watejaKamili.reduce((s, m) => s + m.jumlaManunuzi, 0)
    const wapya = watejaKamili.filter((m) => m.tareheSajili >= '2025-08-01').length
    return { zote, wenyeDeni, jumlaMadeni, jumlaManunuzi, wapya }
  }, [])

  return (
    <div className="space-y-5">
      {/* Header */}
      <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-end sm:justify-between gap-3">
        <div>
          <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold flex items-center gap-2">
            <Users size={24} className="text-brand-600" />
            Wateja - Maduka Yote
          </h1>
          <p className="text-slate-500 text-sm">
            Wateja wote wa maduka yako - madeni, manunuzi, na historia
          </p>
        </div>
        <button className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-4 py-2 bg-slate-100 rounded-lg text-sm hover:bg-slate-200 self-start sm:self-auto">
          <Download size={16} />
          Pakua Orodha
        </button>
      </div>

      {/* Stats */}
      <div className="grid grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4 gap-3">
        <StatCard
          label="Wateja Wote"
          value={String(stats.zote)}
          Icon={Users}
          tone="brand"
          sub={`${stats.wapya} wapya mwaka huu`}
        />
        <StatCard
          label="Wenye Deni"
          value={String(stats.wenyeDeni)}
          Icon={AlertTriangle}
          tone="amber"
        />
        <StatCard
          label="Jumla ya Madeni"
          value={`Tsh ${stats.jumlaMadeni.toLocaleString()}`}
          Icon={Wallet}
          tone="red"
        />
        <StatCard
          label="Jumla ya Manunuzi"
          value={`Tsh ${stats.jumlaManunuzi.toLocaleString()}`}
          Icon={TrendingUp}
          tone="emerald"
        />
      </div>

      {/* Filters */}
      <div className="bg-white rounded-xl border border-slate-200 p-3 md:p-4 space-y-3">
        <div className="relative">
          <Search
            size={18}
            className="absolute left-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-slate-400 pointer-events-none"
          />
          <input
            value={q}
            onChange={(e) => setQ(e.target.value)}
            placeholder="Tafuta kwa jina, simu, mahali, au TIN..."
            className="w-full pl-10 pr-4 py-2.5 rounded-lg border border-slate-200 focus:border-brand-500 outline-none text-sm"
          />
        </div>
        <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2 items-center">
          <Filter size={14} className="text-slate-400" />
          <select
            value={filterDuka}
            onChange={(e) => setFilterDuka(e.target.value)}
            className="px-2 py-1.5 rounded-lg border border-slate-200 text-sm"
          >
            <option value="zote">Maduka Yote</option>
            {maduka.map((d) => (
              <option key={d.id} value={d.id}>{d.jina}</option>
            ))}
          </select>
          <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-1.5">
            {AINA_FILTERS.map((f) => (
              <button
                key={f.key}
                onClick={() => setFilterAina(f.key)}
                className={`px-2.5 py-1.5 rounded-lg text-xs border transition ${
                  filterAina === f.key
                    ? 'bg-brand-600 text-white border-brand-600'
                    : 'bg-white border-slate-200 hover:bg-slate-50'
                }`}
              >
                {f.label}
              </button>
            ))}
          </div>
          <button
            onClick={() => setShowOnlyDeni((v) => !v)}
            className={`ml-auto inline-flex items-center gap-1 px-2.5 py-1.5 rounded-lg text-xs border transition ${
              showOnlyDeni
                ? 'bg-red-600 text-white border-red-600'
                : 'bg-white border-slate-200 hover:bg-slate-50'
            }`}
          >
            <AlertTriangle size={12} />
            Wenye Deni ({stats.wenyeDeni})
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Wateja List */}
      {filtered.length === 0 ? (
        <div className="bg-white rounded-xl border border-slate-200 p-10 text-center">
          <Users size={40} className="mx-auto mb-3 text-slate-300" />
          <div className="font-semibold">Hakuna wateja</div>
          <p className="text-sm text-slate-500 mt-1">Badilisha kichujio au utafutaji.</p>
        </div>
      ) : (
        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 xl:grid-cols-3 gap-3 md:gap-4">
          {filtered.map((m) => {
            const meta = AINA_META[m.aina]
            return (
              <div
                key={m.id}
                className="bg-white rounded-xl border border-slate-200 p-4 hover:border-brand-500 transition"
              >
                <div className="flex items-start gap-3">
                  <div className="w-11 h-11 rounded-full bg-brand-600 text-white grid place-items-center text-sm font-bold shrink-0">
                    {m.jina.split(' ').map((n) => n[0]).slice(0, 2).join('')}
                  </div>
                  <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-2 flex-wrap">
                      <span className="font-semibold text-sm truncate">{m.jina}</span>
                      <span className={`text-[10px] uppercase font-semibold px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded border ${meta.color}`}>
                        {meta.label}
                      </span>
                    </div>
                    <div className="text-xs text-slate-500 flex items-center gap-1 mt-0.5">
                      <Phone size={10} /> {m.simu}
                    </div>
                    {m.mahali && (
                      <div className="text-xs text-slate-500 flex items-center gap-1">
                        <MapPin size={10} /> {m.mahali}
                      </div>
                    )}
                  </div>
                </div>

                {m.dukaJina && (
                  <div className="mt-2 flex items-center gap-1 text-xs text-slate-600">
                    <Store size={10} />
                    {m.dukaJina.replace('Smart Hardware - ', '')}
                  </div>
                )}

                <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2 mt-3 text-xs">
                  <div>
                    <div className="text-slate-500">Manunuzi</div>
                    <div className="font-bold text-brand-700 truncate">
                      Tsh {m.jumlaManunuzi.toLocaleString()}
                    </div>
                  </div>
                  <div>
                    <div className="text-slate-500">Deni</div>
                    <div className={`font-bold truncate ${m.deni > 0 ? 'text-red-600' : 'text-emerald-600'}`}>
                      Tsh {m.deni.toLocaleString()}
                    </div>
                  </div>
                </div>

                <div className="mt-2 flex items-center justify-between text-xs text-slate-500">
                  <span>Orders: {m.jumlaOrders ?? 0}</span>
                  <span>Mwisho: {m.mwishoKununua ?? '-'}</span>
                </div>

                <button
                  onClick={() => setSelected(m)}
                  className="mt-3 w-full inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-2 rounded-lg bg-slate-100 hover:bg-slate-200 text-sm font-medium"
                >
                  <Eye size={14} />
                  Ona Maelezo
                </button>
              </div>
            )
          })}
        </div>
      )}

      {selected && (
        <MtejaDetailModal
          mteja={selected}
          onClose={() => setSelected(null)}
        />
      )}
    </div>
  )
}

// ============================================================
// Mteja Detail Modal
// ============================================================
function MtejaDetailModal({
  mteja,
  onClose,
}: {
  mteja: MtejaKamili
  onClose: () => void
}) {
  const meta = AINA_META[mteja.aina]

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-end sm:items-center justify-center p-0 sm:p-4">
      <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-black/40" onClick={onClose} />
      <div className="relative bg-white w-full sm:max-w-lg rounded-t-2xl sm:rounded-2xl shadow-2xl max-h-[92vh] overflow-y-auto">
        <div className="sticky top-0 bg-white border-b border-slate-200 px-4 py-3 flex justify-between items-center">
          <div>
            <div className="font-semibold">{mteja.jina}</div>
            <div className="text-xs text-slate-500">{mteja.simu}</div>
          </div>
          <button onClick={onClose} className="p-1.5 rounded-lg hover:bg-slate-100">
            <X size={18} />
          </button>
        </div>

        <div className="p-4 space-y-4">
          {/* Header ya mteja */}
          <div className="flex items-center gap-3 p-3 rounded-lg bg-slate-50">
            <div className="w-12 h-12 rounded-full bg-brand-600 text-white grid place-items-center text-sm font-bold shrink-0">
              {mteja.jina.split(' ').map((n) => n[0]).slice(0, 2).join('')}
            </div>
            <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2 flex-wrap">
                <span className="font-semibold text-sm truncate">{mteja.jina}</span>
                <span className={`text-[10px] uppercase font-semibold px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded border ${meta.color}`}>
                  {meta.label}
                </span>
              </div>
              <div className="text-xs text-slate-500 flex items-center gap-1 mt-0.5">
                <Phone size={10} /> {mteja.simu}
              </div>
              {mteja.mahali && (
                <div className="text-xs text-slate-500 flex items-center gap-1">
                  <MapPin size={10} /> {mteja.mahali}
                </div>
              )}
              {mteja.tin && (
                <div className="text-xs text-slate-500 flex items-center gap-1">
                  <Receipt size={10} /> TIN: {mteja.tin}
                </div>
              )}
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Stats */}
          <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2">
            <div className="p-3 rounded-lg bg-brand-50">
              <div className="text-xs text-slate-600">Jumla ya Manunuzi</div>
              <div className="text-lg font-bold text-brand-700 truncate">
                Tsh {mteja.jumlaManunuzi.toLocaleString()}
              </div>
            </div>
            <div className={`p-3 rounded-lg ${mteja.deni > 0 ? 'bg-red-50' : 'bg-emerald-50'}`}>
              <div className="text-xs text-slate-600">Deni</div>
              <div className={`text-lg font-bold truncate ${mteja.deni > 0 ? 'text-red-700' : 'text-emerald-700'}`}>
                Tsh {mteja.deni.toLocaleString()}
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Maelezo */}
          <div className="space-y-2 text-sm">
            <div className="flex justify-between p-2.5 rounded-lg bg-slate-50">
              <span className="text-slate-600">Duka</span>
              <span className="font-semibold">
                {mteja.dukaJina?.replace('Smart Hardware - ', '') ?? '-'}
              </span>
            </div>
            <div className="flex justify-between p-2.5 rounded-lg bg-slate-50">
              <span className="text-slate-600">Orders</span>
              <span className="font-semibold">{mteja.jumlaOrders ?? 0}</span>
            </div>
            <div className="flex justify-between p-2.5 rounded-lg bg-slate-50">
              <span className="text-slate-600">Tarehe ya Kusajiliwa</span>
              <span className="font-semibold">{mteja.tareheSajili}</span>
            </div>
            <div className="flex justify-between p-2.5 rounded-lg bg-slate-50">
              <span className="text-slate-600">Mwisho Kununua</span>
              <span className="font-semibold">{mteja.mwishoKununua ?? '-'}</span>
            </div>
            <div className="flex justify-between p-2.5 rounded-lg bg-slate-50">
              <span className="text-slate-600">Hali</span>
              {mteja.active ? (
                <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 text-xs text-emerald-700 bg-emerald-50 px-2 py-0.5 rounded-md font-semibold">
                  <CheckCircle2 size={10} /> Active
                </span>
              ) : (
                <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 text-xs text-red-700 bg-red-50 px-2 py-0.5 rounded-md font-semibold">
                  <AlertTriangle size={10} /> Imezuiwa
                </span>
              )}
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Actions */}
          <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row gap-2 pt-2 border-t border-slate-200">
            <button className="inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 px-4 py-2.5 rounded-lg bg-brand-600 text-white text-sm font-semibold hover:bg-brand-700">
              <ShoppingBag size={14} />
              Ona Orders
            </button>
            <button className="inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 px-4 py-2.5 rounded-lg bg-slate-100 text-sm hover:bg-slate-200">
              <Phone size={14} />
              Piga Simu
            </button>
            <button
              onClick={onClose}
              className="px-4 py-2.5 rounded-lg bg-slate-100 text-sm hover:bg-slate-200 sm:ml-auto"
            >
              Funga
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}

// ============================================================
// Stat Card
// ============================================================
type Tone = 'brand' | 'emerald' | 'amber' | 'red'
const TONE: Record<Tone, string> = {
  brand: 'bg-brand-50 text-brand-700',
  emerald: 'bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-700',
  amber: 'bg-amber-50 text-amber-700',
  red: 'bg-red-50 text-red-700',
}

function StatCard({
  label,
  value,
  Icon,
  tone,
  sub,
}: {
  label: string
  value: string
  Icon: React.ElementType
  tone: Tone
  sub?: string
}) {
  return (
    <div className="bg-white rounded-xl border border-slate-200 p-4">
      <div className={`inline-flex w-9 h-9 rounded-lg items-center justify-center ${TONE[tone]}`}>
        <Icon size={16} />
      </div>
      <div className="text-xs text-slate-500 mt-2">{label}</div>
      <div className="text-base md:text-lg font-bold mt-0.5 truncate">{value}</div>
      {sub && <div className="text-[10px] text-slate-500 truncate">{sub}</div>}
    </div>
  )
}

import { useMemo, useState } from 'react'
import {
  HandCoins, Calendar, Wallet, CheckCircle2, Clock,
  AlertTriangle, Plus, X, Save, Receipt, Filter,
  TrendingUp, ArrowRight, FileText, PiggyBank,
} from 'lucide-react'
import { useMauzo } from '../../context/MauzoContext'
import { useZaka } from '../../context/ZakaContext'
import {
  hesabuZakaKwaKipindi,
  jumlaZaka,
  chujaKwaKipindi,
} from '../../utils/zaka'
import { formatTsh } from '../../utils/faida'
import type { AinaKipindi, MalipoZaka } from '../../types'

const KIPINDI: { key: AinaKipindi; label: string }[] = [
  { key: 'siku', label: 'Siku' },
  { key: 'wiki', label: 'Wiki' },
  { key: 'mwezi', label: 'Mwezi' },
  { key: 'mwaka', label: 'Mwaka' },
]

export default function MmilikiZaka() {
  const { mauzo, opex } = useMauzo()
  const { malipo, rekodiMalipo, futaMalipo } = useZaka()
  const [kipindi, setKipindi] = useState<AinaKipindi>('wiki')
  const [rekodiOpen, setRekodiOpen] = useState(false)

  const vipindiVote = useMemo(
    () => hesabuZakaKwaKipindi(mauzo, opex),
    [mauzo, opex]
  )

  const vipindiVilivyochujwa = useMemo(
    () => chujaKwaKipindi(vipindiVote, kipindi),
    [vipindiVote, kipindi]
  )

  const jumla = useMemo(() => jumlaZaka(vipindiVilivyochujwa), [vipindiVilivyochujwa])

  const malipoYote = useMemo(
    () =>
      malipo.reduce(
        (a, m) => ({
          zaka: a.zaka + (m.aina === 'zaka' ? m.kiasi : 0),
          sadaka: a.sadaka + (m.aina === 'sadaka' ? m.kiasi : 0),
          jumla: a.jumla + m.kiasi,
        }),
        { zaka: 0, sadaka: 0, jumla: 0 }
      ),
    [malipo]
  )

  const zakaIliyodaiwa = jumla.zaka - malipoYote.zaka
  const sadakaIliyodaiwa = jumla.sadaka - malipoYote.sadaka
  const jumlaIliyodaiwa = zakaIliyodaiwa + sadakaIliyodaiwa

  return (
    <div className="space-y-5">
      {/* ============================================================ */}
      {/* HEADER */}
      {/* ============================================================ */}
      <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-end sm:justify-between gap-3">
        <div>
          <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold flex items-center gap-2">
            <HandCoins size={24} className="text-brand-600" />
            Zaka & Sadaka
          </h1>
          <p className="text-slate-500 text-sm">
            Kanuni: 10% Zaka + 10% Sadaka = 20% ya Faida Safi
          </p>
        </div>
        <button
          onClick={() => setRekodiOpen(true)}
          className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-4 py-2 bg-brand-600 text-white rounded-lg text-sm font-semibold hover:bg-brand-700 self-start sm:self-auto"
        >
          <Plus size={16} />
          Rekodi Malipo
        </button>
      </div>

      {/* ============================================================ */}
      {/* KIPINDI */}
      {/* ============================================================ */}
      <div className="bg-white rounded-xl border border-slate-200 p-3 md:p-4">
        <label className="text-xs font-medium text-slate-700 flex items-center gap-1.5">
          <Calendar size={14} />
          Kipindi
        </label>
        <div className="mt-2 flex flex-wrap gap-2">
          {KIPINDI.map((k) => (
            <button
              key={k.key}
              onClick={() => setKipindi(k.key)}
              className={`inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-2 rounded-lg text-sm border transition ${
                kipindi === k.key
                  ? 'bg-brand-600 text-white border-brand-600 font-semibold'
                  : 'bg-white border-slate-200 hover:bg-slate-50'
              }`}
            >
              {k.label}
            </button>
          ))}
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* ============================================================ */}
      {/* HERO CARD - Bila gradient, nyeupe */}
      {/* ============================================================ */}
      <div className="bg-white rounded-xl border border-slate-200 p-5 md:p-6">
        <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-start sm:justify-between gap-4">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-4">
            <div className="w-14 h-14 rounded-2xl bg-brand-50 text-brand-700 grid place-items-center shrink-0">
              <HandCoins size={28} />
            </div>
            <div>
              <div className="text-sm text-slate-500">
                Jumla ya Bwana - {KIPINDI.find((k) => k.key === kipindi)?.label}
              </div>
              <div className="text-3xl md:text-4xl font-bold text-slate-900 mt-1">
                {formatTsh(jumla.jumlaYaMungu)}
              </div>
              <div className="text-xs text-slate-500 mt-1">
                Faida Halisi: {formatTsh(jumla.faida)}
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
          <button
            onClick={() => setRekodiOpen(true)}
