            className="inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 px-4 py-2.5 bg-slate-100 rounded-lg text-sm font-semibold hover:bg-slate-200 self-start sm:self-auto"
          >
            <Plus size={16} />
            Rekodi Malipo
          </button>
        </div>

        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-3 gap-3 mt-5 pt-5 border-t border-slate-200">
          <div>
            <div className="text-xs text-slate-500">Faida Halisi</div>
            <div className="text-lg font-bold text-slate-900">
              {formatTsh(jumla.faida)}
            </div>
          </div>
          <div>
            <div className="text-xs text-slate-500">Zaka (10%)</div>
            <div className="text-lg font-bold text-brand-700">
              {formatTsh(jumla.zaka)}
            </div>
          </div>
          <div>
            <div className="text-xs text-slate-500">Sadaka (10%)</div>
            <div className="text-lg font-bold text-brand-700">
              {formatTsh(jumla.sadaka)}
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="mt-4 pt-4 border-t border-slate-200 flex items-center justify-between">
          <div>
            <div className="text-xs text-slate-500">Salio Lako</div>
            <div className="text-2xl font-bold text-slate-900">
              {formatTsh(jumla.salioLako)}
            </div>
          </div>
          <div className="w-12 h-12 rounded-xl bg-slate-50 text-slate-600 grid place-items-center">
            <Wallet size={22} />
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* ============================================================ */}
      {/* STATS 4 - cards nyeupe na accent border-l */}
      {/* ============================================================ */}
      <div className="grid grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4 gap-3">
        <ZakaStat
          label="Zaka Inayodaiwa"
          value={formatTsh(Math.max(zakaIliyodaiwa, 0))}
          Icon={Clock}
          accent="amber"
        />
        <ZakaStat
          label="Sadaka Inayodaiwa"
          value={formatTsh(Math.max(sadakaIliyodaiwa, 0))}
          Icon={Clock}
          accent="amber"
        />
        <ZakaStat
          label="Zilizolipwa"
          value={formatTsh(malipoYote.jumla)}
          Icon={CheckCircle2}
          accent="emerald"
        />
        <ZakaStat
          label="Jumla Inayodaiwa"
          value={formatTsh(Math.max(jumlaIliyodaiwa, 0))}
          Icon={AlertTriangle}
          accent={jumlaIliyodaiwa > 0 ? 'red' : 'emerald'}
        />
      </div>

      {/* ============================================================ */}
      {/* RIPOTI YA KILA SIKU */}
      {/* ============================================================ */}
      <div className="bg-white rounded-xl border border-slate-200 p-4 md:p-5">
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-3">
          <h2 className="font-semibold flex items-center gap-2">
            <Receipt size={18} className="text-brand-600" />
            Ripoti ya Kila Siku - {KIPINDI.find((k) => k.key === kipindi)?.label}
          </h2>
          <span className="text-xs text-slate-500">
            {vipindiVilivyochujwa.length} siku
          </span>
        </div>

        {vipindiVilivyochujwa.length === 0 ? (
          <div className="text-sm text-slate-500 text-center py-6">
            Hakuna data kwa kipindi hiki
          </div>
        ) : (
          <div className="overflow-x-auto">
            <table className="w-full text-sm min-w-[720px]">
              <thead className="text-left text-slate-500 border-b border-slate-200">
                <tr>
                  <th className="py-2">Tarehe</th>
                  <th className="py-2 text-right">Faida Halisi</th>
                  <th className="py-2 text-right">Zaka 10%</th>
                  <th className="py-2 text-right">Sadaka 10%</th>
                  <th className="py-2 text-right">Jumla ya Mungu</th>
                  <th className="py-2 text-right">Salio Lako</th>
                </tr>
              </thead>
              <tbody>
                {vipindiVilivyochujwa.map((v) => (
                  <tr key={v.tarehe} className="border-b border-slate-100">
                    <td className="py-2 font-medium">{v.tarehe}</td>
                    <td className="py-2 text-right">
                      {v.faidaHalisi.toLocaleString()}
                    </td>
                    <td className="py-2 text-right text-brand-700 font-medium">
                      {v.zaka.toLocaleString()}
                    </td>
                    <td className="py-2 text-right text-brand-700 font-medium">
                      {v.sadaka.toLocaleString()}
                    </td>
                    <td className="py-2 text-right font-semibold">
                      {v.jumlaYaMungu.toLocaleString()}
                    </td>
                    <td
                      className={`py-2 text-right font-semibold ${
                        v.salioLako < 0 ? 'text-red-700' : 'text-emerald-700'
                      }`}
                    >
                      {v.salioLako.toLocaleString()}
                    </td>
                  </tr>
                ))}
              </tbody>
              <tfoot>
                <tr className="border-t-2 border-slate-300">
                  <td className="py-2 font-bold">JUMLA</td>
                  <td className="py-2 text-right font-bold">
                    {jumla.faida.toLocaleString()}
                  </td>
                  <td className="py-2 text-right font-bold text-brand-700">
                    {jumla.zaka.toLocaleString()}
                  </td>
                  <td className="py-2 text-right font-bold text-brand-700">
                    {jumla.sadaka.toLocaleString()}
                  </td>
                  <td className="py-2 text-right font-bold">
                    {jumla.jumlaYaMungu.toLocaleString()}
                  </td>
                  <td
                    className={`py-2 text-right font-bold ${
                      jumla.salioLako < 0 ? 'text-red-700' : 'text-emerald-700'
                    }`}
                  >
                    {jumla.salioLako.toLocaleString()}
                  </td>
                </tr>
              </tfoot>
            </table>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>

      {/* ============================================================ */}
      {/* MALIPO YALIYOFANYIKA */}
      {/* ============================================================ */}
      <div className="bg-white rounded-xl border border-slate-200 p-4 md:p-5">
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-3">
          <h2 className="font-semibold flex items-center gap-2">
            <CheckCircle2 size={18} className="text-emerald-600" />
            Malipo Yaliyofanyika ({malipo.length})
          </h2>
        </div>

        {malipo.length === 0 ? (
          <div className="text-sm text-slate-500 text-center py-6">
            Hakuna malipo yaliyorekodiwa bado. Bofya "Rekodi Malipo".
          </div>
        ) : (
          <div className="space-y-2">
            {malipo.map((m) => (
              <div
                key={m.id}
                className="flex items-center gap-3 p-3 rounded-lg border border-slate-200 hover:bg-slate-50 transition"
              >
                <div className="w-9 h-9 rounded-lg bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-700 grid place-items-center shrink-0">
                  <CheckCircle2 size={16} />
                </div>
                <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-2 flex-wrap">
                    <span className="text-sm font-semibold">
                      {m.aina === 'zaka' ? 'Zaka' : 'Sadaka'}
                    </span>
                    <span className="text-[10px] uppercase font-semibold px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded bg-slate-100 text-slate-600">
                      {m.risiti}
                    </span>
                  </div>
                  <div className="text-xs text-slate-500">
                    {m.tarehe} {m.maelezo ? `- ${m.maelezo}` : ''}
                  </div>
                </div>
                <div className="text-sm font-bold text-slate-900 whitespace-nowrap">
                  {formatTsh(m.kiasi)}
                </div>
                <button
                  onClick={() => futaMalipo(m.id)}
                  className="p-1.5 rounded-md text-red-500 hover:bg-red-50"
                  aria-label="Futa"
                >
                  <X size={14} />
                </button>
              </div>
            ))}
          </div>
        )}
      </div>

      {/* ============================================================ */}
      {/* MUHTASARI WA ZAKA NA SADAKA */}
      {/* ============================================================ */}
      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-3">
        <div className="bg-white rounded-xl border border-slate-200 p-4">
          <h3 className="text-sm font-semibold mb-3 flex items-center gap-2">
            <TrendingUp size={16} className="text-brand-600" />
            Muhtasari wa Zaka
          </h3>
          <div className="space-y-2 text-sm">
            <div className="flex justify-between p-2.5 rounded-lg bg-slate-50">
              <span className="text-slate-600">Zaka Inayodaiwa</span>
              <span className="font-semibold text-red-600">
                {formatTsh(Math.max(zakaIliyodaiwa, 0))}
              </span>
            </div>
            <div className="flex justify-between p-2.5 rounded-lg bg-slate-50">
              <span className="text-slate-600">Zaka Iliyolipwa</span>
              <span className="font-semibold text-emerald-700">
                {formatTsh(malipoYote.zaka)}
              </span>
            </div>
            <div className="flex justify-between p-2.5 rounded-lg border border-slate-200">
              <span className="text-slate-600">Deni la Zaka</span>
              <span className="font-semibold text-slate-900">
                {formatTsh(Math.max(zakaIliyodaiwa, 0))}
              </span>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="bg-white rounded-xl border border-slate-200 p-4">
          <h3 className="text-sm font-semibold mb-3 flex items-center gap-2">
            <HandCoins size={16} className="text-brand-600" />
            Muhtasari wa Sadaka
          </h3>
          <div className="space-y-2 text-sm">
            <div className="flex justify-between p-2.5 rounded-lg bg-slate-50">
              <span className="text-slate-600">Sadaka Inayodaiwa</span>
              <span className="font-semibold text-red-600">
                {formatTsh(Math.max(sadakaIliyodaiwa, 0))}
              </span>
            </div>
            <div className="flex justify-between p-2.5 rounded-lg bg-slate-50">
              <span className="text-slate-600">Sadaka Iliyolipwa</span>
              <span className="font-semibold text-emerald-700">
                {formatTsh(malipoYote.sadaka)}
              </span>
            </div>
            <div className="flex justify-between p-2.5 rounded-lg border border-slate-200">
              <span className="text-slate-600">Deni la Sadaka</span>
              <span className="font-semibold text-slate-900">
                {formatTsh(Math.max(sadakaIliyodaiwa, 0))}
              </span>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      {rekodiOpen && (
        <FomuRekodiMalipo
          onClose={() => setRekodiOpen(false)}
          onSubmit={rekodiMalipo}
          zakaIliyodaiwa={Math.max(zakaIliyodaiwa, 0)}
          sadakaIliyodaiwa={Math.max(sadakaIliyodaiwa, 0)}
        />
      )}
    </div>
  )
}

// ============================================================
// ZakaStat - card nyeupe na accent border-l
// ============================================================
type Accent = 'brand' | 'emerald' | 'amber' | 'red'

const ACCENT_BORDER: Record<Accent, string> = {
  brand: 'border-l-brand-500',
  emerald: 'border-l-emerald-500',
  amber: 'border-l-amber-500',
  red: 'border-l-red-500',
}

const ACCENT_TEXT: Record<Accent, string> = {
  brand: 'text-brand-700',
  emerald: 'text-emerald-700',
  amber: 'text-amber-700',
  red: 'text-red-700',
}

function ZakaStat({
  label,
  value,
  Icon,
  accent,
}: {
  label: string
  value: string
  Icon: React.ElementType
  accent: Accent
}) {
  return (
    <div
      className={`bg-white rounded-xl border border-slate-200 border-l-4 ${ACCENT_BORDER[accent]} p-4`}
    >
      <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
        <Icon size={16} className={ACCENT_TEXT[accent]} />
        <div className="text-xs text-slate-500">{label}</div>
      </div>
      <div className="text-base md:text-lg font-bold mt-2 truncate text-slate-900">
        {value}
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}

// ============================================================
// Fomu ya Kurekodi Malipo
// ============================================================
function FomuRekodiMalipo({
  onClose,
  onSubmit,
  zakaIliyodaiwa,
  sadakaIliyodaiwa,
}: {
  onClose: () => void
  onSubmit: ReturnType<typeof useZaka>['rekodiMalipo']
  zakaIliyodaiwa: number
  sadakaIliyodaiwa: number
}) {
  const [aina, setAina] = useState<'zaka' | 'sadaka'>('zaka')
  const [kiasi, setKiasi] = useState(0)
  const [risiti, setRisiti] = useState('')
  const [maelezo, setMaelezo] = useState('')
  const [tarehe, setTarehe] = useState(new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0])
  const [error, setError] = useState('')

  const handleSubmit = (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault()
    if (kiasi <= 0) return setError('Kiasi lazima kiwe zaidi ya sifuri')
    if (!risiti.trim()) return setError('Namba ya risiti inahitajika')

    setError('')
    onSubmit({
      tarehe,
      kiasi,
      aina,
      risiti: risiti.trim().toUpperCase(),
      maelezo: maelezo.trim() || undefined,
    })
    onClose()
  }

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-end sm:items-center justify-center p-0 sm:p-4">
      <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-black/40" onClick={onClose} />
      <div className="relative bg-white w-full sm:max-w-md rounded-t-2xl sm:rounded-2xl shadow-2xl max-h-[92vh] overflow-y-auto">
        <div className="sticky top-0 bg-white border-b border-slate-200 px-4 py-3 flex justify-between items-center">
          <div>
            <div className="font-semibold">Rekodi Malipo</div>
            <div className="text-xs text-slate-500">Zaka au Sadaka</div>
          </div>
          <button onClick={onClose} className="p-1.5 rounded-lg hover:bg-slate-100">
            <X size={18} />
          </button>
        </div>

        <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="p-4 space-y-3">
          <div>
            <label className="text-sm font-medium">Aina</label>
            <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2 mt-1">
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setAina('zaka')}
                className={`px-3 py-2.5 rounded-lg border text-sm ${
                  aina === 'zaka'
                    ? 'bg-brand-50 text-brand-700 border-brand-500 font-semibold'
                    : 'border-slate-200 hover:bg-slate-50'
                }`}
              >
                Zaka
                <div className="text-[10px] text-slate-500 mt-0.5">
                  Inayodaiwa: {formatTsh(zakaIliyodaiwa)}
                </div>
              </button>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setAina('sadaka')}
                className={`px-3 py-2.5 rounded-lg border text-sm ${
                  aina === 'sadaka'
                    ? 'bg-brand-50 text-brand-700 border-brand-500 font-semibold'
                    : 'border-slate-200 hover:bg-slate-50'
                }`}
              >
                Sadaka
                <div className="text-[10px] text-slate-500 mt-0.5">
                  Inayodaiwa: {formatTsh(sadakaIliyodaiwa)}
                </div>
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div>
            <label className="text-sm font-medium">Kiasi (Tsh)</label>
            <input
              type="number"
              value={kiasi || ''}
              onChange={(e) => setKiasi(Number(e.target.value))}
              placeholder="50000"
              className="mt-1 w-full px-3 py-2.5 rounded-lg border border-slate-200 focus:border-brand-500 outline-none text-sm"
            />
          </div>

          <div>
            <label className="text-sm font-medium">Tarehe</label>
            <input
              type="date"
              value={tarehe}
              onChange={(e) => setTarehe(e.target.value)}
              className="mt-1 w-full px-3 py-2.5 rounded-lg border border-slate-200 focus:border-brand-500 outline-none text-sm"
            />
          </div>

          <div>
            <label className="text-sm font-medium">Namba ya Risiti</label>
            <input
              value={risiti}
              onChange={(e) => setRisiti(e.target.value.toUpperCase())}
              placeholder="RIS-001"
              className="mt-1 w-full px-3 py-2.5 rounded-lg border border-slate-200 focus:border-brand-500 outline-none text-sm font-mono"
            />
          </div>

          <div>
            <label className="text-sm font-medium">Maelezo (hiari)</label>
            <input
              value={maelezo}
              onChange={(e) => setMaelezo(e.target.value)}
              placeholder="Mfano: Zaka ya wiki ya 1 Nov"
              className="mt-1 w-full px-3 py-2.5 rounded-lg border border-slate-200 focus:border-brand-500 outline-none text-sm"
            />
          </div>

          {error && (
            <div className="flex items-start gap-2 p-3 rounded-lg bg-red-50 text-red-700 text-sm">
              <AlertTriangle size={16} className="mt-0.5 shrink-0" />
              {error}
            </div>
          )}

          <div className="flex gap-2 pt-2">
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={onClose}
              className="px-4 py-2.5 rounded-lg bg-slate-100 text-sm hover:bg-slate-200"
            >
              Ghairi
            </button>
            <button
              type="submit"
              className="flex-1 inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 px-4 py-2.5 rounded-lg bg-brand-600 text-white text-sm font-semibold hover:bg-brand-700"
            >
              <Save size={16} />
              Rekodi Malipo
            </button>
          </div>
        </form>
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}

import { useMemo, useState } from 'react'
import {
  PackagePlus, Plus, Trash2, Save, Search, Package,
  AlertTriangle, CheckCircle2, Clock, XCircle, X,
  Layers, List, Store, ChevronDown, Upload, Eye,
} from 'lucide-react'
import { useDuka } from '../../context/DukaContext'
import { useUingizaji } from '../../context/UingizajiContext'
import { iconForKundi } from '../../utils/icons'
import type {
  AinaUingizaji, BidhaaMpya, KundiBidhaa, HaliUingizaji, UingizajiVifaa,
} from '../../types'

const KUNDI: KundiBidhaa[] = [
  'Saruji', 'Nondo', 'Mabati', 'Mbao', 'Rangi', 'Bomba', 'Umeme', 'Zana',
]

const UOM: string[] = ['Mfuko', 'Kg', 'Mita', 'Lita', 'Kipande', 'Dazeni', 'Futi']

const HALI_META: Record<
  HaliUingizaji,
  { label: string; color: string; Icon: React.ElementType }
> = {
  inasubiri: { label: 'Inasubiri', color: 'bg-amber-50 text-amber-700 border-amber-200', Icon: Clock },
  imepokelewa: { label: 'Imepokelewa', color: 'bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-700 border-emerald-200', Icon: CheckCircle2 },
  imekataliwa: { label: 'Imekataliwa', color: 'bg-red-50 text-red-700 border-red-200', Icon: XCircle },
}

type Tab = 'moja' | 'bulk' | 'historia'

interface UingizaVifaaProps {
  role: 'mmiliki' | 'admin'
}

export default function UingizaVifaa({ role }: UingizaVifaaProps) {
  const { maduka } = useDuka()
  const { uingizaji } = useUingizaji()
  const [tab, setTab] = useState<Tab>('moja')
  const [selectedDukaId, setSelectedDukaId] = useState(maduka[0]?.id || '')

  const duka = maduka.find((d) => d.id === selectedDukaId)
  const mtumiaji = role === 'mmiliki' ? 'Moses Kimaro' : 'Grace Mushi'

  return (
    <div className="space-y-5">
      <div>
        <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold flex items-center gap-2">
          <PackagePlus size={24} className="text-brand-600" />
          Ingiza Vifaa
        </h1>
        <p className="text-slate-500 text-sm">
          Ongeza bidhaa moja moja au kwa bulk, kisha muuzaji atathibitisha "Nimepokea"
        </p>
      </div>

      {/* Chagua duka */}
      <div className="bg-white rounded-xl border border-slate-200 p-3 md:p-4">
        <label className="text-xs font-medium text-slate-700 flex items-center gap-1.5">
          <Store size={14} />
          Chagua Duka la Kuingiza
        </label>
        <div className="mt-2 flex flex-wrap gap-2">
          {maduka.map((d) => {
            const active = selectedDukaId === d.id
            return (
              <button
                key={d.id}
                onClick={() => setSelectedDukaId(d.id)}
                className={`inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-2 rounded-lg text-sm border transition ${
                  active
                    ? 'bg-brand-600 text-white border-brand-600 font-semibold'
                    : 'bg-white border-slate-200 hover:bg-slate-50'
                }`}
              >
                <Store size={14} />
                {d.jina}
              </button>
            )
          })}
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Tabs */}
      <div className="flex gap-2 overflow-x-auto">
        <TabButton active={tab === 'moja'} onClick={() => setTab('moja')} Icon={Plus} label="Moja kwa Moja" />
        <TabButton active={tab === 'bulk'} onClick={() => setTab('bulk')} Icon={Layers} label="Bulk (Wengi)" />
        <TabButton active={tab === 'historia'} onClick={() => setTab('historia')} Icon={List} label="Historia" />
      </div>

      {tab === 'moja' && (
        <FomuMoja
          dukaId={selectedDukaId}
          dukaJina={duka?.jina || ''}
          mtumiaji={mtumiaji}
          nafasi={role}
        />
      )}
      {tab === 'bulk' && (
        <FomuBulk
          dukaId={selectedDukaId}
          dukaJina={duka?.jina || ''}
          mtumiaji={mtumiaji}
          nafasi={role}
        />
      )}
      {tab === 'historia' && <Historia uingizaji={uingizaji} />}
    </div>
  )
}

// ============================================================
// Tab button
// ============================================================
function TabButton({
  active, onClick, Icon, label,
}: {
  active: boolean; onClick: () => void; Icon: React.ElementType; label: string
}) {
  return (
    <button
      onClick={onClick}
      className={`inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-2 rounded-lg text-sm border whitespace-nowrap transition ${
        active
          ? 'bg-brand-600 text-white border-brand-600'
          : 'bg-white border-slate-200 hover:bg-slate-50'
      }`}
    >
      <Icon size={14} />
      {label}
    </button>
  )
}

// ============================================================
// Bidhaa moja kwa moja
// ============================================================
function FomuMoja({
  dukaId, dukaJina, mtumiaji, nafasi,
}: {
  dukaId: string; dukaJina: string; mtumiaji: string; nafasi: 'mmiliki' | 'admin'
}) {
  const { ongezaUingizaji } = useUingizaji()
  const [jina, setJina] = useState('')
  const [sku, setSku] = useState('')
  const [kundi, setKundi] = useState<KundiBidhaa>('Saruji')
  const [beiMauzo, setBeiMauzo] = useState(0)
  const [beiUnunuzi, setBeiUnunuzi] = useState(0)
  const [uom, setUom] = useState('Mfuko')
  const [stoki, setStoki] = useState(0)
  const [location, setLocation] = useState('')
  const [expiry, setExpiry] = useState('')
  const [maelezo, setMaelezo] = useState('')
  const [error, setError] = useState('')
  const [success, setSuccess] = useState('')

  const handleSubmit = (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault()
    if (!jina.trim()) return setError('Jina la bidhaa linahitajika')
    if (!sku.trim()) return setError('SKU inahitajika')
    if (beiMauzo <= 0) return setError('Bei ya mauzo lazima iwe zaidi ya sifuri')
    if (beiUnunuzi <= 0) return setError('Bei ya ununuzi lazima iwe zaidi ya sifuri')
    if (stoki <= 0) return setError('Stoki lazima iwe zaidi ya sifuri')

    setError('')
    const bidhaa: BidhaaMpya = {
      jina: jina.trim(),
      sku: sku.trim().toUpperCase(),
      kundi,
      beiMauzo,
      beiUnunuzi,
      uom,
      stoki,
      location: location.trim() || 'Haijawekwa',
      expiry: expiry || undefined,
      iconKey: kundi,
    }
    const u = ongezaUingizaji({
      aina: 'moja',
      dukaId,
      dukaJina,
      bidhaa: [bidhaa],
      aliyeingiza: mtumiaji,
      nafasi,
      maelezo,
    })
    setSuccess(`Bidhaa imeingizwa! ID: ${u.id} - Muuzaji atathibitisha "Nimepokea"`)
    // Reset
    setJina('')
    setSku('')
    setBeiMauzo(0)
    setBeiUnunuzi(0)
    setStoki(0)
    setLocation('')
    setExpiry('')
    setMaelezo('')
    setTimeout(() => setSuccess(''), 5000)
  }

  const Icon = iconForKundi(kundi)

  return (
    <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="bg-white rounded-xl border border-slate-200 p-4 md:p-5 space-y-4">
      <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-sm text-slate-600">
        <Store size={14} />
        Duka: <span className="font-semibold text-slate-800">{dukaJina}</span>
      </div>

      {success && (
        <div className="flex items-start gap-2 p-3 rounded-lg bg-emerald-50 border border-emerald-200 text-emerald-800 text-sm">
          <CheckCircle2 size={16} className="mt-0.5 shrink-0" />
          {success}
        </div>
      )}

      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-3">
        <div className="md:col-span-2">
          <label className="text-sm font-medium">Jina la Bidhaa</label>
          <input
            value={jina}
            onChange={(e) => setJina(e.target.value)}
            placeholder="Mfano: Saruji Dangote 50kg"
            className="mt-1 w-full px-3 py-2.5 rounded-lg border border-slate-200 focus:border-brand-500 outline-none text-sm"
          />
        </div>
        <div>
          <label className="text-sm font-medium">SKU</label>
          <input
            value={sku}
            onChange={(e) => setSku(e.target.value.toUpperCase())}
            placeholder="SAR-DAN-50"
            className="mt-1 w-full px-3 py-2.5 rounded-lg border border-slate-200 focus:border-brand-500 outline-none text-sm font-mono"
          />
        </div>
        <div>
          <label className="text-sm font-medium">Kundi</label>
          <select
            value={kundi}
            onChange={(e) => setKundi(e.target.value as KundiBidhaa)}
            className="mt-1 w-full px-3 py-2.5 rounded-lg border border-slate-200 focus:border-brand-500 outline-none text-sm"
          >
            {KUNDI.map((k) => <option key={k} value={k}>{k}</option>)}
          </select>
        </div>
        <div>
          <label className="text-sm font-medium">Bei ya Mauzo (Tsh)</label>
          <input
            type="number"
            value={beiMauzo || ''}
            onChange={(e) => setBeiMauzo(Number(e.target.value))}
            placeholder="18500"
            className="mt-1 w-full px-3 py-2.5 rounded-lg border border-slate-200 focus:border-brand-500 outline-none text-sm"
          />
        </div>
        <div>
          <label className="text-sm font-medium">Bei ya Ununuzi (Tsh)</label>
          <input
            type="number"
            value={beiUnunuzi || ''}
            onChange={(e) => setBeiUnunuzi(Number(e.target.value))}
            placeholder="16000"
            className="mt-1 w-full px-3 py-2.5 rounded-lg border border-slate-200 focus:border-brand-500 outline-none text-sm"
          />
        </div>
        <div>
          <label className="text-sm font-medium">Kipimo (UoM)</label>
          <select
            value={uom}
            onChange={(e) => setUom(e.target.value)}
            className="mt-1 w-full px-3 py-2.5 rounded-lg border border-slate-200 focus:border-brand-500 outline-none text-sm"
          >
            {UOM.map((u) => <option key={u} value={u}>{u}</option>)}
          </select>
        </div>
        <div>
          <label className="text-sm font-medium">Stoki</label>
          <input
            type="number"
            value={stoki || ''}
            onChange={(e) => setStoki(Number(e.target.value))}
            placeholder="200"
            className="mt-1 w-full px-3 py-2.5 rounded-lg border border-slate-200 focus:border-brand-500 outline-none text-sm"
          />
        </div>
        <div>
          <label className="text-sm font-medium">Mahali (Location)</label>
          <input
            value={location}
            onChange={(e) => setLocation(e.target.value)}
            placeholder="Shed A, Raka 3"
            className="mt-1 w-full px-3 py-2.5 rounded-lg border border-slate-200 focus:border-brand-500 outline-none text-sm"
          />
        </div>
        <div>
          <label className="text-sm font-medium">Tarehe ya Kuisha (hiari)</label>
          <input
            type="date"
            value={expiry}
            onChange={(e) => setExpiry(e.target.value)}
            className="mt-1 w-full px-3 py-2.5 rounded-lg border border-slate-200 focus:border-brand-500 outline-none text-sm"
          />
        </div>
        <div className="md:col-span-2">
          <label className="text-sm font-medium">Maelezo (hiari)</label>
          <input
            value={maelezo}
            onChange={(e) => setMaelezo(e.target.value)}
            placeholder="Mfano: Saruji mpya kutoka Dangote"
            className="mt-1 w-full px-3 py-2.5 rounded-lg border border-slate-200 focus:border-brand-500 outline-none text-sm"
          />
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Preview */}
      <div className="p-3 rounded-lg bg-slate-50 flex items-center gap-3">
        <div className="w-10 h-10 rounded-lg bg-white border border-slate-200 grid place-items-center text-slate-600">
          <Icon size={18} />
        </div>
        <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
          <div className="text-sm font-medium truncate">{jina || 'Jina la bidhaa'}</div>
          <div className="text-xs text-slate-500">
            {kundi} - {stoki} {uom} - Tsh {beiMauzo.toLocaleString()}
          </div>
        </div>
        <div className="text-sm font-bold text-brand-700 whitespace-nowrap">
          Tsh {(stoki * beiUnunuzi).toLocaleString()}
        </div>
      </div>

      {error && (
        <div className="flex items-start gap-2 p-3 rounded-lg bg-red-50 text-red-700 text-sm">
          <AlertTriangle size={16} className="mt-0.5 shrink-0" />
          {error}
        </div>
      )}

      <button
        type="submit"
        className="w-full py-2.5 rounded-lg bg-brand-600 text-white text-sm font-semibold hover:bg-brand-700 inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-2"
      >
        <Save size={16} />
        Ingiza Bidhaa
      </button>
    </form>
  )
}

// ============================================================
// Bulk (Wengi kwa mkupuo)
// ============================================================
interface BulkRow extends BidhaaMpya {
  _id: string
}

function FomuBulk({
  dukaId, dukaJina, mtumiaji, nafasi,
}: {
  dukaId: string; dukaJina: string; mtumiaji: string; nafasi: 'mmiliki' | 'admin'
}) {
  const { ongezaUingizaji } = useUingizaji()
  const [rows, setRows] = useState<BulkRow[]>([emptyRow()])
  const [maelezo, setMaelezo] = useState('')
  const [error, setError] = useState('')
  const [success, setSuccess] = useState('')

  const jumlaBidhaa = rows.reduce((s, r) => s + (r.stoki || 0), 0)
  const jumlaThamani = rows.reduce((s, r) => s + (r.stoki || 0) * (r.beiUnunuzi || 0), 0)

  const updateRow = (id: string, patch: Partial<BulkRow>) => {
    setRows((prev) => prev.map((r) => (r._id === id ? { ...r, ...patch } : r)))
  }

  const addRow = () => setRows((prev) => [...prev, emptyRow()])
  const removeRow = (id: string) => setRows((prev) => prev.filter((r) => r._id !== id))

  const handleSubmit = (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault()
    if (rows.length === 0) return setError('Ongeza angalau bidhaa moja')
    for (let i = 0; i < rows.length; i++) {
      const r = rows[i]
      if (!r.jina.trim()) return setError(`Mstari ${i + 1}: Jina linahitajika`)
      if (!r.sku.trim()) return setError(`Mstari ${i + 1}: SKU inahitajika`)
      if (r.beiMauzo <= 0) return setError(`Mstari ${i + 1}: Bei ya mauzo si sahihi`)
      if (r.beiUnunuzi <= 0) return setError(`Mstari ${i + 1}: Bei ya ununuzi si sahihi`)
      if (r.stoki <= 0) return setError(`Mstari ${i + 1}: Stoki si sahihi`)
    }

    setError('')
    const bidhaa: BidhaaMpya[] = rows.map((r) => ({
      jina: r.jina.trim(),
      sku: r.sku.trim().toUpperCase(),
      kundi: r.kundi,
      beiMauzo: r.beiMauzo,
      beiUnunuzi: r.beiUnunuzi,
      uom: r.uom,
      stoki: r.stoki,
      location: r.location.trim() || 'Haijawekwa',
      expiry: r.expiry || undefined,
      iconKey: r.kundi,
    }))

    const u = ongezaUingizaji({
      aina: 'bulk',
      dukaId,
      dukaJina,
      bidhaa,
      aliyeingiza: mtumiaji,
      nafasi,
      maelezo,
    })
    setSuccess(`${bidhaa.length} bidhaa zimeingizwa! ID: ${u.id} - Muuzaji atathibitisha "Nimepokea"`)
    setRows([emptyRow()])
    setMaelezo('')
    setTimeout(() => setSuccess(''), 5000)
  }

  return (
    <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="space-y-3">
      <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-sm text-slate-600 bg-white rounded-xl border border-slate-200 p-3">
        <Store size={14} />
        Duka: <span className="font-semibold text-slate-800">{dukaJina}</span>
      </div>

      {success && (
        <div className="flex items-start gap-2 p-3 rounded-lg bg-emerald-50 border border-emerald-200 text-emerald-800 text-sm">
          <CheckCircle2 size={16} className="mt-0.5 shrink-0" />
          {success}
        </div>
      )}

      <div className="bg-white rounded-xl border border-slate-200 p-4 md:p-5 space-y-3">
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
          <h3 className="font-semibold text-sm">Bidhaa ({rows.length})</h3>
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={addRow}
            className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 px-2.5 py-1.5 rounded-lg bg-brand-50 text-brand-700 text-xs font-semibold hover:bg-brand-100"
          >
            <Plus size={12} />
            Ongeza Mstari
          </button>
        </div>

        <div className="space-y-2">
          {rows.map((r, idx) => (
            <BulkRowItem
              key={r._id}
              index={idx}
              row={r}
              onChange={(patch) => updateRow(r._id, patch)}
              onRemove={() => removeRow(r._id)}
              disabled={rows.length === 1}
            />
          ))}
        </div>

        <div>
          <label className="text-sm font-medium">Maelezo ya Jumla (hiari)</label>
          <input
            value={maelezo}
            onChange={(e) => setMaelezo(e.target.value)}
            placeholder="Mfano: Mizigo ya Novemba kutoka msambazaji"
            className="mt-1 w-full px-3 py-2.5 rounded-lg border border-slate-200 focus:border-brand-500 outline-none text-sm"
          />
        </div>

        <div className="p-3 rounded-lg bg-slate-50 grid grid-cols-2 gap-2 text-sm">
          <div>
            <div className="text-xs text-slate-500">Jumla Bidhaa</div>
            <div className="font-bold">{jumlaBidhaa} pcs</div>
          </div>
          <div className="text-right">
            <div className="text-xs text-slate-500">Thamani (Ununuzi)</div>
            <div className="font-bold text-brand-700">Tsh {jumlaThamani.toLocaleString()}</div>
          </div>
        </div>

        {error && (
          <div className="flex items-start gap-2 p-3 rounded-lg bg-red-50 text-red-700 text-sm">
            <AlertTriangle size={16} className="mt-0.5 shrink-0" />
            {error}
          </div>
        )}

        <button
          type="submit"
          className="w-full py-2.5 rounded-lg bg-brand-600 text-white text-sm font-semibold hover:bg-brand-700 inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-2"
        >
          <Save size={16} />
          Ingiza Bidhaa Zote ({rows.length})
        </button>
      </div>
    </form>
  )
}

function BulkRowItem({
  index, row, onChange, onRemove, disabled,
}: {
  index: number
  row: BulkRow
  onChange: (patch: Partial<BulkRow>) => void
  onRemove: () => void
  disabled: boolean
}) {
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(true)
  const Icon = iconForKundi(row.kundi)

  return (
    <div className="border border-slate-200 rounded-lg overflow-hidden">
      <div className="flex items-center gap-2 p-2 bg-slate-50">
        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={() => setOpen((v) => !v)}
          className="p-1 rounded-md hover:bg-slate-200"
        >
          <ChevronDown size={14} className={`transition ${open ? '' : '-rotate-90'}`} />
        </button>
        <div className="w-7 h-7 rounded-md bg-white border border-slate-200 grid place-items-center text-slate-600">
          <Icon size={12} />
        </div>
        <div className="flex-1 min-w-0 text-xs">
          <div className="font-medium truncate">
            {row.jina || `Bidhaa #${index + 1}`}
          </div>
          <div className="text-slate-500">
            {row.stoki} {row.uom} - Tsh {row.beiMauzo.toLocaleString()}
          </div>
        </div>
        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={onRemove}
          disabled={disabled}
          className="p-1.5 rounded-md text-red-500 hover:bg-red-50 disabled:opacity-40 disabled:cursor-not-allowed"
        >
          <Trash2 size={12} />
        </button>
      </div>

      {open && (
        <div className="p-3 grid grid-cols-2 md:grid-cols-4 gap-2">
          <div className="col-span-2 md:col-span-4">
            <input
              value={row.jina}
              onChange={(e) => onChange({ jina: e.target.value })}
              placeholder="Jina la bidhaa"
              className="w-full px-2.5 py-1.5 rounded-md border border-slate-200 text-xs focus:border-brand-500 outline-none"
            />
          </div>
          <input
            value={row.sku}
            onChange={(e) => onChange({ sku: e.target.value.toUpperCase() })}
            placeholder="SKU"
            className="px-2.5 py-1.5 rounded-md border border-slate-200 text-xs font-mono focus:border-brand-500 outline-none"
          />
          <select
            value={row.kundi}
            onChange={(e) => onChange({ kundi: e.target.value as KundiBidhaa, iconKey: e.target.value as KundiBidhaa })}
            className="px-2.5 py-1.5 rounded-md border border-slate-200 text-xs focus:border-brand-500 outline-none"
          >
            {KUNDI.map((k) => <option key={k} value={k}>{k}</option>)}
          </select>
          <input
            type="number"
            value={row.beiMauzo || ''}
            onChange={(e) => onChange({ beiMauzo: Number(e.target.value) })}
            placeholder="Bei mauzo"
            className="px-2.5 py-1.5 rounded-md border border-slate-200 text-xs focus:border-brand-500 outline-none"
          />
          <input
            type="number"
            value={row.beiUnunuzi || ''}
            onChange={(e) => onChange({ beiUnunuzi: Number(e.target.value) })}
            placeholder="Bei ununuzi"
            className="px-2.5 py-1.5 rounded-md border border-slate-200 text-xs focus:border-brand-500 outline-none"
          />
          <select
            value={row.uom}
            onChange={(e) => onChange({ uom: e.target.value })}
            className="px-2.5 py-1.5 rounded-md border border-slate-200 text-xs focus:border-brand-500 outline-none"
          >
            {UOM.map((u) => <option key={u} value={u}>{u}</option>)}
          </select>
          <input
            type="number"
            value={row.stoki || ''}
            onChange={(e) => onChange({ stoki: Number(e.target.value) })}
            placeholder="Stoki"
            className="px-2.5 py-1.5 rounded-md border border-slate-200 text-xs focus:border-brand-500 outline-none"
          />
          <input
            value={row.location}
            onChange={(e) => onChange({ location: e.target.value })}
            placeholder="Mahali"
            className="px-2.5 py-1.5 rounded-md border border-slate-200 text-xs focus:border-brand-500 outline-none"
          />
          <input
            type="date"
            value={row.expiry || ''}
            onChange={(e) => onChange({ expiry: e.target.value })}
            className="px-2.5 py-1.5 rounded-md border border-slate-200 text-xs focus:border-brand-500 outline-none"
          />
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  )
}

function emptyRow(): BulkRow {
  return {
    _id: `row-${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 9)}`,
    jina: '',
    sku: '',
    kundi: 'Saruji',
    beiMauzo: 0,
    beiUnunuzi: 0,
    uom: 'Mfuko',
    stoki: 0,
    location: '',
    expiry: undefined,
    iconKey: 'Saruji',
  }
}

// ============================================================
// Historia
// ============================================================
function Historia({ uingizaji }: { uingizaji: UingizajiVifaa[] }) {
  const [q, setQ] = useState('')
  const [filter, setFilter] = useState<HaliUingizaji | 'zote'>('zote')

  const filtered = useMemo(() => {
    return uingizaji.filter((u) => {
      if (filter !== 'zote' && u.hali !== filter) return false
      const query = q.toLowerCase().trim()
      if (!query) return true
      return (
        u.id.toLowerCase().includes(query) ||
        u.dukaJina.toLowerCase().includes(query) ||
        u.bidhaa.some((b) => b.jina.toLowerCase().includes(query))
      )
    })
  }, [uingizaji, q, filter])

  return (
    <div className="space-y-3">
      <div className="bg-white rounded-xl border border-slate-200 p-3 md:p-4 space-y-3">
        <div className="relative">
          <Search size={16} className="absolute left-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-slate-400" />
          <input
            value={q}
            onChange={(e) => setQ(e.target.value)}
            placeholder="Tafuta kwa ID, duka, au bidhaa..."
            className="w-full pl-9 pr-3 py-2 rounded-lg border border-slate-200 focus:border-brand-500 outline-none text-sm"
          />
        </div>
        <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
          <button onClick={() => setFilter('zote')} className={chipCls(filter === 'zote')}>Zote</button>
          {(Object.keys(HALI_META) as HaliUingizaji[]).map((h) => (
            <button key={h} onClick={() => setFilter(h)} className={chipCls(filter === h)}>
              {HALI_META[h].label}
            </button>
          ))}
        </div>
      </div>

      {filtered.length === 0 ? (
        <div className="bg-white rounded-xl border border-slate-200 p-10 text-center">
          <Package size={32} className="mx-auto mb-2 text-slate-300" />
          <div className="text-sm text-slate-500">Hakuna maingizo yanayolingana</div>
        </div>
      ) : (
        <div className="space-y-2">
          {filtered.map((u) => {
            const meta = HALI_META[u.hali]
            return (
              <div key={u.id} className="bg-white rounded-xl border border-slate-200 p-3 md:p-4">
                <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-2">
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-2 min-w-0">
                    <div className={`w-9 h-9 rounded-lg grid place-items-center shrink-0 ${meta.color}`}>
                      <meta.Icon size={16} />
                    </div>
                    <div className="min-w-0">
                      <div className="flex items-center gap-2 flex-wrap">
                        <span className="font-semibold text-sm">{u.id}</span>
                        <span className={`text-[10px] uppercase font-semibold px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded border ${meta.color}`}>
                          {meta.label}
                        </span>
                        <span className="text-[10px] uppercase font-semibold px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded bg-slate-100 text-slate-600">
                          {u.aina}
                        </span>
                      </div>
                      <div className="text-xs text-slate-500 mt-0.5">
                        {u.dukaJina} - {u.aliyeingiza} ({u.nafasi}) - {u.tarehe}
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                  <div className="text-right shrink-0">
                    <div className="text-sm font-bold text-brand-700">
                      {u.bidhaa.length} bidhaa
                    </div>
                    <div className="text-xs text-slate-500">
                      Tsh {u.jumlaThamani.toLocaleString()}
                    </div>
                  </div>
                </div>

                <div className="mt-2 pt-2 border-t border-slate-100 flex flex-wrap gap-1">
                  {u.bidhaa.slice(0, 4).map((b, i) => (
                    <span key={i} className="text-[10px] px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded bg-slate-100 text-slate-700">
                      {b.jina} ({b.stoki} {b.uom})
                    </span>
                  ))}
                  {u.bidhaa.length > 4 && (
                    <span className="text-[10px] px-1.5 py-0.5 text-slate-500">+{u.bidhaa.length - 4}</span>
                  )}
                </div>

                {u.hali === 'imepokelewa' && u.aliyepokea && (
                  <div className="mt-2 text-xs text-emerald-700 flex items-center gap-1">
                    <CheckCircle2 size={12} />
                    Imepokelewa na {u.aliyepokea} ({u.tareheKupokea})
                  </div>
                )}
              </div>
            )
          })}
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  )
}

function chipCls(active: boolean) {
  return `px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg text-sm border transition ${
    active
      ? 'bg-brand-600 text-white border-brand-600'
      : 'bg-white border-slate-200 hover:bg-slate-50'
  }`
}



import { useState } from 'react'
import { Link, useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom'
import {
  KeyRound, Phone, ArrowLeft, AlertTriangle, CheckCircle2,
  ShieldCheck, Smartphone,
} from 'lucide-react'

export default function ForgotPassword() {
  const navigate = useNavigate()
  const [simu, setSimu] = useState('')
  const [otpSent, setOtpSent] = useState(false)
  const [otp, setOtp] = useState('')
  const [error, setError] = useState('')

  const handleSendOtp = (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault()
    if (!/^0[67]\d{2}-?\d{3}-?\d{3}$/.test(simu.replace(/\s/g, ''))) {
      return setError('Weka namba sahihi ya simu')
    }
    setError('')
    setOtpSent(true)
  }

  const handleVerifyOtp = (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault()
    if (otp.length !== 6) return setError('OTP inapaswa kuwa tarakimu 6')
    setError('')
    navigate('/reset-password')
  }

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-gradient-to-br from-slate-50 via-white to-brand-50/30 flex items-center justify-center p-4">
      <div className="w-full max-w-md">
        <Link
          to="/login"
          className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 text-sm text-slate-600 hover:text-brand-600 mb-6"
        >
          <ArrowLeft size={14} />
          Rudi Login
        </Link>

        <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 p-6 md:p-8 shadow-lg">
          <div className="text-center mb-6">
            <div className="w-14 h-14 rounded-2xl bg-amber-50 text-amber-600 grid place-items-center mx-auto">
              <KeyRound size={28} />
            </div>
            <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold mt-3">Umesahau Nywila?</h1>
            <p className="text-sm text-slate-500 mt-1">
              {otpSent
                ? 'Weka OTP uliyotumiwa kwa simu'
                : 'Weka namba yako ili tukupe OTP'}
            </p>
          </div>

          {!otpSent ? (
            <form onSubmit={handleSendOtp} className="space-y-3">
              <div>
                <label className="text-sm font-medium">Namba ya Simu</label>
                <div className="relative mt-1">
                  <Phone size={16} className="absolute left-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-slate-400" />
                  <input
                    value={simu}
                    onChange={(e) => setSimu(e.target.value)}
                    placeholder="0754-123-456"
                    inputMode="tel"
                    className="w-full pl-10 pr-3 py-2.5 rounded-lg border border-slate-200 focus:border-brand-500 outline-none text-sm"
                  />
                </div>
              </div>

              {error && (
                <div className="flex items-start gap-2 p-3 rounded-lg bg-red-50 text-red-700 text-sm">
                  <AlertTriangle size={16} className="mt-0.5 shrink-0" />
                  {error}
                </div>
              )}

              <button
                type="submit"
                className="w-full inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 py-2.5 rounded-lg bg-brand-600 text-white text-sm font-semibold hover:bg-brand-700"
              >
                <Smartphone size={14} />
                Tuma OTP
              </button>
            </form>
          ) : (
            <form onSubmit={handleVerifyOtp} className="space-y-3">
              <div className="p-3 rounded-lg bg-emerald-50 border border-emerald-200 flex items-start gap-2">
                <CheckCircle2 size={16} className="text-emerald-600 mt-0.5 shrink-0" />
                <div className="text-xs text-emerald-800">
                  OTP imetumwa kwa <span className="font-semibold">{simu}</span>
                </div>
              </div>

              <div>
                <label className="text-sm font-medium">OTP (tarakimu 6)</label>
                <input
                  value={otp}
                  onChange={(e) => setOtp(e.target.value.replace(/\D/g, '').slice(0, 6))}
                  placeholder="123456"
                  inputMode="numeric"
                  maxLength={6}
                  className="mt-1 w-full px-3 py-2.5 rounded-lg border border-slate-200 focus:border-brand-500 outline-none text-sm font-mono text-center text-lg tracking-widest"
                />
              </div>

              {error && (
                <div className="flex items-start gap-2 p-3 rounded-lg bg-red-50 text-red-700 text-sm">
                  <AlertTriangle size={16} className="mt-0.5 shrink-0" />
                  {error}
                </div>
              )}

              <button
                type="submit"
                className="w-full inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 py-2.5 rounded-lg bg-brand-600 text-white text-sm font-semibold hover:bg-brand-700"
              >
                <CheckCircle2 size={14} />
                Thibitisha OTP
              </button>

              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setOtpSent(false)}
                className="w-full text-xs text-slate-500 hover:text-brand-600"
              >
                Badilisha namba ya simu
              </button>
            </form>
          )}

          <div className="mt-6 pt-4 border-t border-slate-100 text-center">
            <div className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs text-slate-500">
              <ShieldCheck size={12} />
              OTP ni salama - usiituma kwa mtu mwingine
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}

import { useState } from 'react'
import { Link, useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom'
import {
  LogIn, Phone, Lock, Eye, EyeOff, ArrowLeft,
  AlertTriangle, ShieldCheck, KeyRound,
} from 'lucide-react'

export default function Login() {
  const navigate = useNavigate()
  const [simu, setSimu] = useState('')
  const [password, setPassword] = useState('')
  const [showPassword, setShowPassword] = useState(false)
  const [error, setError] = useState('')

  const handleSubmit = (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault()
    if (!simu.trim()) return setError('Namba ya simu inahitajika')
    if (!password.trim()) return setError('Nywila inahitajika')

    // Demo: navigate kulingana na simu
    if (simu.includes('0712') || simu.includes('0754')) {
      navigate('/mteja')
    } else if (simu.includes('0713') || simu.includes('0714')) {
      navigate('/muuzaji')
    } else {
      navigate('/mmiliki')
    }
  }

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-gradient-to-br from-slate-50 via-white to-brand-50/30 flex items-center justify-center p-4">
      <div className="w-full max-w-md">
        <Link
          to="/"
          className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 text-sm text-slate-600 hover:text-brand-600 mb-6"
        >
          <ArrowLeft size={14} />
          Rudi Nyumbani
        </Link>

        <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 p-6 md:p-8 shadow-lg">
          <div className="text-center mb-6">
            <div className="w-14 h-14 rounded-2xl bg-brand-50 text-brand-600 grid place-items-center mx-auto">
              <LogIn size={28} />
            </div>
            <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold mt-3">Ingia</h1>
            <p className="text-sm text-slate-500 mt-1">
              Weka namba yako ya simu na nywila
            </p>
          </div>

          <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="space-y-3">
            <div>
              <label className="text-sm font-medium">Namba ya Simu</label>
              <div className="relative mt-1">
                <Phone size={16} className="absolute left-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-slate-400" />
                <input
                  value={simu}
                  onChange={(e) => setSimu(e.target.value)}
                  placeholder="0754-123-456"
                  inputMode="tel"
                  className="w-full pl-10 pr-3 py-2.5 rounded-lg border border-slate-200 focus:border-brand-500 outline-none text-sm"
                />
              </div>
            </div>

            <div>
              <label className="text-sm font-medium">Nywila</label>
              <div className="relative mt-1">
                <Lock size={16} className="absolute left-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-slate-400" />
                <input
                  type={showPassword ? 'text' : 'password'}
                  value={password}
                  onChange={(e) => setPassword(e.target.value)}
                  placeholder="••••••••"
                  className="w-full pl-10 pr-10 py-2.5 rounded-lg border border-slate-200 focus:border-brand-500 outline-none text-sm"
                />
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => setShowPassword(!showPassword)}
                  className="absolute right-2 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 p-1.5 rounded-md hover:bg-slate-100"
                >
                  {showPassword ? <EyeOff size={14} /> : <Eye size={14} />}
                </button>
              </div>
            </div>

            {error && (
              <div className="flex items-start gap-2 p-3 rounded-lg bg-red-50 text-red-700 text-sm">
                <AlertTriangle size={16} className="mt-0.5 shrink-0" />
                {error}
              </div>
            )}

            <button
              type="submit"
              className="w-full py-2.5 rounded-lg bg-brand-600 text-white text-sm font-semibold hover:bg-brand-700"
            >
              Ingia
            </button>
          </form>

          <div className="mt-4 text-center">
            <Link
              to="/forgot-password"
              className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 text-sm text-brand-600 hover:underline"
            >
              <KeyRound size={12} />
              Umesahau Nywila?
            </Link>
          </div>

          <div className="mt-6 pt-4 border-t border-slate-100 text-center">
            <div className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs text-slate-500">
              <ShieldCheck size={12} />
              Usalama wa akaunti yako ni kipaumbele chetu
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}

import { Link } from 'react-router-dom'
import { CheckCircle2, ArrowRight, LogIn } from 'lucide-react'

export default function ResetComplete() {
  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-gradient-to-br from-slate-50 via-white to-brand-50/30 flex items-center justify-center p-4">
      <div className="w-full max-w-md">
        <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 p-6 md:p-8 shadow-lg text-center">
          <div className="w-16 h-16 rounded-full bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-600 grid place-items-center mx-auto">
            <CheckCircle2 size={32} />
          </div>
          <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold mt-4">Nywila Imebadilishwa</h1>
          <p className="text-sm text-slate-500 mt-2">
            Nywila yako mpya imehifadhiwa kwa mafanikio. Sasa unaweza kuingia
            kwenye akaunti yako.
          </p>

          <Link
            to="/login"
            className="mt-6 inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 px-4 py-2.5 rounded-lg bg-brand-600 text-white text-sm font-semibold hover:bg-brand-700"
          >
            <LogIn size={14} />
            Ingia Sasa
            <ArrowRight size={14} />
          </Link>

          <div className="mt-6 pt-4 border-t border-slate-100">
            <Link
              to="/"
              className="text-xs text-slate-500 hover:text-brand-600"
            >
              Rudi Nyumbani
            </Link>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}

import { useState } from 'react'
import { Link, useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom'
import {
  Lock, ArrowLeft, AlertTriangle, Eye, EyeOff,
  CheckCircle2, ShieldCheck, KeyRound,
} from 'lucide-react'

export default function ResetPassword() {
  const navigate = useNavigate()
  const [password, setPassword] = useState('')
  const [confirm, setConfirm] = useState('')
  const [showPassword, setShowPassword] = useState(false)
  const [error, setError] = useState('')

  const handleSubmit = (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault()
    if (password.length < 6) return setError('Nywila iwe angalau herufi 6')
    if (password !== confirm) return setError('Nywila hazifanani')

    setError('')
    navigate('/reset-complete')
  }

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-gradient-to-br from-slate-50 via-white to-brand-50/30 flex items-center justify-center p-4">
      <div className="w-full max-w-md">
        <Link
          to="/login"
          className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 text-sm text-slate-600 hover:text-brand-600 mb-6"
        >
          <ArrowLeft size={14} />
          Rudi Login
        </Link>

        <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 p-6 md:p-8 shadow-lg">
          <div className="text-center mb-6">
            <div className="w-14 h-14 rounded-2xl bg-brand-50 text-brand-600 grid place-items-center mx-auto">
              <Lock size={28} />
            </div>
            <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold mt-3">Weka Nywila Mpya</h1>
            <p className="text-sm text-slate-500 mt-1">
              Chagua nywila mpya yenye usalama
            </p>
          </div>

          <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="space-y-3">
            <div>
              <label className="text-sm font-medium">Nywila Mpya</label>
              <div className="relative mt-1">
                <Lock size={16} className="absolute left-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-slate-400" />
                <input
                  type={showPassword ? 'text' : 'password'}
                  value={password}
                  onChange={(e) => setPassword(e.target.value)}
                  placeholder="••••••••"
                  className="w-full pl-10 pr-10 py-2.5 rounded-lg border border-slate-200 focus:border-brand-500 outline-none text-sm"
                />
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => setShowPassword(!showPassword)}
                  className="absolute right-2 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 p-1.5 rounded-md hover:bg-slate-100"
                >
                  {showPassword ? <EyeOff size={14} /> : <Eye size={14} />}
                </button>
              </div>
            </div>

            <div>
              <label className="text-sm font-medium">Rudia Nywila</label>
              <div className="relative mt-1">
                <Lock size={16} className="absolute left-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-slate-400" />
                <input
                  type={showPassword ? 'text' : 'password'}
                  value={confirm}
                  onChange={(e) => setConfirm(e.target.value)}
                  placeholder="••••••••"
                  className="w-full pl-10 pr-3 py-2.5 rounded-lg border border-slate-200 focus:border-brand-500 outline-none text-sm"
                />
              </div>
            </div>

            {error && (
              <div className="flex items-start gap-2 p-3 rounded-lg bg-red-50 text-red-700 text-sm">
                <AlertTriangle size={16} className="mt-0.5 shrink-0" />
                {error}
              </div>
            )}

            <button
              type="submit"
              className="w-full inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 py-2.5 rounded-lg bg-brand-600 text-white text-sm font-semibold hover:bg-brand-700"
            >
              <KeyRound size={14} />
              Hifadhi Nywila
            </button>
          </form>

          <div className="mt-6 pt-4 border-t border-slate-100 text-center">
            <div className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs text-slate-500">
              <ShieldCheck size={12} />
              Tumia nywila imara yenye herufi na namba
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}

import { createContext, useContext, useState, useMemo, type ReactNode } from 'react'
import { bidhaa } from '../data/demo'
import type { KundiBidhaa } from '../types'

export interface CartLine {
  id: string
  wingi: number
}

export interface CartDetailedLine {
  id: string
  jina: string
  sku: string
  uom: string
  bei: number
  wingi: number
  jumla: number
  stoki: number
  iconKey?: KundiBidhaa
}

interface CartCtx {
  lines: CartLine[]
  detailed: CartDetailedLine[]
  count: number
  total: number
  add: (id: string, wingi?: number) => void
  remove: (id: string) => void
  setWingi: (id: string, wingi: number) => void
  inc: (id: string) => void
  dec: (id: string) => void
  clear: () => void
}

const CartContext = createContext<CartCtx | null>(null)

export function CartProvider({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {
  const [lines, setLines] = useState<CartLine[]>([])

  const add = (id: string, wingi = 1) => {
    setLines((prev) => {
      const ex = prev.find((x) => x.id === id)
      if (ex) return prev.map((x) => (x.id === id ? { ...x, wingi: x.wingi + wingi } : x))
      return [...prev, { id, wingi }]
    })
  }

  const remove = (id: string) => setLines((prev) => prev.filter((x) => x.id !== id))

  const setWingi = (id: string, wingi: number) => {
    if (wingi <= 0) return remove(id)
    setLines((prev) => prev.map((x) => (x.id === id ? { ...x, wingi } : x)))
  }

  const inc = (id: string) =>
    setLines((prev) => prev.map((x) => (x.id === id ? { ...x, wingi: x.wingi + 1 } : x)))

  const dec = (id: string) =>
    setLines((prev) =>
      prev
        .map((x) => (x.id === id ? { ...x, wingi: x.wingi - 1 } : x))
        .filter((x) => x.wingi > 0)
    )

  const clear = () => setLines([])

  const detailed = useMemo<CartDetailedLine[]>(() => {
    const result: CartDetailedLine[] = []
    for (const l of lines) {
      const b = bidhaa.find((x) => x.id === l.id)
      if (!b) continue
      result.push({
        id: b.id,
        jina: b.jina,
        sku: b.sku,
        uom: b.uom,
        bei: b.beiMauzo,
        wingi: l.wingi,
        jumla: b.beiMauzo * l.wingi,
        stoki: b.stoki,
        iconKey: b.iconKey,
      })
    }
    return result
  }, [lines])

  const count = useMemo(() => detailed.reduce((s, l) => s + l.wingi, 0), [detailed])
  const total = useMemo(() => detailed.reduce((s, l) => s + l.jumla, 0), [detailed])

  const value: CartCtx = { lines, detailed, count, total, add, remove, setWingi, inc, dec, clear }

  return <CartContext.Provider value={value}>{children}</CartContext.Provider>
}

export function useCart() {
  const ctx = useContext(CartContext)
  if (!ctx) throw new Error('useCart must be used within CartProvider')
  return ctx
}

import { createContext, useContext, useState, type ReactNode } from 'react'
import type { Mteja } from '../types'
import { wateja as watejaDemo } from '../data/demo'

interface CustomerCtx {
  wateja: Mteja[]
  ongezaMteja: (m: Omit<Mteja, 'id' | 'tareheSajili' | 'deni' | 'jumlaManunuzi' | 'active'>) => Mteja
  tafutaMteja: (q: string) => Mteja[]
  getMteja: (id: string) => Mteja | undefined
}

const CustomerContext = createContext<CustomerCtx | null>(null)

export function CustomerProvider({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {
  const [wateja, setWateja] = useState<Mteja[]>(watejaDemo)

  const ongezaMteja: CustomerCtx['ongezaMteja'] = (data) => {
    const mteja: Mteja = {
      ...data,
      id: `c${Date.now()}`,
      deni: 0,
      jumlaManunuzi: 0,
      tareheSajili: new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0],
      active: true,
    }
    setWateja((prev) => [mteja, ...prev])
    return mteja
  }

  const tafutaMteja = (q: string) => {
    const query = q.toLowerCase().trim()
    if (!query) return wateja
    return wateja.filter(
      (m) =>
        m.jina.toLowerCase().includes(query) ||
        m.simu.includes(query) ||
        m.aina.includes(query)
    )
  }

  const getMteja = (id: string) => wateja.find((m) => m.id === id)

  return (
    <CustomerContext.Provider value={{ wateja, ongezaMteja, tafutaMteja, getMteja }}>
      {children}
    </CustomerContext.Provider>
  )
}

export function useCustomers() {
  const ctx = useContext(CustomerContext)
  if (!ctx) throw new Error('useCustomers must be used within CustomerProvider')
  return ctx
}

import { createContext, useContext, useState, type ReactNode } from 'react'
import type { Document, AinaDocument, HaliDocument } from '../types'

interface OngezaDocumentInput {

  aina: AinaDocument
  format: 'pdf' | 'csv' | 'excel'
  dukaId: string
  dukaJina: string
  ukubwa: string
  kipindi: string
  aliyezalisha: string
}

interface DocumentCtx {
  documents: Document[]
  ongezaDocument: (data: OngezaDocumentInput) => Document
  futa: (id: string) => void
  getDocument: (id: string) => Document | undefined
}

const DocumentContext = createContext<DocumentCtx | null>(null)

    dukaJina: 'Smart Hardware - Kwembe',
    ukubwa: '320 KB',
    tarehe: '06 Nov 2026',
    muda: '09:30',
    hali: 'inasindika',
    aliyezalisha: 'Juma Hassan',
    kipindi: 'Novemba 2026',
  },
