export function DocumentProvider({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {
  const [documents, setDocuments] = useState<Document[]>(DOCUMENTS_DEMO)

  const ongezaDocument: DocumentCtx['ongezaDocument'] = (data) => {
    const doc: Document = {
      ...data,
      id: `DOC-${new Date().getFullYear()}-${String(documents.length + 6).padStart(3, '0')}`,
      tarehe: new Date().toLocaleDateString('sw-TZ', {
        day: '2-digit',
        month: 'short',
        year: 'numeric',
      }),
      muda: new Date().toLocaleTimeString('sw-TZ', {
        hour: '2-digit',
        minute: '2-digit',
      }),
      hali: 'tayari',
    }
    setDocuments((prev) => [doc, ...prev])
    return doc
  }

  const futa = (id: string) => {
    setDocuments((prev) => prev.filter((d) => d.id !== id))
  }

  const getDocument = (id: string) => documents.find((d) => d.id === id)

  return (
    <DocumentContext.Provider value={{ documents, ongezaDocument, futa, getDocument }}>
      {children}
    </DocumentContext.Provider>
  )
}

export function useDocuments() {
  const ctx = useContext(DocumentContext)
  if (!ctx) throw new Error('useDocuments must be used within DocumentProvider')
  return ctx
}

import { createContext, useContext, useState, type ReactNode } from 'react'
import type { DukaKamili, MtumiajiKamili, HaliDuka } from '../types'
import {
  madukaKamili as madukaDemo,
  wafanyakaziKamili as wafanyakaziDemo,
} from '../data/demo'

interface FunguaDukaInput {
  jina: string
  mahali: string
  simu: string
  msimamiziId?: string
}

interface DukaCtx {
  maduka: DukaKamili[]
  wafanyakazi: MtumiajiKamili[]
  funguaDuka: (data: FunguaDukaInput) => DukaKamili
  wekaMsimamizi: (dukaId: string, mtumiajiId: string) => void
  badilishaHali: (dukaId: string, hali: HaliDuka) => void
  ongezaMtumiaji: (data: Omit<MtumiajiKamili, 'id' | 'tareheSajili' | 'active'>) => MtumiajiKamili
  getDuka: (id: string) => DukaKamili | undefined
  wafanyakaziWasioNaDuka: () => MtumiajiKamili[]
  wafanyakaziWaDuka: (dukaId: string) => MtumiajiKamili[]
}

const DukaContext = createContext<DukaCtx | null>(null)

export function DukaProvider({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {
  const [maduka, setMaduka] = useState<DukaKamili[]>(madukaDemo)
  const [wafanyakazi, setWafanyakazi] = useState<MtumiajiKamili[]>(wafanyakaziDemo)

  const funguaDuka: DukaCtx['funguaDuka'] = (data) => {
    const msimamizi = data.msimamiziId
      ? wafanyakazi.find((w) => w.id === data.msimamiziId)
      : undefined

    const duka: DukaKamili = {
      id: `d${Date.now()}`,
      jina: data.jina,
      mahali: data.mahali,
      simu: data.simu,
      msimamiziId: msimamizi?.id,
      msimamiziJina: msimamizi?.jina,
      tareheKufunguliwa: new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0],
      hali: 'hai',
      mauzoLeo: 0,
      faidaLeo: 0,
      wafanyakazi: 0,
      stokiJumla: 0,
    }
    setMaduka((prev) => [duka, ...prev])

    // Mpangie msimamizi kwenye duka
    if (msimamizi) {
      setWafanyakazi((prev) =>
        prev.map((w) =>
          w.id === msimamizi.id ? { ...w, dukaId: duka.id, dukaJina: duka.jina } : w
        )
      )
    }
    return duka
  }

  const wekaMsimamizi = (dukaId: string, mtumiajiId: string) => {
    const mtumiaji = wafanyakazi.find((w) => w.id === mtumiajiId)
    if (!mtumiaji) return

    // Sasisha duka
    setMaduka((prev) =>
      prev.map((d) =>
        d.id === dukaId
          ? { ...d, msimamiziId: mtumiaji.id, msimamiziJina: mtumiaji.jina }
          : d
      )
    )

    // Sasisha mtumiaji
    setWafanyakazi((prev) =>
      prev.map((w) =>
        w.id === mtumiajiId
          ? { ...w, dukaId, dukaJina: maduka.find((d) => d.id === dukaId)?.jina }
          : w
      )
    )
  }

  const badilishaHali = (dukaId: string, hali: HaliDuka) => {
    setMaduka((prev) => prev.map((d) => (d.id === dukaId ? { ...d, hali } : d)))
  }

  const ongezaMtumiaji: DukaCtx['ongezaMtumiaji'] = (data) => {
    const mtumiaji: MtumiajiKamili = {
      ...data,
      id: `u${Date.now()}`,
      tareheSajili: new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0],
      active: true,
    }
    setWafanyakazi((prev) => [mtumiaji, ...prev])
    return mtumiaji
  }

  const getDuka = (id: string) => maduka.find((d) => d.id === id)

  const wafanyakaziWasioNaDuka = () =>
    wafanyakazi.filter((w) => !w.dukaId && w.active)

  const wafanyakaziWaDuka = (dukaId: string) =>
    wafanyakazi.filter((w) => w.dukaId === dukaId)

  return (
    <DukaContext.Provider
      value={{
        maduka,
        wafanyakazi,
        funguaDuka,
        wekaMsimamizi,
        badilishaHali,
        ongezaMtumiaji,
        getDuka,
        wafanyakaziWasioNaDuka,
        wafanyakaziWaDuka,
      }}
    >
      {children}
    </DukaContext.Provider>
  )
}

export function useDuka() {
  const ctx = useContext(DukaContext)
  if (!ctx) throw new Error('useDuka must be used within DukaProvider')
  return ctx
}

import { createContext, useContext, useState, type ReactNode } from 'react'
import type { GRN, GRNItem, HaliGRN } from '../types'
import { grnList as grnDemo } from '../data/demo'

interface GRNCtx {
  grns: GRN[]
  ongezaGRN: (grn: Omit<GRN, 'id' | 'aliyeingiza'>) => GRN
  pokeaBidhaa: (id: string, items: GRNItem[]) => void
  getGRN: (id: string) => GRN | undefined
}

const GRNContext = createContext<GRNCtx | null>(null)

const ALIYEINGIZA = 'Juma Hassan'

function hesabuHali(items: GRNItem[]): HaliGRN {
  const zote = items.every((i) => i.iliyopokelewa >= i.iliyoagizwa)
  const hakuna = items.every((i) => i.iliyopokelewa === 0)
  if (hakuna) return 'inasubiri'
  if (zote) return 'imepokelewa'
  return 'upungufu'
}

export function GRNProvider({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {
  const [grns, setGrns] = useState<GRN[]>(grnDemo)

  const ongezaGRN: GRNCtx['ongezaGRN'] = (data) => {
    const grn: GRN = {
      ...data,
      id: `GRN-${new Date().getFullYear()}-${String(grns.length + 48).padStart(3, '0')}`,
      aliyeingiza: ALIYEINGIZA,
      hali: hesabuHali(data.bidhaa),
    }
    setGrns((prev) => [grn, ...prev])
    return grn
  }

  const pokeaBidhaa = (id: string, items: GRNItem[]) => {
    setGrns((prev) =>
      prev.map((g) =>
        g.id === id
          ? { ...g, bidhaa: items, hali: hesabuHali(items) }
          : g
      )
    )
  }

  const getGRN = (id: string) => grns.find((g) => g.id === id)

  return (
    <GRNContext.Provider value={{ grns, ongezaGRN, pokeaBidhaa, getGRN }}>
      {children}
    </GRNContext.Provider>
  )
}

export function useGRN() {
  const ctx = useContext(GRNContext)
  if (!ctx) throw new Error('useGRN must be used within GRNProvider')
  return ctx
}

import { createContext, useContext, useState, type ReactNode } from 'react'
import type {
  MauzoYaDuka,
  GharamaOpex,
  AinaGharamaOpex,
} from '../types'
import {
  mauzoYaDuka as mauzoDemo,
  gharamaOpex as opexDemo,
} from '../data/demo'

interface OngezaMauzoInput {
  dukaId: string
  dukaJina: string
  muuzaji: string
  bidhaa: MauzoYaDuka['bidhaa']
  cogs: number
}

interface OngezaOpexInput {
  dukaId: string
  dukaJina: string
  aina: AinaGharamaOpex
  maelezo: string
  kiasi: number
  aliyeingiza: string
}

interface MauzoCtx {
  mauzo: MauzoYaDuka[]
  opex: GharamaOpex[]
  ongezaMauzo: (data: OngezaMauzoInput) => MauzoYaDuka
  ongezaOpex: (data: OngezaOpexInput) => GharamaOpex
  futaOpex: (id: string) => void
  getMauzo: (id: string) => MauzoYaDuka | undefined
}

const MauzoContext = createContext<MauzoCtx | null>(null)

export function MauzoProvider({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {
  const [mauzo, setMauzo] = useState<MauzoYaDuka[]>(mauzoDemo)
  const [opex, setOpex] = useState<GharamaOpex[]>(opexDemo)

  const ongezaMauzo: MauzoCtx['ongezaMauzo'] = (data) => {
    const jumlaMauzo = data.bidhaa.reduce((s, b) => s + b.jumla, 0)
    const faidaGhafi = jumlaMauzo - data.cogs

    const m: MauzoYaDuka = {
      id: `SALE-${new Date().getFullYear()}-${String(mauzo.length + 1).padStart(3, '0')}`,
      tarehe: new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0],
      dukaId: data.dukaId,
      dukaJina: data.dukaJina,
      muuzaji: data.muuzaji,
      bidhaa: data.bidhaa,
      jumlaMauzo,
      cogs: data.cogs,
      faidaGhafi,
    }
    setMauzo((prev) => [m, ...prev])
    return m
  }

  const ongezaOpex: MauzoCtx['ongezaOpex'] = (data) => {
    const g: GharamaOpex = {
      ...data,
      id: `OPEX-${Date.now()}`,
      tarehe: new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0],
    }
    setOpex((prev) => [g, ...prev])
    return g
  }

  const futaOpex = (id: string) => {
    setOpex((prev) => prev.filter((g) => g.id !== id))
  }

  const getMauzo = (id: string) => mauzo.find((m) => m.id === id)

  return (
    <MauzoContext.Provider
      value={{ mauzo, opex, ongezaMauzo, ongezaOpex, futaOpex, getMauzo }}
    >
      {children}
    </MauzoContext.Provider>
  )
}

export function useMauzo() {
  const ctx = useContext(MauzoContext)
  if (!ctx) throw new Error('useMauzo must be used within MauzoProvider')
  return ctx
}

import { createContext, useContext, useState, type ReactNode } from 'react'
import type {
  Mshahara,
  MalipoMshahara,
  NjiaMalipoMshahara,
  AkauntiMfanyakazi,
  HaliMshahara,
  BulkPaymentResult,
} from '../types'
import {
  mishahara as mishaharaDemo,
  akauntiWafanyakazi as akauntiDemo,
} from '../data/demo'

// ============================================================
// Types za ndani
// ============================================================
interface OngezaMshaharaInput {
  mtumiajiId: string
  jinaMtumiaji: string
  nafasi: string
  dukaId: string
  dukaJina: string
  mwezi: string
  kipindi: string
  mshaharaMsingi: number
  posho: number
  overtime: number
  mafao: number
  makato: number
  maelezo?: string
}

interface OngezaAkauntiInput {
  mtumiajiId: string
  jinaMtumiaji: string
  aina: 'bank' | 'mobile'
  jina: string
  namba: string
  jinaMmiliki: string
  ndiyoMsingi: boolean
}

interface MshaharaCtx {
  mishahara: Mshahara[]
  malipo: MalipoMshahara[]
  akaunti: AkauntiMfanyakazi[]
  ongezaMshahara: (data: OngezaMshaharaInput) => Mshahara
  lipaMshaharaMmoja: (mshaharaId: string) => MalipoMshahara | null
  lipaBulk: (mishaharaIds: string[]) => BulkPaymentResult
  lipaZoteZinazosubiri: () => BulkPaymentResult
  ongezaAkaunti: (data: OngezaAkauntiInput) => AkauntiMfanyakazi
  futaAkaunti: (id: string) => void
  futaMshahara: (id: string) => void
  getMshahara: (id: string) => Mshahara | undefined
  getAkauntiMsingi: (mtumiajiId: string) => AkauntiMfanyakazi | undefined
  mishaharaYaMtumiaji: (mtumiajiId: string) => Mshahara[]
}

const MshaharaContext = createContext<MshaharaCtx | null>(null)

const ALIYEIDHINISHA = 'Moses Kimaro'

// ============================================================
// Ramani ya njia kutoka akaunti
// ============================================================
const AINA_TO_NJIA: Record<string, NjiaMalipoMshahara> = {
  'M-Pesa': 'mpesa',
  'Tigo Pesa': 'tigopesa',
  'Airtel Money': 'airtel',
  'HaloPesa': 'halopesa',
  'CRDB Bank': 'crdb',
  'NMB Bank': 'nmb',
  'NBC Bank': 'nbc',
  'Stanbic Bank': 'stanbic',
}

function njiaKutokaAkaunti(akaunti: AkauntiMfanyakazi): NjiaMalipoMshahara {
  return AINA_TO_NJIA[akaunti.jina] ?? 'other'
}

// ============================================================
// Tengeneza kumbukumbu (prefix + tarehe + namba)
// ============================================================
function tengenezaKumbukumbu(
  njia: NjiaMalipoMshahara,
  tarehe: string,
  idx: number
): string {
  const prefix =
    njia === 'mpesa'
      ? 'MPESA'
      : njia === 'tigopesa'
      ? 'TIGO'
      : njia === 'airtel'
      ? 'AIRTEL'
      : njia === 'halopesa'
      ? 'HALO'
      : 'BANK'
  const tareheFmt = tarehe.replace(/-/g, '')
  return `${prefix}-${tareheFmt}-${String(idx + 1).padStart(3, '0')}`
}

// ============================================================
// Tengeneza tarehe na muda wa sasa
// ============================================================
function tareheNaMudaSasa() {
  const sasa = new Date()
  return {
    tareheISO: sasa.toISOString().split('T')[0],
    tareheFmt: sasa.toLocaleDateString('sw-TZ', {
      day: '2-digit',
      month: 'short',
      year: 'numeric',
    }),
    muda: sasa.toLocaleTimeString('sw-TZ', {
      hour: '2-digit',
      minute: '2-digit',
    }),
  }
}

// ============================================================
// Provider
// ============================================================
export function MshaharaProvider({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {
  const [mishahara, setMishahara] = useState<Mshahara[]>(mishaharaDemo)
  const [malipo, setMalipo] = useState<MalipoMshahara[]>([])
  const [akaunti, setAkaunti] = useState<AkauntiMfanyakazi[]>(akauntiDemo)

  // ============================================================
  // Ongeza mshahara mpya
  // ============================================================
  const ongezaMshahara: MshaharaCtx['ongezaMshahara'] = (data) => {
    const jumla =
      data.mshaharaMsingi +
      data.posho +
      data.overtime +
      data.mafao -
      data.makato
    const m: Mshahara = {
      ...data,
      id: `SAL-${new Date().getFullYear()}-${String(
        mishahara.length + 7
      ).padStart(3, '0')}`,
      jumla,
      hali: 'inasubiri',
    } as Mshahara
    setMishahara((prev) => [m, ...prev])
    return m
  }

  // ============================================================
  // Lipa mshahara mmoja
  // ============================================================
  const lipaMshaharaMmoja: MshaharaCtx['lipaMshaharaMmoja'] = (mshaharaId) => {
    const mshahara = mishahara.find((m) => m.id === mshaharaId)
    if (!mshahara) return null
    if (mshahara.hali !== 'inasubiri') return null

    // Tafuta akaunti msingi ya mfanyakazi
    const akauntiMfanyakazi = akaunti.find(
      (a) => a.mtumiajiId === mshahara.mtumiajiId && a.ndiyoMsingi
    )
    if (!akauntiMfanyakazi) return null

    const njia = njiaKutokaAkaunti(akauntiMfanyakazi)
    const { tareheISO, tareheFmt, muda } = tareheNaMudaSasa()
    const kumbukumbu = tengenezaKumbukumbu(njia, tareheISO, 0)

    const malipoMpya: MalipoMshahara = {
      id: `PAY-${Date.now()}`,
      mshaharaId,
      mtumiajiId: mshahara.mtumiajiId,
      jinaMtumiaji: mshahara.jinaMtumiaji,
      kiasi: mshahara.jumla,
      njia,
      akaunti: akauntiMfanyakazi.namba,
      kumbukumbu,
      tarehe: tareheFmt,
      muda,
      hali: 'imelipwa',
    }

    // Sasisha malipo
    setMalipo((prev) => [malipoMpya, ...prev])

    // Sasisha mshahara
    setMishahara((prev) =>
      prev.map((m) =>
        m.id === mshaharaId
          ? {
              ...m,
              hali: 'imelipwa' as HaliMshahara,
              tareheMalipo: tareheISO,
              njiaMalipo: njia,
              akauntiMalipo: akauntiMfanyakazi.namba,
              kumbukumbu,
              aliyeidhinisha: ALIYEIDHINISHA,
              tareheIdhini: tareheISO,
            }
          : m
      )
    )
    return malipoMpya
  }

  // ============================================================
  // Lipa bulk (wafanyakazi waliochaguliwa)
  // ============================================================
  const lipaBulk: MshaharaCtx['lipaBulk'] = (mishaharaIds) => {
    // Kwanza hesabu malipo yote
    const { tareheISO, tareheFmt, muda } = tareheNaMudaSasa()
    const malipoMapya: MalipoMshahara[] = []
    const zilizofanikiwa: string[] = []
    const zilizoshindwa: string[] = []
    const waliokosaAkaunti: string[] = []
    let jumla = 0

    // Ramani ya kumbukumbu kwa kila mshaharaId
    const kumbukumbuMap = new Map<string, string>()

    mishaharaIds.forEach((id, idx) => {
      const mshahara = mishahara.find((m) => m.id === id)
      if (!mshahara || mshahara.hali !== 'inasubiri') {
        zilizoshindwa.push(id)
        return
      }

      const akauntiMfanyakazi = akaunti.find(
        (a) => a.mtumiajiId === mshahara.mtumiajiId && a.ndiyoMsingi
      )
      if (!akauntiMfanyakazi) {
        waliokosaAkaunti.push(mshahara.jinaMtumiaji)
        zilizoshindwa.push(id)
        return
      }

      const njia = njiaKutokaAkaunti(akauntiMfanyakazi)
      const kumbukumbu = tengenezaKumbukumbu(njia, tareheISO, idx)

      const malipoMpya: MalipoMshahara = {
        id: `PAY-${Date.now()}-${idx}`,
        mshaharaId: id,
        mtumiajiId: mshahara.mtumiajiId,
        jinaMtumiaji: mshahara.jinaMtumiaji,
        kiasi: mshahara.jumla,
        njia,
        akaunti: akauntiMfanyakazi.namba,
        kumbukumbu,
        tarehe: tareheFmt,
        muda,
        hali: 'imelipwa',
      }

      malipoMapya.push(malipoMpya)
      zilizofanikiwa.push(id)
      kumbukumbuMap.set(id, kumbukumbu)
      jumla += mshahara.jumla
    })

    // Sasisha mishahara kwa kutumia kumbukumbuMap
    setMishahara((prev) =>
      prev.map((m) => {
        if (!zilizofanikiwa.includes(m.id)) return m

        const mshahara = mishahara.find((x) => x.id === m.id)
        if (!mshahara) return m

        const akauntiMfanyakazi = akaunti.find(
          (a) => a.mtumiajiId === mshahara.mtumiajiId && a.ndiyoMsingi
        )
        if (!akauntiMfanyakazi) return m

        const njia = njiaKutokaAkaunti(akauntiMfanyakazi)
        const kumbukumbu = kumbukumbuMap.get(m.id) ?? ''

        return {
          ...m,
          hali: 'imelipwa' as HaliMshahara,
          tareheMalipo: tareheISO,
          njiaMalipo: njia,
          akauntiMalipo: akauntiMfanyakazi.namba,
          kumbukumbu,
          aliyeidhinisha: ALIYEIDHINISHA,
          tareheIdhini: tareheISO,
        }
      })
    )

    // Sasisha malipo
    setMalipo((prev) => [...malipoMapya, ...prev])

    return {
      jumla,
      zilizofanikiwa: zilizofanikiwa.length,
      zilizoshindwa: zilizoshindwa.length,
      malipo: malipoMapya,
      waliokosaAkaunti,
    }
  }

  // ============================================================
  // Lipa zote zinazosubiri (button moja)
  // ============================================================
  const lipaZoteZinazosubiri: MshaharaCtx['lipaZoteZinazosubiri'] = () => {
    const zinazosubiri = mishahara.filter((m) => m.hali === 'inasubiri')
    return lipaBulk(zinazosubiri.map((m) => m.id))
  }

  // ============================================================
  // Akaunti za wafanyakazi
  // ============================================================
  const ongezaAkaunti: MshaharaCtx['ongezaAkaunti'] = (data) => {
    const a: AkauntiMfanyakazi = {
      ...data,
      id: `acc-${Date.now()}`,
    }
    setAkaunti((prev) => [...prev, a])
    return a
  }

  const futaAkaunti = (id: string) => {
    setAkaunti((prev) => prev.filter((a) => a.id !== id))
  }

  const futaMshahara = (id: string) => {
    setMishahara((prev) => prev.filter((m) => m.id !== id))
  }

  const getMshahara = (id: string) => mishahara.find((m) => m.id === id)

  const getAkauntiMsingi = (mtumiajiId: string) =>
    akaunti.find((a) => a.mtumiajiId === mtumiajiId && a.ndiyoMsingi)

  const mishaharaYaMtumiaji = (mtumiajiId: string) =>
    mishahara.filter((m) => m.mtumiajiId === mtumiajiId)

  return (
    <MshaharaContext.Provider
      value={{
        mishahara,
        malipo,
        akaunti,
        ongezaMshahara,
        lipaMshaharaMmoja,
        lipaBulk,
        lipaZoteZinazosubiri,
        ongezaAkaunti,
        futaAkaunti,
        futaMshahara,
        getMshahara,
        getAkauntiMsingi,
        mishaharaYaMtumiaji,
      }}
    >
      {children}
    </MshaharaContext.Provider>
  )
}

export function useMshahara() {
  const ctx = useContext(MshaharaContext)
  if (!ctx) throw new Error('useMshahara must be used within MshaharaProvider')
  return ctx
}

import { createContext, useContext, useState, type ReactNode } from 'react'
import type { Notification, KundiNotification } from '../types'
import { notifications as notificationsDemo } from '../data/demo'

interface NotificationCtx {
  notifications: Notification[]
  unreadCount: number
  markAsRead: (id: string) => void
  markAllRead: () => void
  futa: (id: string) => void
  futaZote: () => void
  getNotification: (id: string) => Notification | undefined
  filterByKundi: (kundi: KundiNotification | 'zote') => Notification[]
  search: (q: string) => Notification[]
}

const NotificationContext = createContext<NotificationCtx | null>(null)

export function NotificationProvider({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {
  const [notifications, setNotifications] = useState<Notification[]>(
    notificationsDemo
  )

  const unreadCount = notifications.filter((n) => !n.read).length

  const markAsRead = (id: string) => {
    setNotifications((prev) =>
      prev.map((n) => (n.id === id ? { ...n, read: true } : n))
    )
  }

  const markAllRead = () => {
    setNotifications((prev) => prev.map((n) => ({ ...n, read: true })))
  }

  const futa = (id: string) => {
    setNotifications((prev) => prev.filter((n) => n.id !== id))
  }

  const futaZote = () => {
    setNotifications([])
  }

  const getNotification = (id: string) =>
    notifications.find((n) => n.id === id)

  const filterByKundi = (kundi: KundiNotification | 'zote') => {
    if (kundi === 'zote') return notifications
    return notifications.filter((n) => n.kundi === kundi)
  }

  const search = (q: string) => {
    const query = q.toLowerCase().trim()
    if (!query) return notifications
    return notifications.filter(
      (n) =>
        n.title.toLowerCase().includes(query) ||
        n.message.toLowerCase().includes(query) ||
        (n.maelezo?.toLowerCase().includes(query) ?? false)
    )
  }

  return (
    <NotificationContext.Provider
      value={{
        notifications,
        unreadCount,
        markAsRead,
        markAllRead,
        futa,
        futaZote,
        getNotification,
        filterByKundi,
        search,
      }}
    >
      {children}
    </NotificationContext.Provider>
  )
}

export function useNotifications() {
  const ctx = useContext(NotificationContext)
  if (!ctx)
    throw new Error('useNotifications must be used within NotificationProvider')
  return ctx
}

import { createContext, useContext, useState, type ReactNode } from 'react'
import type { OmbiMatumizi, HaliOmbi } from '../types'
import { maombiMatumizi as maombiDemo } from '../data/demo'

interface OngezaOmbiInput {
  aina: string
  kiasi: number
  maelezo: string
  simu: string
  sababu: string
  risitiUrl?: string
  aliyeomba: string
  duka: string
}

interface OmbiCtx {
  maombi: OmbiMatumizi[]
  ongezaOmbi: (data: OngezaOmbiInput) => OmbiMatumizi
  idhinisha: (id: string, maoni?: string) => void
  kataa: (id: string, maoni: string) => void
  futa: (id: string) => void
  hariri: (id: string, patch: Partial<OmbiMatumizi>) => void
  getOmbi: (id: string) => OmbiMatumizi | undefined
}

const OmbiContext = createContext<OmbiCtx | null>(null)

const MMILIKI = 'Moses Kimaro'

export function OmbiProvider({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {
  const [maombi, setMaombi] = useState<OmbiMatumizi[]>(maombiDemo)

  const ongezaOmbi: OmbiCtx['ongezaOmbi'] = (data) => {
    const ombi: OmbiMatumizi = {
      ...data,
      id: `EXP-${new Date().getFullYear()}-${String(maombi.length + 1).padStart(3, '0')}`,
      tarehe: new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0],
      hali: 'inasubiri',
    }
    setMaombi((prev) => [ombi, ...prev])
    return ombi
  }

  const idhinisha = (id: string, maoni?: string) => {
    setMaombi((prev) =>
      prev.map((m) =>
        m.id === id
          ? {
              ...m,
              hali: 'imekubaliwa' as HaliOmbi,
              maoni,
              aliyeidhinisha: MMILIKI,
              tareheIdhini: new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0],
            }
          : m
      )
    )
  }

  const kataa = (id: string, maoni: string) => {
    setMaombi((prev) =>
      prev.map((m) =>
        m.id === id
          ? {
              ...m,
              hali: 'imekataliwa' as HaliOmbi,
              maoni,
              aliyeidhinisha: MMILIKI,
              tareheIdhini: new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0],
            }
          : m
      )
    )
  }

  const futa = (id: string) => {
    setMaombi((prev) => prev.filter((m) => m.id !== id))
  }

  const hariri = (id: string, patch: Partial<OmbiMatumizi>) => {
    setMaombi((prev) =>
      prev.map((m) => (m.id === id ? { ...m, ...patch } : m))
    )
  }

  const getOmbi = (id: string) => maombi.find((m) => m.id === id)

  return (
    <OmbiContext.Provider
      value={{ maombi, ongezaOmbi, idhinisha, kataa, futa, hariri, getOmbi }}
    >
      {children}
    </OmbiContext.Provider>
  )
}

export function useMaombi() {
  const ctx = useContext(OmbiContext)
  if (!ctx) throw new Error('useMaombi must be used within OmbiProvider')
  return ctx
}


import { createContext, useContext, useState, type ReactNode } from 'react'
import type { OrderAdmin, HaliOrderAdmin } from '../types'
import { ordersAdmin as ordersDemo } from '../data/demo'

interface OrderAdminCtx {
  orders: OrderAdmin[]
  badilishaHali: (id: string, hali: HaliOrderAdmin) => void
  thibitishaMalipo: (orderId: string, malipoId: string) => void
  futa: (id: string) => void
  getOrder: (id: string) => OrderAdmin | undefined
}

const OrderAdminContext = createContext<OrderAdminCtx | null>(null)

export function OrderAdminProvider({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {
  const [orders, setOrders] = useState<OrderAdmin[]>(ordersDemo)

  const badilishaHali = (id: string, hali: HaliOrderAdmin) => {
    setOrders((prev) => prev.map((o) => (o.id === id ? { ...o, hali } : o)))
  }

  const thibitishaMalipo = (orderId: string, malipoId: string) => {
    setOrders((prev) =>
      prev.map((o) => {
        if (o.id !== orderId) return o
        return {
          ...o,
          malipo: o.malipo.map((m) =>
            m.id === malipoId ? { ...m, hali: 'imethibitishwa' } : m
          ),
        }
      })
    )
  }

  const futa = (id: string) => {
    setOrders((prev) => prev.filter((o) => o.id !== id))
  }

  const getOrder = (id: string) => orders.find((o) => o.id === id)

  return (
    <OrderAdminContext.Provider
      value={{ orders, badilishaHali, thibitishaMalipo, futa, getOrder }}
    >
      {children}
    </OrderAdminContext.Provider>
  )
}

export function useOrderAdmin() {
  const ctx = useContext(OrderAdminContext)
  if (!ctx) throw new Error('useOrderAdmin must be used within OrderAdminProvider')
  return ctx
}

import { createContext, useContext, useState, type ReactNode } from 'react'
import type { Order, Malipo, OrderItem } from '../types'

interface OrderCtx {
  orders: Order[]
  createOrder: (items: OrderItem[], total: number, malipo: Malipo[]) => Order
  getOrder: (id: string) => Order | undefined
}

const OrderContext = createContext<OrderCtx | null>(null)

const MTEOA_DEMO = 'Mjenzi Mwakyusa'
const SIMU_DEMO = '0754-111-222'

export function OrderProvider({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {
  const [orders, setOrders] = useState<Order[]>([])

  const createOrder = (
    items: OrderItem[],
    total: number,
    malipo: Malipo[]
  ): Order => {
    const order: Order = {
      id: `ORD-${Date.now().toString().slice(-6)}`,
      quotationId: `Q-2026-${Math.floor(1000 + Math.random() * 9000)}`,
      mteja: MTEOA_DEMO,
      simu: SIMU_DEMO,
      bidhaa: items,
      jumla: total,
      malipo,
      hali: 'imekamilika',
      tarehe: new Date().toLocaleString('sw-TZ'),
    }
    setOrders((prev) => [order, ...prev])
    return order
  }

  const getOrder = (id: string) => orders.find((o) => o.id === id)

  return (
    <OrderContext.Provider value={{ orders, createOrder, getOrder }}>
      {children}
    </OrderContext.Provider>
  )
}

export function useOrders() {
  const ctx = useContext(OrderContext)
  if (!ctx) throw new Error('useOrders must be used within OrderProvider')
  return ctx
}

import { createContext, useContext, useState, type ReactNode } from 'react'
import type { PendingCart } from '../types'
import { pendingCarts as cartsDemo } from '../data/demo'

interface PendingCartCtx {
  carts: PendingCart[]
  tumaNotify: (id: string) => void
  kamilisha: (id: string) => void
  getCart: (id: string) => PendingCart | undefined
}

const PendingCartContext = createContext<PendingCartCtx | null>(null)

export function PendingCartProvider({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {
  const [carts, setCarts] = useState<PendingCart[]>(cartsDemo)

  const tumaNotify = (id: string) => {
    setCarts((prev) =>
      prev.map((c) => (c.id === id ? { ...c, imetumwa: true } : c))
    )
  }

  const kamilisha = (id: string) => {
    setCarts((prev) =>
      prev.map((c) =>
        c.id === id
          ? { ...c, aina: 'imekamilika', imelipwa: c.jumla }
          : c
      )
    )
  }

  const getCart = (id: string) => carts.find((c) => c.id === id)

  return (
    <PendingCartContext.Provider value={{ carts, tumaNotify, kamilisha, getCart }}>
      {children}
    </PendingCartContext.Provider>
  )
}

export function usePendingCarts() {
  const ctx = useContext(PendingCartContext)
  if (!ctx) throw new Error('usePendingCarts must be used within PendingCartProvider')
  return ctx
}

import { createContext, useContext, useState, type ReactNode } from 'react'
import type {
  RipotiBidhaa,
  AinaRipotiBidhaa,
  HaliRipoti,
} from '../types'
import { ripotiBidhaa as ripotiDemo } from '../data/demo'

interface OngezaRipotiInput {
  bidhaaId: string
  jina: string
  sku: string
  uom: string
  kundi: string
  dukaId: string
  dukaJina: string
  aina: AinaRipotiBidhaa
  wingi: number
  beiUnunuzi: number
  sababu: string
  maelezo?: string
  pichaUrl?: string
  expiry?: string
  mtengenezaji?: string
  uzito?: string
}

interface RipotiBidhaaCtx {
  ripoti: RipotiBidhaa[]
  ongezaRipoti: (data: OngezaRipotiInput) => RipotiBidhaa
  thibitisha: (id: string) => void
  kataa: (id: string, maelezo?: string) => void
  futa: (id: string) => void
  getRipoti: (id: string) => RipotiBidhaa | undefined
}

const RipotiBidhaaContext = createContext<RipotiBidhaaCtx | null>(null)

const ALIYEINGIZA = 'Juma Hassan'
const DUKA_ID = 'd1'
const DUKA_JINA = 'Smart Hardware - Kwembe'

export function RipotiBidhaaProvider({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {
  const [ripoti, setRipoti] = useState<RipotiBidhaa[]>(ripotiDemo)

  const ongezaRipoti: RipotiBidhaaCtx['ongezaRipoti'] = (data) => {
    const r: RipotiBidhaa = {
      ...data,
      id: `RIP-${new Date().getFullYear()}-${String(ripoti.length + 5).padStart(3, '0')}`,
      thamani: data.wingi * data.beiUnunuzi,
      aliyeingiza: ALIYEINGIZA,
      dukaId: DUKA_ID,
      dukaJina: DUKA_JINA,
      tarehe: new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0],
      hali: 'inasubiri',
    } as RipotiBidhaa
    setRipoti((prev) => [r, ...prev])
    return r
  }

  const thibitisha = (id: string) => {
    setRipoti((prev) =>
      prev.map((r) => (r.id === id ? { ...r, hali: 'imethibitishwa' as HaliRipoti } : r))
    )
  }

  const kataa = (id: string, maelezo?: string) => {
    setRipoti((prev) =>
      prev.map((r) =>
        r.id === id
          ? { ...r, hali: 'imekataliwa' as HaliRipoti, maelezo: maelezo || r.maelezo }
          : r
      )
    )
  }

  const futa = (id: string) => {
    setRipoti((prev) => prev.filter((r) => r.id !== id))
  }

  const getRipoti = (id: string) => ripoti.find((r) => r.id === id)

  return (
    <RipotiBidhaaContext.Provider
      value={{ ripoti, ongezaRipoti, thibitisha, kataa, futa, getRipoti }}
    >
      {children}
    </RipotiBidhaaContext.Provider>
  )
}

export function useRipotiBidhaa() {
  const ctx = useContext(RipotiBidhaaContext)
  if (!ctx) throw new Error('useRipotiBidhaa must be used within RipotiBidhaaProvider')
  return ctx
}

import { createContext, useContext, useState, type ReactNode } from 'react'
import type {
  UingizajiVifaa,
  BidhaaMpya,
  AinaUingizaji,
  HaliUingizaji,
} from '../types'
import { uingizajiVifaa as uingizajiDemo } from '../data/demo'

// ============================================================
// Input ya kuongeza uingizaji mpya
// ============================================================
interface OngezaInput {
  aina: AinaUingizaji
  dukaId: string
  dukaJina: string
  bidhaa: BidhaaMpya[]
  aliyeingiza: string
  nafasi: 'mmiliki' | 'admin'
  maelezo?: string
}

interface UingizajiCtx {
  uingizaji: UingizajiVifaa[]
  ongezaUingizaji: (data: OngezaInput) => UingizajiVifaa
  pokea: (id: string, aliyepokea: string) => void
  kataa: (id: string, aliyepokea: string, maelezo?: string) => void
  getUingizaji: (id: string) => UingizajiVifaa | undefined
}

const UingizajiContext = createContext<UingizajiCtx | null>(null)

export function UingizajiProvider({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {
  const [uingizaji, setUingizaji] = useState<UingizajiVifaa[]>(uingizajiDemo)

  const ongezaUingizaji: UingizajiCtx['ongezaUingizaji'] = (data) => {
    const jumlaBidhaa = data.bidhaa.reduce((s, b) => s + b.stoki, 0)
    const jumlaThamani = data.bidhaa.reduce((s, b) => s + b.stoki * b.beiUnunuzi, 0)

    const u: UingizajiVifaa = {
      id: `INP-${new Date().getFullYear()}-${String(uingizaji.length + 1).padStart(3, '0')}`,
      aina: data.aina,
      dukaId: data.dukaId,
      dukaJina: data.dukaJina,
      bidhaa: data.bidhaa,
      jumlaBidhaa,
      jumlaThamani,
      aliyeingiza: data.aliyeingiza,
      nafasi: data.nafasi,
      tarehe: new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0],
      hali: 'inasubiri',
      maelezo: data.maelezo,
    }
    setUingizaji((prev) => [u, ...prev])
    return u
  }

  const pokea = (id: string, aliyepokea: string) => {
    setUingizaji((prev) =>
      prev.map((u) =>
        u.id === id
          ? {
              ...u,
              hali: 'imepokelewa' as HaliUingizaji,
              aliyepokea,
              tareheKupokea: new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0],
            }
          : u
      )
    )
  }

  const kataa = (id: string, aliyepokea: string, maelezo?: string) => {
    setUingizaji((prev) =>
      prev.map((u) =>
        u.id === id
          ? {
              ...u,
              hali: 'imekataliwa' as HaliUingizaji,
              aliyepokea,
              tareheKupokea: new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0],
              maelezo: maelezo || u.maelezo,
            }
          : u
      )
    )
  }

  const getUingizaji = (id: string) => uingizaji.find((u) => u.id === id)

  return (
    <UingizajiContext.Provider value={{ uingizaji, ongezaUingizaji, pokea, kataa, getUingizaji }}>
      {children}
    </UingizajiContext.Provider>
  )
}

export function useUingizaji() {
  const ctx = useContext(UingizajiContext)
  if (!ctx) throw new Error('useUingizaji must be used within UingizajiProvider')
  return ctx
}


import { createContext, useContext, useState, type ReactNode } from 'react'
import type { MalipoZaka } from '../types'

interface ZakaCtx {
  malipo: MalipoZaka[]
  rekodiMalipo: (data: Omit<MalipoZaka, 'id'>) => MalipoZaka
  futaMalipo: (id: string) => void
}

const ZakaContext = createContext<ZakaCtx | null>(null)

const MALIPO_DEMO: MalipoZaka[] = [
  {
    id: 'ZAKA-001',
    tarehe: '2026-11-08',
    kiasi: 50000,
    aina: 'zaka',
    risiti: 'RIS-001',
    maelezo: 'Zaka ya wiki iliyopita',
  },
]

export function ZakaProvider({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {
  const [malipo, setMalipo] = useState<MalipoZaka[]>(MALIPO_DEMO)

  const rekodiMalipo: ZakaCtx['rekodiMalipo'] = (data) => {
    const m: MalipoZaka = {
      ...data,
      id: `ZAKA-${Date.now()}`,
    }
    setMalipo((prev) => [m, ...prev])
    return m
  }

  const futaMalipo = (id: string) => {
    setMalipo((prev) => prev.filter((m) => m.id !== id))
  }

  return (
    <ZakaContext.Provider value={{ malipo, rekodiMalipo, futaMalipo }}>
      {children}
    </ZakaContext.Provider>
  )
}

export function useZaka() {
  const ctx = useContext(ZakaContext)
  if (!ctx) throw new Error('useZaka must be used within ZakaProvider')
  return ctx
}

import { useState, useRef, useEffect, useMemo } from 'react'
import { useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom'
import {
  Search,
  Bell,
  Menu,
  Settings,
  User,
  LogOut,
  ChevronDown,
  X,
  ShoppingCart,
  Plus,
  Minus,
  Trash2,
  AlertTriangle,
  CheckCircle2,
  Info,
  XCircle,
  ArrowRight,
} from 'lucide-react'
import { searchEverything, type SearchType } from '../utils/search'
import { useCart } from '../context/CartContext'
import { useNotifications } from '../context/NotificationContext'
import { iconForKundi } from '../utils/icons'

type Role = 'mteja' | 'muuzaji' | 'mmiliki'

interface TopBarProps {
  role: Role
  onMenuClick: () => void
}

interface UserProfile {
  name: string
  email: string
  initials: string
}

const USERS: Record<Role, UserProfile> = {
  mteja: { name: 'Mjenzi Mwakyusa', email: 'mwakyusa@email.com', initials: 'MM' },
  muuzaji: { name: 'Juma Hassan', email: 'juma@smarthardware.co.tz', initials: 'JH' },
  mmiliki: { name: 'Moses Kimaro', email: 'moses@smarthardware.co.tz', initials: 'MK' },
}

const ROLE_LABEL: Record<Role, string> = {
  mteja: 'Mteja',
  muuzaji: 'Muuzaji',
  mmiliki: 'Mmiliki',
}

const TYPE_LABEL: Record<SearchType, string> = {
  bidhaa: 'Bidhaa',
  duka: 'Duka',
  mtumiaji: 'Mtumiaji',
  deni: 'Deni',
  gharama: 'Gharama',
}

export default function TopBar({ role, onMenuClick }: TopBarProps) {
  const navigate = useNavigate()
  const user = USERS[role]

  const [query, setQuery] = useState('')
  const [searchOpen, setSearchOpen] = useState(false)

  const [notifOpen, setNotifOpen] = useState(false)

  const [cartOpen, setCartOpen] = useState(false)
  const cart = useCart()
  const showCart = role === 'mteja' || role === 'muuzaji'

  const [profileOpen, setProfileOpen] = useState(false)

  const {
    notifications: notifList,
    unreadCount,
    markAsRead,
    markAllRead,
  } = useNotifications()

  const searchRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null)
  const notifRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null)
  const cartRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null)
  const profileRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null)

  const results = useMemo(() => searchEverything(query), [query])

  useEffect(() => {
    function handleClick(e: MouseEvent) {
      const t = e.target as Node
      if (searchRef.current && !searchRef.current.contains(t)) setSearchOpen(false)
      if (notifRef.current && !notifRef.current.contains(t)) setNotifOpen(false)
      if (cartRef.current && !cartRef.current.contains(t)) setCartOpen(false)
      if (profileRef.current && !profileRef.current.contains(t)) setProfileOpen(false)
    }
    document.addEventListener('mousedown', handleClick)
    return () => document.removeEventListener('mousedown', handleClick)
  }, [])

  useEffect(() => {
    function onKey(e: KeyboardEvent) {
      if (e.key === 'Escape') {
        setSearchOpen(false)
        setNotifOpen(false)
        setCartOpen(false)
        setProfileOpen(false)
      }
    }
    document.addEventListener('keydown', onKey)
    return () => document.removeEventListener('keydown', onKey)
  }, [])

  const handleSearchResult = (route?: string) => {
    if (route) navigate(route)
    setQuery('')
    setSearchOpen(false)
  }

  const toggleNotif = () => {
    setNotifOpen((v) => !v)
    setCartOpen(false)
    setProfileOpen(false)
  }

  const toggleCart = () => {
    setCartOpen((v) => !v)
    setNotifOpen(false)
    setProfileOpen(false)
  }

  const toggleProfile = () => {
    setProfileOpen((v) => !v)
    setNotifOpen(false)
    setCartOpen(false)
  }

  // ============================================================
  // Notification click - navigate
  // ============================================================
  const handleNotifClick = (n: typeof notifList[0]) => {
    markAsRead(n.id)
    setNotifOpen(false)
    if (n.route) {
      navigate(n.route)
    } else {
      navigate(`/${role}/notifications/${n.id}`)
    }
  }

  const handleOnaZote = () => {
    setNotifOpen(false)
    navigate(`/${role}/notifications`)
  }

  const goToQuotation = () => {
    setCartOpen(false)
    navigate('/mteja/quotation')
  }

  const goToBidhaa = () => {
    setCartOpen(false)
    navigate(role === 'muuzaji' ? '/muuzaji' : '/mteja/bidhaa')
  }

  const notifIcon = (type: string) => {
    switch (type) {
      case 'danger':
        return <XCircle size={16} className="text-red-500" />
      case 'warning':
        return <AlertTriangle size={16} className="text-amber-500" />
      case 'success':
        return <CheckCircle2 size={16} className="text-emerald-500" />
      default:
        return <Info size={16} className="text-brand-500" />
    }
  }

  return (
    <header className="sticky top-0 z-30 bg-white border-b border-slate-200">
      <div className="h-16 px-3 md:px-6 flex items-center gap-2 md:gap-3">
        <button
          onClick={onMenuClick}
          className="lg:hidden p-2 -ml-1 rounded-lg hover:bg-slate-100 shrink-0"
          aria-label="Fungua menyu"
        >
          <Menu size={22} />
        </button>

        {/* ===== SEARCH ===== */}
        <div ref={searchRef} className="flex-1 min-w-0 relative">
          <div className="relative">
            <Search
              size={18}
              className="absolute left-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-slate-400 pointer-events-none"
            />
            <input
              value={query}
              onChange={(e) => {
                setQuery(e.target.value)
                setSearchOpen(true)
              }}
              onFocus={() => setSearchOpen(true)}
              placeholder="Tafuta bidhaa, duka, mtu, deni..."
              className="w-full pl-10 pr-9 py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-slate-100 border border-transparent focus:bg-white focus:border-brand-500 outline-none text-sm"
            />
            {query && (
              <button
                onClick={() => {
                  setQuery('')
                  setSearchOpen(false)
                }}
                className="absolute right-2 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 p-1 rounded-md hover:bg-slate-200"
                aria-label="Futa"
              >
                <X size={14} />
              </button>
            )}
          </div>

          {searchOpen && query && (
            <div className="absolute top-full mt-2 left-0 right-0 bg-white rounded-xl border border-slate-200 shadow-xl max-h-[70vh] overflow-auto">
              {results.length === 0 ? (
                <div className="p-6 text-center text-sm text-slate-500">
                  Hakuna matokeo ya "
                  <span className="font-medium text-slate-700">{query}</span>"
                </div>
              ) : (
                <div className="py-1">
                  {results.map((r, i) => (
                    <button
                      key={`${r.type}-${i}`}
                      onClick={() => handleSearchResult(r.route)}
                      className="w-full px-3 py-2.5 flex items-center gap-3 hover:bg-slate-50 text-left"
                    >
                      <span className="w-8 h-8 rounded-lg bg-slate-100 text-slate-600 grid place-items-center shrink-0">
                        <r.Icon size={16} />
                      </span>
                      <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
                        <div className="text-sm font-medium truncate">{r.title}</div>
                        <div className="text-xs text-slate-500 truncate">{r.subtitle}</div>
                      </div>
                      <span className="hidden sm:inline text-[10px] uppercase font-semibold text-slate-400 bg-slate-100 px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded shrink-0">
                        {TYPE_LABEL[r.type]}
                      </span>
                    </button>
                  ))}
                </div>
              )}
            </div>
          )}
        </div>

        {/* ===== RIGHT ACTIONS ===== */}
        <div className="flex items-center gap-0.5 md:gap-1 shrink-0">
          {/* CART */}
          {showCart && (
            <div ref={cartRef} className="relative">
              <button
                onClick={toggleCart}
                className="relative flex items-center gap-2 px-2 md:px-2.5 py-2 rounded-lg hover:bg-slate-100"
                aria-label="Cart"
              >
                <div className="relative">
                  <ShoppingCart size={20} />
                  {cart.count > 0 && (
                    <span className="absolute -top-2 -right-2 min-w-4 h-4 px-1 bg-brand-600 text-white text-[10px] rounded-full grid place-items-center font-bold">
                      {cart.count > 99 ? '99+' : cart.count}
                    </span>
                  )}
                </div>
                {cart.total > 0 && (
                  <span className="hidden md:inline text-xs font-semibold text-brand-700 bg-brand-50 px-2 py-1 rounded-md whitespace-nowrap">
                    Tsh {cart.total.toLocaleString()}
                  </span>
                )}
              </button>

              {cartOpen && (
                <div className="absolute right-0 top-full mt-2 w-[calc(100vw-1.5rem)] sm:w-96 max-w-sm bg-white rounded-xl border border-slate-200 shadow-xl overflow-hidden">
                  <div className="p-3 border-b border-slate-100 flex justify-between items-center">
                    <div>
                      <div className="font-semibold text-sm">Cart Yako</div>
                      <div className="text-xs text-slate-500">
                        {cart.count} bidhaa - Tsh {cart.total.toLocaleString()}
                      </div>
                    </div>
                    {cart.detailed.length > 0 && (
                      <button
                        onClick={cart.clear}
                        className="text-xs text-red-600 hover:underline"
                      >
                        Ondoa zote
                      </button>
                    )}
                  </div>

                  <div className="max-h-80 overflow-auto divide-y divide-slate-100">
                    {cart.detailed.length === 0 ? (
                      <div className="p-8 text-center text-sm text-slate-500">
                        <ShoppingCart size={32} className="mx-auto mb-2 text-slate-300" />
                        Cart yako ni tupu
                      </div>
                    ) : (
                      cart.detailed.map((l) => {
                        const Icon = iconForKundi(l.iconKey)
                        return (
                          <div key={l.id} className="p-3 flex gap-3">
                            <div className="w-10 h-10 rounded-lg bg-slate-100 text-slate-600 grid place-items-center shrink-0">
                              <Icon size={18} />
                            </div>
                            <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
                              <div className="text-sm font-medium truncate">{l.jina}</div>
                              <div className="text-xs text-slate-500">
                                Tsh {l.bei.toLocaleString()} / {l.uom}
                              </div>
                              <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 mt-2">
                                <button
                                  onClick={() => cart.dec(l.id)}
                                  className="w-6 h-6 rounded-md bg-slate-100 hover:bg-slate-200 grid place-items-center"
                                  aria-label="Punguza"
                                >
                                  <Minus size={12} />
                                </button>
                                <span className="text-sm font-semibold w-6 text-center">
                                  {l.wingi}
                                </span>
                                <button
                                  onClick={() => cart.inc(l.id)}
                                  className="w-6 h-6 rounded-md bg-slate-100 hover:bg-slate-200 grid place-items-center"
                                  aria-label="Ongeza"
                                >
                                  <Plus size={12} />
                                </button>
                                <button
                                  onClick={() => cart.remove(l.id)}
                                  className="ml-auto p-1 rounded-md text-red-500 hover:bg-red-50"
                                  aria-label="Ondoa"
                                >
                                  <Trash2 size={14} />
                                </button>
                              </div>
                            </div>
                            <div className="text-sm font-semibold self-start whitespace-nowrap">
                              Tsh {l.jumla.toLocaleString()}
                            </div>
                          </div>
                        )
                      })
                    )}
                  </div>

                  <div className="p-3 border-t border-slate-100 space-y-2">
                    <div className="flex justify-between text-sm">
                      <span>Jumla</span>
                      <span className="font-bold">Tsh {cart.total.toLocaleString()}</span>
                    </div>
                    <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2">
                      <button
                        onClick={goToBidhaa}
                        className="px-3 py-2 rounded-lg bg-slate-100 text-sm hover:bg-slate-200"
                      >
                        Endelea Kununua
                      </button>
                      <button
                        onClick={goToQuotation}
                        disabled={cart.detailed.length === 0}
                        className="px-3 py-2 rounded-lg bg-brand-600 text-white text-sm font-semibold hover:bg-brand-700 disabled:opacity-50 disabled:cursor-not-allowed"
                      >
                        Nenda Quotation
                      </button>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                </div>
              )}
            </div>
          )}

          {/* NOTIFICATIONS */}
          <div ref={notifRef} className="relative">
            <button
              onClick={toggleNotif}
              className="relative p-2 rounded-lg hover:bg-slate-100"
              aria-label="Taarifa"
            >
              <Bell size={20} />
              {unreadCount > 0 && (
                <span className="absolute top-1 right-1 min-w-4 h-4 px-1 bg-red-500 text-white text-[10px] rounded-full grid place-items-center font-bold">
                  {unreadCount > 99 ? '99+' : unreadCount}
                </span>
              )}
            </button>

            {notifOpen && (
              <div className="absolute right-0 top-full mt-2 w-[calc(100vw-1.5rem)] sm:w-96 max-w-sm bg-white rounded-xl border border-slate-200 shadow-xl overflow-hidden">
                <div className="p-3 border-b border-slate-100 flex justify-between items-center">
                  <div className="font-semibold text-sm">Taarifa</div>
                  {unreadCount > 0 && (
                    <button
                      onClick={markAllRead}
                      className="text-xs text-brand-600 hover:underline"
                    >
                      Weka zote kama zimesomwa
                    </button>
                  )}
                </div>
                <div className="max-h-96 overflow-auto divide-y divide-slate-100">
                  {notifList.length === 0 ? (
                    <div className="p-8 text-center text-sm text-slate-500">
                      <Bell size={32} className="mx-auto mb-2 text-slate-300" />
                      Hakuna taarifa
                    </div>
                  ) : (
                    notifList.map((n) => (
                      <button
                        key={n.id}
                        onClick={() => handleNotifClick(n)}
                        className={`w-full text-left p-3 hover:bg-slate-50 transition ${
                          !n.read ? 'bg-brand-50/40' : ''
                        }`}
                      >
                        <div className="flex gap-2">
                          <div className="mt-0.5 shrink-0">{notifIcon(n.type)}</div>
                          <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
                            <div className="text-sm font-medium flex items-center gap-2">
                              <span className="truncate">{n.title}</span>
                              {!n.read && (
                                <span className="w-1.5 h-1.5 rounded-full bg-brand-600 shrink-0" />
                              )}
                            </div>
                            <div className="text-xs text-slate-500 mt-0.5">{n.message}</div>
                            <div className="text-[10px] text-slate-400 mt-1">{n.time}</div>
                          </div>
                          <ArrowRight size={14} className="text-slate-300 shrink-0 mt-1" />
                        </div>
                      </button>
                    ))
                  )}
                </div>
                <div className="p-2 border-t border-slate-100 text-center">
                  <button
                    onClick={handleOnaZote}
                    className="text-xs text-brand-600 hover:underline"
                  >
                    Ona taarifa zote
                  </button>
                </div>
              </div>
            )}
          </div>

          {/* PROFILE */}
          <div ref={profileRef} className="relative">
            <button
              onClick={toggleProfile}
              className="flex items-center gap-2 p-1.5 rounded-lg hover:bg-slate-100"
              aria-label="Wasifu"
            >
              <div className="w-8 h-8 rounded-full bg-brand-600 text-white grid place-items-center text-xs font-bold">
                {user.initials}
              </div>
              <div className="hidden md:block text-left">
                <div className="text-xs font-semibold leading-tight">{user.name}</div>
                <div className="text-[10px] text-slate-500 leading-tight">
                  {ROLE_LABEL[role]}
                </div>
              </div>
              <ChevronDown size={14} className="hidden md:block text-slate-400" />
            </button>

            {profileOpen && (
              <div className="absolute right-0 top-full mt-2 w-60 bg-white rounded-xl border border-slate-200 shadow-xl overflow-hidden">
                <div className="p-3 border-b border-slate-100">
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                    <div className="w-10 h-10 rounded-full bg-brand-600 text-white grid place-items-center text-sm font-bold">
                      {user.initials}
                    </div>
                    <div className="min-w-0">
                      <div className="text-sm font-semibold truncate">{user.name}</div>
                      <div className="text-xs text-slate-500 truncate">{user.email}</div>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                </div>
                <div className="p-1">
                  <button className="w-full px-3 py-2 flex items-center gap-2 text-sm rounded-lg hover:bg-slate-100">
                    <User size={16} className="text-slate-500" /> Wasifu Wangu
                  </button>
                  <button className="w-full px-3 py-2 flex items-center gap-2 text-sm rounded-lg hover:bg-slate-100">
                    <Settings size={16} className="text-slate-500" /> Mipangilio
                  </button>
                  <div className="border-t border-slate-100 my-1" />
                  <button
                    onClick={() => navigate('/')}
                    className="w-full px-3 py-2 flex items-center gap-2 text-sm rounded-lg hover:bg-red-50 text-red-600"
                  >
                    <LogOut size={16} /> Toka (Logout)
                  </button>
                </div>
              </div>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </header>
  )
}

export default function StatCard({
  label, value, sub, color = 'brand',
}: { label: string; value: string; sub?: string; color?: 'brand' | 'green' | 'amber' | 'red' }) {
  const colors = {
    brand: 'bg-brand-50 text-brand-700',
    green: 'bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-700',
    amber: 'bg-amber-50 text-amber-700',
    red: 'bg-red-50 text-red-700',
  }
  return (
    <div className="bg-white rounded-xl border border-slate-200 p-5">
      <div className={`inline-flex px-2 py-1 rounded-md text-xs font-medium ${colors[color]}`}>{label}</div>
      <div className="text-2xl font-bold mt-2">{value}</div>
      {sub && <div className="text-xs text-slate-500 mt-1">{sub}</div>}
    </div>
  )
}

      { to: '/mmiliki/store', label: 'store', icon: HouseCog },

     { to: '/', label: 'toka', icon: LogOutIcon }, 
   

],
}

const roleLabels = {
  mteja: 'Mteja Portal',
  muuzaji: 'Muuzaji Duka',
  mmiliki: 'Mmiliki Dashboard',
}

interface SidebarProps {
  role: 'mteja' | 'muuzaji' | 'mmiliki'
  open: boolean
  onClose: () => void
}

export default function Sidebar({ role, open, onClose }: SidebarProps) {
  const navigate = useNavigate()

  const content = (
    <>
      <div className="p-5 border-b border-slate-200 flex items-center justify-between shrink-0">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
          <div className="w-9 h-9 rounded-lg bg-brand-600 text-white grid place-items-center font-bold">S</div>
          <div>
            <div className="font-semibold text-sm">SMART HARDWARE</div>
            <div className="text-xs text-slate-500">{roleLabels[role]}</div>
          </div>
        </div>
        <button onClick={onClose} className="lg:hidden p-1.5 rounded-lg hover:bg-slate-100">
          <X size={18} />
        </button>
      </div>

      {/* Nav — scrollable */}
      <nav className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto p-3 space-y-1">
        {navByRole[role].map((n) => (
          <NavLink
            key={n.to}
            to={n.to}
            end={n.to === `/${role}`}
            onClick={onClose}
            className={({ isActive }) =>
              `flex items-center gap-3 px-3 py-2.5 rounded-lg text-sm font-medium transition ${
                isActive ? 'bg-brand-600 text-white' : 'text-slate-700 hover:bg-slate-100'
              }`
            }
          >
            <n.icon size={18} />
            {n.label}
          </NavLink>
        ))}
      </nav>

      <div className="p-3 border-t border-slate-200 shrink-0">
        <button
          onClick={() => navigate('/')}
          className="w-full text-sm text-slate-600 hover:text-brand-600 px-3 py-2 rounded-lg hover:bg-slate-100 text-left"
        >
          <ArrowBigLeft/>Badilisha Mtumiaji
        </button>
      </div>
    </>
  )

  return (
    <>
      {/* Desktop sidebar */}
      <aside className="hidden lg:flex w-64 bg-white border-r border-slate-200 flex-col h-screen sticky top-0">
        {content}
      </aside>

      {/* Mobile drawer */}
      {open && (
        <div className="lg:hidden fixed inset-0 z-50">
          <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-black/40" onClick={onClose} />
          <aside className="absolute left-0 top-0 bottom-0 w-72 bg-white flex flex-col shadow-2xl">
            {content}
          </aside>
        </div>
      )}
    </>
  )
}

import { useState } from 'react'
import {
  X,
  Smartphone,
  Banknote,
  Building2,
  Check,
  AlertCircle,
  Loader2,
} from 'lucide-react'
import type { Malipo, NjiaMalipo } from '../types'
import { NJIA_MALIPO } from '../data/demo'

interface PaymentModalProps {
  open: boolean
  jumla: number
  onClose: () => void
  onSuccess: (malipo: Malipo) => void
}

const NJIA_ICONS: Record<NjiaMalipo, React.ElementType> = {
  mpesa: Smartphone,
  tigopesa: Smartphone,
  airtel: Smartphone,
  halopesa: Smartphone,
  cash: Banknote,
  benki: Building2,
}

export default function PaymentModal({
  open,
  jumla,
  onClose,
  onSuccess,
}: PaymentModalProps) {
  const [njia, setNjia] = useState<NjiaMalipo>('mpesa')
  const [simu, setSimu] = useState('')
  const [kumbukumbu, setKumbukumbu] = useState('')
  const [kiasi, setKiasi] = useState(jumla)
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false)
  const [error, setError] = useState('')

  if (!open) return null

  const isMobile = njia !== 'cash' && njia !== 'benki'

  const validate = (): string => {
    if (kiasi <= 0) return 'Kiasi kinapaswa kuwa zaidi ya sifuri'
    if (kiasi > jumla) return 'Kiasi hakiwezi kuzidi jumla'
    if (isMobile) {
      if (!/^0[67]\d{2}-?\d{3}-?\d{3}$/.test(simu.replace(/\s/g, ''))) {
        return 'Weka namba sahihi ya simu (mfano: 0754-123-456)'
      }
      if (kumbukumbu.trim().length < 6) {
        return 'Kumbukumbu namba iwe angalau herufi/namba 6'
      }
    }
    return ''
  }

  const handleSubmit = (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault()
    const err = validate()
    if (err) {
      setError(err)
      return
    }
    setError('')
    setLoading(true)

    setTimeout(() => {
      const malipo: Malipo = {
        id: `M-${Date.now()}`,
        njia,
        simu: isMobile ? simu : 'Cash / Benki',
        kumbukumbu: isMobile ? kumbukumbu.trim().toUpperCase() : 'CASH',
        kiasi,
        tarehe: new Date().toLocaleString('sw-TZ'),
        hali: 'imethibitishwa',
      }
      onSuccess(malipo)
      setLoading(false)
      setSimu('')
      setKumbukumbu('')
      setKiasi(jumla)
      onClose()
    }, 1200)
  }

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-end sm:items-center justify-center p-0 sm:p-4">
      <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-black/40" onClick={onClose} />

      <div className="relative bg-white w-full sm:max-w-lg rounded-t-2xl sm:rounded-2xl shadow-2xl max-h-[92vh] overflow-y-auto">
        <div className="sticky top-0 bg-white border-b border-slate-200 px-4 py-3 flex justify-between items-center">
          <div>
            <div className="font-semibold">Lipa kwa Namba ya Simu</div>
            <div className="text-xs text-slate-500">
              Jumla: Tsh {jumla.toLocaleString()}
            </div>
          </div>
          <button
            onClick={onClose}
            className="p-1.5 rounded-lg hover:bg-slate-100"
            aria-label="Funga"
          >
            <X size={18} />
          </button>
        </div>

        <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="p-4 space-y-4">
          <div>
            <label className="text-sm font-medium text-slate-700">
              Njia ya Malipo
            </label>
            <div className="grid grid-cols-2 sm:grid-cols-3 gap-2 mt-2">
              {NJIA_MALIPO.map((n) => {
                const Icon = NJIA_ICONS[n.key]
                const active = njia === n.key
                return (
                  <button
                    key={n.key}
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => setNjia(n.key)}
                    className={`flex items-center gap-2 px-3 py-2.5 rounded-lg border text-sm transition ${
                      active
                        ? 'border-brand-500 bg-brand-50 text-brand-700 font-semibold'
                        : 'border-slate-200 text-slate-700 hover:bg-slate-50'
                    }`}
                  >
                    <Icon size={16} />
                    {n.label}
                  </button>
                )
              })}
            </div>
          </div>

          {isMobile && (
            <>
              <div>
                <label className="text-sm font-medium text-slate-700">
                  Namba ya Simu ya Mlipaji
                </label>
                <input
                  value={simu}
                  onChange={(e) => setSimu(e.target.value)}
                  placeholder="0754-123-456"
                  inputMode="tel"
                  className="mt-1 w-full px-3 py-2.5 rounded-lg border border-slate-200 focus:border-brand-500 outline-none text-sm"
                />
              </div>

              <div>
                <label className="text-sm font-medium text-slate-700">
                  Kumbukumbu Namba (Transaction ID)
                </label>
                <input
                  value={kumbukumbu}
                  onChange={(e) => setKumbukumbu(e.target.value.toUpperCase())}
                  placeholder="Mfano: QGR7XK2P9L"
                  className="mt-1 w-full px-3 py-2.5 rounded-lg border border-slate-200 focus:border-brand-500 outline-none text-sm font-mono tracking-wide"
                />
                <div className="text-xs text-slate-500 mt-1 flex items-start gap-1">
                  <AlertCircle size={12} className="mt-0.5 shrink-0" />
                  Utapata kumbukumbu hii kwenye SMS ya malipo kutoka kwa mtandao wako.
                </div>
              </div>
            </>
          )}

          <div>
            <label className="text-sm font-medium text-slate-700">
              Kiasi Unacholipa (Tsh)
            </label>
            <input
              type="number"
              value={kiasi}
              onChange={(e) => setKiasi(Number(e.target.value))}
              max={jumla}
              min={1}
              className="mt-1 w-full px-3 py-2.5 rounded-lg border border-slate-200 focus:border-brand-500 outline-none text-sm"
            />
            <div className="flex gap-2 mt-2">
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setKiasi(jumla)}
                className="text-xs px-2 py-1 rounded-md bg-slate-100 hover:bg-slate-200"
              >
                Lipa Zote
              </button>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setKiasi(Math.floor(jumla / 2))}
                className="text-xs px-2 py-1 rounded-md bg-slate-100 hover:bg-slate-200"
              >
                Nusu
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>

          {error && (
            <div className="flex items-start gap-2 p-3 rounded-lg bg-red-50 text-red-700 text-sm">
              <AlertCircle size={16} className="mt-0.5 shrink-0" />
              {error}
            </div>
          )}

          <div className="border-t border-slate-200 pt-3 flex flex-col sm:flex-row gap-2">
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={onClose}
              className="px-4 py-2.5 rounded-lg bg-slate-100 text-sm hover:bg-slate-200 order-2 sm:order-1"
            >
              Ghairi
            </button>
            <button
              type="submit"
              disabled={loading}
              className="flex-1 inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-2 px-4 py-2.5 rounded-lg bg-emerald-600 text-white text-sm font-semibold hover:bg-emerald-700 disabled:opacity-60 order-1 sm:order-2"
            >
              {loading ? (
                <>
                  <Loader2 size={16} className="animate-spin" />
                  Inathibitisha...
                </>
              ) : (
                <>
                  <Check size={16} />
                  Thibitisha Malipo
                </>
              )}
            </button>
          </div>
        </form>
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}


import { useState } from 'react'
import { Outlet } from 'react-router-dom'
import Sidebar from './Sidebar'
import TopBar from './TopBar'
import Footer from './Footer'

export default function Layout({ role }: { role: 'mteja' | 'muuzaji' | 'mmiliki' }) {
  const [sidebarOpen, setSidebarOpen] = useState(false)

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen flex bg-slate-50">
      <Sidebar role={role} open={sidebarOpen} onClose={() => setSidebarOpen(false)} />

      <div className="flex-1 flex flex-col min-w-0 min-h-screen">
        <TopBar role={role} onMenuClick={() => setSidebarOpen(true)} />

        <main className="flex-1 p-4 md:p-6 lg:p-8 overflow-x-hidden">
          <Outlet />
        </main>

        <Footer />
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}

import { useEffect, useState } from 'react'
import { Wifi, WifiOff, Heart } from 'lucide-react'

export default function Footer() {
  const [online, setOnline] = useState(navigator.onLine)
  const [now, setNow] = useState(new Date())

  useEffect(() => {
    const onOnline = () => setOnline(true)
    const onOffline = () => setOnline(false)
    window.addEventListener('online', onOnline)
    window.addEventListener('offline', onOffline)

    const timer = setInterval(() => setNow(new Date()), 1000)

    return () => {
      window.removeEventListener('online', onOnline)
      window.removeEventListener('offline', onOffline)
      clearInterval(timer)
    }
  }, [])

  const time = now.toLocaleTimeString('sw-TZ', {
    hour: '2-digit',
    minute: '2-digit',
    second: '2-digit',
  })
  const date = now.toLocaleDateString('sw-TZ', {
    day: '2-digit',
    month: 'short',
    year: 'numeric',
  })

  return (
    <footer className="border-t border-slate-200 bg-white">
      <div className="px-4 md:px-6 py-3 flex flex-col sm:flex-row items-center justify-between gap-2 text-xs text-slate-500">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
          <span className="font-semibold text-slate-700">SMART HARDWARE</span>
          <span className="text-slate-300">|</span>
          <span>v1.0.0</span>
          <span className="text-slate-300">|</span>
          <span className="hidden sm:inline">
            {date} - {time}
          </span>
        </div>

        <div className="flex items-center gap-4">
          <button className="hover:text-brand-600">Msaada</button>
          <button className="hover:text-brand-600">Sheria</button>
          <span className="flex items-center gap-1">
            {online ? (
              <>
                <Wifi size={12} className="text-emerald-500" />
                <span className="text-emerald-600 font-medium">Online</span>
              </>
            ) : (
              <>
                <WifiOff size={12} className="text-red-500" />
                <span className="text-red-600 font-medium">Offline</span>
              </>
            )}
          </span>
        </div>

        <div className="flex items-center gap-1">
          <span className="hidden md:inline">Imetengenezwa kwa</span>
          <Heart size={12} className="text-red-500 fill-red-500" />
          <span className="hidden md:inline">Tanzania</span>
          <span className="md:hidden">2026</span>
        </div>
      </div>
    </footer>
  )
}

import { useState, useRef, useEffect } from 'react'
import { useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom'
import { ShoppingCart, Plus, Minus, Trash2, X } from 'lucide-react'
import { useCart } from '../context/CartContext'

export default function CartButton() {
  const navigate = useNavigate()
  const { detailed, count, total, inc, dec, remove, clear } = useCart()
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false)
  const ref = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null)

  useEffect(() => {
    function onClick(e: MouseEvent) {
      if (ref.current && !ref.current.contains(e.target as Node)) setOpen(false)
    }
    document.addEventListener('mousedown', onClick)
    return () => document.removeEventListener('mousedown', onClick)
  }, [])

  return (
    <div ref={ref} className="relative">
      <button
        onClick={() => setOpen((v) => !v)}
        className="relative flex items-center gap-2 px-2.5 py-2 rounded-lg hover:bg-slate-100"
        aria-label="Cart"
      >
        <div className="relative">
          <ShoppingCart size={20} />
          {count > 0 && (
            <span className="absolute -top-2 -right-2 min-w-4 h-4 px-1 bg-brand-600 text-white text-[10px] rounded-full grid place-items-center font-bold">
              {count}
            </span>
          )}
        </div>
        {total > 0 && (
          <span className="hidden md:inline text-xs font-semibold text-brand-700 bg-brand-50 px-2 py-1 rounded-md">
            Tsh {total.toLocaleString()}
          </span>
        )}
      </button>

      {open && (
        <div className="absolute right-0 top-full mt-2 w-[320px] sm:w-96 bg-white rounded-xl border border-slate-200 shadow-xl overflow-hidden z-40">
          <div className="p-3 border-b border-slate-100 flex justify-between items-center">
            <div>
              <div className="font-semibold text-sm">Cart Yako</div>
              <div className="text-xs text-slate-500">{count} bidhaa • Tsh {total.toLocaleString()}</div>
            </div>
            {detailed.length > 0 && (
              <button onClick={clear} className="text-xs text-red-600 hover:underline">Ondoa zote</button>
            )}
          </div>

          <div className="max-h-80 overflow-auto divide-y divide-slate-100">
            {detailed.length === 0 ? (
              <div className="p-8 text-center text-sm text-slate-500">
                <ShoppingCart size={32} className="mx-auto mb-2 text-slate-300" />
                Cart yako ni tupu
              </div>
            ) : (
              detailed.map((l) => (
                <div key={l.id} className="p-3 flex gap-3">
                  <div className="text-2xl">{l.picha}</div>
                  <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
                    <div className="text-sm font-medium truncate">{l.jina}</div>
                    <div className="text-xs text-slate-500">Tsh {l.bei.toLocaleString()} / {l.uom}</div>
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mt-2">
                      <button
                        onClick={() => dec(l.id)}
                        className="w-6 h-6 rounded-md bg-slate-100 hover:bg-slate-200 grid place-items-center"
                      >
                        <Minus size={12} />
                      </button>
                      <span className="text-sm font-semibold w-6 text-center">{l.wingi}</span>
                      <button
                        onClick={() => inc(l.id)}
                        className="w-6 h-6 rounded-md bg-slate-100 hover:bg-slate-200 grid place-items-center"
                      >
                        <Plus size={12} />
                      </button>
                      <button
                        onClick={() => remove(l.id)}
                        className="ml-auto p-1 rounded-md text-red-500 hover:bg-red-50"
                        aria-label="Ondoa"
                      >
                        <Trash2 size={14} />
                      </button>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                  <div className="text-sm font-semibold self-start">Tsh {l.jumla.toLocaleString()}</div>
                </div>
              ))
            )}
          </div>

          <div className="p-3 border-t border-slate-100 space-y-2">
            <div className="flex justify-between text-sm">
              <span>Jumla</span>
              <span className="font-bold">Tsh {total.toLocaleString()}</span>
            </div>
            <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2">
              <button
                onClick={() => { setOpen(false); navigate('/mteja') }}
                className="px-3 py-2 rounded-lg bg-slate-100 text-sm hover:bg-slate-200"
              >
                Endelea Kununua
              </button>
              <button
                onClick={() => { setOpen(false); navigate('/mteja/quotation') }}
                disabled={detailed.length === 0}
                className="px-3 py-2 rounded-lg bg-brand-600 text-white text-sm font-semibold hover:bg-brand-700 disabled:opacity-50 disabled:cursor-not-allowed"
              >
                Nenda Quotation
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  )
}

export default function Badge({ children, color = 'slate' }: { children: React.ReactNode; color?: 'slate' | 'green' | 'red' | 'amber' }) {
  const map = {
    slate: 'bg-slate-100 text-slate-700',
    green: 'bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-700',
    red: 'bg-red-100 text-red-700',
    amber: 'bg-amber-100 text-amber-700',
  }
  return <span className={`px-2 py-0.5 rounded-md text-xs font-medium ${map[color]}`}>{children}</span>
}

  RefreshCcw, Clock, CheckCircle2, XCircle, TrendingDown,
} from 'lucide-react'
import type {
  AinaMarekebishoStoki,
  HaliMarekebisho,
} from '../../types'

// ============================================================
// AINA META
// ============================================================
export const AINA_META: Record<
  AinaMarekebishoStoki,
  { label: string; color: string; Icon: React.ElementType; desc: string }
> = {
  mauzo: {
    label: 'Mauzo',
    color: 'text-brand-700 border-brand-200',
    Icon: ShoppingBag,
    desc: 'Bidhaa imeuzwa',
  },
  uharibifu: {
    label: 'Uharibifu',
    color: 'text-red-700 border-red-200',
    Icon: AlertTriangle,
    desc: 'Bidhaa imeharibika (mfano: saruji imekauka)',
  },
  expiry: {
    label: 'Expiry',
    color: 'text-amber-700 border-amber-200',
    Icon: Calendar,
    desc: 'Bidhaa imeisha muda (mfano: rangi imeganda)',
  },
  upotevu: {

    color: 'text-orange-700 border-orange-200',

    desc: 'Bidhaa imepotea (wizi, hesabu mbaya)',
  },
  kurudisha_msambazaji: {

    Icon: RotateCcw,
    desc: 'Bidhaa inarudishwa kwa msambazaji',
  },
  marekebisho: {
    label: 'Marekebisho',
    color: 'text-slate-700 border-slate-200',
    Icon: RefreshCcw,
    desc: 'Marekebisho ya stoki',
  },
}

// ============================================================
// HALI META
// ============================================================
export const HALI_META: Record<
  HaliMarekebisho,
  { label: string; Icon: React.ElementType; color: string }
> = {
  inasubiri: { label: 'Inasubiri', Icon: Clock, color: 'text-amber-700' },
  imethibitishwa: { label: 'Imethibitishwa', Icon: CheckCircle2, color: 'text-emerald-700' },
  imekataliwa: { label: 'Imekataliwa', Icon: XCircle, color: 'text-red-700' },
}

// ============================================================
// STAT CARD (nyeupe + accent border-l)
// ============================================================
type Accent = 'brand' | 'emerald' | 'amber' | 'red'
const ACCENT_BORDER: Record<Accent, string> = {
  brand: 'border-l-brand-500',
  emerald: 'border-l-emerald-500',
  amber: 'border-l-amber-500',
  red: 'border-l-red-500',
}
const ACCENT_TEXT: Record<Accent, string> = {
  brand: 'text-brand-700',
  emerald: 'text-emerald-700',
  amber: 'text-amber-700',
  red: 'text-red-700',
}

export function StokiStat({
  label,
  value,
  Icon,
  accent,
  sub,
}: {
  label: string
  value: string
  Icon: React.ElementType
  accent: Accent
  sub?: string
}) {
  return (
    <div
      className={`bg-white rounded-xl border border-slate-200 border-l-4 ${ACCENT_BORDER[accent]} p-4`}
    >
      <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
        <Icon size={16} className={ACCENT_TEXT[accent]} />
        <div className="text-xs text-slate-500">{label}</div>
      </div>
      <div className="text-base md:text-lg font-bold mt-2 truncate text-slate-900">
        {value}
      </div>
      {sub && <div className="text-[10px] text-slate-500 truncate">{sub}</div>}
    </div>
  )
}

export { TrendingDown }
